### [1] [Free Energy-Driven Reinforcement Learning with Adaptive Advantage Shaping for Unsupervised Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04065)
*Yiming Huang,Zhenbo Shi,Xin-Cheng Wen,Jichuan Zeng,Cuiyun Gao,Peiyi Han,Chuanyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FREIA是一种新型无监督强化学习算法，通过自由能驱动的奖励机制和自适应优势调整来解决策略优化误导问题，在多个推理任务中展现了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督强化学习方法缺乏对模型推理能力演化的自适应能力，在缺乏真实监督的情况下容易误导策略优化方向。

Method: FREIA算法包含两个核心创新：(1) Free Energy-Driven Reward (FER)基于自由能原理，自适应调整奖励以平衡共识与探索；(2) Adaptive Advantage Shaping (AAS)基于采样奖励的统计特征，自适应调整学习信号。

Result: 在9个数据集、3类推理任务的评估中，FREIA优于其他无监督RL基线方法。在数学推理任务中，使用DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B模型，FREIA相比其他方法平均提升0.5至3.5个Pass@1分数。

Conclusion: FREIA通过结合自由能驱动的奖励机制和自适应优势调整，有效解决了无监督强化学习方法缺乏自适应能力的核心问题，在数学推理等任务上实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Unsupervised reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling self-improvement in large language models (LLMs). However, existing unsupervised RL-based methods often lack the capacity to adapt to the model's evolving reasoning capabilities during training. Therefore, these methods can misdirect policy optimization in the absence of ground-truth supervision. To address this issue, we introduce FREIA, a novel RL-based algorithm built on two key innovations: (1) Free Energy-Driven Reward (FER) adapts rewards to balance consensus and exploration based on the Free Energy Principle. (2) Adaptive Advantage Shaping (AAS) adaptively adjusts learning signals based on the statistical characteristics of sampled rewards. Empirical evaluations on nine datasets across three reasoning tasks showcase that FREIA outperforms other unsupervised RL-based baselines. Notably, in mathematical reasoning tasks, FREIA surpasses other methods by an average of 0.5 to 3.5 points in Pass@1 using the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B model.

</details>


### [2] [Adapt to Thrive! Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization for Improved LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04066)
*Yiming Huang,Zhenbo Shi,Shuzheng Gao,Cuiyun Gao,Peiyi Han,Chuanyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出APMPO方法，通过幂均值策略优化和自适应裁剪机制，解决了强化学习式可验证奖励（RLVR）中静态策略优化与模型能力演化不匹配的问题，在多个推理任务上取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法采用静态策略优化方案，无法与模型不断演化的推理能力相匹配，导致学习动态和推理性能受限。

Method: 提出自适应幂均值策略优化(APMPO)，包含两个核心组件：1) PMPO（幂均值策略优化）：引入广义幂均值目标，实现从算术均值（信号放大）到几何均值（一致性强化）的自适应过渡；2) FAC（反馈自适应裁剪）：基于实时奖励统计信息自适应调整裁剪边界，克服静态机制的局限性。

Result: 在3个推理任务的9个数据集上验证了APMPO的优越性。例如，在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，APMPO相比GRRO在数学推理基准测试上平均Pass@1分数提升3.0个百分点。

Conclusion: APMPO通过结合PMPO的广义幂均值目标与FAC的自适应裁剪机制，有效解决了静态策略优化与模型推理能力演化之间的不匹配问题，显著提升了LLM的推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is an essential paradigm that enhances the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on static policy optimization schemes that misalign with the model's evolving reasoning capabilities. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Power-Mean Policy Optimization (APMPO), which comprises two main innovations: Power-Mean Policy Optimization (PMPO) and Feedback-Adaptive Clipping (FAC). Specifically, PMPO introduces a generalized power-mean objective. This enables the model to adaptively transition from the signal-amplifying behavior of the arithmetic mean to the consistency-enforcing behavior of the geometric mean. FAC adaptively adjusts clipping bounds based on real-time reward statistics to overcome the limitations of static mechanisms. Capitalizing on these innovations, APMPO improves learning dynamics and reasoning performance. Extensive experiments on nine datasets across three reasoning tasks showcase the superiority of APMPO over state-of-the-art RLVR-based baselines. For instance, APMPO boosts the average Pass@1 score on mathematical reasoning benchmarks by 3.0 points compared to GRPO when using Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [3] [Connecting online criminal behavior with machine learning: Using authorship attribution to analyze and link potential online traffickers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04080)
*Vageesh Kumar Saxena*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将数据驱动的机器学习方法应用于非法在线市场（如人口贩运、违禁品交易）的分析，核心发现是：即使匿名并频繁更换身份，犯罪者在广告文本撰写风格与图像展示方式上仍会保留稳定的行为指纹。据此可以在大规模广告语料上实现相关账号的自动关联与跨平台重复行为识别，同时提出保障隐私、公平与透明的责任使用准则，为执法部门提供可操作的分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量非法活动已迁移到在线平台，犯罪者普遍使用匿名账号并频繁更换身份，使执法机构难以判断非法网络/市场的真实规模，也难以判断不同线上档案（账号、商家、广告）之间是否属于同一主体。由于传统线索（支付信息、IP、通讯关系）常被刻意规避或不可获取，需要从内容与行为层面寻找可量化的、可跨账号保持一致性的特征，从而支撑网络规模评估与账号链接；同时，随着此类监测技术的应用范围扩大，如何在效果与公民隐私之间取得平衡、如何避免对弱势群体与边缘社群的误伤、如何保证方法与结论的可解释与可审计，也成为必须同步回答的方法论问题。

Method: 1）大规模数据采集与语料构建：收集非法在线市场中的广告帖子（文本 + 图片）构成分析语料，覆盖多个平台/市场；2）特征工程：提取书写与表达层面的风格特征（如措辞习惯、标点与排版偏好、模板化结构、词汇选择、语法与拼写模式），并结合图像呈现方面的视觉风格特征（排版布局、图像组织与呈现偏好等）以刻画“行为指纹”；3）建模与训练：采用监督式机器学习分类器，从单个账号的历史行为预测其风格/身份归属特征，并评估这些特征在时间与跨平台迁移下的稳定性；4）账号链接与实体解析：以风格相似度/分类输出作为链接信号，把疑似同一经营者的不同账号、不同市场中的重复广告与重复行为聚类成实体，从而恢复隐藏的关联网络与市场结构；5）伦理与治理层面：提出配套的原则性使用规范与操作准则，涵盖数据最小化与匿名性保护、算法公平性（偏差与误判影响评估）、方法与结果的透明度与可解释性、以及应用边界与问责机制。

Result: 研究显示，个体在广告文本的写作方式与图像的呈现方式上表现出高度一致性与可辨识的模式，且这些模式在其主动试图匿名、跨账号或跨平台活动时仍然保留；因此基于这些模式可以在大规模广告集合上可靠地链接相关账号、发现重复发布的广告与重复的犯罪行为，并估算非法网络/市场的规模与结构，说明内容与行为特征可作为匿名身份的“伪影”被有效利用。在方法层面，作者同时强调此类识别技术必须结合责任使用规范，才能避免隐私侵害与不公平误判。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，写作与视觉展示中的行为一致性能作为连接匿名账号的有效信号，数据驱动方法可为执法部门理解非法在线网络的规模、结构和跨市场关联提供切实可行的技术支撑；同时，作者主张此类方法必须以负责任的方式部署——确立明确的隐私保护、公平性与透明度要求及使用边界（而非无条件、无限度的全域监控），在实用效能与公民权利保护之间取得可论证的平衡。

Abstract: This research investigated how online criminal activities can be better understood and connected using data-driven machine learning methods. Many illegal activities, such as human trafficking and illicit trade, have moved to online platforms where offenders hide behind anonymous accounts and frequently change identities. This makes it difficult for authorities to understand how large these networks are and how different online profiles may be linked.
  The research shows that people tend to maintain consistent patterns in how they write advertisements and present images online, even when they try to stay anonymous. By analysing these patterns across large collections of online advertisements, the research demonstrates how to link related accounts and identify repeated behaviour across illegal online markets.
  In addition, the research also addresses how such methods should be used responsibly. It proposes clear guidelines to ensure that privacy, fairness, and transparency are respected when these tools are applied. Overall, the research provides practical ways to support law enforcement investigations while emphasising careful and ethical use.

</details>


### [4] [FMI_SU_Yotkova_Kastreva at SemEval-2026 Task 13: Lightweight Detection of LLM-Generated Code via Stylometric Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04157)
*Elitsa Yotkova,Violeta Kastreva,Dimitar Dimitrov,Ivan Koychev,Preslav Nakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种机器生成代码检测的轻量级方法，通过比率特征、解析引擎、语言分类器和代码-文本行分类器提取信号，结合决策树与启发式规则，在保持高效率（CPU训练、即时推理）的同时实现跨语言和跨领域的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 13旨在跨多种编程语言和应用场景检测机器生成的代码，要求参赛系统能够泛化到未见过的语言和领域。

Method: 采用预训练代码编码器与轻量级特征方法相结合，设计对代码片段长度不敏感的比率特征，利用解析引擎和编程语言分类器提取描述性信号，训练独立的代码-文本行分类器识别自然语言片段，最终通过浅层决策树与启发式规则的组合产生预测结果。

Result: 未明确提及具体性能指标。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种轻量级替代方案，无需大型预训练模型的计算资源，仅使用CPU即可完成训练，实现近乎即时的推理速度。

Abstract: SemEval-2026 Task 13 investigates machine-generated code detection across multiple programming languages and application scenarios, asking participating systems to generalize to unseen languages and domains. This paper describes our participation in Subtask A (binary classification) and explores both pretrained code encoders and lightweight feature-based methods. We design ratio-based features that are less sensitive to snippet length. To support the extraction of descriptiveness-related signals, we use parsing engines and a programming-language classifier. Additionally, we train a separate code-vs-text line classifier to identify raw natural language segments embedded within samples. We combine a shallow decision tree with heuristic rules derived from data analysis to produce the final predictions. Our approach is computationally efficient, requires only CPU resources for training, and achieves near-instant inference time, offering a lightweight alternative to large pretrained models.

</details>


### [5] [Not All That Is Fluent Is Factual: Investigating Hallucinations of Large Language Models in Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04171)
*Humam Khan,Md Tabrez Nafis,Shahab Saquib Sohail,Aqeel Khalique,Rehan Hasan Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了四种主流LLM（ChatGPT、Grok、Gemini、Copilot）在学术写作中的幻觉问题，通过80个不同类型的提示词进行测试，并提出了一种新的加权指标——幻觉指数（HI）来量化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在学术内容生成中存在幻觉问题，可能导致学术不端或误导性信息的传播，因此有必要系统性地评估和比较不同LLM在学术写作任务中的表现。

Method: 设计了80个提示词，涵盖四类任务：参考文献生成、事实解释、摘要生成和写作改进；采用0-5分的评分标准评估事实准确性、参考文献有效性、连贯性、风格一致性和学术语气；引入了一种新的加权指标——幻觉指数（HI）。

Result: Grok和Copilot在参考文献生成任务上表现更好（HI分别为0.67和0.70），但处理摘要或风格类提示时存在困难；Gemini和ChatGPT的语气控制更强，但在事实性写作任务上表现不佳，幻觉风险更高（HI分别为0.53和0.57）。

Conclusion: LLM的幻觉行为不仅取决于模型架构，还与任务类型和提示条件密切相关；研究为后续研究者提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language models (LLMs) show extraordinary abilities, but they are still prone to hallucinations, especially when we use them for generating Academic content. We have investigated four popular LLMs, ChatGPT, Grok, Gemini, and Copilot for hallucinations specifically for academic writing. We have designed 80 prompts across four categories, namely, reference generation, factual explanation, abstract generation, and writing improvement. We evaluated the model using a 0-5 rubric score, which checks factual accuracy, reference validity, coherence, style consistency, and academic tone. A novel weighted metric, Hallucination Index (HI), was introduced to measure hallucination in the responses generated by the models. Some of the most widely used evaluation metrics often fail to check errors which alter sentiment in machine-translated text. We found that Grok and Copilot perform better on reference generation tasks, but they often struggle with abstract or stylistic prompts, with HI values of 0.67 and 0.70, respectively. Whereas, Gemini and ChatGPT have done well with having stronger tone control, but they lack in writing factual tasks and higher hallucination risk with HI scores of 0.53 and 0.57, respectively. Our study found that hallucination behavior does not depend solely on model architecture but also on the type of task and the prompting conditions we are providing. We propose that our work opens new research dimensions for future researchers.

</details>


### [6] [Are LLMs Ready for Conflict Monitoring? Empirical Evidence from West Africa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04177)
*Hoffmann Muki,Olukunle Owolabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大语言模型在冲突事件分类中的系统性偏差。开源模型存在显著的虚假非法化偏见，而领域适配模型虽能纠正方向性偏差却无法消除行为者偏见。开源模型对词汇扰动高度敏感，领域适配模型则表现出良好的鲁棒性。研究者呼吁开展公平意识微调、强制对抗性评估以及情境特定的人类监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型进入冲突监测领域，理解其输出的系统性扭曲对于人道主义问责至关重要。

Method: 使用ACLED黄金标准数据集（具有多阶段验证），对四款开源模型（Gemma 3 4B、Llama 3.2 3B、Mistral 7B、OLMo 2 7B）和两款领域适配模型（AfroConfliBERT、AfroConfliLLAMA）在尼日利亚和喀麦隆进行冲突事件分类评估。

Result: 开源模型存在显著的"虚假非法化"偏见（Gemma将18.29%合法战斗误分类为针对平民的暴力）；领域适配模型虽在规范性方向性上接近中立，但未能消除基于行为者的选择偏见（尼日利亚中国营行为者比非国家行为者被合法化的比例高出36.5%）；开源模型对地理特定词汇框架脆弱，扰动翻转率高达66.7%；模型通过不忠实的推理虚构来掩盖规范性偏见。

Conclusion: 当前模型尚未准备好用于冲突监测的无监督部署，需要开展公平意识微调、强制对抗性鲁棒性评估以及情境特定的人类监督。

Abstract: As LLMs enter conflict monitoring, understanding systematic distortions in their outputs is critical for humanitarian accountability. We evaluate four vanilla open-weight models Gemma 3 4B, Llama 3.2 3B, Mistral 7B, and OLMo 2 7B and two domain-adapted models, AfroConfliBERT and AfroConfliLLAMA, on Nigeria and Cameroon conflict-event classification against ACLED, a gold-standard dataset with multi-stage verification. We find a bifurcated divergence in normative directionality. Open-weight models exhibit statistically significant False Illegitimation bias: Gemma misclassifies to 18.29% of legitimate battles as civilian-targeted violence while making zero False Legitimation errors. By contrast, AfroConfliBERT and AfroConfliLLAMA achieve near-directional neutrality, with Legitimization Bias differences indistinguishable from zero. Yet domain adaptation does not eliminate actor-based selection bias. Both adapted models show statistically significant actor bias comparable to vanilla LLMs; in Nigeria, state actors are legitimized 36.5% more often than non-state actors in identical tactical contexts. Open-weight outputs are also fragile to geography-specific lexical framing: delegitimizing phrases produce flip rates up to 66.7% in Cameroon and 34.2% in Nigeria, while perturbations salient in one context may not matter in another. Error trace profiling shows models mask normative bias through unfaithful rationale confabulations. In contrast, AfroConfliBERT and AfroConfliLLAMA are largely robust, with near-zero flip rates across perturbation categories. Overall, current models are not ready for unsupervised deployment in conflict monitoring. We call for fairness-aware fine-tuning to reduce actor-based selection bias, mandatory adversarial robustness evaluation against lexical manipulation, and context-specific human-in-the-loop oversight calibrated to regional difficulty.

</details>


### [7] [DoGMaTiQ: Automated Generation of Question-and-Answer Nuggets for Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04458)
*Bryan Li,William Walden,Yu Hou,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Dawn Lawrie,Jame Mayfield,Eugene Yang,Chris Callison-Burch,Laura Dietz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DoGMaTiQ管道，通过文档 grounding、释义聚类和基于质量标准的细粒化子选择三个阶段，自动生成高质量的基于问答的nugget集，以解决nugget评估中需要人工标注的瓶颈问题，并集成AutoArgue框架实现全自动报告评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nugget-based评估框架需要为每个主题手动策划nugget集，这一过程劳动密集且难以扩展到新的信息需求；在跨语言场景下尤为严峻，因为相关信息散布在多语言源文档中。

Method: 提出DoGMaTiQ管道，包含三阶段：(1)基于文档的nugget生成，(2)释义聚类，(3)基于原则性质量标准的nugget子选择。将DoGMaTiQ与AutoArgue评估框架集成，在NeuCLIR和RAGTIME两个跨语言TREC共享任务上进行实验。

Result: DoGMaTiQ生成的nugget与人类在环和全手动判断均表现出强排名相关性，表明其质量可靠。消融分析显示强大的LLM nugget生成器是关键组件，且DoGMaTiQ诱导的系统排名对异常系统具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DoGMaTiQ能够有效自动化QA-based nugget生成流程，显著降低人工标注负担，在跨语言信息检索评估中具有实用价值，为报告评估的未来研究提供了开源代码和 artifacts。

Abstract: Evaluation of long-form, citation-backed reports has lately received significant attention due to the wide-scale adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Core to many evaluation frameworks is the use of atomic facts, or nuggets, to assess a report's coverage of query-relevant information attested in the underlying collection. While nuggets have traditionally been represented as short statements, recent work has used question-answer (QA) representations, enabling fine-grained evaluations that decouple the information need (i.e. the question) from the potentially diverse content that satisfies it (i.e. its answers).
  A persistent challenge for nugget-based evaluation is the need to manually curate sets of nuggets for each topic in a test collection -- a laborious process that scales poorly to novel information needs. This challenge is acute in cross-lingual settings, where information is found in multilingual source documents. Accordingly, we introduce DoGMaTiQ, a pipeline for generating high-quality QA-based nugget sets in three stages: (1) document-grounded nugget generation, (2) paraphrase clustering, and (3) nugget subselection based on principled quality criteria. We integrate DoGMaTiQ nuggets with AutoArgue -- a recent nugget-based evaluation framework -- to enable fully automatic evaluation of generated reports. We conduct extensive experiments on two cross-lingual TREC shared tasks, NeuCLIR and RAGTIME, showing strong rank correlations with both human-in-the-loop and fully manual judgments. Finally, detailed analysis of our pipeline reveals that a strong LLM nugget generator is key, and that the system rankings induced by DoGMaTiQ are robust to outlier systems. We facilitate future research in report evaluation by publicly releasing our code and artifacts at https://github.com/manestay/dogmatiq.

</details>


### [8] [MedFabric and EtHER: A Data-Centric Framework for Word-Level Fabrication Generation and Detection in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04180)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Qian Qian,Xiaofeng Lin,Dongxu Zhang,Jun Han,Zhichao Yang,Davin Hill,Tamer Soliman,Sanjit Singh Batra,Robert Tillman,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对医学大模型中的幻觉问题，提出MedFabric数据集和ETHER检测器，通过数据驱动方法生成保留句法风格的真实医学伪造内容，显著提升词级伪造检测性能超过15%，为医疗AI的事实性检测提供新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学幻觉数据集存在三大缺陷：伪造覆盖范围有限、人类文本与LLM生成文本间存在风格差异、以及幻觉样本合成过程中的分布漂移，难以有效捕捉医学场景下的伪造现象。

Method: 提出数据驱动的管道生成逼真的词汇级伪造内容（MedFabric），并基于该数据集开发ETHER模块化检测器，包含Text2Table分解、词级掩码填充和混合句子对评估三个核心组件。

Result: MedFabric在词级伪造基准测试中超越最先进检测器超过15%，且在不同结构相似性下保持一致性能。

Conclusion: MedFabric为医学领域的事实性检测提供了一个全面可靠的框架，ETHER通过模块化设计实现了超过15%的性能提升，为医疗AI系统的可信度保障提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit strong reasoning and semantic understanding capabilities but often hallucinate in domains that require expert knowledge, among which fabrications, the generation of factually incorrect yet fluent statements, pose the greatest risk in medical contexts. Existing medical hallucination datasets inadequately capture fabrication phenomena due to limited fabrication coverage, stylistic disparities between human and LLM-authored texts, and distributional drift during hallucinated sample synthesis. To address this, we propose a data-centric pipeline to generate realistic and word-level fabrications that preserve syntactic and stylistic fidelity while introducing subtle factual deviations, resulting in MedFabric. Building upon this dataset, we introduce ETHER, a modular word-level fabrication detector integrating Text2Table Decomposition, Word Masking and Filling and Hybrid Sentence Pair Evaluation to enhance factual alignment. Empirical results demonstrate that MedFabric outperforms state-of-the-art detectors by over 15% on word-level fabrication benchmarks while maintaining consistent performance across structural similarities, offering a comprehensive framework for reliable and domain-specific factuality detection.

</details>


### [9] [Storage Is Not Memory: A Retrieval-Centered Architecture for Agent Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04897)
*Joshua Adler,Guy Zehavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: True Memory提出了一种新的agent记忆架构，核心思想是不要在摄取阶段预先提取内容，而是在检索时动态处理。实验表明这种方法在多个基准测试上显著优于现有系统如Mem0、Supermemory等，且仅需单文件SQLite实现，成本效益突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current agent memory systems use extraction at ingestion as the primitive, which discards content before the query is known. This content cannot be recovered at retrieval time, creating a fundamental limitation in memory systems.

Method: Six-layer architecture with multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim. Implemented as a single SQLite file on commodity CPU without external databases, vector indices, graph stores, or GPUs.

Result: True Memory Pro achieves 93.0% on LoCoMo (vs 61.4% Mem0, 65.4% Supermemory, ~71% Zep, 94.5% EverMemOS), 87.8% on LongMemEval, and 76.6% on BEAM-1M (vs 73.9% Hindsight). Ablation study shows 1.3 percentage point spread within top configuration family.

Conclusion: True Memory's architecture demonstrates that preserving events verbatim with multi-stage retrieval significantly outperforms extraction-based approaches. The system achieves strong performance across different scales (from multi-session conversations to 1M-token context) while running on commodity hardware, validating the shift from storage-centric to retrieval-centric design.

Abstract: Extraction at ingestion is the wrong primitive for agent memory: content discarded before the query is known cannot be recovered at retrieval time. We propose True Memory, a six-layer architecture that shifts the center of the system from a storage schema to a multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim. The full system runs as a single SQLite file on commodity CPU with no external database, vector index, graph store, or GPU. On LoCoMo (1,540 questions across 10 multi-session conversations), True Memory Pro reaches 93.0% accuracy (3-run mean) against 61.4% for Mem0, 65.4% for Supermemory, approximately 71% for Zep, and 94.5% for EverMemOS under a matched gpt-4.1-mini answer model. On LongMemEval (500 questions), True Memory Pro reaches 87.8% (3-run mean). On BEAM-1M (700 questions at the 1-million-token scale), True Memory Pro reaches 76.6% (3-run mean), above the prior published result of 73.9% for Hindsight. A 56-configuration ablation shows a 1.3-percentage-point spread within the top-performing configuration family.

</details>


### [10] [The Impact of Vocabulary Overlaps on Knowledge Transfer in Multilingual Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04196)
*Oona Itkonen,Jörg Tiedemann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统实验探讨了多语言神经机器翻译中词汇表类型（联合vs分离）和辅助语言相关程度对知识迁移的影响。研究采用域外实验设置以突出迁移效应，结果表明：领域匹配和语言相关性才是决定迁移效果的关键因素，联合词汇表的优势可能主要源于语言相关性而非词汇本身的重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用联合词汇表进行多语言神经机器翻译，但词汇重叠对模型性能的独立影响缺乏共识，且知识跨语言迁移的真正驱动因素尚不明确。

Method: 设计域外（out-of-domain）实验设置，系统对比联合词汇表与分离词汇表在不同相关程度辅助语言下的表现，变量涵盖词汇重叠度、语言相关性和领域匹配。

Result: 相关语言间更高的词汇重叠确实带来性能提升，但关键发现是：领域匹配和语言相关性比联合词汇表本身更重要。

Conclusion: 领域匹配和语言相关性比共享词汇更重要；联合词汇表的收益主要源于语言相关性而非词汇重叠本身。

Abstract: Knowledge transfer, especially across related languages, has been found beneficial for multilingual neural machine translation (MNMT), but some aspects are still under-explored and deserve further investigation. A joint vocabulary is most often applied to form a uniform word embedding space, but since the impact of a disjoint vocabulary on model performance is far less studied, there is no consensus on how much knowledge transfer is mainly due to vocabulary overlap. In this paper, we present systematic experiments with joint and disjoint vocabularies, and auxiliary languages related and unrelated to the source language. We design this experiment in an out-of-domain setup in order to emphasize transfer and the impact of the auxiliary language. As expected, we yield better results with more extensive vocabulary overlaps typical for related languages, but our experiments also show that domain-match and language relatedness are more important than a joint vocabulary.

</details>


### [11] [TabEmbed: Benchmarking and Learning Generalist Embeddings for Tabular Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04962)
*Minjie Qiang,Mingming Zhang,Xiaoyi Bao,Xing Fu,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Zhongqing Wang,Ningtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对表格数据提出TabBench基准和TabEmbed统一嵌入模型。TabEmbed首次在统一嵌入空间中实现表格分类和检索任务，通过将表格任务重新定义为语义匹配问题，并利用大规模对比学习和硬负样本挖掘策略来捕捉表格的结构和数值特征。实验表明TabEmbed显著超越了现有文本嵌入模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在根本性局限：基于LLM的方法缺乏检索兼容的向量输出，而文本嵌入模型通常无法捕捉表格结构和数值语义，导致表格数据的统一表示学习尚未实现突破。

Method: 将多样化的表格任务重新定义为语义匹配问题，采用大规模对比学习框架，并引入正样本感知硬负样本挖掘策略来识别细微的结构和数值差异。

Result: 在TabBench基准测试中，TabEmbed显著优于最先进的文本嵌入模型。

Conclusion: TabEmbed通过大规模对比学习和正样本感知硬负样本挖掘，在表格分类和检索任务中显著优于现有的文本嵌入模型，为通用表格表示学习建立了新的基准。

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [12] [Nsanku: Evaluating Zero-Shot Translation Performance of LLMs for Ghanaian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04208)
*Stephen E. Moore,Mich-Seth Owusu,Akwasi Asare,Lawrence Adu Gyamfi,Paul Azunre,Joel Budu,Jonathan Asiamah,Elias Dzobo,Kelvin Newman,Edmund O. Benefo,Gerhardt Datsomor,Onesimus Addo Appiah,Ama Branoa Banful,Lucas Woedem Kpatah,Saani Mustapha Deishini,John Ayernor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Nsanku基准，首次系统评估19个大语言模型在43种加纳语言与英语之间的零样本翻译能力。研究发现即使最强模型也未能达到可靠应用标准，揭示了当前LLM在非洲低资源语言处理中的显著局限性，同时为社区提供了可扩展的评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在低资源非洲语言上的翻译能力尚缺乏系统性评估，而加纳作为语言多样性极高的国家，其43种语言的翻译能力评估存在显著空白。

Method: 采用零样本机器翻译评估范式，从YouVersion圣经平台获取每个语言300个句子对，使用BLEU和chrF两项自动评估指标，并引入平均准确率得分和跨语言一致性维度进行综合评估。

Result: gemini-2.5-flash以26.88分位居榜首，claude-sonnet-4-5和gpt-4.1分列二三名；开源模型中kimi-k2-instruct-0905表现最佳；Siwu语表现最好(25.73分)，Nkonya语得分最低(11.65分)；一致性分析显示所有模型和语言均未进入高绩效-高一致性象限。

Conclusion: 当前所有模型均未能在高翻译性能和高一致性之间同时达到"领导者象限"标准，表明大语言模型尚未达到在加纳语言翻译场景中规模化可靠应用的水平，但Nsanku为非洲语言NLP研究提供了可扩展的社区评估基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive multilingual capabilities for well-resourced languages, yet their performance on low-resource African languages remains poorly understood and largely unevaluated. This paper presents Nsanku, a systematic benchmark that evaluates the zero-shot machine translation performance of 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs across 43 Ghanaian languages paired with English. Evaluation sentences were sourced from the YouVersion Bible platform, providing 300 sentence pairs per language. Two complementary automatic metrics are employed: Bilingual Evaluation Understudy (BLEU) and Character n-gram F-Score (chrF), alongside an average accuracy score and a cross-language consistency dimension. Nsanku represents the most comprehensive LLM translation evaluation for Ghanaian languages conducted to date. Results show that gemini-2.5-flash achieves the highest overall average score of 26.88 (BLEU: 24.60, chrF: 29.16), followed by claude-sonnet-4-5 at 24.87 (BLEU: 22.46, chrF: 27.28) and gpt-4.1 at 23.20 (BLEU: 21.15, chrF: 25.24). Among open-weight models, kimi-k2-instruct-0905 leads at an average score of 20.87. A critical finding from the consistency analysis is that no model and no language reached the Leaders quadrant of high performance and high consistency simultaneously, indicating that current LLMs are not yet reliably usable for Ghanaian language translation at scale. Siwu achieved the highest per-language average score at 25.73 while Nkonya scored lowest at 11.65. Nsanku establishes a publicly available, community-extensible evaluation infrastructure for African language NLP research.

</details>


### [13] [Self-Prompting Small Language Models for Privacy-Sensitive Clinical Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04221)
*Yao-Shun Chuang,Tushti Mody,Uday Pratap Singh,Shirindokht Shiraz,Chun-Teh Lee,Ryan Brandon,Muhammad F Walji,Xiaoqian Jiang,Bunmi Tokede*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对牙科临床笔记的命名实体识别任务，提出一种结合自优化提示工程、QLoRA微调与DPO偏好优化的本地部署小型语言模型框架，在1200份标注数据上验证了方法的可行性，Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct经DPO后F1达0.864。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科病历记录高度非结构化、领域特定且涉及隐私，现有NER方法面临挑战，需要可在本地部署的解决方案以保护患者隐私。

Method: 开发本地可部署框架，使小型语言模型能够自生成、验证、细化并评估针对特定实体的提示模板；采用多提示集成推理；使用QLoRA微调及DPO偏好优化；基于1,200份标注牙科病历进行评估。

Result: Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct基线性能最强；经DPO后，Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct分别达到micro/macro F1为0.864/0.837和0.806/0.797；模型性能差异显著，凸显任务特定评估的必要性。

Conclusion: 自动化提示优化结合轻量级偏好后训练可支持本地部署的小型语言模型实现可扩展的临床信息抽取；Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct在DPO后达micro/macro F1为0.864/0.837，Llama-3.1-8B达0.806/0.797；需进行任务特定评估而非依赖通用基准。

Abstract: Clinical named entity recognition from dental progress notes is challenging because documentation is highly unstructured, domain-specific, and often privacy-sensitive. We developed a locally deployable framework that enables small language models to self-generate, verify, refine, and evaluate entity-specific prompts for extracting multiple clinical entities from dental notes. Using 1,200 annotated notes, we evaluated candidate open-weight models with multi-prompt ensemble inference and further adapted selected models using QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization. Model performance varied substantially, highlighting the need for task-specific evaluation rather than reliance on generic benchmarks. Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct achieved the strongest baseline performance. After DPO, Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct achieved micro/macro F1 scores of 0.864/0.837 and 0.806/0.797, respectively. These findings suggest that automated prompt optimization combined with lightweight preference-based post-training can support scalable clinical information extraction using locally deployed small language models.

</details>


### [14] [Material Database Agent: A Multimodal Agentic Framework for Scientific Literature Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04278)
*Achuth Chandrasekhar,Omid Barati Farimani,Radheesh Sharma Meda,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A modular multi-agent system (MDA) that uses multimodal large language models to automatically extract structured data from materials science research papers (PDFs), processing text and figures in parallel through specialized agents to create comprehensive material databases at production scale.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Most experimental details in materials science literature remain buried in text, tables, graphs, and figures. Constructing databases from this data is manual, time-consuming, and hard-to-scale, creating a need for automated, production-scale extraction systems.

Method: A modular, multi-agent system architecture where: (1) article PDFs are processed in parallel into markdown files and figures; (2) multiple sub-agents read markdown files and figures in parallel to assemble sub-databases for each paper; (3) a compilation agent merges sub-databases into a single tabular database. This differs from rule-based approaches or single-pass pipelines.

Result: The system achieves high-speed and accurate information extraction from text and scientific figures, demonstrating feasibility of creating production-scale material databases from research literature.

Conclusion: MDA provides a specialized architecture for materials science literature-to-database transformation, establishing multimodal agentic information extraction as a viable approach for constructing next-generation scientific databases from primary literature.

Abstract: Materials science workflows rely on structured and unstructured data from the vast body of available scientific literature. However, most of the experimental details remain buried in text, tables, graphs and figures. Thus, constructing databases that incorporate this data is a manual, time-consuming, and hard-to-scale process. Multimodal large language models have made it feasible to extract information from text and scientific figures with high speed and accuracy. This opens the possibility of an AI system that can create production-scale material databases. Material Database Agent (MDA) is a modular, multi-agent system architecture for converting research literature into structured databases. MDA accepts article PDFs as input, which are subsequently processed in parallel into markdown files and figures. Multiple sub-agents read these markdown files and figures in parallel to assemble sub-databases for each paper. These sub-databases are then compiled into a single tabular database by an agent. As opposed to using either a rule-based approach or a single-pass pipeline for extracting information, MDA is a specialized architecture for transforming the literature into a database in the field of materials science. More generally, this study provides a basis for positioning multimodal agentic information extraction as a viable means for constructing next-generation scientific databases from the primary literature.

</details>


### [15] [SWAN: Semantic Watermarking with Abstract Meaning Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04305)
*Ziping Ye,Gourab Dey,Christos Christodoulopoulos,Charith Peris,Anil Ramakrishna,Weitong Ruan,Aram Galstyan,Kai-Wei Chang,Rahul Gupta,Ninareh Mehrabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWAN，一个利用 AMR 将水印签名嵌入句子语义结构的新框架。由于签名编码在语义层面，任何保持原意的 paraphrase 都会自动保留签名，无需训练即可实现水印注入与检测，在 paraphrase 攻击下仍能保持有效的水印检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本水印方法通常通过在文本生成过程中调整 token 选择偏好来编码签名，这种方式容易被 paraphrase 攻击破坏，导致水印检测失效。

Method: SWAN 是一个无需训练的框架。通过提示 LLM 根据选定的 AMR 模板生成句子，同时保持上下文连贯性来完成水印注入。检测阶段使用现成的 AMR 解析器进行解析，然后进行简单的单比例 z 检验。

Result: SWAN 在未修改的水印文本上达到了 SOTA 检测性能，同时显著提升了抗 paraphrase 鲁棒性，检测 AUC 相比先前方法最高提升了 13.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将水印锚定在AMR语义结构中，为抗 paraphrasing 的文本来源验证提供了一种简单、有效且基于 prompt 的方法，开辟了语义级水印研究的新方向。

Abstract: We introduce SWAN (Semantic Watermarking with Abstract Meaning Representation), a novel framework that embeds watermark signatures into the semantic structure of a sentence using Abstract Meaning Representation (AMR). In contrast to existing watermarking methods, which typically encode signatures by adjusting token selection preferences during text generation, SWAN embeds the signature directly in the sentence's semantic representation. As the signature is encoded at the semantic structure level, any paraphrase that preserves meaning automatically preserves the signature. SWAN is training-free: watermark injection is achieved by prompting an LLM to generate sentences guided by a selected AMR template while maintaining contextual coherence, and detection uses an off-the-shelf AMR parser followed by a simple one-proportion z-test. Empirical evaluation on the RealNews benchmark shows SWAN matches state-of-the-art detection performance on unaltered watermarked text, while significantly improving robustness against paraphrasing, increasing detection AUC by up to 13.9 percentage points compared to prior methods. These results demonstrate that SWAN's approach of anchoring watermarks in AMR semantic structures provides a simple, effective, and prompt-based method for robust text provenance verification under paraphrasing, opening new avenues for semantic-level watermarking research.

</details>


### [16] [Towards Self-Referential Analytic Assessment: A Profile-Based Approach to L2 Writing Evaluation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04298)
*Stefano Bannò,Kate Knill,Mark Gales*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对自动作文评分（AES）中传统排名相关指标忽视作文评分维度间的内在关联性和晕轮效应的问题，提出了一种新的自我参照评估框架，聚焦于识别学习者内部的优劣势而非学习者间的排名比较。通过对ICNALE GRA数据集应用双面Rasch模型校正评分者严厉度，比较了人类评分员与三个大语言模型在零样本设置下的分析性评分表现，发现LLM在识别相对弱点方面优于人类评分员，而人类在识别相对优势方面仍具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES研究依赖排名相关性指标验证评分效果，但这类指标存在局限性：掩盖了写作能力结构固有的分析维度间相关性，以及评分者印象泛化导致的晕轮效应，导致高相关性可能掩盖系统的真实诊断行为。因此需要新的评估框架来揭示LLM在精细化分析性评分中的实际表现。

Method: 采用ICNALE GRA数据集（包含80名训练有素的评分者对二语写作的整全性和分析性标注），应用双面Rasch模型校正评分者严厉度并推导10个分析维度及整全性熟练度的公平平均分作为可靠参考分数。在零样本设置下比较三类LLM与人类运营评分员的分析性评分表现。

Result: LLM在识别多个熟练度维度的相对弱点（负面反馈）方面优于单个人类评分员，而人类评分员在识别相对优势（正面反馈）方面仍具优势。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了排名相关性指标在分析性评估中的局限性，论证了学习者内部轮廓分析方法的评估价值，为AES中LLM的评估与部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) research often relies on rank-based correlation metrics to validate analytic assessment. However, such metrics obscure both intrinsic intercorrelations among analytic dimensions that arise from the structure of writing proficiency itself and halo effects, whereby holistic impressions bleed into fine-grained component scores. As a result, high correlations may mask a system's true diagnostic behaviour. In this study, we propose a novel self-referential assessment evaluation framework that focuses on identifying intra-learner strengths and weaknesses rather than assessing inter-learner rankings. We conduct experiments on the publicly available ICNALE GRA, a uniquely dense second-language writing dataset annotated holistically and analytically by up to 80 trained raters. To obtain reliable reference scores, we apply two-facet Rasch modelling to calibrate rater severity and derive fair average scores across ten analytic aspects and holistic proficiency. We compare the analytic scoring performance of human operational raters and three large language models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting. Our results show that LLMs tend to outperform single human raters in identifying relative weaknesses (negative feedback) across several proficiency aspects, while human raters remain stronger at identifying relative strengths (positive feedback). Overall, our findings highlight the limitations of rank-based evaluation for analytic assessment and demonstrate the value of intra-learner, profile-based methods for assessing and deploying LLMs in AES.

</details>


### [17] [Anticipating Innovation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04875)
*Enrico Maria Fenoaltea,Filippo Santoro,Giordano De Marzo,Segun Taofeek Aroyehun,Andrea Tacchella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 TechToken：把国际专利分类（IPC）代码作为“技术词元”直接加入语言模型词表，在专利文本上微调，使注意力机制在专利文字与 IPC 代码 token 之间建立连接；通过“上下文相似度（CS）”指标度量两类技术语言的趋同，可在技术首次结合前数十年预测新的 IPC 共现组合，并在 IPC 分类、引文预测、标题-摘要匹配等专利任务上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创新预测存在一个根本张力：真正的创新按定义是前所未有的，但政策与企业战略需要提前预判技术变迁。熊彼特以来的“重组创新理论”认为创新是既有要素的新组合，库夫曼的“临近可能”（adjacent possible）与 Polya  urn 模型则把问题转化为：哪些既有技术组最可能结合。专利数据天然记录了重组事件（每件专利链接一组 IPC 代码），首次共现即是一个此前不可实现组合的实现。因此任务从“预测前所未有的事”变为“基于趋同模式预测哪些组合将被实现”，可自然建模为技术代码网络上的链接预测。已有工作（网络拓扑、文献计量、Word2Vec 共现序列嵌入、主题模型）只能使用静态、粗粒度的共现结构，无法刻画围绕技术的语言语境的语义演化，因此作者希望检验：技术的“临近可能”是否在语言中留下可测量的早期痕迹——即在两技术被组合之前，其各自所处的语言语境已经先开始趋同。

Method: 方法分三部分。(1) TechToken 模型：扩展预训练 BERT 的词表，为每个 IPC group 级代码（截断至 group 层，7200 个）新增一个技术 token（TT）；以 [CLS] title [SEP] abstract [SEP] TT1…TTn 的形式做标准掩码语言建模微调，使注意力在专利文本词元与 IPC token 之间、以及 IPC token 彼此之间建立连接；推理时从最后隐藏层读取每个专利中对应 TT 的向量，从而为每个代码在每篇专利中获得语境相关嵌入，捕捉技术的一词多义。(2) 上下文相似度 CS：平均嵌入法下 CS 即两代码平均专利嵌入的余弦相似度；TechToken 下 CS 取两代码所有 token 嵌入对中最高相似度前 1%（top-x%，主文 x=1%）的均值，以聚焦最相关的语义区域、降低异质语境带来的偏差。(3) 严格创新定义与评估：以 Chung-Lu 随机二部图零模型（专利-技术网络，度序列保持）计算期望共现 E 与标准差 σ，得 z 分数 Z=(O−E)/σ，仅将 z 分数最高的 K 对代码标为“已实现创新”，从而固定类别不平衡比并与不同年份稳健比较；预测实验为按时间滚动窗口（2006–2010 计算 CS，预测 2011–2015 起逐年平移的 5 年窗口）的 AUC-ROC，并做严格样本外对照（2019–2023 计算 CS，2024 年专利为测试集，所有模型均未在测试数据上微调）。基线包括 BERT4Patents、Mirror-BERT 继续微调、LLM2Vec+LoRA 微调的 LLaMA 3.1 8B、以及 TechToken-CLS 变体；此外在 IPC 分类（token 概率阈值 vs kNN）、引文预测、标题-摘要匹配三个专利任务上与 PatentSBERTa、Paecter 等 SOTA 比较。数据为欧洲专利公报（EPAB）1980–2024 年、仅英文、摘要与权利要求齐全的 1,302,500 件专利；微调使用 1980–2005 年专利，预测使用 2006–2024 年。

Result: 1) 三个 2020 年首次共现的代码对案例显示：CS 在共现前数年即开始上升并超出随机对分布的 3σ 带；全样本上首次共现的平均 CS 信号平均提前约 20 年出现，2006 年窗口已显著高于随机对置信区间，并在共现当年出现跳变。2) 严格样本外对比（2019–2023 → 2024）中，TechToken BERT (IPC) AUC-ROC 在 CI=0.005%/0.004%/0.003%/0.0025% 下分别为 0.936/0.938/0.940/0.940，显著高于 LLaMA Patents (0.856/0.832/0.846/0.840)、BERT4Patents FT (0.765…) 与 BERT4Patents (0.725…)；TechToken CLS 亦达 0.908/0.911/0.903/0.904，说明增益不仅来自模型规模（BERT 规模模型击败约 25 倍参数的 LLaMA 3.1 8B），也来自 IPC token 嵌入本身；类别不平衡越小（创新定义越严格）TechToken 相对优势越大，表明其捕捉的是技术语义而非专利量趋势。3) 通用专利任务上 TechToken 全面领先：IPC 分类 Macro F1 0.488 / Micro F1 0.449（对比 Paecter 0.420/0.378、PatentSBERTa 0.356/0.303），引文预测 RFR 1.59、MAP 59.73、MRR@10 79.70，在 Paecter 数据集上继续微调后达 RFR 1.26、MAP 68.96、MRR@10 89.54；标题-摘要匹配 AUC-ROC 0.994、MRR 0.903（仅 MRR 略低于 PatentSBERTa 的 0.912）。

Conclusion: 核心结论：技术趋同之前先发生语义趋同，且语义趋同是可在语料整体层面测量的涌现现象——不能定位到任何单篇专利或单个发明人，只能从上千名独立行动者的分布式书写漂移中观察到；即“临近可能并非沉默，它在实现前数十年就于发明者的集体话语中留下可读的痕迹”，因此专利集体语言是技术变迁的领先指标。方法论上，将受控词表中的符号化分类代码嵌入语言模型词表是一种普适配方，可迁移到论文-MeSH/arXiv 类别、商品-HS 编码等场景，让注意力显式连接文本与结构化标签，显著提升内部表示质量。局限与展望：仅在 IPC group 级（粗粒度，组内重组不可见）、仅考虑二元组合（未处理三技术及以上的高阶组装），未来可在同一逻辑下细化到关键词/术语/概念层级的收敛与多技术同步对齐，绘制更完整的技术前沿图谱；对创新政策与企业研发战略，该方法提供了原则化、早期且有实证基础的“预判重组”窗口。

Abstract: Forecasting innovation, intended as the emergence of new technological combinations, is a fundamental challenge for science and policy. We show that forthcoming combinations leave an early trace in the collective language of patents, with predictive signals detectable even decades in advance. We show that signal is not attributable to any single inventor, but emerges as a collective shift in how technologies are described across thousands of patents. To this end, we introduce TechToken, a transformer-based model that treats technologies, classified by International Patent Classification codes, as words in its vocabulary, learning the language of technologies by embedding these codes during fine-tuning. We define context similarity between code embeddings as a measure of linguistic convergence and show that it accurately predicts first technological combinations. TechToken also improves general representation quality, outperforming state-of-the-art models across different patent-related tasks.

</details>


### [18] [NoisyCausal: A Benchmark for Evaluating Causal Reasoning Under Structured Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04313)
*Zhi Xu,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NoisyCausal基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在结构化噪声下的因果推理能力，并设计了一种将LLM与显式因果结构相结合的模块化推理框架。该方法通过从上下文提取变量、构建因果图并将推理任务重构为基于图的提示，显著提升了LLM的因果推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在因果推理中存在核心缺陷：难以区分相关性与因果性，且当观察数据存在噪声、包含无关干扰信息或部分不可观测时表现显著下降。这限制了其在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 提出两大贡献：(1) NoisyCausal基准——基于真实因果图生成，注入可控噪声（无关干扰项、值扰动、混杂偏倚、部分可观测性）；(2) 模块化推理框架——引导LLM提取变量、从上下文构建因果图、将推理任务重构为基于该图的结构化提示，而非依赖统计模式。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在NoisyCausal上显著优于标准提示和推理基线，并可零样本泛化至Cladder等外部基准，无需任务特定调优。

Conclusion: 将因果抽象与语言驱动推理相结合是实现LLM忠实、鲁棒因果理解的关键路径。符号化因果结构的引入增强了可解释性和推理稳健性。

Abstract: Causal reasoning in natural language requires identifying relevant variables, understanding their interactions, and reasoning about effects and interventions, often under noisy or ambiguous conditions. While large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong general reasoning abilities, they struggle to disentangle correlation from causation, particularly when observations are partially incorrect or irrelevant information is present. In this work, we introduce NoisyCausal, a new benchmark designed to evaluate causal reasoning under structured noise. Each instance is generated from a ground-truth causal graph and contextualized with a natural language scenario by injecting controllable forms of noise, such as irrelevant distractors, value perturbations, confounding, and partial observability. Moreover, we propose a modular reasoning framework that combines LLMs with explicit causal structure to address these challenges. Our method prompts the LLM to extract variables, construct a causal graph from context, and then reformulates the reasoning task as a structured prompt grounded in this graph. Rather than relying on statistical patterns alone, the LLM is guided by symbolic structure, enabling more interpretable and robust inference. Experimental results show that our method significantly outperforms standard prompting and reasoning baselines on NoisyCausal. Furthermore, it generalizes well to external benchmarks such as Cladder without task-specific tuning. Our findings highlight the importance of combining causal abstractions with language-driven reasoning to achieve faithful and robust causal understanding in LLMs.

</details>


### [19] [Misaligned by Reward: Socially Undesirable Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05003)
*Gayane Ghazaryan,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将奖励模型评估扩展到偏见、安全、道德和伦理推理四个社会重要领域，发现现有奖励模型普遍缺乏社会智能，经常偏好社会不良响应，并揭示了避免偏见与保持上下文敏感性之间的对齐权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要关注广泛指令遵循基准，对模型是否捕捉社会期望偏好的洞察有限，导致重要的社会对齐失败可能被隐藏。

Method: 将社会评估数据集转换为成对偏好数据，利用金标签和方向性偏见指标，测试奖励模型是否偏好社会不良响应，以及其偏好是否产生系统性偏见分布。

Result: 在5个公开奖励模型和2个指令微调模型中发现：各领域间存在显著差异；无单一模型整体表现最佳；模型普遍偏好社会不良选项；产生系统性偏见分布；更强的偏见规避会降低对上下文的敏感性，形成避免偏见结果与保持上下文忠实性之间的关键对齐权衡。

Conclusion: 标准奖励模型基准测试不足以评估社会对齐，需要直接测量奖励模型中编码的社会偏好的评估方法。研究强调了未来开发更全面的社会对齐评估框架的必要性。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of large language model alignment, serving as proxies for human preferences during training. However, existing evaluations focus primarily on broad instruction-following benchmarks, providing limited insight into whether these models capture socially desirable preferences. As a result, important failures in social alignment can remain hidden.
  We extend reward-model benchmarking to four socially consequential domains: bias, safety, morality, and ethical reasoning. We introduce a framework that converts social evaluation datasets into pairwise preference data, leveraging gold labels where available and directional bias indicators otherwise. This enables us to test whether reward models prefer socially undesirable responses, and whether their preferences produce systematically biased distributions over selected outputs.
  Across five publicly available reward models and two instruction-tuned models used as reward proxies, we find substantial variation across domains, with no single model performing best overall. The models fall well short of strong social intelligence: they often prefer socially undesirable options, and their preferences produce systematically biased distributions. Moreover, stronger bias avoidance can reduce sensitivity to context, revealing a key alignment trade-off between avoiding biased outcomes and preserving contextual faithfulness. These findings show that standard reward benchmarks are insufficient for assessing social alignment and highlight the need for evaluations that directly measure the social preferences encoded in reward models.

</details>


### [20] [Telegraph English: Semantic Prompt Compression via Structured Symbolic Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04426)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Sisong Bei,Ziwei Dong,Dmitri Kalaev,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Telegraph English是一种新型提示压缩协议，通过语义重写而非token删除实现压缩，将输入分解为原子事实行并用符号替换冗长短语，压缩比自适应且输出同时具备语义索引功能。在保持99.1%准确率的同时全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token删除方法（如LLMLingua-2）以固定比例删除低重要性token，无法适应不同文档的信息密度，且丢失了结构化语义关系。

Method: TE采用全语义重写方法：将输入分解为原子事实行，用约40个逻辑/关系符号替换冗长短语，压缩比自适应于文档信息密度。压缩结果天然形成语义索引。

Result: 在4,081个LongBench-v2问答对上测试5个OpenAI模型，TE在50%压缩率下保持99.1%准确率，全面优于LLMLingua-2；小模型差距更大，表明结构化表示对有限容量模型补偿价值更高。

Conclusion: TE在约50% token reduction下达到99.1% accuracy，关键事实保留率极高；在相同压缩率下全面超越LLMLingua-2；小模型提升更大（最高11个百分点），表明显式关系结构能补偿模型容量不足。

Abstract: We introduce Telegraph English (TE), a prompt-compression protocol that rewrites natural language into a symbol-rich, formally-structured dialect. Where token-deletion methods such as LLMLingua-2 train a classifier to delete low-importance tokens at a fixed ratio, TE performs a full semantic rewrite: it decomposes the input into atomic fact lines, substitutes verbose phrases with $\sim$40 logical and relational symbols, and lets the compression ratio adapt to each document's information density. A consequence of the line-structure rule is that compression and semantic chunking become the same operation -- each output line is an independently addressable fact, so the compressed representation is simultaneously a semantic index. We evaluate TE on 4{,}081 question-answer pairs from LongBench-v2 across five OpenAI models and two difficulty levels. At roughly 50\% token reduction, TE preserves 99.1\% accuracy on key facts with GPT-4.1 and outperforms LLMLingua-2 at matched compression ratios on every model and task tested. The gap widens on smaller models -- up to 11 percentage points on fine-detail tasks -- suggesting that explicit relational structure compensates for limited model capacity. We release the grammar specification, compression prompt, benchmark data, and reference implementation.

</details>


### [21] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念场（Concept Field）方法，在句子嵌入空间中建模语料库的局部漂移场，通过计算连续句子间的增量（delta）来评估新句子的语义一致性与新颖性。该方法具有黑盒性、可归因性和概率可解释性，并引入向量序列数据库（VSDB）支持高效计算。在联邦法规和古腾堡计划数据集上验证了在幻觉检测和新鲜度检测任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前幻觉检测和新鲜度检测方法主要依赖检索或LLM评判，存在：1）检索方法在不同领域表现不一致；2）LLM评判计算成本高且缺乏概率解释；3）缺乏可追溯至语料库具体句子的可解释性机制。研究需要一种轻量级、可解释、跨领域迁移性强的检测方法。

Method: 1）定义概念场为句子嵌入空间中的局部漂移场，带点态不确定性估计；2）通过连续句子间的delta估计局部高斯分布；3）使用ζ分数（观测delta与局部高斯估计的z距离均值绝对值）评估候选句子转换与概念场的一致性；4）设计VSDB存储嵌入、序列位置和增量元数据；5）可选分析：计算概念场的散度和旋度以发现语义模式。

Result: 概念场在两种不同领域（联邦法规和古腾堡文学）上表现出相似的覆盖-风险行为，验证了概率解释的跨领域迁移性。在受控LLM重写测试中实现了强选择性分类性能，优于检索中心基线。定性分析表明散度和旋度可揭示语义源/汇和隐含主题。

Conclusion: 概念场为幻觉检测和新颖性检测提供了一种快速、轻量、可解释的信号，补充而非替代LLM评判和可解释白盒检测器。该方法的核心优势在于概率可读性和跨领域一致性，为概率校准和可控AI系统提供新思路。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [22] [GEM: Graph-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts with ReAct Agents for Dialogue State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04449)
*Ziqi Zhu,Adithya Suresh,Tomal Deb,Iman Abbasnejad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GEM 将图神经网络（GNN/GAT）与微调 T5-Small 组成混合专家（MoE），由基于领域分类器的投票路由器在两个专家间动态选择，再结合 ReAct 智能体与检索增强的 LLM 完成槽位值生成。在 MultiWOZ 2.2 上取得 65.19% JGA / 97.65% JTA，超过 TOATOD（63.79%）等 SOTA 与端到端 LLM 方法（最佳 38.43%），且仅用约 341M 参数专家（每轮 12ms），显著优于 D3ST-xxl 的 11B 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话状态跟踪（DST）要求从多域多轮对话中精确抽取结构化状态，但通用大语言模型虽具备较强语言理解能力，在结构化信息抽取、一致性维护与细粒度槽位预测上仍表现不佳（零样本 JGA 仅 10%–32%），且存在幻觉、推理不可靠与计算开销大的问题。已有知识图谱融合方案（语义解析易产生不可执行查询、RAG/知识图谱提示对结构信息利用不足、基于智能体的方法需多轮交互）各有缺陷。因此需要一种既能建模跨轮次结构关系、又计算高效、同时具备精确值生成能力的 DST 架构。

Method: 框架分为三个模块：(1) 意图与槽位检测双专家——GATv2 图神经网络将对话表示为图 G=(V,E)，节点为含 [USER]/[ASSISTANT] 特殊标记的话语嵌入，边为相邻话语间的有向时序连接，用多层多头图注意力（式1-3）做话语级多标签意图/槽位分类，并联合预测领域；T5-Small（QLoRA 微调）以 seq2seq 方式生成 domain-intent-slot 结构化输出。(2) MoE 路由器——基于 BERT 的多标签领域分类器识别当前轮活跃领域，按各专家在该领域的槽位准确率进行"领域加权投票"（算法1），多数票决定交给 GNN 还是 T5，实现选择性专家激活。(3) 槽位值生成——用 Cohere 嵌入将"上一轮系统回复+当前用户话轮+已识别槽位"编码，在 ChromaDB 中按余弦相似度阈值 τ_sim 检索 TopK 动态少样本示例 E_t；既可用预训练 decoder LLM（Llama 3.1 8B Instruct / Claude 3.7 Sonnet）以少样本提示生成 JSON 槽值对，也可用 ReAct 智能体按 f_analysis→f_reasoning→f_json 的组合式推理链单轮生成，显式提升可解释性。训练损失采用归一化加权多任务目标 L=(α·L_intent+β·L_domain+γ·L_slot)/(α+β+γ)。

Result: 意图/槽位检测：T5-Small 为 86.69/73.76，最优 GNN 配置（BERT-base+GNN-Large+opt，残差连接、3层12头512维、上下文窗口全历史、损失权重 1:0.5:2）为 86.53/74.54。消融显示上下文窗口从 0 轮到 5 轮提升最大（槽位 50.08→71.76），BGE-small/DistilUSE 均逊于 BERT-base，GNN 规模增大主要提升槽位而意图偏好紧凑 Baseline。端到端 LLM：Llama 3.1 8B 零样本 10.33 JGA → 少样本 32.62 → ReAct 37.07；Claude 3.7 零样本 32.50、少样本 38.43、ReAct 37.09。MoE 路由呈明显领域分化：attraction/train 路由至 GNN（91.31%/93.48%），hotel/restaurant/taxi 路由至 T5（89.99%/85.82%/87.27%），路由器准确率 56%–78%。整体 DST：GEM + Claude 3.7 Sonnet 达 65.19 JGA / 97.65 JTA，为全表最佳；GEM + Llama 3.1 8B 为 63.57 JGA；单专家最高 64.66（T5-Small+Claude）。参数量与时延：BERT+GNN 271M/5ms，T5-Small 70M/21ms，混合 341M/12ms；LLM 值生成 Llama 2050ms/轮、Claude 3950ms/轮（48% 差距）。

Conclusion: GEM 证明"结构化图表示 + 动态专家路由 + 智能体式推理"的组合能有效弥补通用 LLM 在 DST 上的短板：以约 341M 参数的专家部分（不到 D3ST-xxl 11B 参数的 1%）取得 65.19% JGA 的新 SOTA，并兼顾部署效率；核心洞见是不同域对话的结构特征差异明显（attraction/train 依赖关系型结构推理，hotel/restaurant/taxi 偏好序列建模），因此按领域选择性激活专家优于统一架构。局限与后续方向：仅在 MultiWOZ 2.2 单数据集验证、路由器仅使用领域特征而未利用对话文本与历史领域/意图/槽位链、LLM 值生成时延仍达秒级且依赖外部大模型调用；未来将做跨数据集泛化评测、以多维特征建模路由器、并蒸馏为小统一模型以适配资源受限场景。

Abstract: Dialogue State Tracking (DST) requires precise extraction of structured information from multi-domain conversations, a task where Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle despite their impressive general capabilities. We present GEM (Graph-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts), a novel framework that combines language models and graph-structured dialogue understanding with ReAct agent-based reasoning for superior DST performance. Our approach dynamically routes between specialized experts: a Graph Neural Network that captures dialogue structure and turn-level dependencies, and a finetuned T5-Small encoder-decoder for sequence modeling, coordinated by an intelligent router. For complex value generation tasks, we integrate ReAct agents that perform structured reasoning over dialogue context. On MultiWOZ 2.2, GEM achieves 65.19% Joint Goal Accuracy, substantially outperforming end-to-end LLM approaches (best: 38.43%) and surpassing state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods including TOATOD (63.79%), D3ST (58.70%), and Diable (56.48%). Our graph-enhanced mixture-of-experts architecture with ReAct integration demonstrates that combining structured dialogue representation with dynamic expert routing and agent-based reasoning provides a powerful paradigm for dialogue state tracking, achieving superior accuracy while maintaining computational efficiency through selective expert activation.

</details>


### [23] [CAR: Query-Guided Confidence-Aware Reranking for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04495)
*Zhipeng Song,Yizhi Zhou,Xiangyu Kong,Jiulong Jiao,Xuezhou Ye,Chunqi Gao,Xueqing Shi,Yuhang Zhou,Heng Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAR 是一种免训练、黑盒可用的 RAG 排序修正层：以「仅查询」条件下的多次采样答案语义一致性作为查询级对照，度量每个候选文档加入后带来的置信度变化（Δ），据此把文档分入提升/保持/降级三类，再以最小 Kendall 距离投影回基线排序，在不必要处保留原顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索与重排方法（BM25、DPR/Contriever、ColBERT、RankT5、YesNo/QLM/RankGPT）优化的是 query-document 相关性，但相关性并不等价于对生成器的有用性：相关文档可能引入歧义或干扰，排名靠后的文档反而能稳定答案。因此需要一个「生成器侧」的有用性信号来补充相关性排序，同时避免粗暴地重排或覆盖检索器/重排器已有的排序信息。

Method: 形式化：给定基线严格全序 π_q^ψ=[d_1..d_n]，目标是「证据约束下的最小扰动排序修正」。信号：采样 k 个答案，按 entailment/contradiction/neutral 判定做语义聚类，稳定性 ĉ=max|C|/k（语义集中度，非正确性）；受控对比 Δ̂(q,d)=ĉ(q,d)−ĉ(q)，以仅查询条件为查询级对照，剔除查询难度混杂。两级约束：查询门控 QT（若 ĉ(q)≥T_q 则完全返回基线，不做任何文档级评估）；置信边际 CM（m>0 时 Δ≥m→+1，−m<Δ<m→0，Δ≤−m→−1；m=0 时退化为按符号的两分箱，零变化按实现约定归入提升箱）。修正：可行集 F_q(b) 要求高标签文档排在低标签文档前，求 argmin d_K(σ, π)，命题 1 证明唯一解为稳定拼接 B+⊕B0⊕B−，即箱内保留基线相对序。性质：恒等性、证据优先、箱内保序、幂等、决策稳定。复杂度：标签已知后 O(n) 构造；样本量 N=k[1+n·1{ĉ(q)<T_q}]，生成与语义判定为主成本；仅适用于 top-n 修正，不适用于语料级检索。实验：BEIR 的 NQ/HotpotQA/FEVER（1:9 验证/测试划分，10 次重复），BM25 与 Contriever top-10，7 种基线排序（Retriever-only、YesNo、QLM、RankGPT、ColBERT、Cross-Encoder、RankT5），生成器 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（另测 GLM-4-9B-Chat、InternLM2.5-7B-Chat），k=10、temperature 1.0；下游用人工对齐的 NQ-a（2,728 条）子集，top-5 输入，token 级 F1。

Result: BM25 主分析：21 个（数据集×排序器）单元格全部非负，平均相对 NDCG@5 提升 +5.53%，绝对 +0.70 点（12 格提升、9 格持平）；YesNo 提升最大（平均 +29.9%，绝对 +3.94 点），QLM 仅 +0.7%，RankGPT +4.0%，监督式重排器最多 +0.1%。Contriever 下 +4.64%，NQ 全部 14 格为正。组件消融：去掉 QT 增益与扰动同时放大且方向一致性变差（HotpotQA/BM25 上 85.7% 的 ranker-run 差值为非正）；CM=0 扰动最小但收益最低；三箱规则在可控扰动下取得更高收益。全分辨率连续排序（按 Δ 直接排序）nKD≈CAR 的 2.1 倍而收益差距仅 0.81 个百分点。鲁棒性：QT 0–1、CM 0–1 全部非负；三个生成器家族 9 个聚合全为正（Qwen +0.96、GLM +1.11、InternLM +1.78）；采样预算 k=2..10 增益 +0.48~+0.87 且总体随 k 上升。下游 NQ-a：token F1 由 14.74→15.17（+0.43 点），7 个方法中 5 个的 95% 区间高于零，排序增益与生成增益 Spearman ρ=0.93。案例：相关文档从第 8 位提升至第 1，负向文档从第 4 降至第 10，查询 NDCG@5 由 0→1。

Conclusion: CAR 提供了一个生成器感知的、免训练、黑盒兼容的保守排序修正层，用「查询条件下语义稳定性的变化」作为文档有用性信号，通过粗粒度前置约束与最小 Kendall 距离投影，在保留检索/重排先验信息的前提下取得稳定非负的排序与生成收益。其边界明确：稳定性不等于正确性（可能稳定地错）、成本随候选深度与采样预算增长、无法补充候选池中缺失的证据且未建模多文档交互。后续方向为融合事实核验/忠实度的信号、边界感知的自适应采样与缓存、以及多文档互补性与冲突建模。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) depends on document ranking to provide useful evidence for generation, but conventional reranking methods mainly optimize query-document relevance rather than generation usefulness. A relevant document may still introduce noise, while a lower-ranked document may better reduce the generator's uncertainty. We propose CAR (Confidence-Aware Reranking), a query-guided, training-free, and plug-and-play reranking framework that uses generator confidence change as a document usefulness signal. CAR estimates confidence through the semantic consistency of multiple sampled answers under query-only and query-document conditions. Documents that significantly increase confidence are promoted, those that decrease confidence are demoted, and uncertain cases preserve the baseline order, while a query-level gate avoids unnecessary intervention on already confident queries. Experiments on four BEIR datasets show that CAR consistently improves NDCG@5 across sparse and dense retrievers, LLM-based and supervised rerankers, and four LLM backbones. Notably, CAR improves the YesNo reranker by 25.4 percent on average under Contriever retrieval, and its ranking gains strongly correlate with downstream generation F1 improvements, achieving Spearman rho = 0.964.

</details>


### [24] [SCOUT: Active Information Foraging for Long-Text Understanding with Decoupled Epistemic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04496)
*Zhenliang Zhang,Wenqing Wang,Yong Hu,Yaming Yang,Jiaheng Gao,Chen Shen,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOUT提出了一种新的长文本理解范式，通过主动信息觅食替代被动处理，在保持与前沿专有模型相当性能的同时，将token消耗降低8倍，并有效缓解了长上下文场景下的成本-性能权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长上下文LLM面临高token消耗和注意力稀释问题，而专门的LTU智能体通过图构建或索引等任务无关的抽象化方法牺牲了推理保真度。

Method: SCOUT将文档视为可探索环境，基于查询充分性原则，通过状态级差距诊断指导，在粗细粒度探索与锚定状态更新之间自适应交替，逐步将认知状态收缩至查询充分的核心信息子集。

Result: SCOUT在匹配SOTA专有模型性能的同时，token消耗降低高达8倍，且随上下文长度增加保持稳定。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过主动信息觅食范式有效缓解了长文本理解中的成本-性能权衡问题，为百万token级别的长文本处理提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-Text Understanding (LTU) at million-token scale requires balancing reasoning fidelity with computational efficiency. Frontier long-context LLMs can process millions of token contexts end-to-end, but they suffer from high token consumption and attention dilution. In parallel, specialized LTU agents often sacrifice fidelity through task-agnostic abstractions like graph construction or indexing. We identify a key insight for LTU: query-relevant information is typically sparse relative to the full document, so effective reasoning should rely on a query-sufficient subset rather than the entire context. To address this, we propose SCOUT, a new paradigm for LTU that shifts from passive processing to active information foraging. It treats the document as an explorable environment and answers from a compact, provenance-grounded epistemic state. Guided by state-level gap diagnosis, SCOUT adaptively alternates between coarse-to-fine exploration and anchored state updates that progressively contract its epistemic state toward query sufficiency. Experiments show that SCOUT matches state-of-the-art proprietary models while reducing token consumption by up to 8x. Moreover, SCOUT remains stable as context length scales, substantially alleviating the practical cost-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [25] [Harnessing Linguistic Dissimilarity for Language Generalization on Unseen Low-Resource Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04500)
*Jinju Kim,Haeji Jung,Youjeong Roh,Jong Hwan Ko,David R. Mortensen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言变体在多语言模型中长期被忽视的问题，提出两阶段语言泛化框架。第一阶段TOPPing专门为低资源变体设计源语言选择方法；第二阶段VACAI-Bowl架构通过双分支设计学习变体特定和变体不变属性，配合对抗训练实现有效跨语言迁移。实验表明该方法在10种低资源变体的依赖解析任务上平均提升54.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言变体在多语言模型开发中长期被忽视。现有跨语言迁移研究主要关注同族语言间的对齐和差异最小化，但低资源变体恰恰需要利用语言差异性来泛化到未见过的变体，这是一个被忽视的重要线索。

Method: 采用两阶段语言泛化框架：第一阶段使用TOPPing进行源语言选择，识别最适合低资源变体的源语言；第二阶段使用VACAI-Bowl轻量级架构，通过双分支设计（一支学习变体特定属性，一支通过对抗训练捕获变体不变属性）实现知识迁移。

Result: 在10种低资源语言变体的依赖解析任务上，使用VACAI-Bowl配合TOPPing获得了平均54.62%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过结合TOPPing源语言选择方法与VACAI-Bowl架构，在10种低资源语言变体的依赖解析任务上实现了平均54.62%的性能提升，有效解决了低资源语言在多语言模型中的适应问题。

Abstract: Low-resource language varieties used by specific groups remain neglected in the development of Multilingual Language Models. A great deal of cross-lingual research focuses on inter-lingual language transfer which strives to align allied varieties and minimize differences between them. However, for low-resource varieties, linguistic dissimilarity is also an important cue allowing generalization to unseen varieties. Unlike prior approaches, we propose a two-stage Language Generalization framework that focuses on capturing variety-specific cues while also exploiting rich overlap offered by high-resource source variety. First, we propose TOPPing, a source-selection method specifically designed for low-resource varieties. Second, we suggest a lightweight VACAI-Bowl architecture that learns variety-specific attributes with one branch while a parallel branch captures variety-invariant attributes using adversarial training. We evaluate our framework on structural prediction tasks, which are among the few tasks available, as proxy for performance on other downstream tasks. Using VACAI-Bowl with TOPPing yields an average 54.62% improvement in the dependency parsing task, which serves as a proxy for performance on other downstream tasks across 10 low-resource varieties.

</details>


### [26] [Distilling Bayesian Belief States into Language Models for Auditable Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04507)
*Zongqi Cui,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BOND通过贝叶斯后验推理和蒸馏技术实现了可审计的谈判agent。8B模型超越70B基线证明信念校准能力不完全取决于模型规模，蒸馏方法有效保留了可评分的信念报告能力，使隐式的弱信念-行动耦合变得可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端大语言模型在模拟谈判对话时，对手信念通常是隐式的，难以检查和审计。这阻碍了谈判agent的透明度和可信度，特别是在需要明确理解对手优先级以做出合理决策的场景中。

Method: BOND框架包含：(1)基于LLM的贝叶斯教师，对话上下文针对六种可能的对手机优先排序进行评分，更新后验分布，用于基于菜单的决策；(2)8B学生语言模型，以标签文本形式输出谈判行动和归一化后验信念。通过蒸馏将教师的信念推理能力传递给学生模型。

Result: 在CaSiNo谈判数据集上，BOND超越最优方法，后验信念的Brier分数达0.085。蒸馏后的学生模型保留了大部分信念信号，Brier分数为0.114，显著低于均匀六排序参考值0.139。相比70B结构化CoT基线，8B学生模型实现了明显更好的后验校准。

Conclusion: 蒸馏过程有效传递了信念报告能力，使弱信念-行动耦合变得可见而非隐藏。8B学生模型显著优于70B结构化CoT基线，证明信念校准与模型规模并非强相关。信念诊断工具揭示了蒸馏保留了更强的可评分信念报告能力。

Abstract: Negotiation agents must infer what their counterpart values, update those beliefs over dialogue turns, and choose actions under uncertainty. End-to-end large language models (LLMs) can imitate negotiation dialogue, but their opponent beliefs are usually implicit and difficult to inspect. We propose BOND (Bayesian Opponent-belief Negotiation Distillation), a framework for auditable negotiation. BOND consists of an LLM-based Bayesian teacher that scores dialogue contexts against the six possible opponent priority orderings, updates a posterior over those orderings, and uses the posterior for menu-based decision making, as well as a smaller 8B student language model that emits both negotiation actions and normalized posterior beliefs as tagged text. In the CaSiNo negotiation dataset, BOND outperforms the state-of-the-art and achieves mean Brier score 0.085 over opponent-priority posteriors. The distilled student preserves much of this belief signal, achieving Brier 0.114, below the uniform six-ordering reference of 5/36, approximately 0.139. Compared with a 70B structured-CoT baseline, the significantly smaller 8B student model yields substantially better elicited posterior calibration. We further showcase auditability through posterior trajectories, belief-versus-policy error decomposition, and posterior-prefix interventions. These diagnostics reveal that distillation preserves a scoreable belief report more strongly than causal belief-conditioned control, making weak belief-action coupling visible, not hidden.

</details>


### [27] [RaguTeam at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Meno and Friends in a Judge-Orchestrated LLM Ensemble for Faithful Multi-Turn Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04523)
*Ivan Bondarenko,Roman Derunets,Oleg Sedukhin,Mikhail Komarov,Ivan Chernov,Mikhail Kulakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SemEval-2026 Task 8 MTRAGEval中Task B（带参考段落的内容生成）的冠军系统，采用7个LLM异构集成+两套提示策略+GPT-4o-mini裁判选择的方案，在26支队伍中排名第一，得分0.7827，显著超过最强基线（0.6390）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 8 MTRAGEval需要评估多轮响应生成质量，Task B涉及基于参考段落的生成任务。

Method: 异构集成方法：7个不同LLM + 两种提示变体 + GPT-4o-mini作为裁判为每个实例选择最佳候选输出。消融实验证明模型家族、规模和提示策略的多样性至关重要。

Result: 1. 排名第一（26支队伍）；2. conditioned harmonic mean达0.7827；3. 超越最强基线gpt-oss-120b（0.6390）；4. 集成始终优于任何单一模型；5. 提出Meno-Lite-0.1（7B参数领域适配模型），具有优秀的成本-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 集成策略的有效性已验证；MTRAGEval存在标注局限性，需要改进；代码已开源（GitHub链接已提供）。

Abstract: We present our winning system for Task~B (generation with reference passages) in SemEval-2026 Task~8: MTRAGEval. Our method is a heterogeneous ensemble of seven LLMs with two prompting variants, where a GPT-4o-mini judge selects the best candidate per instance. We ranked 1st out of 26 teams, achieving a conditioned harmonic mean of 0.7827 and outperforming the strongest baseline (gpt-oss-120b, 0.6390). Ablations show that diversity in model families, scales, and prompting strategies is essential, with the ensemble consistently beating any single model. We also introduce Meno-Lite-0.1, a 7B domain-adapted model with a strong cost--performance trade-off, and analyse MTRAGEval, highlighting annotation limitations and directions for improvement. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/RaguTeam/ragu_mtrag_semeval

</details>


### [28] [RLearner-LLM: Balancing Logical Grounding and Fluency in Large Language Models via Hybrid Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04539)
*Qiming Bao,Juho Leinonen,Paul Denny,Michael J. Witbrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对DPO在知识密集型生成任务中因偏好信号存在冗长性偏差而导致逻辑对齐不足的问题，提出了RLearner-LLM与Hybrid-DPO自动化偏好管道，融合DeBERTa-v3 NLI信号与验证器LLM评分，在多个领域和模型架构上实现了显著的NLI提升，并证明逻辑感知指标优于LLM-as-a-judge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO作为PPO-RLHF的高效替代方案，在知识密集型生成任务中表现不佳：标准偏好信号（人工标注或LLM评判）存在系统性冗长性偏差，奖励流畅性而非逻辑正确性。这导致SFT模型尽管文本流畅，但NLI蕴含率仅0.05-0.22，存在明显的"逻辑对齐差距"。

Method: 提出RLearner-LLM与Hybrid-DPO：自动化偏好管道，融合DeBERTa-v3 NLI信号（捕获逻辑关系）与验证器LLM评分（评估答案覆盖），无需人工标注，同时克服单信号优化的"对齐税"。在五个学术领域（Biology、Medicine、Law）和三种基础架构（LLaMA-2-13B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma 4 E4B-it）上进行评估。

Result: RLearner-LLM在15个测试单元中的11个实现NLI提升，最高提升6倍；在Gemma 4 E4B-it上，5个领域中有4个实现NLI提升（+11.9%至+2.4倍），且所有领域推理速度加快。Qwen3-8B版本在成对比较中以95%胜率击败自身SFT基线。实验复现了前沿模型的冗长性偏差：GPT-4o-mini以95%胜率偏好verbose SFT的冗长输出，证明NLI等逻辑感知指标优于LLM-as-a-judge。

Conclusion: Hybrid-DPO通过混合NLI信号有效弥补了知识密集型任务中的逻辑对齐差距，且可扩展至紧凑模型而不失效果。LLM-as-a-judge存在系统性冗长性偏差，在知识密集型生成任务中应采用逻辑感知指标（NLI、ACR）替代。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), the efficient alternative to PPO-based RLHF, falls short on knowledge-intensive generation: standard preference signals from human annotators or LLM judges exhibit a systematic verbosity bias that rewards fluency over logical correctness. This blindspot leaves a logical alignment gap -- SFT models reach NLI entailment of only 0.05-0.22 despite producing fluent text. We propose RLearner-LLM with Hybrid-DPO: an automated preference pipeline that fuses a DeBERTa-v3 NLI signal with a verifier LLM score, removing human annotation while overcoming the "alignment tax" of single-signal optimization. Evaluated across five academic domains (Biology, Medicine, Law) with three base architectures (LLaMA-2-13B, Qwen3-8B, Gemma 4 E4B-it), RLearner-LLM yields up to 6x NLI improvement over SFT, with NLI gains in 11 of 15 cells and consistent answer-coverage gains. On Gemma 4 E4B-it (4.5B effective params), Hybrid-DPO lifts NLI in four of five domains (+11.9% to +2.4x) with faster inference across all five, scaling down to compact base models without losing the alignment-tax mitigation. Our Qwen3-8B RLearner-LLM wins 95% of pairwise comparisons against its own SFT baseline; GPT-4o-mini in turn wins 95% against our concise output -- alongside the 69% win the same judge gives a verbose SFT over our DPO model, this replicates verbosity bias on a frontier comparator and motivates logic-aware metrics (NLI, ACR) over LLM-as-a-judge for knowledge-intensive generation.

</details>


### [29] [UniVer: A Unified Perspective for Multi-step and Multi-draft Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04543)
*Yepeng Weng,Qiao Hu,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniVer算法，将树结构验证统一为条件最优传输(OT)问题，通过前缀接受概率抽象垂直依赖关系，联合优化树层级间的草案选择，实现比递归拒绝采样高4.2%-8.5%的接受长度，同时保持与目标模型的精确分布对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将多草案与多步验证分离处理——扁平OT仅用于单步草案，而树结构候选者采用逐token拒绝采样。这种分离导致联合优化区间（多步依赖与多草案分支的交汇处）优化不足，局部验证规则无法利用候选树水平与垂直维度的耦合关系。

Method: 核心思想：将垂直依赖关系抽象为前缀接受概率（作为动态缩放因子引导水平草案选择）。具体提出UniVer算法：在前缀约束下，通过组合局部最优传输计划实现跨树层级的联合优化，将树结构验证问题形式化为条件最优传输问题。

Result: 实验表明：1) 在不同任务和模型上，UniVer相比标准递归拒绝采样（无放回）将接受长度提升4.2%至8.5%；2) 始终保持与目标模型的精确分布对齐（无损失）。

Conclusion: UniVer在条件最优传输框架下保持无损失特性，并实现最优接受率。理论保证与实验验证均支持该方法能有效联合优化树结构验证的水平与垂直维度。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Models via draft-then-verify, where verification can be framed as an Optimal Transport (OT) problem. Existing approaches typically handle multi-draft and multi-step aspects in isolation, applying either flat OT to single-step drafts or per-token rejection sampling to tree-structured candidates. This separation leaves the joint regime (where multi-step dependencies meet multi-draft branching) poorly optimized, as local verification rules fail to exploit the coupling between horizontal and vertical dimensions of candidate trees. In this paper, we propose a unified perspective that casts tree-based verification as a conditional OT problem. Our key insight is that vertical dependencies can be abstracted through prefix acceptance probabilities, which act as dynamic scaling factors to actively guide horizontal draft selection. Based on this principle, we introduce UniVer, a verification algorithm that jointly optimizes across tree levels by composing local optimal transport plans under prefix constraints. We prove that UniVer remains lossless and achieves the optimal acceptance rate under the proposed conditional framework. Extensive experiments across different tasks and models demonstrate that UniVer improves acceptance length by 4.2% to 8.5% over standard recursive rejection sampling without replacement, while maintaining exact distributional alignment with the target model.

</details>


### [30] [The Newsworthiness of Brazilian Distress: A Peak Analysis on Time Series of International Media Attention to Disasters in Brazil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04552)
*Brielen Madureira,Andreas Niekler,Marc Keuschnigg,Mariana Madruga de Brito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过对德国报纸中2000-2024年间关于巴西火灾和滑坡的新闻进行峰值分析，探索国际媒体对地方灾难的关注规律。研究采用时间序列分割方法检测新闻事件峰值，并将其与灾难数据库进行比对，为理解国际媒体如何响应本地灾难事件提供了方法论框架和数据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际媒体对地方性灾难的关注程度存在显著差异，但驱动国际媒体注意力形成的因素尚不清楚。巴西提供了一个典型案例，其部分自然灾害和技术事故偶尔获得国际关注，但缺乏系统性实证分析。

Method: 使用时间序列分割方法检测新闻峰值，对2000-2024年间德国报纸中关于巴西火灾和滑坡的2000条新闻进行峰值分析，并与国家和全球灾难数据库进行时间对齐验证。

Result: 该研究构建了针对巴西灾难新闻的代表性、经过验证的国家特定新闻数据集，并验证了新闻峰值与灾难数据库观测值之间的时间对应关系。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种整合新闻时间序列分割与灾难数据库的跨学科方法，可用于系统性地分析国际媒体如何响应巴西本地灾难事件。

Abstract: Media coverage influences disaster response, yet the drivers of international media attention to local events remain unevenly understood. Brazil offers a compelling case: some of its natural and technological disasters occasionally hit the international headlines. However, systematic analyses of what makes these events be discussed abroad are still missing. Addressing this gap requires representative, validated and country-specific news datasets. This paper presents a peak analysis of 2k news about Brazilian fires and landslides in German newspapers from 2000 to 2024. Using time series segmentation to detect news event peaks, we examine the extent to which they can be temporally aligned with observations in national and global disaster databases.

</details>


### [31] [Benchmarking POS Tagging for the Tajik Language: A Comparative Study of Neural Architectures on the TajPersParallel Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04576)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次为塔吉克语建立了词性标注（POS tagging）基准：以作者自建的 TajPersParallel 平行词典语料（43,819 条词条、12 个词性类别）为基础，比较 BiLSTM-CRF 基线与四种经 LoRA 微调的多语言/单语 Transformer（XLM-RoBERTa-large、mBERT、ParsBERT、ruBERT）。最佳模型为 mBERT + LoRA（准确率 0.651、加权 F1 0.618、宏平均 F1 仅 0.111）。核心结论是：在缺乏句法上下文的孤立词分类设定下，所有模型仅能识别名词与形容词两个高频类别，其余 10 类 F1 全为 0，且名词—形容词混淆在形态上不可消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语虽与波斯语同属伊朗语族西支、词汇结构高度接近，却因使用西里尔字母（1940 年起）与长期社会语言隔离，几乎完全被现代 NLP 技术与资源（如针对波斯语的 DadmaTools、APARSIN 等工具）排除在外：既无标准形态标注语料、无训练好的标注器，也无任何公开基准，无法评估现有多语言预训练模型对塔吉克语的形态分析能力。与此同时，作者自建的 TajPersParallel 塔吉克—波斯平行词典语料首次提供了带词性标注的 4.3 万余条数据，而 LoRA 等参数高效微调方法使得在有限算力下适配大型多语言模型成为可能。因此本文的目标是建立首个可复现的塔吉克语 POS 标注基准，并对经典循环网络与多语言 Transformer 做系统对比。

Method: 数据：TajPersParallel 语料，43,819 条词典条目（43,162 个唯一塔吉克词形），字段包括西里尔文塔吉克词、阿拉伯文波斯语对应词、词性标签及部分例句；12 个类别严重不均衡（名词 55.2%、形容词 35.6%、副词 3.5%、动词 3.2%、专有名词 1.0%、其余 6 类合计不足 1%）。由于例句不构成连贯语料，任务被设定为“孤立词元、无上下文”的 12 类分类；按 80/10/10 分层划分为训练 34,529、验证 4,316、测试 4,317 条。模型：(1) 基线 BiLSTM-CRF——随机初始化的 128 维词嵌入、双向 LSTM（各方向 128 隐层）、线性投影 + CRF（因输入均为单词，CRF 的转移建模优势实际退化为普通交叉熵分类器）；(2) 四个预训练模型 XLM-RoBERTa-large、mBERT（bert-base-multilingual-cased，104 语种含波斯语但不含塔吉克语）、ParsBERT（波斯语专用）、ruBERT（俄语专用，共享西里尔文字且俄语为当地教育/科研语言），均采用 LoRA 适配（rank=8、α=16，作用于自注意力层的 query/value 矩阵），微调 3 个 epoch、batch size 16、AdamW、学习率 2×10⁻⁵、线性衰减并含 warm-up。评测：准确率、宏平均 F1、加权 F1；对每种配置使用 2 个随机种子重复以评估稳定性与标准差；另做零样本评测（分类头随机初始化、未经任何塔吉克语微调），并对最佳模型作逐类 F1 与混淆矩阵分析。

Result: 所有经 LoRA 适配的 Transformer 在三项指标上全面超越 BiLSTM-CRF 基线。测试集结果：mBERT + LoRA 最优（Acc 0.651 / Macro-F1 0.111 / Weighted-F1 0.618），其后为 ruBERT（0.614 / 0.101 / 0.572）、XLM-RoBERTa-large（0.616 / 0.098 / 0.563）、ParsBERT（0.601 / 0.099 / 0.561），基线 BiLSTM-CRF 为 0.551 / 0.059 / 0.392（mBERT 加权 F1 高出约 23 个百分点，Accuracy 高出 10 个百分点以上）；XLM-RoBERTa-large 尽管规模最大，其准确率标准差达 0.025，而其余模型不超过 0.003，说明稳定性更差。零样本评测整体接近随机猜测（12 类随机准确率约 0.083）：ParsBERT 准确率最高 0.290、mBERT 0.128、XLM-R 0.055，ruBERT 加权 F1 最高 0.252，验证了塔吉克语与波斯语类型学接近以及西里尔文字共享/Russian 作为中介语言的作用，但宏 F1 均低于 0.05，表明不微调则无法从多语言隐空间分离塔吉克形态。逐类分析显示所有模型行为近似二元：名词 F1 0.71–0.74、形容词 0.001（基线）至 0.587（mBERT），其余 10 类（动词、副词、连词、代词、介词等稀有功能词）F1 全部为 0。混淆矩阵（mBERT + LoRA）显示误判集中于名词/形容词互混块（正确分类名词 1893、形容词 919，交叉误分 619 与 487），原因是塔吉克语定语位置的形容词与名词形式重合，需借助 izafet 结构或句法位置消歧，而当前任务设定无法提供。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地建立了塔吉克语 POS 标注基准，确立了无句法上下文条件下可达到的质量下限，并开放发布了预训练模型与实验源码以便独立复现。结论要点：多语言预训练模型即使未包含塔吉克语，仍能通过跨语言知识迁移提取形态信息，LoRA 是低成本适配的有效路径；然而宏 F1 与加权 F1 的巨大落差及 10/12 类全零说明，性能主要由严重类别不均衡与无上下文设定共同导致，而非架构缺陷——孤立词形式下功能词与同形内容词（如 «ки»、«ар»）本质上不可判定，需扩充语料至带标注的连贯句子才能转向真正的上下文标注。作者提出的后续方向包括：扩展 TajPersParallel 语料以支持上下文标注、引入数据增强与加权损失应对不均衡、对比不同 LoRA 方案配置、将模型整合进塔吉克语专用 NLP 工具库。

Abstract: This paper presents the first benchmark for the task of automatic part-of-speech (POS) tagging for the Tajik language. Despite the existence of multilingual language models demonstrating high effectiveness for many of the world's languages, their capacity for grammatical analysis of Tajik has remained unexplored until now. The aim of this study is to fill this gap through a systematic comparison of classical neural network architectures and modern multilingual transformers.
  Experiments were conducted on the TajPersParallel corpus, a parallel lexical resource comprising approximately 44,000 dictionary entries. Due to the absence of full-fledged example sentences in the current version of the corpus, the task was performed at the level of isolated lexical units, representing a challenging case of context-independent classification. The study compares the following architectures: a recurrent BiLSTM-CRF model, as well as multilingual models XLM-RoBERTa (large), mBERT, ParsBERT (Persian), and ruBERT (Russian), adapted using the parameter-efficient fine-tuning method LoRA.
  The testing results showed that the best performance is achieved by the mBERT + LoRA model (macro F1-score = 0.11, weighted F1-score = 0.62). It was established that in the absence of syntactic context, all models experience significant difficulty in resolving morphological ambiguity, successfully classifying primarily high-frequency classes ("noun," "adjective") while demonstrating zero effectiveness for rare function words. Zero-shot evaluation revealed the greatest typological proximity of Tajik to Persian (ParsBERT) and Russian (ruBERT). The obtained results form a foundation for further research and development in the field of automatic processing of the Tajik language.

</details>


### [32] [TajikNLP: An Open-Source Toolkit for Comprehensive Text Processing of Tajik (Cyrillic Script)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04583)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TajikNLP是一个开源Python库，提供首个完整的塔吉克语（Cyrillic文字）NLP处理流水线，包含文本清洗、正规化、分词、词性标注、词干提取、词形还原、情感分析和预训练词向量等功能，并配套发布4个语言数据集（POS语料库52.5k、情感词典3.5k、地名词典5.6k、人名数据集3.8k）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语作为Cyrillic文字语言，在NLP工具资源方面严重匮乏，制约了语言学研究和应用开发。现有NLP工具无法有效处理塔吉克语的黏着性名词和动词形态变化。

Method: 采用模块化架构设计，以统一Doc对象为中心，支持组件串行处理；开发新型统一形态学引擎，提供受控分析和深度分析两种模式处理塔吉克语黏着形态；整合基于词典的情感分析器和Hugging Face Hub上的预训练Word2Vec/FastText词向量。

Result: 发布616个自动化测试，源代码覆盖率93%；四个配套语言数据集均以宽松许可证开源发布。

Conclusion: TajikNLP为塔吉克语NLP建立了基础技术设施，降低了低资源Cyrillic文字语言环境下的学术和工业应用门槛。

Abstract: The Tajik language, written in Cyrillic script, remains severely under-resourced in terms of publicly available natural language processing (NLP) toolkits, hindering both linguistic research and applied development. This paper introduces TajikNLP, an open-source Python library that provides the first comprehensive pipeline for processing authentic Tajik text while preserving the original Cyrillic orthography. The library implements a modular architecture centered around a unified Doc object, enabling sequential application of components for cleaning, normalization, tokenization (including subword BPE), morphemic segmentation, part-of-speech tagging, stemming, lemmatization, and sentence splitting. A novel unified morphology engine is introduced, offering controlled and deep analysis modes that significantly improve handling of Tajik's agglutinative nominal and verbal inflections. The release further incorporates a lexicon-based sentiment analyser and pre-trained Word2Vec/FastText embeddings loaded directly from the Hugging Face Hub. To ensure reproducibility and facilitate future research, four accompanying linguistic datasets -- a POS-tagged corpus (52.5k entries), a sentiment lexicon (3.5k entries), a toponym gazetteer (5.6k entries), and a personal names dataset (3.8k entries) -- have been openly published under permissive licenses. The library's reliability is validated by an extensive test suite of 616 automated tests achieving 93% source code coverage. TajikNLP thus establishes a foundational technological infrastructure for Tajik language processing, lowering the barrier to entry for both academic and industrial applications in low-resource Cyrillic-script environments.

</details>


### [33] [Gradients with Respect to Semantics Preserving Embeddings Tell the Uncertainty of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04638)
*Mingda Li,Rundong Lv,Xinyu Li,Weinan Zhang,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于梯度的无采样自由形式生成不确定性量化方法SemGrad，通过语义空间梯度评估LLM置信度，并提出HybridGrad结合语义梯度与参数梯度提升效果。


<details>
