Finally, we show that EngThrive functions as a general-purpose evaluation language, applicable not only to developer tools and AI, but to organizational policies, work environments, and other factors that shape how developers experience their work. We offer EngThrive as a concrete model for organizations seeking to move beyond measuring activity toward improving outcomes.

</details>


### [114] [Reproduction Test Generation for Java SWE Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04320)
*Toufique Ahmed,Jatin Ganhotra,Avraham Shinnar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: First work to address repository-level reproduction test generation for Java, introducing TDD-Bench-Java benchmark and e-Otter++ solution to bring better diagnosis and validation capabilities to Java software development.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing reproduction test generation research focused on Python, neglecting Java which is critical for enterprise software development.

Method: Adapted a state-of-the-art Python reproduction test generator to Java at the repository level, creating TDD-Bench-Java benchmark with 250 instances from popular open-source Java repositories.

Result: Successful adaptation of reproduction test generation techniques to Java, demonstrated through empirical evaluation on both open-source and contamination-free proprietary datasets.

Conclusion: The paper provides TDD-Bench-Java benchmark and e-Otter++ solution for Java, validated on both open-source and industry proprietary datasets.

Abstract: Given an issue on a software repository, a reproduction test confirms its presence in the code before it gets fixed and its absence after. Reproduction tests provide crucial execution-based feedback for diagnosis and validation during software development. Unfortunately, they are usually missing. Therefore, recent work has introduced both benchmarks and a thriving literature on solutions for reproduction test generation from issues. However, that work has focused on Python and neglected other languages such as Java, which is important for enterprise software. This paper introduces both a benchmark and a solution for Java repository-level reproduction test generation. The benchmark, TDD-Bench-Java, is the first to model this problem and comprises 250 instances sourced from popular open-source repositories. The solution, e-Otter++ for Java, adapts a state-of-the-art reproduction test generator for Python to yield high performance on Java. To evaluate in an industry setting, besides empirical results with TDD-Bench-Java, this paper also presents results with a contamination-free proprietary dataset. Overall, we hope that this paper contributes to bringing better diagnosis and validation to Java software development.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Robust LLM Post-Training: Automatic Failure Management for Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04431)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Yunpeng Zhai,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hongyi Liu,Xiaosong Huang,Philip S. Yu,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究强化微调中的自动化故障管理，构建了首个细粒度RFT故障基准RFT-FaultBench，并通过实证研究证明RFT故障可通过训练动态和故障指纹进行观察和区分，进而提出统一异常检测、故障诊断与自动修复的RFT-FM闭环框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（Post-training LLM的关键范式）的训练过程高度脆弱，但现有改进主要在系统层面或针对单个子问题，缺乏训练过程层面的系统性故障管理自动化方法。

Method: 构建了RFT-FaultBench基准（5个故障家族、16种故障类型、779次训练运行、22,549条训练步记录和1,457,288条轨迹级记录），并进行综合实证研究，证明RFT故障既可从训练动态中观察，也能通过经验故障指纹区分；基于此提出RFT-FM框架，将异常检测、故障诊断和自动修复统一为闭环系统。

Result: RFT-FaultBench既非trivial也非saturated，呈现清晰异常结构且在微妙故障设置下仍有挑战；RFT-FM在检测、诊断和缓解RFT故障上表现出强能力。

Conclusion: RFT-FM框架展示了在检测、诊断和缓解RFT故障方面的强能力，填补了强化微调自动化故障管理的空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) has become a core paradigm for post-training large language models, yet its training process remains highly fragile. Existing efforts mainly improve reliability at the system level or address specific issues in individual subproblems by modifying RFT algorithms. Despite their effectiveness, they largely overlook the problem of failure management at the training-process level. When training goes wrong, practitioners still rely heavily on expert-driven manual inspection and correction, and automatic failure management for RFT remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we take a first step toward systematic failure management for reinforcement fine-tuning. To understand the empirical structure of RFT failures, we first construct RFT-FaultBench, the first benchmark for fine-grained failures in reinforcement fine-tuning, covering 5 fault families, 16 fault types, 779 training runs, 22,549 train-step records, and 1,457,288 trajectory-level records. Based on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study showing that RFT failures are both observable from training dynamics and distinguishable through their empirical fault fingerprints. Building on these findings, we propose RFT-FM, an automatic failure management framework for reinforcement fine-tuning that unifies anomaly detection, failure diagnosis, and auto remediation in a closed loop. Experimental results show that RFT-FaultBench is neither trivial nor saturated: it exhibits clear anomaly structure while still posing substantial challenges, especially under subtle fault settings. Moreover, RFT-FM shows strong capability in detecting, diagnosing, and mitigating RFT failures.

</details>


### [116] [Accountable Agents in Software Engineering: An Analysis of Terms of Service and a Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04532)
*Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇愿景论文（vision paper），对主流AI编码助手和智能体开发工具的服务条款进行对比分析，揭示责任分配倾向，并提出负责任AI智能体软件工程的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注AI编码工具的生产力提升，而对责任归属问题关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过分析服务条款（ToS）来理解AI开发工具的责任分配机制。

Method: 对多种主流AI编码助手和智能体开发工具的服务条款进行对比分析，评估所有权、责任、责任免除、信息披露等方面的条款规定。

Result: 发现提供商普遍将正确性、安全性和法律合规的责任转移给用户；在赔偿、数据重用、可接受使用等方面的政策存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 现有政策框架与日益智能化的软件开发工作流程不匹配；提出包含责任建模、治理设计、支持问责的工具开发、开发者认知实证研究等方向的未来研究路线图。

Abstract: AI coding assistants and autonomous agents are becoming integral to software development workflows, reshaping how code is produced, reviewed, and maintained. While recent research has focused mainly on the capabilities and impacts of productivity of these systems, much less attention has been paid to accountability: who is responsible when agents generate, modify, or recommend code? In practice, accountability is defined through the Terms of Service (ToS) and related policy documents that govern the use of AI-powered development tools.
  In this vision paper, we present a comparative analysis of the Terms of Service for widely used AI coding assistants and agent-enabled development tools. We examine how these documents allocate ownership, responsibility, liability, and disclosure obligations between tool providers and software developers, and we identify common patterns and divergences between providers. Our analysis reveals a consistent tendency to shift responsibility for correctness, safety, and legal compliance onto users, as well as substantial variation in how providers address issues such as indemnification, data reuse, and acceptable use.
  Based on these findings, we argue that existing policy frameworks are poorly aligned with increasingly agent-mediated and autonomous software development workflows. We outline a research roadmap for accountable agents in software engineering, identifying challenges and opportunities for modeling responsibility, designing governance artifacts, developing tooling that supports accountability, and conducting empirical studies of developers' perceptions and practices.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Retrieval: A Multitask Benchmark and Model for Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04615)
*Siqiao Xue,Zihan Liao,Jin Qin,Ziyin Zhang,Yixiang Mu,Fan Zhou,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoREB基准，用于评估代码检索和重排的完整流程，解决了现有基准的数据污染和二元相关性退化问题。实验表明代码专用嵌入在代码到代码任务中表现优异但无通用赢家，短关键词查询对所有模型构成挑战，现成重排器存在任务不对称性，而本文微调的CoREB-Reranker首次在全部三个任务中取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码搜索评估仅关注第一阶段检索，而生产系统依赖更广泛的管道（包括重排）；现有基准存在数据污染、标签噪声和退化的二元相关性等问题。

Method: 构建了CoREB基准，使用反事实重写的LiveCodeBench问题，涵盖5种编程语言，以计时发布和分级相关性判断为特点。评测了11种嵌入模型和5种重排器，覆盖文本到代码、代码到文本、代码到代码三种任务。

Result: 代码专用嵌入在代码到代码检索中领先（约为通用编码器的2倍），但无单一模型在三项任务中均胜出；短关键词查询使所有模型nDCG@10接近零；现成重排器任务不对称，代码到代码任务波动12点，无基线在所有任务中净正增益；CoREB-Reranker首次实现所有任务一致提升。

Conclusion: CoREB-Reranker是首个在所有三个任务中实现一致提升的模型。数据和模型已公开，为代码搜索领域提供了更可靠的基准测试工具。

Abstract: Code search has usually been evaluated as first-stage retrieval, even though production systems rely on broader pipelines with reranking and developer-style queries. Existing benchmarks also suffer from data contamination, label noise, and degenerate binary relevance. In this paper, we introduce \textsc{CoREB}, a contamination-limited, multitask \underline{co}de \underline{r}etrieval and r\underline{e}ranking \underline{b}enchmark, together with a fine-tuned code reranker, that goes beyond retrieval to cover the full code search pipeline. \textsc{CoREB} is built from counterfactually rewritten LiveCodeBench problems in five programming languages and delivered as timed releases with graded relevance judgments. We benchmark eleven embedding models and five rerankers across three tasks: text-to-code, code-to-text, and code-to-code. Our experiments reveal that: \circone code-specialised embeddings dominate code-to-code retrieval (${\sim}2{\times}$ over general encoders), yet no single model wins all three tasks; \circtwo short keyword queries, the format closest to real developer search, collapse every model to near-zero nDCG@10; \circthree off-the-shelf rerankers are task-asymmetric, with a 12-point swing on code-to-code and no baseline net-positive across all tasks; \circfour our fine-tuned \textsc{CoREB-Reranker} is the first to achieve consistent gains across all three tasks. The data and model are released.

</details>


### [118] [CodeEvolve: LLM-Driven Evolutionary Optimization with Runtime-Enriched Target Selection for Multi-Language Code Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04677)
*Ajay Krishna Borra,Wenzhuo Yang,Samarth Arora,Akhilesh Deepak Gotmare,Gokulakrishnan Gopalakrishnan,Tharun Gali,Madhav Rathi,Doyen Sahoo,Manpreet Singh,Mayuresh Verma,Laksh Venka,Shuchita Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeEvolve是一个基于LLM的进化式代码优化框架，通过运行时引导的目标选择、MCTS搜索和自动化代码精化来提升程序性能和代码质量，在企业级Java代码库上实现平均15.22倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码性能优化通常依赖人工识别瓶颈，效率低下且易出错。现有基于LLM的优化方法多为单次生成，缺乏系统性搜索和验证，难以保证功能正确性。

Method: CodeEvolve扩展OpenEvolve框架，集成四项核心能力：1) 使用JFR运行时分析构建加权组件图进行目标选择；2) MCTS搜索引导优化方向；3) 多阶段评估流水线（构建验证、单元测试、性能检查、静态分析、LLM审查）；4) 针对Java和Salesforce Apex的领域特定评估管道。

Result: 在企业级Java代码库上，CodeEvolve对7个热点函数实现平均15.22倍加速，5/7函数优于单次LLM优化。Ablation实验表明，完整MCTS配置在Apex优化中平均产出19.5个有效程序（总20个），验证了搜索、过滤和精化各环节的贡献。

Conclusion: 运行时引导的瓶颈识别结合系统性搜索能显著提升LLM驱动优化的效果，多阶段验证机制有效保证了功能正确性，框架具有良好的领域扩展性。

Abstract: We present CodeEvolve, an evolutionary framework for improving program performance and code quality with Large Language Models (LLMs). CodeEvolve extends OpenEvolve with runtime-guided target selection, Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), automated code refinement, and language-specific evaluation pipelines for Java and Salesforce Apex. The system uses Java Flight Recorder (JFR) profiles to build weighted component graphs and select optimization targets that account for most execution cost, reducing reliance on manual bottleneck identification. For each target, CodeEvolve generates candidate edits, evaluates them through build validation, unit tests, performance checks, static analysis, and LLM-based review, and retains only variants that preserve functional correctness. Across real-world optimization tasks, CodeEvolve improves performance and code metrics while maintaining correctness. On a large enterprise Java codebase, it achieves an average speedup of 15.22$\times$ across seven hotspot functions and outperforms single-pass LLM optimization on five of them. An ablation study on Apex optimization shows that the full MCTS-augmented configuration produces 19.5 valid programs out of 20 on average, indicating that search, filtering, and refinement each contribute to more reliable optimization.

</details>


### [119] [How Does Chunking Affect Retrieval-Augmented Code Completion? A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04763)
*Xinjian Wu,Jingzhi Gong,Gunel Jahangirova,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于RAG代码补全中分块策略有效性的实证研究，在864个实验配置下证明了分块策略有显著影响，Function分块策略最差，而Sliding Window和cAST在成本-质量权衡上占优，上下文长度是主导参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chunking strategies in RAG pipelines for code completion lack empirical justification; practitioner recommendations are inconsistent.

Method: Controlled experiment crossing 4 chunking strategies × 4 retrievers × 5 generators × 9 parameter configs = 864 settings on 2 benchmarks (RepoEval, CrossCodeEval).

Result: Chunking significantly impacts completion quality; Function chunking underperforms others by 3.57–5.64 pp (Cliff's δ=–1.0); cross-file context length is dominant parameter (doubling yields up to 4.2 pp improvement); chunk size has weak, non-monotonic effect.

Conclusion: Function-based chunking is suboptimal for RAG code completion, never achieving Pareto-optimality; Sliding Window and cAST strategies dominate the cost-quality tradeoff. Cross-file context length (not chunk size) is the dominant parameter.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines for code completion rely on chunking to segment source files into retrievable units, yet chunking strategies are typically adopted without empirical justification, and practitioner recommendations are notably inconsistent. We present a controlled empirical study isolating the effect of chunking on code completion quality by crossing four representative strategies (Function, Declaration, Sliding Window, and cAST) with four retrievers, five generators, and nine parameter configurations on two benchmarks (RepoEval and CrossCodeEval), totaling 864 experimental settings. Our results reveal that chunking strategy has a statistically significant effect on RAG-based code completion. Contrary to intuition, chunking based on functions underperforms all other strategies by 3.57--5.64 percentage points on RepoEval (Cliff's delta = -1.0), while the remaining chunking strategies perform comparably. Our further analysis demonstrates that this observation holds across all retriever--generator combinations. We also find that cross-file context length is the dominant parameter: doubling from 2,048 to 8,192 tokens yields up to 4.2 percentage points of improvement, whereas chunk size has a weaker, non-monotonic effect. On the cost--quality Pareto front, Sliding Window and cAST dominate both benchmarks; Function chunking is never Pareto-optimal.

</details>


### [120] [A meta-analysis of the effect of generative AI on productivity and learning in programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04779)
*Sebastian Maier,Moritz Gunzenhäuser,Jonas Schweisthal,Manuel Schneider,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该meta分析综合了23项研究、27个效应量，发现GenAI编程助手对开发者生产力有中等程度的正向效应(g=0.33)，但对学习成果无显著影响(g=0.14)。生产力提升在受控实验环境中更明显，而在开源和企业环境中效应较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在编程中的应用日益广泛，但其对程序员生产力和长期编程技能发展的影响尚不明确，现有证据不一致。

Method: 系统检索ACM、arXiv、Scopus和Web of Science（2019-2025年），纳入27个效应量。使用RoB2和ROBINS-I评估偏倚风险，采用Hedges' g标准化效应量进行对比。

Result: 生产力：GenAI辅助有显著正向效应(g=0.33, 95%CI: [0.09, 0.58])，但存在显著异质性。受控实验环境中效应更大，开源和企业环境中效应较小。学习：无显著效应(g=0.14, 95%CI: [-0.18, 0.47])。

Conclusion: GenAI可提升开发者生产力，但效果高度依赖情境。在教育场景中，GenAI未能持续转化为学习或技能提升，计算机科学教育中需审慎整合GenAI工具。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is increasingly used for programming, yet it remains unclear when and where GenAI tools lead to productivity gains. Evidence on the effects of GenAI on the long-term development of programming skills is similarly mixed. Here, we present a meta-analysis of $n = 23$ studies reporting $k = 27$ effect sizes to quantify the effect of GenAI-powered coding assistants on productivity and learning. We systematically searched (i) ACM, (ii) arXiv, (iii) Scopus, and (iv) Web of Science for studies published between 2019 and 2025. Studies were required to compare GenAI-assisted with unassisted programming using quantitative measures of (1) productivity (i.e., task completion time, commits, and lines of code) and (2) learning (i.e., exam performance). We assessed the risk of bias using RoB2 and ROBINS-I and compared standardized effect sizes using Hedges' $g$. We find a statistically significant, but moderate positive effect of GenAI assistance on developer productivity ($g = 0.33$, $95\%$ CI: $[0.09, 0.58]$), yet with substantial heterogeneity across settings. Notably, productivity gains tend to be larger in controlled experimental settings, while effects are smaller in open-source and enterprise contexts. In contrast, we find no statistically significant effect of GenAI assistance on learning outcomes ($g = 0.14$, $95\%$ CI: $[-0.18, 0.47]$). Overall, these results highlight that GenAI coding assistants can increase developer productivity, although these gains depend strongly on context. In educational settings, however, the use of GenAI does not consistently translate into improved learning or skill development, which highlights the need for careful integration of GenAI into computer science education.

</details>


### [121] [Toward an Understanding of Developer Behaviour while Using Bug Localization Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04828)
*Pablo Diaz Pedreira,Tamara Lopez,Michel Wermelinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过用户研究探讨开发者如何在实践中使用bug定位工具，发现工具的采用不仅取决于准确性，还需要考虑实际工作流程和社交上下文等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Bug定位研究主要关注自动化工具的准确性，但缺乏关于开发者实际如何使用这些工具的研究。

Method: 进行了一项定性用户研究：11名参与者在受控环境中完成4个真实bug定位任务，使用专门的工具并提供不同级别的支持信息。采用出声思考协议和半结构化访谈收集数据。

Result: 初步发现揭示了三个实践方面的洞察：开发者如何与工具交互、社交和上下文信息的角色作用、以及问题解决过程。

Conclusion: Bug localization是一个复杂的过程，工具的采用取决于比准确性更多的因素。单纯提高工具准确性不足以保证其在实际开发中的有效应用。

Abstract: Bug fixing is a complex and time-consuming task in software development. Bug localization research tends to focus on the accuracy of automated tools that suggest source code files for developers to look at. However, little is known about how developers use these tools in practice. This paper reports on an ongoing qualitative user study. Eleven participants worked through four realistic bug localization tasks in a controlled environment and were given varying levels of support information offered by a specialized tool. Participants were asked to think aloud in a semi-structured interview session. The preliminary findings provide insight into three aspects of practice: how developers interact with tools, the role social and contextual information plays, and problem solving. The study demonstrates that bug localization is complex and suggests that the adoption of effective tools depends on more than their accuracy.

</details>


### [122] [Patterns of Developer Adoption of LLM-Generated Code Refactoring Suggestions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04835)
*David Schön,Faiza Amjad,Tehreem Asif,Ranim Khojah,Mazen Mohamad,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究分析了169个GitHub提交，发现开发者大多直接接受ChatGPT的代码重构建议而不做修改。当有修改时，主要涉及5种模式，取决于重构活动类型、开发者提示词和ChatGPT响应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注评估LLM生成的重构建议质量，但缺乏对开发者如何在实践中应用这些建议的理解。

Method: 分析方法学研究，收集并分析了169个GitHub提交，这些提交中开发者基于ChatGPT对话内容进行代码重构，并在提交信息中链接了相关ChatGPT对话。

Result: 发现开发者大多数情况下直接采纳ChatGPT的重构建议而未做修改。当进行修改时，这些修改大多是重大变更，分为5种不同的模式。

Conclusion: LLM生成的重构建议在实践中被广泛接受；开发者对建议的修改模式取决于三个关键因素：重构活动类型、开发者提示词和ChatGPT响应的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have gained widespread popularity and have steadily improved over time, enabling software developers to use them for various code-related tasks. One common task is code refactoring, where the LLM suggests changes for the developer to apply to their code to improve quality attributes such as readability or maintainability. While current research focuses on evaluating LLM-generated refactoring suggestions, there is a limited understanding of how developers apply these suggestions in practice. To explore this, we analyze 169 GitHub commits where developers refactor their code based on a ChatGPT conversation linked in the commit message. We found that developers mostly accept and use the suggestions without modifications. When changes are made, they are mostly major and fall into five different patterns that depend on the refactoring activity, the developer's prompt, and the validity of the response from ChatGPT.

</details>


### [123] [Agentic Repository Mining: A Multi-Task Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04845)
*Johannes Härtel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文（EASE 2026）研究"代理式软件仓库挖掘"：让 LLM 智能体仅凭起点（如 commit SHA）通过标准 bash 命令自主探索仓库并分类软件工件，与接收人工预加工上下文的简单 LLM 基线做对比。结论：两者精度相当，但智能体的核心优势是对上下文规模的鲁棒性（不出现上下文窗口溢出、成本与工件大小解耦），代价是平均成本高 1.2–3.2 倍；通过对 100 例分歧案例的人工诊断，发现大量分歧源于标注规范歧义与原始标签在受限上下文下产生，说明以此类"真值"衡量精度可能低估拥有更广上下文访问的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件仓库挖掘（MSR）常需对 commit、代码评审评论、代码行、整个仓库等工件进行分类，是缺陷预测、安全缺陷识别、维护活动分类等实证研究的基础。人工标注昂贵、易出错且常因上下文不足而误标或给出不确定标签；ML 分类器又依赖已有标注数据（主动学习等混合方案亦然）。虽然零样本 LLM 提示已能缓解标注数据稀缺，但"提示中应放入哪些上下文"成为需要大量人工工程化的问题。人类标注者会像"git clone → git checkout → rg"那样自回归地、按需探索仓库；本文考察智能体能否以同样方式自主获取上下文，从而免去逐任务的上下文工程，并达到甚至优于预加工上下文方案的效果。

Method: 提出一个简单的"代理式仓库挖掘"框架：在隔离的 Docker 沙箱（无外网，仓库克隆其中）中，LLM 通过 bash 工具调用逐步探索仓库，直到形成最终分类答案；额外数据（如 GitHub PR JSON）以文件形式提供，步数上限 50。智能体工具有两种变体：Native（Amazon Bedrock Converse API 原生工具调用，每步限一次）和 Stop-Sequence（模型输出 <bash>…</bash>，以闭合标签作为停止序列、字符串解析执行，API 无关且完全透明）；并利用 Claude 3.7 Sonnet 的提示缓存（原生缓存 + 手工网格缓存）降低多轮重复前缀成本。基线为无工具的简单 LLM：仅 CoT、无 CoT、以及无任何上下文的"记忆"基线（用于检测标签是否泄漏于训练数据）。模型为 Claude 3.7 Sonnet、Mistral Large 3、Llama 3.3 70B（Llama 因工具调用不可靠被排除出智能体实验）。四个任务来自已有研究并带人工真值：Herbold（bug-fix commit 中改动行的分类，212 行）、Härtel（PR 评审评论是否涉及潜在安全缺陷，135 条）、Levin（commit 维护意图 corrective/adaptive/perfective，129 条）、Munaiah（仓库是否为工程化软件项目，172 个仓库）。共 4 任务 × 8 配置 × 648 样本 = 5184 次尝试，剔除 212 次系统性不适用与 29 次真实错误后得到 4943 次有效分类。测量准确率、token、时延、成本、探索步数与工具使用行为；用 Stan 拟合层次准确率模型（方法效应 + 样本难度随机效应）并给出后验预测准确率与成对差异的可信区间；对至少两种方法与真值分歧的 254 例中分层抽样 100 例，按"更新标签/保留标签/规范歧义/无法判定"四类做人工诊断。

Result: 精度：智能体与对应简单基线在四个任务上大体实践等效（±5 精度点等价域内概率多在 83%–91%）。Herbold（最难的 tangled commits 任务）各方法 46.2%–56.1%；Härtel（安全评论）58%–66%；Levin（维护意图）87.6%–93.8%，超过原论文基于 commit 消息训练的 76%；Munaiah（仓库分类）跨度最大 65.1%–87.8%，其中 Agent Sonnet(native) 87.8% 最高、Agent Mistral 78.5% 优于 Simple Mistral 70.9%（78% 概率显著更优），是代理优势最强的任务。Härtel 任务中若把上下文溢出计为错误，所有简单方法的精度均低于智能体；Agent Sonnet(stopseq) 有 67% 概率显著优于 Simple Sonnet。记忆基线：Härtel 31.9%（低于随机/多数），Herbold 不适用，Levin 45.2%（略低于多数 50.4%），Munaiah 82.0%（高于多数 52.9%），提示训练数据泄漏影响有限但仓库任务存在部分重叠。成本：简单方法平均最便宜（Llama $0.0009、Simple Mistral $0.0044、Simple Sonnet $0.026），智能体为 1.2–3.2 倍（Agent Mistral $0.0051、stopseq $0.067、native $0.085）；但简单方法成本与预加工上下文大小几乎完全正相关，而智能体近似零相关，当上下文超过约 30K token 时 Agent Sonnet(stopseq) 反而更划算；提示缓存为 Sonnet native 节省 35.1%、stopseq 节省 40.4%。token：简单方法输入约 5–8K，智能体 8.5K–14.2K 新输入 + 18K 左右缓存读；简单方法出现上下文溢出（Llama 9、Sonnet 7、no-CoT 6、Mistral 3），智能体为零。鲁棒性：智能体工具调用错误率仅 1.7%–2.2% 且可自恢复，无效类别输出罕见。时延：智能体 21–29 秒/次 vs 简单方法 1.5–6 秒（属次要因素，可并行离线）。探索：Sonnet 平均 10.4/10.5 步、6.1 条命令量级（14 条命令），Mistral 5.7 步、6.1 命令；仓库分类与安全评审需更多步，commit 类任务更自足；无智能体触及 50 步上限；Sonnet 更偏好管道组合（更"高级"的探索），Mistral 偏好链式命令。不确定性：模型极少输出 unclear，简单 Llama 9%、Mistral 2%、Sonnet 1%，智能体几乎为 0——更广的上下文反而使模型过度自信、倾向给出确定类别。分歧诊断（100 例）：37 例应更新标签、17 例保留原标签（方法误判）、40 例属规范歧义、6 例信息不足；如 whitespace 与 refactoring、refactoring 与 documentation/test 的优先级、测试代码是否算修复的一部分、仓库"工程化"的文档量/单次应用/弃用项目阈值、非英语命名等均存在灰区。

Conclusion: 代理式仓库挖掘在准确率上可与人工预加工上下文的简单 LLM 相媲美，且其自我检索上下文这一前提使这种"等效"更具说服力。其最突出的优势是对上下文规模的鲁棒性：不发生上下文窗口溢出，成本与耗时不随工件最大可用大小增长；缺点是平均成本更高、运行时更久。超过约 30K token 的上下文场景下智能体更具成本效益。100 例分歧的人工诊断表明相当比例的错误源于原始标注规范本身存在歧义、或真值是在上下文不足时产生的，因此以该真值衡量的准确率可能系统性低估拥有更广上下文访问的方法（如智能体）。作者认为标注规范需要更新，并把代码、提示与全部 4943 条轨迹公开以支持复现。局限包括：数据泄漏（以记忆基线与同模型对比加以控制）、真值质量、提示设置的偏差、仓库可访问性与体积过滤带来的抽样偏斜、仅依赖三个特定模型版本、仅比较基础代理特征（未含摘要策略、代理规划、截断策略、更高步数上限、联网访问）以及未纳入 RAG 与学习型基线。

Abstract: Mining software repositories often requires classifying artifacts like commits, reviews, code lines, or entire repositories into categories. Human labeling is expensive and error-prone; limited context frequently leads to misclassifications or uncertainty in labels. We investigate whether LLM agents that dynamically explore repositories through standard bash commands can match the classification quality of simple LLMs that receive pre-engineered context. Across four tasks, eight approach configurations, and 4943 classifications, agents achieve competitive accuracy despite retrieving their own context. The primary advantage is robustness: agents avoid context-window overflows and scale independently of artifact size. A manual diagnosis of 100 cases where approaches disagree with the ground truth reveals specification ambiguities and labels produced under limited context, suggesting that accuracy against such ground truth may underestimate approaches with broader context access.

</details>


### [124] [SynConfRoute: Syntax-Aware Routing for Efficient Code Completion with Small CodeLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04894)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对企业代码补全的隐私与质量矛盾，提出SynConfRoute方法：先用小型本地模型生成代码，通过置信度与语法验证自动判断质量，决定是否升级到大模型。实验表明3B模型经代码专业化训练可媲美10倍大的32B模型，SynConfRoute在常规任务上提升6.4%，多语言任务提升31%，同时减少58%计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需要在代码补全的高质量与隐私保护之间取得平衡：专有模型效果好但存在代码泄露风险，而自托管大模型成本高昂难以维护。小型CodeLLM（1B-3B）虽可本地运行保护隐私，但在复杂任务上表现不佳。

Method: 评估了来自12个家族的29个代码专用LLM（0.5B-480B），在Python、Java和C++的执行基准测试上进行fill-in-the-middle代码补全评测；提出了SynConfRoute方法，结合token置信度与语法验证来自动决定是保留本地补全还是升级到更大的自托管模型。

Result: SynConfRoute在常规补全任务上pass@1提升6.4%，在更难的多语言任务上提升达31%；最终pipeline在常规补全上达到78.9%，比单独使用480B模型高7.4%，同时减少58%的加速器使用量；在三种语言上均有效泛化，从未误拒正确本地补全。

Conclusion: SynConfRoute无需额外训练，使用现成模型即可部署，在Python、Java和C++三种语言上均优于仅用置信度路由的方法，且从未拒绝过正确的本地补全结果。该方案在保持代码隐私的同时，显著提升了企业级代码补全的效率与质量。

Abstract: Enterprises want AI code completion that is both high-quality and private, but they face a tension: proprietary models yield better results yet risk exposing proprietary code, while self-hosting large models is expensive and hard to maintain. As a lighter alternative, small CodeLLMs (1B-3B) can run on a developer's workstation accelerator with code never leaving the machine, but they fail on harder tasks. A practical solution is to use the small model for most requests and selectively route difficult ones to a larger self-hosted model. In this study, we evaluate 29 code specialized LLMs (0.5B-480B) from 12 families on execution-based fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code completion benchmarks across Python, Java, and C++, and find that model family and code specialized training matter more than size: a 3B model matches a 32B model despite being 10x smaller. Analyzing the 3B model's failures, we discover that 46% of its incorrect completions are not valid code. To enable efficient code completion, we propose SynConfRoute, a training-free method that combines token confidence with syntax validation to automatically decide per-request whether to keep the local completion or escalate to a larger self-hosted model. SynConfRoute improves pass@1 by 6.4% over confidence only routing on routine completions and by up to 31% on harder multi-language tasks, and the resulting pipeline achieves 78.9% on routine completions, 7.4% higher than always using the 480B model alone, while reducing accelerator usage by 58%. SynConfRoute generalizes across Python, Java, and C++, improving over confidence only routing on all three languages without ever rejecting a correct local completion. The pipeline uses off-the-shelf models with no custom training, making it immediately deployable in practice.

</details>


### [125] [Conflict Essences for Transformation Rules with Nested Application Conditions -- Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04947)
*Alexander Lauer,Jens Kosiol,Leen Lambers,Gabriele Taentzer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将禁用本质(disabling essences)的概念扩展到支持任意嵌套条件，并引入符号冲突本质(symbolic conflict essences)来捕获两条规则之间的交互关系。证明了转换对平行依赖当且仅当符号冲突本质可嵌入其中，并建立了符号冲突本质与带应用条件的转换规则的初始冲突之间的关系。结果适用于粘性HLR范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关键对和初始冲突分析方法虽然有效，但包含冲突转换对关键信息的结构过大。研究者已证明冲突信息实际上包含在更小的结构中（禁用本质/冲突本质），但现有工作仅支持ANF形式的应用条件，无法处理任意嵌套条件，限制了实际应用范围。

Method: 1. 将禁用本质的概念从ANF形式的应用条件扩展到任意嵌套条件；2. 引入符号冲突本质，由禁用本质构造，用于捕获两条规则之间的交互；3. 在粘性HLR范畴的框架下进行形式化。

Result: 证明了转换对平行依赖当且仅当其可嵌入某个符号冲突本质；建立了符号冲突本质与带应用条件的转换规则的初始冲突之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 符号冲突本质能够有效地表示和分析图转换规则之间的冲突关系，且具有更小的结构表示和更强的表达能力。该方法扩展了现有冲突分析技术的适用范围，为基于图转换的软件建模提供了更精细的静态分析工具。

Abstract: Conflict and dependency analysis is an important static analysis tool that provides an overview of the potential interactions of (graph) transformation rules. This analysis is based on critical pairs and initial conflicts, which represent conflicting transformations in a minimal context. However, the crucial information about a conflicting transformation pair is contained in much smaller structures, called disabling/conflict essences in existing research. Recently, we introduced disabling essences for rules with application conditions which contain the information on how an application condition can be violated by another rule. In this paper, we extend the notion of disabling essences to support not only application conditions in Alternating Quantifier Normal Form, but also arbitrary nested conditions. We introduce (symbolic) conflict essences that are constructed from disabling essences and which capture the interaction between two rules. We show that a transformation pair is parallel dependent if and only if a symbolic conflict essence can be embedded into it and relate symbolic conflict essences to initial conflicts for transformation rules with application conditions. We present our results for adhesive HLR categories, which includes several types of graph-like structures.

</details>


### [126] [Architectural Constraints Alignment in AI-assisted, Platform-based Service Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04973)
*Julius Irion,Moritz Leugers,Paul Hartwig,Simon Kling,Tachmyrat Annayev,Alexander Schwind,Maria C. Borges,Sebastian Werner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检索增强的脚手架方法，将基于平台的代码生成与智能体澄清循环相结合，在服务开发早期暴露并解决架构约束模糊性问题，从而提升AI辅助开发的生产就绪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助开发工具虽能实现服务快速原型设计，但缺乏对生产环境所需的架构约束、基础设施依赖和组织标准的感知能力，导致生成的代码行为脆弱、部署能力有限。

Method: 提出检索增强脚手架方法，结合平台化代码生成与智能体澄清循环机制。通过模板检索与结构化交互，在服务脚手架阶段嵌入生产相关考量要素。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在架构一致性和部署就绪性方面均优于通用AI代码生成工作流。

Conclusion: 约束感知型检索对于将AI辅助服务开发与生产软件工程实践相协调至关重要。

Abstract: AI-assisted development tools enable rapid prototyping of services but often lack awareness of architectural constraints, infrastructure dependencies, and organizational standards required in production environments. Consequently, generated artifacts may exhibit brittle behavior and limited deployability. We propose a retrieval-augmented scaffolding approach that combines platform-based code generation with agentic clarification loops to expose and resolve architectural constraint ambiguities. By combining template retrieval with structured interaction, the method embeds production-relevant considerations during service scaffolding. Evaluation indicates improved architectural consistency and deployability compared to general-purpose AI code generation workflows, suggesting that constraint-aware retrieval is essential for aligning AI-assisted service development with production software engineering practices.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Endogenous Regime Switching Driven by Scalar-Irreducible Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04054)
*Sheng Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了标量可约与不可约动力学的分类框架，指出大多数现有ML系统属于前者，而后者可通过快变量与慢结构的反馈机制实现无需外部干预的内生性制度转换，为构建真正自主学习的智能系统提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习框架难以实现内生性制度切换，制度转换通常依赖外部施加而非内部生成，限制了真正自主智能的涌现。

Method: 引入标量可约/不可约动力学的分类体系，使用最小动力学模型展示快变量与慢结构适应之间的反馈机制如何产生内生性制度转换。

Result: 证明标量不可约动力学能够通过快变量与慢结构适应之间的反馈，在无外部调度的情况下产生持续的内生性制度转换。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的动力学范式，为实现内部组织而非外部预设的自适应行为的自主学习系统提供了潜在路径。

Abstract: Achieving endogenous regime switching is crucial for the emergence of autonomous intelligence, yet remains a central challenge for existing machine learning frameworks, where such transitions are typically externally imposed. In this work, we introduce a classification that distinguishes scalar-reducible dynamics, which can be expressed as gradient flows driven by a scalar objective, from scalar-irreducible dynamics that cannot be reduced to such a form. While most existing machine learning systems operate within the scalar-reducible class, we demonstrate that scalar-irreducible dynamics naturally enable internally generated regime switching through feedback between fast dynamical variables and slow structural adaptation. Using a minimal dynamical model, we illustrate how this mechanism produces sustained endogenous regime transitions without external scheduling. Our results suggest a new dynamical paradigm for regime exploration and provide a potential route toward autonomous learning systems whose adaptive behavior is organized internally rather than externally prescribed.

</details>


### [128] [A Physics-Aware Framework for Short-Term GPU Power Forecasting of AI Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04074)
*Mohammad AlShaikh Saleh,Sanjay Chawla,Sertac Bayhan,Haitham Abu-Rub,Ali Ghrayeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向AI数据中心的物理信息DLinear模型（PI-DLinear），在DLinear的线性分解骨架上，把基于牛顿冷却定律、耦合GPU–显存双节点集总热阻-热容（RC）网络新推导的时变ODE作为可微物理约束，并结合GPU功耗限频（throttling）约束损失，在MIT Supercloud真实数据上以5–80分钟短期预测GPU功耗，精度优于16个SOTA时序模型（MSE提升0.782%–39.08%，MAE 0.993%–51.82%，RMSE 0.370%–22.28%），同时保持与DLinear完全相同的参数量（96k）与显存占用（0.376 MB），仅训练时间增加约1.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心承载LLM训练/推理等异质任务，GPU单卡功率密度已达300–1200 W、机架级功率突变更可超过132 kW，秒级数兆瓦的波动会冲击电网频率控制与AGC，因此需要5–80分钟的短期功率预测以支撑一次/二次/三次调频、需求响应与电网规划。现有基于Transformer/深度学习的预测模型缺乏物理约束，会产生物理上不可实现的预测，且在训练分布外场景（功耗限频、负载突变、限频后恢复）泛化与鲁棒性差；ARIMA等传统方法难以处理高维非平稳特征。更关键的是，此前没有可用的、能把功率消耗与GPU/显存利用率及温度相互耦合的时变ODE/PDE，无法构造物理感知框架。

Method: 模型：以DLinear为骨干（移动平均分解为趋势项与季节/残差项，各接单层线性投影后相加），在损失中注入三项可微约束：①L_data（标准MSE）；②L_r物理残差——基于热-电类比，用基尔霍夫电流定律在GPU节点与显存节点分别列写能量平衡ODE（Cg dTg/dt = αP − (Tg−Ta)/Rga − (Tg−Tm)/Rgm；Cm dTm/dt = (1−α)P − (Tm−Ta)/Rma + (Tg−Tm)/Rgm），并反解出功率变化率约束 dP/dt = (1/α)[Cg d²Tg/dt² + (1/Rga)dTg/dt + (1/Rgm)(dTg/dt − dTm/dt)]；由于只观测到总功率，引入潜在功率分配系数α（Pg=αP，Pm=(1−α)P）；用AutoDiff计算代理模型对时间的一/二阶导数，残差损失仅在观测到的Tg/Tm序列上计算，用预测功率驱动RC模型滚动外推温度。RC参数（Cg=5.408e6 J，Cm=5.481e6 J，Rga=2.037e-3 K/W，Rma=2.055e-3 K/W，Rgm=6.064e-4 K/W，Ta=27 °C，α=0.5085）由训练集经递归最小二乘预估计后固定。③L_throttle：基于数据洞察（限频事件表现为功率骤降>15%，与持续高利用率强相关而非极端温度），设利用率阈值θU≈90%、温度阈值θT取95分位数，对违反“ΔP̂≤0”的情形施铰链型平方惩罚。损失权重采用对数空间梯度上升自自适应并裁剪以保证正值与稳定。数据：MIT Supercloud（2021.2–2021.10，Nvidia V100集群），100 ms原始日志聚合到1分钟粒度，约33万步、238天，选取5个协变量（GPU利用率、显存利用率、GPU温度、显存温度、功率），Min-Max归一化。回看窗口240/360/480/600分钟，预测步长5/10/20/40/80分钟，与Transformer、iTransformer、TimeXer、TiDE、TSMixer、Reformer、PatchTST、Nonstationary Transformer、LightTS、FiLM、FEDformer、Pyraformer、DLinear、Crossformer、NLinear、Linear共16个基线对比，5个随机种子。

Result: 1) 精度：PI-DLinear在所有回看长度上取得最佳MSE（0.1546/0.1549/0.1559/0.1561）与最佳RMSE（0.3895–0.3914），MAE与DLinear持平或极近（0.1420→0.1439）；相对Transformer类与非Transformer类SOTA，MSE改善0.782%–39.08%、MAE 0.993%–51.82%、RMSE 0.370%–22.28%。FEDformer、Crossformer在长回看窗口明显失稳（FEDformer MAE达0.2747–0.3028，MAPE>2.3）。2) 场景行为：限频感知预测MAE/RMSE 0.4454/0.5103 vs DLinear 0.4703/0.5528；负载突变后恢复1.4650/2.3061 vs 2.5082/2.8610；限频消退后稳定性0.1112/0.1469 vs 0.1795/0.2274。3) 限频检测：平均检测率提升6.88 pp，L=360/T=10时65.29%→85.03%（+19.75 pp），L=480/T=80达99.12%；限频段RMSE平均改善3.92%，L=360/T=20改善28.61%（0.396→0.283归一化，即1256 W→897 W）；但T=5极短视野下检测率反而下降（61.92%→44.35%），MAE平均仅提升0.15%。4) 消融：固定λ=0.005与自适权重版本在各视野均优于DLinear，自适应版最佳（80分钟：MAPE 1.4987、MSE 0.2155）；仅用ODE解直接重构也可给出竞争力结果，验证ODE本身有效。5) 效率：PI-DLinear参数96,160、内存0.376 MB与DLinear完全一致，训练20.27 s/epoch（DLinear 10.43 s，约1.9×）；TiDE 162万参数/29.10 s/6.21 MB，FiLM 1290万参数/271.38 s/49.30 MB且精度无增益，被判定为被支配；在input-240/predict-80设置下MSE 0.213 vs DLinear 0.2190（-0.006，约3%）。6) 可解释性：权重热图显示趋势分支集中于最近观测（正权重集中于回看窗口右端），季节分支呈符号交替结构并随预测步变化。

Conclusion: 物理感知的线性预测（PI-DLinear）为AI数据中心GPU功率短期预测提供了强鲁棒性先验：以双节点RC热网络和牛顿冷却定律导出的时变ODE把功率预测转化为受能量守恒与热力学约束的动态推断问题，同时通过利用率—温度的限频约束编码了“持续高负载→功率必须下降”的运维物理机制，从而在限频、负载突变、限频后恢复等分布外/瞬态工况下保持物理一致性。关键经验是物理正则权重必须恰当：过强约束会过度约束数据驱动的最优解而损害精度，自适应权重最稳健；同时物理项仅增加训练开销、不增加模型规模与推理成本（96k参数、0.376 MB），适合部署在数据中心的监控/控制单元或微控制器。局限在于验证仅基于单一批量（MIT Supercloud，V100时代数据、约250 W功率封顶工况），α与RC参数采用RLS预估计并固定而非端到端学习，MAE等稳健指标上的增益相对有限，且极短预测视野(5 min)的限频检测率出现退化。

Abstract: AI data centers experience rapid fluctuations in power demand due to the heterogeneity of computational tasks that they have to support. For example, the power profile of inference and training of large language models (LLMs) is quite distinct and big divergences can result in the instability of the underlying electricity grid. In this paper we propose, to the best of our knowledge, the first physics-informed DLinear time-series model that can accurately forecast power utilization of an AI data center 5-80 minutes (short-term forecasting) into the future. The physics, based on a multi-node lumped thermal resistance-capacitance (RC) network consistent with Newton's law of cooling, is captured using newly derived time-dependent ordinary differential equations (ODE) that separately models and interlinks power consumption with the GPU compute and memory utilization and temperature. The resulting model, that we refer to as PI-DLinear, trained and evaluated on a real AI data center dataset and is not only more accurate than the state-of-the-art (SOTA) models tested, but the forecast profile respects the underlying physics under power throttling and load transient events. Relative to the SOTA transformer-based and non-transformer-based models, improvements in forecasting accuracy (averaged across all look-back and prediction windows) range from 0.782%-39.08% for MSE, 0.993%-51.82% for MAE, and 0.370%-22.28% for RMSE.

</details>


### [129] [A Self-Attentive Meta-Optimizer with Group-Adaptive Learning Rates and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04055)
*JiangBo Zhao,ZhaoXin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaAdamW uses a Transformer-based attention mechanism to dynamically adapt learning rates and weight decay per parameter group, achieving better performance and efficiency than AdamW across diverse tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules, leading to suboptimal convergence.

Method: Integrate lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) from parameter groups to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay; use meta-learning objective combining gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap; extend homoscedastic uncertainty weighting with task-specific priorities.

Result: MetaAdamW consistently outperforms standard AdamW baseline in validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity, reducing training time by up to 17.11% or improving performance by up to 11.08%; ablation studies validate component effectiveness.

Conclusion: MetaAdamW outperforms AdamW across multiple domains (time series forecasting, language modeling, machine translation, image classification, sentiment analysis), reducing training time by up to 17.11% or improving performance by up to 11.08%, with acceptable computational overhead.

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules. We address this limitation by proposing MetaAdamW - a new optimizer that integrates a self-attention mechanism to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay. The modulation factors are produced by a lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) extracted from each parameter group. To train the attention module, we introduce a meta-learning objective that combines gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap. A key novel contribution is the extension of homoscedastic uncertainty weighting (HUW) with task-specific priorities that directly scale the regularization terms - enabling domain knowledge to guide automatic loss balancing. Extensive experiments on five diverse tasks-time series forecasting (ETT), language modeling (WikiText-2), machine translation (Multi30k), image classification (CIFAR-10), and sentiment analysis (IMDB) - demonstrate that MetaAdamW consistently outperforms the standard AdamW baseline in terms of validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity. Depending on the task, MetaAdamW either reduces overall training time (by up to 17.11%) or improves performance (by up to 11.08%) while introducing only moderate overhead; in some cases, it can also mitigate issues of insufficient convergence caused by premature early stopping. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component, including feature versions, grouping strategies, and the proposed priority-injected uncertainty weighting.

</details>


### [130] [Transformation Categorization Based on Group Decomposition Theory Using Parameter Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04056)
*Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于伽罗瓦群分解理论（正规子群 + 同态映射）的无监督"变换分类"学习框架。其核心做法是"参数分割"：把单个变换 g 的参数拆成 θ 与 φ₁,…,φₙ，定义同态 f: g(θ,φ) ↦ θ 并施加 θ₁,₂ = θ₁·θ₂ 的同态约束，从而把 θ 固定为单位元所得到的变换集合作为正规子群 Ker(f)。该方法摆脱了前作依赖的"匀速线性运动""等距因子变换"等辅助假设，可处理含缩放等非等距变换。在旋转+平移、缩放+旋转、缩放+平移三组合成数据（各1000对图像）上的消融实验表明，加入同态损失 L_h 后归一化指标 I_h 显著降低（p<0.05），核分量 g(θ_e,φ) 只保留被假定为正规子群的单一因子，说明是理论约束（而非辅助假设）驱动了恰当的变换分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示学习旨在无监督地获得有意义的感知表示，并可作为人类发展的计算模型，但"什么才是好的表示"缺乏有原则的刻画。经典解纠缠方法追求各维度统计独立，遇到耦合因子（如平移与旋转不交换）便失效。作者此前提出基于伽罗瓦代数（正规子群分解）的学习框架，能处理可交换与不可交换两类情形，但其实现引入了群分解理论并不需要的辅助假设：(1) 图像序列由匀速线性运动生成，且复合形式为 (v₁∘g₁)^i 而非 v₁^i∘g₁^i；(2) 因子变换 v_i、g_i 必须是等距（isometric）的，从而排除了缩放、错切等非等距解。此外前作未做消融，无法证明恰当分类来自理论约束本身而非辅助假设。因此需要一种更贴合原始群分解理论表述、适用面更广且可验证的方法。

Method: 1) 数学框架：将变换 g 参数化为 g(θ,φ₁,…,φₙ)，定义 f: g(θ,φ…) ↦ θ，规定 θ 所在群 H 上的二元运算 ·。由同态条件 f(g₁∘g₂)=f(g₁)·f(g₂) 推出参数约束 θ₁,₂ = θ₁·θ₂；正规子群即 Ker(f) = { g(θ_e,φ₁,…,φₙ) }，即把 θ 置为单位元时的全部变换。相比前作，H 的二元运算不再必与 G 相同、H 也不必是 G 的子群，突破了两条过强假设。2) 学习模型：g 由位移生成模块 g_p 与逐像素变换模块组成，g_p 用两层 MLP（权重与偏置由权重生成器 ξ(θ,φ) 动态生成，ReLU 激活）实现，以表达旋转、缩放等复合变换，并借空间变换网络完成图像重采样。3) 损失设计：重构损失 L_r；逆元损失 L_i（同时约束正向与反向复合为单位变换）；单位元损失 L_e（θ_e,φ_e 为可学习参数）；闭合性损失 L_cn（用位置型参数估计器 E_p 从多点坐标对回归复合后的参数，n 取 2–8）；同态损失 L_hn（MSE(θ₁·…·θₙ, θ̂₁,…,n)，二元运算 · 由一个可学习 MLP 实现）；方差损失 L_v（约束 θ 与 φ 的批内方差接近 1，排除 Ker(f)=G 或 Ker(f)={e} 的平凡解）；唯一性损失 L_u（参数可从变换反推，避免冗余信息使同态约束"作弊"）。总损失为上述各项加权和。4) 实验：基于 Syn-obj 构造图像对数据集（64×64，物体占 32×32，先做物体中心变换再做全局变换；旋转 ±40°、平移 0–16 像素随机方向、缩放 0.7–1.4，各 1000 对）；d_θ=d_φ=4；使用图像型参数估计器 E_i 与位置型估计器 E_p（8×8 网格采样 64 个像素点）；批大小 100，每步采样 1000 组复合，种子 1–40。评估用 I_h = MSE(θ_n, e_θ)/MSE(θ_c, e_θ)，以 δ=0（去掉同态损失）作消融对照。

Result: 三组数据集上，加入同态约束的训练得到的 I_h 显著小于消融条件（t 检验 p<0.05），说明被假定为正规子群的变换 g_n 的参数确实被映射为单位元，即 g_n 确为同态 f 的核。定性结果（图3）显示：学习到的整体变换 g(θ,φ)x 与目标图像一致；消融条件下核分量 g(θ_e,φ) 混入两个因子的成分，而加入同态约束后核分量只保留被假定构成正规子群的那个单一因子。作者称这是首次以定量方式证明基于群分解的方法在含非等距变换（缩放）的场景中能实现恰当的变换分类。

Conclusion: 本文通过重新审视既有同态表述中不必要的理论假设，将同态约束重构为"单一变换的参数分割"，在不做匀速运动、等距性等先验约束的前提下，实现了更一般的群分解式变换分类学习，并通过消融实验验证理论约束的必要性。其意义在于为表示学习提供了一个层次化结构学习的视角（高层类 G 与低层子类 N 通过同态 f 显式关联），区别于以往仅关注低层类间关系的研究。作者指出当前仅处理 G 与 N⊴G 的两层关系，未来的扩展方向是对已被同态约束支配的参数进一步分解并再施加同态约束，从而获得三层及以上的层次结构，这有望为婴儿获得结构化知识的计算建模作出贡献。

Abstract: Representation learning seeks meaningful sensory representations without supervision and can model aspects of human development. Although many neural networks empirically learn useful features, a principled account of what makes a representation "good" remains elusive. We study unsupervised categorization of transformations between pairs of inputs under algebraic constraints. Classical disentanglement favors mutually independent factors and fails when factors are coupled. Our prior Galois-theoretic approach decomposes a group via normal subgroups by learning a product of two transformations with one factor constrained to a normal subgroup, covering both commutative and non-commutative cases. That method, however, relied on auxiliary assumptions (e.g., motion and isometry restrictions) not required by decomposition theory, and ablations did not separate theory-based from auxiliary effects. We propose parameter division for a single transformation: we split its parameter into components, impose homomorphism constraints mapping the full transformation to one component, and identify the normal subgroup as the set of transformations when that component is fixed to the identity. This formulation drops the previous auxiliary assumptions and applies more broadly. We evaluate on image pairs involving rotation, translation, and scale; ablations show that group-decomposition constraints drive appropriate categorization.

</details>


### [131] [LUCAS-MEGA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Representation Learning in Soil-Environment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04323)
*Kuangdai Leng,Simon Jeffery,Panos Panagos,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCAS-MEGA是一个大规模多模态土壤数据集，融合68个数据源、超70,000样本和1,000+特征，通过SoilFuser多智能体融合 pipeline实现标准化和统一，再通过SoilFormer自监督Transformer进行预训练，实现了stable training、strong predictive performance和uncertainty-aware predictions，并可复现established soil processes。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Soil science is hindered by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets, limiting modeling to small-scale predictions rather than large-scale representation learning.

Method: SoilFuser: a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous formats, resolves unit/codebook inconsistencies and erroneous values, incorporates NLP annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes into a unified ML-ready feature space. SoilFormer: a multimodal tabular transformer pretrained via self-supervised feature masking.

Result: Stable training, strong predictive performance, and uncertainty-aware representations that recover relationships consistent with established soil processes.

Conclusion: LUCAS-MEGA is released as an open-access resource with agent-friendly APIs for structured querying, enabling scalable, data-driven soil modeling workflows.

Abstract: Understanding soil is fundamental to agriculture, carbon cycling, and environmental sustainability, yet progress is limited by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets that constrain modeling to small-scale predictive settings rather than high-dimensional representation learning. We introduce LUCAS-MEGA, a large-scale multimodal dataset constructed through systematic data fusion of European soil-environment observations, with the LUCAS survey as its backbone. The fused dataset comprises over 70,000 samples and more than 1,000 features spanning physical, chemical, environmental, biological, and visual attributes, aggregated from 68 source datasets. To enable integration at scale, we develop SoilFuser, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop data fusion pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous data formats and measurement protocols, resolves inconsistencies and invalid entries (e.g., unit inconsistencies, codebook mismatches, and erroneous values), incorporates natural language annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes and metadata into a unified, machine learning-ready feature space. The resulting dataset captures key characteristics of real-world soil observations, including multimodality, uneven feature coverage, and heterogeneous uncertainty. To demonstrate the usability of LUCAS-MEGA for data-driven modeling, we pretrain a multimodal tabular transformer (SoilFormer) using a self-supervised objective based on feature masking, achieving stable training, strong predictive performance, and representations that support uncertainty-aware prediction. We further show that the learned representations recover relationships consistent with established soil processes. LUCAS-MEGA is released with open access and is accompanied by composable, agent-friendly APIs that support structured querying and data-driven workflows.

</details>


### [132] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对LLM辅助NAS中的功能纠缠问题，提出SPARK方法通过因子条件化编辑实现更精准的架构修改，在CLRS-DFS上取得28.1倍采样效率提升和22.9%的OOD准确率改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索(NAS)面临关键挑战：需在昂贵评估下整合既有架构知识的同时探索新设计。虽然大语言模型(LLM)有潜力将丰富的架构和编码先验转化为可执行代码，但由于功能纠缠现象——单一编辑可能无意中耦合多个交互功能因素，导致局部修改产生非局部行为和性能偏移——使LLM的实际应用受限。

Method: 提出SPARK方法，通过显式选择待修改的功能因子并基于该因子条件化编辑来激活相关先验知识，从而减少纠缠副作用，实现更精准的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS上实现了28.1倍采样效率的架构进化加速，OOD准确率相对提升22.9%。

Conclusion: SPARK通过因子条件化编辑使LLM知识在功能纠缠条件下可用，实现了更有针对性、更可靠的架构修改，为LLM辅助NAS提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

</details>


### [133] [Cross-Model Consistency of Feature Importance in Electrospinning: Separating Robust from Model-Dependent Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04905)
*Mehrab Mahdian,Ferenc Ender,Tamas Pardy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以96组PVA静电纺丝实验数据，训练21个跨线性、树集成、核方法、神经网络与实例基五类ML模型，统一用SHAP计算特征重要性，并以基于秩的统计（平均秩、秩标准差、Spearman相关、top-k一致性）量化跨模型一致性。结论：预测性能与解释可靠性是两种不同属性——浓度为唯一完全稳健参数（σr=0），流量与电压高度模型依赖（σr>0.9），提示单一模型给出的特征重要性在小数据集上不可靠，需做跨模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电纺丝中过程参数（浓度、电压、流量、针尖-接收距离）通过"过程—结构—性能"链强烈影响纤维直径，ML被广泛用于建模该关系并推断参数重要性，多数研究仅依赖单一模型，默认其特征重要性稳健可靠；而静电纺丝数据集普遍小（几十至百量级）、异质性强，不同模型族可能在同样拟合精度下学到完全不同的内部表示，若把单一模型的特征重要性当作物理证据（如认定流量主导纤维直径），会导致实验资源浪费，尤其在逆向设计中型替代理的错误归因会直接传递到推荐参数集。作者因此提出核心问题：在小规模静电纺丝数据集上，能否信任ML模型给出的特征重要性结论？此前无工作系统评估静电纺丝中跨模型族的特征重要性一致性。

Method: 数据：源自Ziabari等报道的96组PVA静电纺丝实验（经Cogni-e-SpinDB 1.0发布），RSM设计，4因子各4水平（浓度8/9/10/12 wt%，电压15/20/22.5/25 kV，流量0.20/0.25/0.30/0.40 mL/h，针尖-接收距离10/12.5/15/20 cm），目标为SEM测得平均纤维直径（约200–350 nm，均值259 nm，SD 34.5 nm），环境条件恒定使浓度成为黏度代理，特征标准化。模型：21个模型覆盖五族——线性（Linear/Ridge/Lasso/Elastic Net）、树集成（RF、GB、HistGB、XGBoost、LightGBM、AdaBoost、DT）、核方法（SVR-RBF/Poly）、神经网络（MLP 128×64、64×32）、实例基（KNN），使用默认或轻微调参以使差异反映模型族特性而非配置。评估：5折交叉验证报告平均R²。解释：统一用SHAP（树模型用TreeSHAP，其余用KernelSHAP），全局重要性为样本上SHAP值绝对值的均值φ_j，再按降序排名。可靠性分析：计算每个特征跨模型的平均秩、秩标准差σ_r、模型两两Spearman秩相关、top-k一致性，将特征置于"稳健—模型依赖"可靠性谱上。实现：Python 3.10、scikit-learn/xgboost/lightgbm、shap、scipy/numpy。

Result: 树集成模型R²最高（接近0.9），线性模型中等，核方法与实例基居中，神经网络较低且折间不稳定；但不同族中存在一批精度相近的模型，构成可靠性分析的前提。SHAP秩矩阵显示浓度在全部21个模型中一致排第一（平均秩1.000，σ_r=0.000）；电压与针尖-接收距离一般居前但相互次序有波动（针尖-接收距离σ_r=0.526，平均秩3.095，属中等稳定）；流量秩波动最大（平均秩2.905，σ_r=0.921），在部分模型中从中等重要降至最不重要，与电压（平均秩3.000，σ_r=0.926）同属模型依赖参数。跨模型平均两两Spearman相关为0.584，属中等一致性，树族内部相关性更高、线性与神经网络之间更低；top-1一致性1.000，top-3降至0.778（±0.157）。结果表明预测性能与解释可靠性本质上是两种不同属性：精度相近的模型可能给出相互矛盾的重要性排序。

Conclusion: 在96样本的小数据静电纺丝数据集中，仅溶液浓度是跨模型完全稳健的参数（零秩方差），与黏度/链缠结控制射流拉伸的物理机制一致；电压与针尖-接收距离为中等可靠，流量与电压高度模型依赖，其重要性结论不应依据任何单一模型给出。作者强调单模型特征重要性在小数据中可能反映模型归纳偏置而非过程物理，需显式跨模型验证；该框架可推广到任何存在多模型等精度情形的领域。局限：单一聚合物（PVA）、受控环境、96样本，未纳入温湿度等环境变量，SHAP仅刻画关联而非因果（RSM水平非完全独立随机化），默认超参可能低估各族最大性能。展望：扩展到更大规模、多聚合物数据集，纳入环境变量，并推广到形态分类与不稳定性检测等分类任务。数据与代码已公开（Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.16731638；GitHub: taltechloc/Cogni-E-Spin-FIRE）。

Abstract: Electrospinning is a highly sensitive fabrication process in which small variations in operating parameters can significantly influence fiber morphology and material performance. Machine learning (ML) methods are increasingly employed to model these process-structure relationships and to identify the relative importance of processing variables. However, most existing studies rely on a single ML model, implicitly assuming that the resulting feature importance is robust and reproducible. In this study, the consistency of feature importance across multiple ML model families was systematically evaluated using a curated dataset of 96 polyvinyl alcohol (PVA) electrospinning experiments. Twenty-one ML models representing linear, tree-based, kernel-based, neural network, and instance-based approaches were trained and compared. To provide a unified interpretability framework, SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) values were used to calculate feature importance consistently across all models. A rank-based statistical analysis was then performed to quantify inter-model agreement and assess the robustness of parameter rankings. The results demonstrate that predictive performance and interpretive reliability are fundamentally distinct properties. Although several models achieved comparable predictive accuracy, substantial differences were observed in their feature importance rankings. Solution concentration emerged as the most robust and consistently influential parameter (variability = 0), whereas flow rate and applied voltage exhibited high ranking variability (variability > 0.9), indicating strong model dependence. These findings suggest that feature importance derived from a single ML model may be unreliable, particularly for small experimental datasets, and highlight the importance of cross-model validation for achieving trustworthy interpretation in ML-assisted electrospinning research.

</details>


### [134] [MP-ISMoE: Mixed-Precision Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04058)
*Yutong Zhang,Zimeng Wu,Shangcai Liao,Shujiang Wu,Jiaxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MP-ISMoE 把「骨干网络 8-bit 量化 + 侧网络稀疏 MoE 扩容」组合成一个混合精度微调框架：GNP-IQ（高斯噪声扰动的迭代重量化）在保持 LayerNorm 全精度可训练的同时压缩骨干显存，ISMoE（交互式稀疏专家混合）用节省下来的显存扩大侧网络容量，并借助骨干 [CLS] 显著 token 引导专家选择以抑制知识遗忘。实验表明其在多类视觉-语言与纯语言任务上以与 SOTA METL 方法相当的显存/参数量取得更优精度（如 GLUE T5-base 上 3.2GB vs 全量微调 17.6GB，MSCOCO1K Rsum 542.6 对 SHERL 537.5），且稀疏 MoE 不带来额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对参数高效迁移学习（PETL）与显存高效迁移学习（METL）的两类固有缺陷展开：PETL（偏置微调、Prompt、Adapter 等）虽然可训练参数极少，但梯度仍需穿过庞大骨干网络，训练显存开销与参数削减不成比例；METL（侧网络/梯子模块结构，如 LST、UniPT、SHERL）通过冻结骨干、只对轻量侧网络回传梯度来规避显存问题，但其严格显存预算反过来 severely 限制了侧网络的学习容量。作者明确指出既有 METL 的三大不足：(1) 显存预算分配次优——骨干占主导显存，侧网络仅获小份额且参数量受硬约束，压制了表征能力；(2) 侧网络结构僵化简单——通常按骨干比例等比缩小，继承密集激活特征，固定设计导致显存-容量权衡次优；(3) 对骨干引导信息的利用不充分——仅以加权求和方式直接融合骨干特征，未能挖掘互补知识，因而易过拟合并发生知识遗忘。目标是让显存预算在骨干与侧网络间更合理重分配，并在严格显存下扩大侧网络容量。

Method: 整体框架（Figure 1）为「混合精度骨干 + 可训练侧网络 ISMoE」：训练时骨干大部分权重冻结于 8-bit（weight-only 后训练量化），LayerNorm 参数保持全精度可训练，侧网络以 16-bit 训练。

一、GNP-IQ（高斯噪声扰动迭代量化）
1. 非对称 weight-only PTQ：对除 Φ_LN 外的权重做逐层（非逐通道）量化，w_q = clamp(⌊w_f/s⌋+z, 0, 2^n−1)，s=(r_max−r_min)/(2^n−1)，z 由量化区间与 r_max 推出；前向时反量化为 w_d = s·(w_q−z)。
2. 迭代重量化：由于部分量化权重在微调中仍会更新，原有 s、z 失配导致量化误差 Error_q（以 MSE 衡量）上升；因此每 M 个 epoch 随机抽取 p% 的量化权重重算 s、z，共执行 T=N_epoch/M 次，以跟随权重分布漂移。
3. 高斯噪声扰动：在每次重量化前对权重注入 ε_t ~ N(μ_t, σ_t² I)，其中 μ_t、σ_t 为可学习参数。该扰动模拟两次重量化之间潜在参数更新的累积效应，使骨干能预判其最优量化状态，缓解精度损失。

二、ISMoE（交互式侧网络专家混合）
1. 稀疏 MoE 侧网络：复制原侧网络 FFN 块构成 N 个专家 E_i；线性门控 g(·)∈R^N 计算原始门控分数，经 masked top-k 操作 M_k（保留 top-k，其余置 −∞）后 Softmax 得到路由概率 G(·)；输出为被选专家输出的概率加权和 x_out = Σ_i G_i(x_in)·E_i(x_in)。相比直接加宽稠密侧网络，稀疏 MoE 能以更少可训练参数与显存换取更大容量，且推理时仅激活固定数量专家、无额外推理开销。
2. 跨网络交互引导的专家选择：从冻结骨干提取显著 token h_Φ^0（Transformer 中通常为 [CLS]）作为通用知识代理；为每个专家引入可学习的代表性 token r_i∈R^D（r∈R^{N×D}）；计算相关性分数 c = Norm(h_Φ^0 × r)，并将其作为通用知识先验调制路由：g′(x) = (Softmax(g(x)) + c)/2，再按 Eq.(6) 执行 top-k 选择与输出加权。该机制显式串联骨干与侧网络能力，使侧专家不过拟合任务特有模式、缓解知识遗忘。

三、实验设置：与 UniPT/SHERL 保持一致的优化器、warm-up、batch size、训练轮数等；骨干含 VSE∞（ITR）、CLIP4Clip（VTR）、CLIP-ViL（QA）、MDETR（VG）、T5-base/large（NLP）；指标含 R@1/Rsum、QA 准确率、mAP、GLUE 的 Acc/F1/MCC/Pearson-Spearman，以及可训练参数量与训练显存。

Result: 1) 视觉-语言任务（Table 1）：与 UniPT/SHERL 结合（Ours†/‡）相比，跨模态检索 R@1 平均提升 1.4%/1.2%、Rsum 提升 4.6%/4.4%；问答提升 0.80%/0.79%；视觉定位提升 5.47%/4.92%；与全量微调差距显著缩小（如 MSCOCO1K Rsum 542.6 vs 全量 542.7，而 SHERL 为 537.5；RefCOCO Test 87.26 vs 86.39）。2) 显存效率：多数情况下训练显存比 LST 降低约 50%，相对 UniPT/SHERL 仅增加约 1GB；参数量与基线相当（如 Flickr30K 11.8M vs SHERL 11.3M）。3) 纯语言任务（Table 2，GLUE）：T5-base 上训练显存从全量微调的 17.6GB 降至 3.2GB（降幅 81.8%），而 Adapter/LoRA 在同等可训练参数规模下仅约 25.6%；平均分 84.9（Ours‡）优于 SHERL 84.3、UniPT 84.3、Adapter/LoRA 85.3 附近水平，且比 BitFit 高 0.8%、比 Prompt 高 12.7%，显存仅为二者的 29.9%/14.4%；T5-large 上平均 88.1，比 Prompt 高 15.7%，显存 7.6GB 低于全量 17.6GB。4) 消融（Table 3/4）：单独 GNP-IQ 将显存 24.4GB→18.4GB（−24.6%），精度略降；单独 ISMoE 提升 R@1 2.2%、Rsum 6.7%，显存上升；两者结合相对基线 R@1 +1.5%、Rsum +5.1%，显存仅微增（25.5GB）。精度策略对比显示统一低精度与混合精度分别省显存 36.9%/39.3% 但精度明显下降，混合精度叠加高斯噪声后可恢复精度（Rsum 534.8 vs 531.4）；MoE 单独 +1.6%/+5.2%，再加入骨干相关性引导再 +2.2%/+6.7%，且参数量与显存基本不变。此外还分析了重量化比例 p 与专家数 N 的影响（详见扩展版）。

Conclusion: MP-ISMoE 通过「量化压缩骨干 → 显存重分配 → MoE 扩容侧网络 → 骨干显著 token 交互引导专家选择」这一闭环，突破了 METL 侧网络容量受显存硬约束的核心瓶颈：在保持可训练参数量与训练显存与 SOTA METL 方法相当（且显著低于全量微调）的前提下，于多种视觉-语言与纯语言任务上取得更优精度，稀疏 MoE 保证无额外推理开销。论文表明混合精度训练与骨干—侧网络跨分支交互可在资源受限场景下提供更优的参数/显存/性能折中，为大规模基础模型的高效适配提供了新路径（代码开源，扩展版含更多超参分析）。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient transfer learning (PETL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for adapting pre-trained foundation models to downstream tasks, significantly reducing trainable parameters yet suffering from substantial memory overhead caused by gradient backpropagation during fine-tuning. While memory-efficient transfer learning (METL) circumvents this challenge by bypassing backbone gradient computation via lightweight small side networks, its stringent memory constraint severely limits learning capacity of side networks, thereby significantly compromising performance. To address these limitations, we propose a novel Mixed-Precision Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts framework (MP-ISMoE). Specifically, we first propose a Gaussian Noise Perturbed Iterative Quantization (GNP-IQ) scheme to quantize weights into lower-bits while effectively decreasing quantization errors. By leveraging memory conserved from GNP-IQ, we subsequently employ Interactive Side Mixture-of-Experts (ISMoE) to scaling up side networks without sacrificing overall memory efficiency. Different from conventional mixture-of-experts, ISMoE learns to select optimal experts by interacting with salient features from frozen backbones, thus suppressing knowledge forgetting and boosting performance. Extensive experiments across diverse vision-language and language-only tasks demonstrate that MP-ISMoE remarkably promotes accuracy compared to state-of-the-art METL approaches, while maintaining comparable parameter and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [135] [Continual Distillation of Teachers from Different Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04059)
*Nicolas Michel,Maorong Wang,Jiangpeng He,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型存储需求日益增长的问题，提出持续蒸馏（CD）范式，使学生模型可从不断变化的教师模型流中学习，但无需保留早期教师。引入外部无标签数据实现未见知识迁移（UKT），同时识别并解决顺序蒸馏中的未见知识遗忘（UKF）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型规模持续增长，部分模型所需存储空间已超过许多大规模数据集，亟需新的知识迁移范式来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出自外部数据蒸馏（SE2D）方法，通过在外部数据上保留logits来稳定异构教师间的学习；同时利用外部无标签数据实现跨领域知识迁移。

Result: SE2D方法有效减少了UKF现象，提升了跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续蒸馏是处理大规模模型知识迁移的有效范式，外部数据的使用和logits保留机制是解决知识遗忘问题的关键。代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning models continue to scale, with some requiring more storage than many large-scale datasets. Thus, we introduce a new paradigm: Continual Distillation (CD), where a student learns sequentially from a stream of teacher models without retaining access to earlier teachers. CD faces two challenges: teacher training data is unavailable, and teachers have varying expertise. We show that external unlabeled data enables Unseen Knowledge Transfer (UKT), allowing the student to acquire information from domains not present in the training data, while known to the teacher. We also show that sequential distillation causes Unseen Knowledge Forgetting (UKF) when transferred knowledge is lost after training on later teachers. To better trade off between UKT and UKF, we propose Self External Data Distillation (SE2D), a method that preserves logits on external data to stabilize learning across heterogeneous teachers. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SE2D reduces UKF and improves cross-domain generalization. The code and implementation for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation.

</details>


### [136] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出lookahead drifting model，通过顺序计算多个drifting项并利用先前项的信息，捕获更高阶梯度信息来改进分布映射任务的模型训练，在图像生成任务上取得了优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统drifting model虽然通过一步神经功能评估(NFE)在ImageNet上取得了SOTA图像生成性能，但仍有提升空间，需要更好的梯度利用方式来改进模型训练。

Method: 在每个训练迭代中顺序计算一组drifting项，利用先前计算的drifting项、正样本和模型输出来计算后续项，后续计算的drifting项能够捕获更高阶的梯度信息，最后将模型输出推向所有drifting项的加权求和方向进行优化。

Result: 在toy示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，该方法性能优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，lookahead drifting model在toy示例和CIFAR10数据集上优于基线模型，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

</details>


### [137] [Investigating Trustworthiness of Nonparametric Deep Survival Models for Alzheimer's Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04063)
*Jacob Thrasher,Kaitlyn Heintzelman,Peter Martone,David Kotlowski,Binod Bhattarai,Donald Adjeroh,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对AD进展分析中的深度学习生存模型的公平性进行系统研究，提出两个新指标(Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity和Kaplan-Meier Fairness)，揭示了现有模型在性别、种族、教育等敏感属性上存在显著偏差，为实现公正可靠的AD预测模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD是进行性神经退行性疾病，当前深度学习生存模型缺乏公平性考量，可能对边缘化群体产生不公平预测，亟需系统性的公平性研究。

Method: 采用非参数生存模型进行AD进展分析，通过时间依赖一致性纯度和Kaplan-Meier公平性两个新指标量化敏感属性(性别、种族、教育程度)偏差，并进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 研究发现深度学习生存模型虽能有效辅助临床决策，但普遍存在对敏感属性的显著偏差，为未来改进方向提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: 深度学习生存模型虽可辅助临床决策，但存在显著偏差。未来需关注模型公平性研究，解决对弱势群体的不公平预测问题。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Dementia (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease marked by irreversible decline, making reliable modeling of its progression essential for effective patient care. Progression-aware methods such as survival analysis are therefore crucial tools for the early detection and monitoring of AD. Recent advancements in deep learning have demonstrated remarkable performance in survival tasks, but alarmingly fewer studies have been conducted in the domain of AD. Further, the studies that do exist do not consider learned bias within the model itself, which could result in unfair and unreliable predictions toward certain marginalized groups. As such, we conduct a rigorous study of fairness in AD progression analysis along with a thorough feature importance study to determine the characteristics which are most important for reliable AD predictions. Furthermore, we propose two novel fairness metrics, called Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity and Kaplan-Meier Fairness, to quantify bias with respect to sensitive attributes such as sex, race, and education in nonparametric survival models. Our study demonstrates that while deep learning powered survival models are robust tools which can aid clinicians in AD care decisions, they often exhibit considerable bias, representing important avenues for future research.

</details>


### [138] [Single-Position Intervention Fails: Distributed Output Templates Drive In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04061)
*Bryan Cheng,Jasper Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过激活干预实验揭示了LLM上下文学习(ICL)任务身份编码的关键因果结构:单点干预完全失败(所有28层0%任务迁移,尽管同位置线性探针准确率达100%),而同时替换所有示例输出token的激活可在网络深度约30%处(layer 8)实现高达96%的任务迁移(N=50, 95% CI [87%, 99%])。该结果在LLaMA、Qwen、Gemma三个架构家族的四个模型上复现,并提出"分布式模板假设":ICL任务身份以输出格式模板的形式分布式编码在示例输出token中,而非局部化于单一位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性领域长期依赖线性探针来定位LLM中的任务表征,并普遍报告在特定层/位置上具有很高的分类准确率,这使研究者默认这些位置即为因果上重要的"任务表示所在处"。但"可解码性(decodability)"并不等于"因果相关性(causal relevance)"(Ravfogel et al., 2020; Elazar et al., 2021; Belinkov & Glass, 2019)。此前关于函数向量/任务向量(function vectors, task vectors; Todd et al., 2024; Hendel et al., 2023)的工作虽能成功干预任务行为,其机制却未被阐明——一个关键未解问题是:任务身份究竟编码在哪些(层, 位置)对上?单点干预能成功吗?本文正是针对这一空白,以严格的对照实验检验"可解码 ⇒ 因果重要"这一假设,并追问迁移的真正决定因素(表面相似度 vs 内部表示兼容性)。

Method: 以Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct(28层)为主模型,在8个确定性任务(程序化:uppercase/first_letter/repeat_word/length;数值:linear_2x;语义:sentiment/antonym/pattern_completion)上做5-shot ICL,采用"[In: x1 Out: y1] ... [In: xq Out:]"提示格式,并区分三类位置:示例输入位置、示例输出位置、查询位置。(1)探针:在每个(层,位置)对训练最近质心分类器(测last_demo_token、separator、first_query_token)。(2)单点激活移植:用源任务10条提示在该位置的均值激活替换目标提示对应激活,遍历全部28层;对照为零消融、随机消融、随机源激活、幅值匹配噪声、打乱位置。(3)多点干预:定义all_demo / input_only / output_only / last_demo四种位置集合,同步替换,测试layer 8/12/14/16,计算迁移率τ。(4)因果追踪:向位置注入高斯噪声(σ_noise=3σ_act),以扰动率δ衡量必要性,与移植实验的充分性相对照。(5)格式兼容性:构造操作相同但输出格式不同的任务变体(uppercase_period、length_word、repeat_comma、repeat_n、spell_out、情感标签变体)。(6)跨模型复现:Llama-3B、Llama-1B、Qwen2.5-1.5B、Gemma-2-2B。(7)辅助分析:第8层注意力分布、逐层消融、干预时机(注意力前/后/整层/持续)、Ward层次聚类与轮廓系数。指标为迁移率τ与扰动率δ,主结果用N=50并给出Wilson 95%置信区间。

Result: 1) 探针在示例位置各层达100%、查询位置峰值83%(L12),但单点移植在全部28层、全部三个位置上均为0%迁移;零消融与随机消融仍保持100%准确率,说明单点既不充分也不必要。2) 多点干预成功:layer 8处all_demo 96%(CI [87%,99%])、output_only 94%(CI [84%,98%]),input_only 0%、last_demo 0%;逐层扫描呈尖峰(L8=96%,L12=30%,L16≈10%,L0=5%)。3) 迁移稀疏但可预测:56对中仅7对(13%)≥50%,高迁移对全部属于{uppercase, repeat_word, length, linear_2x}程序化簇;语义任务(sentiment↔antonym)近乎隔离。4) 存在尖阈值:被替换输出位置数1–7→0–1%,10→10%,全部(~19)→90%;源示例数1–3→0%,5→93%。5) 因果不对称:查询位置加噪在L0–14造成53–100%扰动(必要但不充分),单个示例位置扰动率0%(集体充分但个体不必要)。6) 格式兼容决定迁移:uppercase→uppercase_period 0%、length→length_word 0%、reverse→reverse_spaced 5%、repeat_word→repeat_comma 90%、repeat_word→repeat_n(N=2)100%而N≥3为0%。7) 表面相似度不预测迁移(r=-0.05,p=0.69),激活相似度弱相关(r=0.31),任务簇归属才是有效启发式;tokenization混淆被排除(r=-0.35,token匹配对反而0%)。8) 跨模型复现:最优层一致位于~30%深度(Llama-3B 8/28、Llama-1B 5/16、Qwen 8/28、Gemma 8/26),迁移率分别90%/60%/90%/40%。9) 支持性证据:第8层查询token对示例输出注意力0.38 vs 示例输入0.12;干预时机以整层后替换最优(90%),持续干预反而下降(65%);错误分析显示失败样本中45.2%为保留目标任务、28.4%为部分迁移。

Conclusion: 本文提出并支持"分布式模板假设(distributed template hypothesis)":ICL任务身份并非以抽象规则或局部化的任务向量形式存储,而是以输出格式模板("[WORD]"、"[WORD] [SEP] [WORD]"等)的形式,分布式编码在所有示例输出token的残差流中;模板在约30%网络深度处(层0–8)完成编码,经注意力聚合到查询位置(层8–14, 查询对示例输出注意力显著高于示例输入),至层16左右格式不可逆地"定型"(commitment window)。方法论含义是:探针只能证明信息"存在",而行为断言必须经因果验证;与事实性知识可在单层MLP编辑不同,ICL任务干预需要跨位置协同替换,因此函数向量的成功本质在于跨提示平均隐式捕获了分布式编码。实用启发式为:输出token数匹配的程序化任务可互相迁移,语义任务不可;模板是抽象的(情感标签变体可95–100%迁移)但token计数刚性。局限包括模型规模仅1B–3B、任务局限于确定性变换与分类、部分分析样本量偏小(N=10)、整向量替换粒度粗,且不排除"抽象推理被投影到输出模板(readout format)"的替代解释;未来方向包括更大模型、编码器-解码器、开放式生成、多步推理、任务方向子空间与模板一般性度量。

Abstract: Understanding how large language models encode task identity from few-shot demonstrations is a central open problem in mechanistic interpretability. Prior work uses linear probing to localize task representations, reporting high classification accuracy at specific layers. We reveal a striking dissociation: probing accuracy completely fails to predict causal importance. Single-position activation intervention achieves 0% task transfer across all 28 layers of Llama-3.2-3B-despite 100% probing accuracy at those same positions. This null result is itself a key finding, demonstrating that task encoding is fundamentally distributed. Multi-position intervention-replacing activations at all demonstration output tokens simultaneously-achieves up to 96% transfer (N=50, 95% CI: [87%, 99%]) at layer 8, pinpointing for the first time the causal locus of ICL task identity. We establish the generality of these findings across four models spanning three architecture families (LLaMA, Qwen, Gemma), discovering a universal intervention window at ~30% network depth. Causal tracing uncovers an asymmetric architecture: the query position is strictly necessary (53-100% disruption) while no individual demonstration position is necessary (0% disruption)-resolving a key ambiguity in prior accounts. Crucially, transfer depends on internal representation compatibility, not surface similarity (r=-0.05 vs r=0.31), ruling out trivial explanations. These results establish the distributed template hypothesis: ICL task identity is encoded as output format templates distributed across demonstration tokens, fundamentally reshaping our understanding of how in-context learning operates.

</details>


### [139] [A Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection in U.S. Banking: Integrating OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN Compliance Requirements for Model Development, Validation, and Monitoring Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04076)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了RGF-AFFD监管治理框架，旨在解决美国金融机构AI欺诈检测系统面临的四框架碎片化合规问题。框架采用三层级治理架构，通过LSTM+XGBoost集成模型（ROC-AUC 0.9289）实现高性能检测，并通过RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型将模型指标转化为监管特定健康评分，实现OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN四项监管要求的统一满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国金融机构在部署AI欺诈检测系统时面临四个监管框架（OCC Bulletin 2011-12、SR 11-7、CFPB AI公告、FinCEN BSA/SAR要求）构成的碎片化合规景观，缺乏将监管要求与模型开发、验证和监控实践相连接的综合治理生命周期。

Method: 采用三层级治理架构，基于IEEE-CIS数据集(590,540笔交易)和ULB基准数据集(284,807笔交易)进行基准测试，对六种架构进行消融分析、时间漂移分析、SHAP可解释性分析和BISG公平性分析。核心创新包括LSTM+XGBoost集成模型和RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型，该元模型将评估指标转化为四个监管机构特定的健康评分和一个综合监管适应度指数。

Result: LSTM+XGBoost集成模型达到ROC-AUC 0.9289（F1: 0.6360），收益成本比为6:1。XGBoost展现出最强的时间稳定性（delta-AUC = -0.0017 vs LSTM的-0.0626）。RDT-FG元模型可生成针对四家监管机构的具体健康评分和综合监管适应度指数。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了第一个集成部署蓝图，同时满足OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN四项监管要求，包含社区银行实施案例和四项循证政策建议。框架通过RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型实现持续合规监控，为AI驱动金融欺诈检测提供了系统性的监管治理解决方案。

Abstract: U.S. financial institutions deploying AI-based fraud detection face a fragmented compliance landscape spanning four regulatory frameworks -- OCC Bulletin 2011-12, SR 11-7, the CFPB AI circular, and FinCEN BSA/SAR requirements -- with no integrated governance life cycle connecting these requirements to model development, validation, and monitoring practice. This paper presents the Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection (RGF-AFFD), a three-tier governance architecture empirically anchored in a multi-study empirical program. Using the IEEE-CIS dataset (590,540 transactions) and ULB benchmark (284,807 transactions), we benchmark six architectures including an LSTM+XGBoost ensemble, and conduct ablation, temporal drift, SHAP interpretability, and BISG fairness analyses. The LSTM+XGBoost ensemble achieves ROC-AUC of 0.9289 (F1: 0.6360) with a benefit-cost ratio of 6:1. XGBoost demonstrates the strongest temporal stability (delta-AUC = -0.0017 versus -0.0626 for LSTM). The RDT-FG Regulatory Digital Twin meta-model translates metrics into four regulator-specific health scores and a composite Regulatory Fitness Index for continuous compliance monitoring. The RGF-AFFD is the first integrated deployment blueprint to simultaneously satisfy OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN requirements, supported by a community bank implementation vignette and four evidence-based policy recommendations.

</details>


### [140] [EdgeRazor: A Lightweight Framework for Large Language Models via Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04062)
*Shu-Hao Zhang,Le-Tong Huang,Xiang-Sheng Deng,Xin-Yi Zou,Chen Wu,Nan Li,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeRazor是一个针对大语言模型(LLM)的轻量级混合精度极低比特量化框架，通过混合精度量化感知蒸馏、自适应特征蒸馏和熵感知KL散度三个模块，实现1.88比特量化性能超越3比特方法，存储压缩5倍，解码加速15.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型部署到资源受限边缘设备的需求下，传统量化方法存在局限：后训练量化(PTQ)在4比特以下性能严重下降，量化感知训练(QAT)计算资源需求大，而量化感知蒸馏需要手动选择蒸馏特征且依赖教师模型数据。

Method: 提出EdgeRazor框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)混合精度量化感知蒸馏实现细粒度精度控制；(2)自适应特征蒸馏从16位教师模型提取知识；(3)熵感知KL散度在人工标注和蒸馏数据集上训练，前向-反向平衡由教师输出分布决定。

Result: 在基础模型、指令微调模型和多模态LLM上验证：1.88比特EdgeRazor性能超越所有3比特方法，比领先的2比特PTQ方法高11.3分，训练预算仅为领先QAT方法的1/4-1/10；1.58比特的Qwen3-0.6B存储从1.41GB降至0.28GB，解码加速15.1倍。

Conclusion: EdgeRazor在所有比特宽度下均实现更高压缩比，为边缘设备部署极低比特LLM提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an increasing interest in deploying LLMs on resource-constrained devices, among which quantization has emerged as a promising lightweight technique that converts full-precision model weights and activations into lower-bit formats. Existing weight quantization approaches can be roughly divided into three categories: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) that calibrates quantized parameters on a small dataset without retraining but suffers from severe performance degradation below 4-bit, Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) that searches low-bit parameters using surrogate gradients but demands substantial computational resources, and Quantization-Aware Distillation that integrates QAT with knowledge transfer from a full-precision teacher but manually selects features to distill and relies heavily on teacher-specific data. In this paper, we propose EdgeRazor, a lightweight framework for LLMs with mixed-precision and extremely low-bit weight quantization. The EdgeRazor framework contains three modules: Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation for the fine-grained control of precision, Adaptive Feature Distillation that derives an $n$-bit student from its 16-bit teacher, and Entropy-Aware KL Divergence on both human-annotated and distilled datasets, whose forward-reverse balance is determined solely by the teacher's output distribution. Empirical investigations of EdgeRazor are conducted on base, instruction-tuned, and multimodal LLMs. Notably, EdgeRazor with 1.88-bit surpasses all contenders with the 3-bit precision, especially outperforms the leading 2-bit PTQ methods by 11.3 points, within a 4-10$\times$ lower training budget than the leading QAT approach. EdgeRazor delivers higher compression ratios at all bit width; the 1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B reduces storage from 1.41 GB to 0.28 GB while accelerating decoding by 15.1$\times$ relative to the 16-bit baseline.

</details>


### [141] [Position: the Stochastic Parrot in the Coal Mine. Model Collapse is a Threat to Low-Resource Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04127)
*Devon Jarvis,Richard Klein,Benjamin Rosman,Steven James,Stefano Sarao Mannelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型崩溃指生成模型在先前模型输出上训练时出现的性能退化现象，该现象威胁AI民主化努力，因其降低训练效率并使数据分布偏离边缘模式，从而对低资源和边缘化社区造成不成比例的损害


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容的激增，生成模型在先前模型输出上训练时会出现性能退化；大语言模型倾向于复现训练数据中的常见模式、依赖庞大数据集并产生高昂环境成本，这些因素共同导致数据退化、文化偏见强化和资源浪费

Method: 立场论文，综合分析环境与文化两方面影响

Result: 模型崩溃会降低训练效率，并将数据分布扭曲，使其偏离分布的尾部区域

Conclusion: 提出了行动号召，并概述了缓解模型崩溃效应的初步方向

Abstract: Model collapse, the degradation in performance that arises when generative models are trained on the outputs of prior models, is an increasing concern as artificially generated content proliferates. Related critiques of large language models have highlighted their tendency to reproduce frequent patterns in training data, their reliance on vast datasets, and their substantial environmental cost. Together, these factors contribute to data degradation, the reinforcement of cultural biases, and inefficient resource use. In this position paper we aim to combine these views and argue that model collapse threatens current efforts to democratize AI. By reducing training efficiency and skewing data distributions away from the tails of their support, model collapse disproportionately impacts low-resource and marginalized communities. We examine both the environmental and cultural implications of this phenomenon, situate our position within recent position papers on model collapse, and conclude with a call to action. Finally, we outline initial directions for mitigating these effects.

</details>


### [142] [Improving Medical VQA through Trajectory-Aware Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04064)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合DTW过程奖励的两阶段训练框架，提升医学VQA模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA数据集缺乏推理解释，推理能力对可靠医学VQA至关重要。

Method: 使用COMCTS算法生成推理轨迹，LLM作为验证法官。两阶段训练框架：监督微调(SFT)后接GRPO，采用基于DTW的过程奖励，通过句子 transformer嵌入推理步骤并计算向量序列间的动态时间规整距离来衡量生成推理与真实推理的相似度。

Result: DTW过程奖励+精确匹配奖励一致优于仅SFT训练：平均准确率从0.598提升至0.689，BERTScore从0.845提升至0.881，ROUGE-L从0.665提升至0.748。

Conclusion: 通过结合DTW过程奖励和精确匹配奖励的两阶段训练框架，显著提升医学VLM的推理能力，证明过程监督对医学VLM推理训练的重要性。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are crucial for reliable medical visual question answering (VQA); however, existing datasets rarely include reasoning explanations.
  We address this by generating reasoning trajectories for six medical VQA benchmarks using the COMCTS algorithm with open-source vision-language models, with an LLM serving as the verification judge.
  Building on these generated datasets, we propose a two-stage training framework: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel process-based reward.
  While standard approaches rely solely on exact-match rewards for final answers, we introduce a trajectory-aware reward that measures the similarity between generated and ground-truth reasoning processes.
  Specifically, we embed reasoning steps using sentence transformers and compute the Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) distance between the resulting vector sequences.
  Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that combining the DTW-based process reward with exact-match reward consistently outperforms SFT-only training, raising mean accuracy from 0.598 to 0.689, mean BERTScore from 0.845 to 0.881, and mean ROUGE-L from 0.665 to 0.748.
  Our results highlight the importance of process supervision in training reasoning-capable medical VLMs.
  We make our code and generated reasoning datasets publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MICCAI-R1-MED-VQA-code-B14B/

</details>


### [143] [Designing a double deep reinforcement learning selection tool for resilient demand prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04068)
*Bilel Abderrahmane Benziane,Benoit Lardeux,Ayoub Mcharek,Maher Jridi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRFFNN-ARIRBES 架构：以卷积+循环+前馈神经网络（CNN-RNN-FFNN）作为 Double Deep Q-Learning（DDQN）智能体的 Q 网络，在预测时刻自动从 6 个神经网络预测器组成的委员会中选出最优模型；同时提出基于平均奖励改进率（IR）的两阶段早停策略（ARIRBES）。在 Favorita 超市销售与客户零食需求两个数据集上，SMAPE/MASE 误差均优于委员会单个模型及均值/中位数/Stacking/FFNN-DDQL 等基线，且误差标准差最小、收敛更稳定，早停使训练时间从 19841s 降至约 5120s（Favorita）、10190s 降至 2635s（零食）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
