Method: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL将GMM-DaDiL框架扩展为完全去中心化的联邦架构。各客户端独立使用高斯混合模型对其本地数据集建模，通过共享的可学习GMM原子(atom)的标记Wasserstein重心进行联合近似，实现分布式协作学习。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)跨客户端的共享表示具有稳定性和一致性；(2)能有效重构目标域中缺失的类别；(3)在多源域适应基准数据集上达到具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL成功实现了无中心服务器的隐私保护型多源域适应，在类别缺失场景下展现出鲁棒的表示学习能力，为去中心化联邦学习在领域自适应任务中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-source domain adaptation seeks to transfer knowledge from multiple heterogeneous and related source domains to an unlabeled target domain in a decentralized setting. We address this challenge through a fully decentralized federated approach, DeFed-GMM-DaDiL, an extension of the GMM-Dataset Dictionary Learning (DaDiL) framework. Each client models its dataset as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and the federation jointly approximates them via labeled Wasserstein barycenters of shared, learnable GMM atoms. This design enables adaptation without a central server while preserving clients' privacy. We empirically study the stability of the learned representations in scenarios where the target domain has missing classes. Empirical results demonstrate that DeFed-GMM-DaDiL maintains stable and consistent shared representations across clients, effectively reconstructs missing classes, and achieves competitive performance on multi-source domain adaptation benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [On the Architectural Complexity of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04325)
*Nicholas J. Cooper,François G. Meyer,Michael L. Roberts,Carlos Zapata-Carratalá,Lijun Chen,Danna Gurari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个显式建模"张量运算内部结构"的统一框架——基于分层组合复形（HCC），将神经网络表示为秩5的HCC；据此一方面定量刻画近40年八类主流架构的架构复杂度演化（并指出已知架构的复杂度边界），另一方面按指定复杂度签名随机生成并开源3,028个由高元数/高耦合元数张量运算构成的新架构，其中最优样本（红星架构）仅约2万参数即在CIFAR-100上超过250万参数的MobileNetV2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络的统一数学框架（几何深度学习、范畴深度学习、神经代数几何、copresheaf网络）都建立在张量运算的高层抽象之上：把层视为抽象线性变换、把模型视为抽象参数化函数，因而将矩阵乘法、Hadamard积、多头投影、unfolding/patchify等低层结构整体抽象掉。后果有三：(1) 无法给出严格、可比较的"架构复杂度"定义；(2) 高元数（>2操作数）张量运算被实践界与理论界共同忽略，mode map（张量变换）也长期无人系统研究；(3) NAS 只在固定算子集合上搜索连接关系，高阶深度学习的两条路线（模型中心的多项式网络、数据中心的拓扑深度学习）各自孤立，缺乏统一语言。作者的目标是把"张量运算的结构"提升为显式、可分析、可构造的一等对象，从而同时支撑"回溯分析架构演化"与"系统构造新架构"两件事。

Method: 建立基于分层组合复形（Hierarchical Combinatorial Complex, HCC）的五层秩结构：元素（实值变量，rank0）→ 切片（严格弱序列表，rank1）→ 模式（对rank1单元格的划分，rank2）→ 广义张量（rank3，由PWO-超图刻画，含切片序兼容性条件SOCC，覆盖可注入MDA、jagged tensor与hyper-tensor）→ 张量运算与mode map（rank4）→ 神经网络（rank5，由运算与mode map组成，即"架构图"的组合化）。核心对象：广义张量（Def 4.1）、mode map及其mode map component（Def 4.3）、α元张量运算与coupling/contraction（Def 4.5），并给出组合表示：TOM（张量运算矩阵，编码coupling与张量的关联/收缩）与TEM（张量方程矩阵，编码运算与张量的关联，即架构图）。关键定理：Thm 4.2（可注入MDA与广义张量的对应）、Lemma 4.4（任意MDA可表示为mode map）、Thm 4.6（运算求值分两步：先由contraction数确定hyper-tensor的k-切片空间，再由两个基础运算折叠；广播机制统一适用于任意元数与任意阶复杂度，并保证jagged张量可参与运算）、Thm A.34/Cor A.35（当基础运算为实数乘加时，兼容的二元运算序列可合并为单一高元数运算，反之给出arity分解条件）。在此之上定义五种复杂度度量：运算复杂度C_op、张量复杂度C_T、元数复杂度C_α、阶复杂度C_O、耦合元数复杂度C_A，并以"最低秩编码"为唯一化准则进行架构编码。应用侧：先对FCNN/CNN/ResNet/Transformer/Poly-Net/MO-Net/ViM/TT-Net八类架构核心块做复杂度签名计算；再按目标复杂度签名采样（先采C_op×C_T的0/1矩阵作TEM，再对每个运算采C_α×C_O的0/1矩阵作TOM，随机采中间张量形状与非线性激活），并在ResNet34/Swin_T的stage-1上做图像分类训练评测。

Result: （1）复杂度演化（Table 1）：FCNN最简（C_op=1,C_T=2,C_α=2,C_O=3,C_A=2）；卷积首次把order复杂度提到6并引入unfolding mode map；残差块把运算/张量数提到3/6；自注意力在最低秩编码下首次表现为三元操作（C_α=3），是首个以高元数核心块构建的DNN；Post-Transformer架构复杂度激增（Poly-Net 4/9/3/6/2，MO-Net 4/9/4/4/2，ViM 27/45/3/4/2，TT-Net 5/11/3/6/3）。作者发现已知架构的边界：直到2025年才出现 coupling-arity>2 的核心块；所有已知 C_α>2 的架构都是"偶然"发现——原始描述均为二元运算，但经Thm A.34化简后暴露出更简洁的高元数编码。（2）数据集：开源3,028个高复杂度架构（其中C_α=4者2,330个、C_A=3者1,888个），附TEM/TOM/激活/参数量/训练曲线/权重；采样范围2≤C_op≤5、5≤C_T≤16、2≤C_α,C_A≤4、2≤C_O≤11。训练用AdamW+OneCycle、50 epochs、batch 128，3次独立运行取标准差。（3）效率结果：红星架构在CIFAR-100达65.52%±0.22（MobileNetV2为64.29%±0.04），总参数198,342，其中采样块仅46,342（约5层），120 epochs下仍领先+0.63，验证50 epoch足够收敛且样本排序在长训练下稳定；红星与若干高性能样本使用的新操作在深度学习语境与张量代数理论中均属新颖，例如C_α=4、C_O=7、C_A=3的操作（三个操作数为可学习张量，相当于被"因子分解"的全连接层，隐含结构偏置）。

Conclusion: 本文给出了首个显式建模张量运算结构的神经网络统一框架，并把"架构复杂度"从直觉概念变成可计算、可比较、可逆向设计的对象，揭示了开创性架构与复杂度跃升的对应关系以及已知架构的复杂度边界，同时展示了高元数张量运算在参数/深度效率上的潜力，将NAS从"在既有空间搜连接"推进到"构造新的高复杂度架构空间"。作者明确指出局限：该框架不涉及网络的功能性质与训练动力学（更适合用范畴深度学习或神经代数几何），架构为随机生成故红星并非最优，且数据集覆盖的复杂度区间有限；后续方向包括从HCC表示主动估计任意架构潜力、以及用神经代数方法研究高复杂度张量运算的表达力。

Abstract: We introduce a unified theoretical framework for the rigorous analysis and systematic construction of deep neural networks (DNNs). This framework addresses a gap in existing theory by explicitly modeling the structure of tensor operations -- lower level information that is often abstracted. Our framework enables two novel objectives: (1) analysis of the evolution of architectural complexity over deep learning history, and (2) automatic construction of novel architectures based on new types of tensor operations. Our study of DNNs introduced over the past 40 years reveals a connection between groundbreaking architectures and increases in different types of architectural complexity. Moreover, we identify several large classes of higher complexity architectures that have not yet been explored. We then collect a dataset of 3,000+ higher complexity architectures, which we publicly release at: https://github.com/combinatoriallabs/ArchitecturalComplexity.

</details>


### [193] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的统计精炼方法，用于改进已有去噪器的结果。该方法无需知道精确噪声分布、干净图像或标定数据，仅利用噪声数据本身的统计信息，通过贝叶斯框架评估去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，从而优化去噪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法依赖精确的图像与噪声建模，实际中因模型不完美、噪声假设不可靠或数据质量低，去噪结果往往不是最优的。特别是在缺乏干净图像样本且未知噪声分布的情况下，去噪结果难以与噪声统计特性对齐。

Method: 基于贝叶斯框架，构造含噪数据中的辅助信号来评估去噪结果的一致性，在仅知道噪声为像素级独立（以干净信号为条件）的前提下，利用噪声数据的统计信息进行优化。

Result: 方法能够增强去噪结果的统计噪声一致性，在无需标定数据的情况下有效提升去噪质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用噪声数据的统计信息，无需精确的噪声分布或干净图像即可提升去噪质量，实现去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性增强。

Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

</details>


### [194] [Budgeted LoRA: Distillation as Structured Compute Allocation for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04341)
*Mohammed Sabry,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Budgeted LoRA是一种用于大语言模型蒸馏的框架，通过结构化计算分配来同时压缩训练成本和推理成本。该方法在模块级别分配密集和低秩计算预算，在中等预算下达到1.74x加速（与标准LoRA困惑度相当），在激进预算下达到4.05x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效蒸馏方法（如LoRA）虽然降低了微调成本，但密集 backbone 保持不变，因此无法提供推理阶段的实际加速。需要在训练成本和推理效率之间取得真正的平衡。

Method: 提出Budgeted LoRA，将模型压缩建模为结构化计算分配问题。核心机制包括：(i) 模块级密集保留系数，(ii) 自适应低秩分配，(iii) 后训练压缩（选择性移除、近似或保留密集组件）。通过单一预算调节器控制学生模型家族。

Result: 中等预算下：困惑度与标准LoRA相当，模块级加速1.74倍；激进预算下：加速4.05倍，困惑度略有下降；在函数风格上下文学习探测上保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: 在计算受限蒸馏场景下，关键不在于匹配困惑度或移除更多参数，而在于控制密集计算如何转移到低秩路径。Budgeted LoRA证明了通过结构化计算分配可实现训练与推理成本的双重压缩。

Abstract: We study distillation for large language models under explicit compute constraints, with the goal of producing student models that are not only cheaper to train, but structurally efficient at inference time. While prior approaches to parameter-efficient distillation, such as LoRA, reduce adaptation cost, they leave the dense backbone unchanged and therefore fail to deliver meaningful inference savings. We propose Budgeted LoRA, a distillation framework that treats model compression as a structured compute allocation problem. Instead of using a fixed student architecture, we introduce a global compute budget that sets the final target fraction of dense computation retained. Under this constraint, the model redistributes capacity across dense and low-rank pathways via (i) module-level dense retention coefficients, (ii) adaptive low-rank allocation, and (iii) post-training compression that selectively removes, approximates, or preserves dense components. This formulation yields a family of students controlled by a single budget dial. Empirically, Budgeted LoRA matches standard LoRA perplexity at a moderate budget with a 1.74x compressed-module speedup; at an aggressive budget it achieves a 4.05x speedup with moderate perplexity degradation, and it preserves higher accuracy on function-style in-context learning probes. These results suggest that, under compute-constrained distillation, retaining behavior is less about matching perplexity or removing more parameters than it is about controlling how dense computation is transferred to low-rank pathways.

</details>


### [195] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化证明了合作部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟(OD)与动作延迟(AD)的等价性，指出两者生成相同的联合策略集和状态-动作-观测轨迹分布，在Dec-POMDPs中具有相同的最优解。然而，通过数值实验发现，尽管解空间结构同构，实际学习动态存在本质差异：非转移独立条件下等价性失效，TD算法的因果信用分配误差导致不同学习行为，并成功实现了从OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在无限时域单智能体场景下讨论OD与AD的等价性，缺乏对任意时域部分可观测合作多智能体问题的形式化证明，同时未探索该等价性在学习动态层面的实际影响。

Method: 1) 利用observation-action历史形式化建立OD与AD的等价性证明框架；2) 在TI-MDPs中推导可处理的最小局部增强状态；3) 设计数值实验对比两种延迟机制下的学习动态；4) 验证零样本策略迁移的可行性。

Result: 1) 形式化证明OD与AD在合作多智能体系统中生成相同 admissible joint-policy 集，状态-动作-观测轨迹分布相同；2) TI-MDPs中observation-action历史可压缩为最小局部增强状态；3) 数值实验揭示：(a) 非转移独立条件下等价性失效，(b) TD算法的操作约束和因果信用分配误差导致不同学习行为，(c) 成功实现OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 本文将单智能体无限时域结果推广至任意时域分散式合作多智能体问题，证明了混合延迟配置可简化为纯OD系统。尽管最优解空间结构同构，实际学习动态存在本质差异，零样本策略迁移的可行性为复杂延迟系统提供了统一高效解决方案的路径。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [196] [Covariance-Aware Goodness for Scalable Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04346)
*Xiaoyi Jiang,Bashir M. Al-Hashimi,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向卷积网络的免反向传播（BP-free）Forward-Forward（FF）局部学习框架，核心是 Bi-axis Covariance Goodness（BiCovG）：在通道轴与空间轴两个正交维度上把"goodness"从一阶/对角能量扩展到二阶协方差结构，从而突破标准逐通道平方和goodness在ImageNet-100/Tiny-ImageNet上大幅落后BP的瓶颈。配合轻量Logistic Fusion（跨层logit加权融合）与Feature Alignment Layer（块边界零初始化通道校正），BP-free模型在ImageNet-100达73.01%、Tiny-ImageNet达50.30%，并把可用的FF训练深度从浅层基线扩展到VGG-16（16层）。进一步提出Hybrid Goodness Blocks（HGB，可配置块大小m），在m=4时ImageNet-100达83.98%（与BP差距3.6%）、Tiny-ImageNet达54.48%（与BP持平），同时峰值显存较BP降低约47.8%~53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward (FF) 算法通过逐层局部goodness目标消除全局梯度流与全网激活存储，但在卷积场景下现有免BP的FF方法（如CwC、DeeperForward、ASGE、SCFF）在ImageNet-100、Tiny-ImageNet等复杂基准上显著落后端到端BP，差距从CIFAR-100的约5%扩大到ImageNet-100的约28%。作者诊断出结构性瓶颈在于goodness提取本身：标准逐通道平方和 g_c = Σ_{h,w} f_{c,h,w}^2 把 C×H×W 特征张量压缩为 C 维向量，仅保留非中心化协方差矩阵的对角元素（各通道边缘方差），丢弃了编码跨通道相关性的非对角项；而卷积表示的判别信息往往体现在通道间共现模式而非单通道幅度上（类比风格迁移中的Gram矩阵、分类头中 W^T f 的全通道组合）。空间轴上，全局池化把特征图视为空间平稳信号，抹掉了邻域共激活与跨尺度演化等二阶空间依赖。此外，随类数与深度增长还存在两个复合问题：固定随机读出投影容量饱和，以及梯度隔离块之间的表示错配。现有方案或停留在CIFAR尺度，或（如CwC）在组内仍用独立通道能量、ASGE随深度退化只能支持VGG8，均无法解决上述层次错位问题。

Method: 整体框架含五个协同组件，基于VGG-16（16个卷积块，Conv3×3→ReLU→Dropout→RMSPool→RMSNorm，块间detach）。(1) goodness-based 局部监督：每块从后ReLU激活提取goodness向量，经可学习线性读出 W_l（取代传统FF的固定随机投影）映射为类别logits，用本地交叉熵 L_l = CE(ŷ_l, y) 训练，梯度不跨块传播。(2) BiCovG：沿通道轴用可学习投影 W_cc ∈ R^{N×C}（N=C/r，r=8）线性混合所有通道后平方求区域均值，得到 N 个交叉通道能量探测，近似以低维子空间采样协方差 Σ_Ω 的 C/r 个方向，避开显式 O(C²) 估计；沿空间轴把 H×W 均匀划分为 s² 个区域，逐区域计算逐通道平方能量，形成 per-channel spatial (PCS) 分支；两个分支在两个尺度 s1<s2 上嵌套聚合并拼接为 g^BiCovG ∈ R^{2880}（各层组按通道档位选尺度对以统一维度）。(3) Feature Alignment Layer：在梯度隔离组边界施加通道级加性校正 h' = h + Δ⊗1，Δ = W2 ReLU(W1 GAP(h))，W2 零初始化使起始为恒等映射，其参数由后一组首层的本地损失优化，保持贪心局部性质。(4) Logistic Fusion：主干冻结后，用 L=16 个标量参数 α（softmax 权重、Adam lr=0.01、500 epochs、不到一分钟）加权融合各层logits；同时报告零参数的 Best Pred 基线。(5) Hybrid Goodness Blocks：将网络分为 m 层连续组，组内用标准BP联合优化、仅在块出口计算BiCovG并挂读出，组间detach并由FAL保持独立性，产出 L/m 组logits再进入融合；m=1 为严格逐层BP-free，m=L 退化为全BP，形成可调的梯度视野连续谱。分析上提出 Decline Area、Tail Retention、Shallow/Deep Gain 和融合权重参与比 N_eff 等逐层诊断指标。

Result: 消融（Logistic Fusion，未含FAL）：逐通道goodness在Tiny-IN/IN-100为43.25%/64.87%；加交叉通道分支(CC)为46.12%/67.16%；加多尺度分支(MS)为47.91%/68.55%；BiCovG两者合并达50.38%/71.60%（+7.13%/+6.73%，接近两者增益之和），叠加FAL后IN-100再+1.4%。最终BP-free（BiCovG+FAL，VGG-16，Logistic Fusion）：ImageNet-100 73.01±0.49%、Tiny-ImageNet 50.30±0.21%、CIFAR-100（VGG-8）70.80%（略超BP基线70.22%）；Best Pred分别为72.88%/48.41%，显示Logistic Fusion一致优于单层最优。横向对比大幅领先既有FF方法（SCFF在Tiny-IN 35.67%、ASGE复现59.38% IN-100、DeeperForward仅13% Fusion IN-100）。HGB扩展：m=2时IN-100 78.22%、Tiny-IN 50.20%；m=4时IN-100 83.98±0.02%（与BP 87.61%差距3.6%）、Tiny-IN 54.48±0.08%（BP为54.17%，持平）。机制分析：BiCovG同时带来Shallow Gain +2.45%、Deep Gain +3.90%，Decline Area降低6.6、Tail Retention +0.035、N_eff +0.73，说明其延缓深层饱和；FAL不降低DA但带来SG +1.06%/DG +1.67%，作用轴不同。效率：参数量由134.7M降至34.9M；单卡A100(batch 128)峰值显存BP 16.75GB vs BiCovG+FAL 10.18GB（−39.2%）、HGB(4×4) 8.74GB（−47.8%）；DDP双卡下HGB(4×4) 达−53.1%。HGB显存呈U形（m=1时L个goodness图并存为峰值，m=L时全网激活占主导），m=4最优。吞吐与BP相当（5090上BP 559 img/s vs 550~551 img/s），块独立性带来最多5.22×理论并行加速（HGB 4×4为1.88×），并可用交错执行把激活显存从O(L)降至O(1)。

Conclusion: 本文将"goodness提取"定位为BP-free FF可扩展性的关键瓶颈，并给出首个在通道轴与空间轴上同时超越对角协差的goodness编码BiCovG：可学习跨通道投影以远低于O(C²)的代价近似非对角协方差，嵌套多尺度聚合捕获空间二阶共激活结构。配合Logistic Fusion释放多层层次信息的互补性、Feature Alignment Layer修正梯度隔离块边界的表示错配，BP-free局部学习首次在VGG-16深度上稳定工作，有效将可用深度翻倍，并在ImageNet-100 (73.01%) 与 Tiny-ImageNet (50.30%) 上取得据称最强的CNN-based全局BP-free FF结果，显著超越ASGE、SCFF、CwC等基线。作为实用桥梁，HGB通过可配置块大小选择性放宽梯度视野，在m=4时把ImageNet-100差距压至3.6%、Tiny-ImageNet与BP持平，同时保持约50%的峰值显存下降与可比吞吐。局限与未来工作：在ImageNet-1K上验证跨通道/多尺度假设；刻画FAL收益随分辨率、深度、类粒度的变化规律；将HGB的梯度视野概念扩展到混合精度与神经形态硬件；并进一步发展把峰值激活显存降至O(1)的交错执行方案。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward algorithm eliminates global gradient flow and full network activations storage. However, in convolutional settings, existing BP-free FF methods significantly under-perform backpropagation on complex benchmarks such as ImageNet-100 and Tiny-ImageNet. We identify this gap as a structural bottleneck in goodness extraction: standard sum-of-squares formulation collapses feature volumes into channel-wise activation energies which omits critical second-order dependencies. To address this, we propose a framework centered on three key components. First, Bi-axis Covariance Goodness(BiCovG) explicitly augments the standard goodness function with structured second-order information along two axes: cross-channel projections that model inter-feature covariance, and nested multi-scale aggregation that encodes spatial correlation statistics. This provides a tractable approximation to covariance-aware goodness without the prohibitive O(C^2) complexity of explicit matrix estimation. Second, a lightweight Logistic Fusion module aggregates layer-wise predictions, amplifying the contribution of deeper representations. Third, the Feature Alignment Layer(FAL) introduces a zero-initialized correction at block boundaries to mitigate representation misalignment in deep locally trained networks. By introducing these three components, we effectively double the depth of viable Forward-Forward learning, extending robust layer utilization from shallow baselines to 16 layer architectures like VGG-16. The resulting BP-free model achieves 73.01% on ImageNet-100 and 50.30% on Tiny-ImageNet. As a practical extension, Hybrid Goodness Blocks control the scope of gradient propagation via configurable block sizes, further narrowing the ImageNet-100 gap to 3.6% and matching BP on Tiny-ImageNet, while still reducing peak memory by approximately 50% relative to BP.

</details>


### [197] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个用于评估语言模型结构化数学推理能力的基准测试套件，基于SL(3, Z)中的子群构造问题，通过密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性设计，可区分具备内化代数先验知识的模型与依赖通用计算的模型，并揭示模型行为的四种分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估方法无法区分具备深层代数先验知识的语言模型与依赖通用计算的模型，特别是在处理开放可判定性边界问题时。

Method: 构建基于SL(3, Z)子群构造问题的基准测试，每个实例提供整数矩阵生成子群，要求推理者（缺乏构造时间信息）通过Aschbacher分类分析或Membership查询推导出算术不变量（指数、素数满射性或成员资格）。

Result: 在指数变体任务中，某一模型花费152分钟推理，识别出核成员资格问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证，最终以"DON'T KNOW"作答而非提交计算出的余核候选值——展示了在基准测试所探测的开放可判定性边界上的校准元认知能力。

Conclusion: 基准测试暴露了模型行为的四向分类（正确提交、错误提交、正确弃权、错误弃权），而传统答案评分会将这些不同行为混为一谈。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [198] [Efficiently Aligning Language Models with Online Natural Language Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04356)
*Christine Ye,Joe Benton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何把稀缺的专家自然语言反馈高效地转化为可扩展的监督信号：在「模糊（不可自动验证）」任务上，通过迭代式在线 RLHF（对代理奖励过优化时停下→收集新专家反馈→更新代理奖励）来提升专家监督的数据效率。作者用两个「三明治式」设定（Qwen3-8B 写创意短文 + Opus 4.1 专家；Haiku 4.5 写对齐研究实验计划 + Opus 4.5 专家）验证：基于上下文学习（ICL）的代理奖励方法最高可恢复 35% 的性能差距、专家样本减少最多 50 倍；基于微调（SFT/蒸馏）的方法可恢复 80%–100% 性能差距，样本减少最多 20 倍。结论是：在线自然语言反馈能大幅提升专家监督的数据效率，且「权重内更新」优于提示脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）在数学、代码、逻辑等有验证器的领域已取得成功，但在「模糊任务」上（如对齐研究、外交、道德哲学、创意写作）无法用验证器廉价扩展监督：这属于可扩展监督（scalable oversight）问题，且属于「人类仍能监督、但输出规模使人监督不可行」的子区间——例如只有少数人能提供高质量反馈，或逐条完整评估耗时过高。因此核心诉求是：如何提高专家监督（尤其是其长文本自然语言反馈）的数据效率，从而在同样专家预算下恢复更多可激发性能。已有工作表明自然语言反馈比偏好对/标量奖励包含更丰富监督（可提升样本效率），且反思式提示优化等无梯度方法在部分场景优于权重更新，但这些结论尚未系统用于「用在线反馈在线训练 RL 策略」的闭环。同时人们缺乏在无需真正做 RL 的情况下预判代理奖励是否会被过优化的诊断手段。

Method: 研究采用「三明治」（sandwiched）缩小版设定模拟人类监督更强模型：专家=强前沿 LLM（Opus 4.1 / Opus 4.5）提供长文本反馈与标量分数；策略=明显更弱的模型（Qwen3-8B thinking / Haiku 4.5）；代理奖励模型与策略同源但强度相近。任务一：Qwen3-8B 写 1000 词创意短文（500 训练 / 150 评测提示词，Opus 4.1 评分噪声标准差 4–5 分）；任务二：Haiku 4.5 为对齐研究提案写 1000 词可实施实验计划（约 300 训练 / 60 评测提案，来自 ML Alignment and Theory Scholars 与 Anthropic 安全研究员项目）。所有实验用 GRPO 系算法（组内优势归一化、token 级归一化、全 on-policy + 重要性采样、无 KL 惩罚）。核心协议（迭代式在线 RLHF）：1）对代理奖励做 RL，直到对真实奖励过优化；2）停下收集专家自然语言反馈；3）将反馈高效蒸馏进代理奖励模型。两种代理奖励更新路径：(a) ICL 路线——用专家反馈生成 rubric 评分提示、或构造 in-prompt / pre-filled few-shot 示例外加 per-task 提示，在候选池中并行搜索（n_search=20/10）并集成 top-k=3 个对齐度最高的提示构成代理奖励；每完成 S=50 步 RL 用 n_check=100 条专家样本监测过优化；样本选择上对比随机、最大方差、最大分歧三种策略。(b) 微调路线——完整打分轨迹蒸馏（full-trace distillation）、上下文蒸馏（context distillation，分「含反馈+分数 deliberation」与「仅自然语言反馈」两版，对同一条专家轨迹生成改写并过滤提及原文的轨迹）、以及附加标量头直接回归专家分数的标量奖励建模；按固定步数间隔用新 on-policy 反馈重训代理奖励（heavy/light/ultra-light 三档预算）。基线：直接用专家奖励训练（性能上限/高水位）、直接用代理奖励训练（过优化，Qwen 恢复 8%、Haiku 恢复 39%）、反思式提示优化（GEPA 式，Qwen 无提升、Haiku 25%）。评测指标：performance gap recovered（PGR，相对基线与最大可得性能之间的差距恢复比例）与专家样本数；分析层面用一阶近似把 ΔJ_exp 化为代理与真实优势的内积，定义「按提示平均的优势 Pearson 相关」与「原始奖励相关」作为奖励对齐统计量，并用分层 bootstrap 估计不确定性。

Result: ICL 路线：Qwen3-8B 上 rubric 迭代 4 次、约 3K 专家样本恢复最高 35% 性能差距（约 50 倍样本节省），但首次重对齐后收益基本饱和、rubric 难以继续提升代理对齐度；per-task few-shot（每提示 2 例、约 5K 样本）同样可达约 35%。Haiku 4.5 上 rubric 3 次迭代、约 30 倍样本节省恢复约 35%，但迭代协议并未超过基线代理（基线本身较强），per-task rubric/few-shot 完全无提升。全局 few-shot（in-prompt 或 pre-filled）基本无效，且比 rubric 更快过优化。三种反馈样本选择策略（随机/最大方差/最大分歧）对代理对齐度无显著影响。微调路线：Qwen3-8B 上 full-trace 蒸馏在 heavy（20K 初始+每轮 10K）下完全恢复性能差距，light/ultra-light（约 80%），后者差距主要来自后期训练不稳定；Haiku 4.5 上 heavy/light 均 100% 恢复（10 倍样本节省）。上下文蒸馏与等数据量的 full-trace 蒸馏表现相当，均约恢复 80%，且「仅用自然语言反馈」与「用完整轨迹」效果相同，说明反馈文本本身即承载主要监督信息。仅用标量奖励训练代理奖励模型鲁棒性显著更差，每轮 RL 约 200 步内即过优化、优势对齐迅速下滑——完整自然语言反馈至关重要。从头训练实验显示第 2/3 轮蒸馏出的代理奖励可用全新策略训练完全恢复差距，而仅在基座策略输出上监督的第 1 轮不行。诊断学：初始优势对齐度可预测下游 RL 表现；RL 优化过程中代理与专家对齐度整体下降，越易被过优化的代理下降越快；一阶近似系统性高估真实奖励提升，甚至在真实奖励下降时仍预测正向变化（因奖励塌陷具有突发性与不对称性）。

Conclusion: 在线自然语言反馈是提升专家监督数据效率的有效手段：ICL 方法虽只恢复约三分之一性能差距，但可带来最高 50 倍的样本节省；SFT/蒸馏方法可几乎完全恢复性能差距并带来约 20 倍样本节省。核心经验是「从反馈中做权重内更新最有效」，而提示层面的脚手架（rubric、few-shot）对更强模型收益递减甚至无效——更强的策略/代理本身已能较好逼近专家判断，额外显式奖励指导反而更易被过优化。此外专家奖励相关性（优势 Pearson 相关）可作为解读与选择代理奖励模型的实用启发式，但一阶估计偏乐观，不能替代真实 RL 轨迹。作者指出局限：以 LLM 代替人类专家、任务与模型仅两组、每方法仅 1–2 个随机种子；真实人类反馈的变异性与更复杂的失效模式（幻觉、冗长、语言切换导致的突然崩盘）需要在后续工作中处理。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been used to elicit impressive performance from language models in many domains. But, broadly beneficial deployments of AI may require us to train models with strong capabilities in "fuzzy", hard-to-supervise domains. In this paper, we develop methods to align language models in fuzzy domains where human experts are still able to provide high-quality supervision signal, but only for a small number of model outputs, using online natural language feedback. Specifically, we train models by iteratively optimizing against proxy reward signals, stopping at the point of over-optimization, collecting fresh expert supervision, and updating the proxy reward. We construct proxy reward models from language models using in-context learning (ICL) and fine-tuning. We test our methods by eliciting creative writing and alignment research capabilities in Qwen3-8B and Haiku 4.5 respectively. For Qwen3-8B, ICL methods recover up to 35% of performance with 50x fewer expert samples, while fine-tuning methods recover 80% with up to 20x fewer samples and 100% with 3x fewer samples. For Haiku 4.5, ICL methods recover up to 35% of performance with 30x fewer samples, and fine-tuning methods recover 100% with 10x fewer samples. Our results suggest that online natural language feedback can substantially improve the data efficiency of expert supervision.

</details>


### [199] [Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04363)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,SoonYoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN是一种用于表格数据的Foundation Model，但在标签偏移场景下容易过拟合多数类。本文提出DistPFN，通过测试时调整预测概率（降低训练先验影响、增强后验贡献）来解决这一问题，并进一步提出DistPFN-T（引入温度缩放）。在250+数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN是一种新兴的表格数据基础模型，通过在合成数据上的上下文学习实现强性能。然而，研究发现TabPFN存在标签偏移脆弱性问题——在训练数据中往往过度拟合多数类，导致分布偏移场景下性能严重下降。

Method: 本文提出DistPFN，这是首个专为表格基础模型设计的测试时后验调整方法。核心思路是：1）重新调整预测的类别概率；2）降低训练先验（即上下文类别分布）的影响权重；3）强调模型预测后验的贡献。方法无需架构修改或额外训练。同时提出DistPFN-T，引入温度缩放机制，根据先验与后验之间的差异自适应控制调整强度。

Result: 在超过250个OpenML数据集上评估，结果表明：DistPFN/DistPFN-T在多种TabPFN变体模型的标签偏移分类任务中均取得实质性改进，同时在无标签偏移的标准设置下保持原有性能。

Conclusion: 通过测试时的概率调整策略，DistPFN系列方法有效解决了TabPFN的标签偏移脆弱性问题，无需改变模型架构或进行额外训练，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: TabPFN has recently gained attention as a foundation model for tabular datasets, achieving strong performance by leveraging in-context learning on synthetic data. However, we find that TabPFN is vulnerable to label shift, often overfitting to the majority class in the training dataset. To address this limitation, we propose DistPFN, the first test-time posterior adjustment method designed for tabular foundation models. DistPFN rescales predicted class probabilities by downweighting the influence of the training prior (i.e., the class distribution of the context) and emphasizing the contribution of the model's predicted posterior, without architectural modification or additional training. We further introduce DistPFN-T, which incorporates temperature scaling to adaptively control the adjustment strength based on the discrepancy between prior and posterior. We evaluate our methods on over 250 OpenML datasets, demonstrating substantial improvements for various TabPFN-based models in classification tasks under label shift, while maintaining strong performance in standard settings without label shift. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/seunghan96/DistPFN.

</details>


### [200] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了边际稳定、部分观测线性系统在非随机扰动下的在线预测问题。通过设计基于Luenberger预测器的定制提示来稳定无约束在线最小二乘学习过程，在有模型和无模型两种情况下均实现了对数遗憾，突破了传统方法依赖有界域/梯度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习方法依赖有界域/梯度假设，而边际稳定系统在非随机扰动下可能产生无界轨迹，导致传统保证失效。需要突破有界性假设的限制，为边际稳定系统设计能处理无界轨迹的在线预测方法。

Method: 引入了无约束在线最小二乘法，通过从任意稳定化Luenberger预测器构造的定制预测提示来稳定学习过程。利用模型知识证明提示残差一致有界，并采用尾部界追踪技术处理系统轨道的潜在无界增长，实现对数遗憾保证。对于无模型预测，提出了对称系统的通用提示设计。

Result: 对于有模型情况，从任何稳定化Luenberger预测器构造的提示可确保提示残差一致有界，实现对数遗憾；对于无模型情况，对称系统存在简单通用提示可维持对数遗憾，无需模型知识即可实现实例级最优的自适应在线预测器。

Conclusion: 该方法通过稳定性分析和尾部界的追踪技术，证明了在边界增长的情况下能够达到对数遗憾，实现了与经典固定增益观测器相比的实例级最优自适应预测器，为非随机扰动下的部分观测线性系统提供了有效的在线预测理论框架。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [201] [Extending Differential Temporal Difference Methods for Episodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04368)
*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文将差分时序差分（TD）方法扩展到情节性（episodic）强化学习问题。通过泛化差分TD并结合终止感知的奖励中心化，作者证明了该方法保持策略排序并与线性TD等价，从而继承其理论保证。实验表明奖励中心化可提升情节问题中的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard differential TD methods with reward centering can alter optimal policies in episodic problems, limiting applicability. Need to extend differential TD's benefits (bounded returns, offset removal) to episodic settings.

Method: Generalized differential TD via reward centering with termination-aware offset; established equivalence to linear TD for theoretical guarantees; extended multiple streaming RL algorithms to differential counterparts.

Result: The generalized differential TD maintains policy ordering in the presence of termination, inherits linear TD's theoretical guarantees, and empirically improves sample efficiency across various base algorithms and environments.

Conclusion: The proposed generalization of differential TD successfully extends value-based RL to episodic problems while preserving policy ordering guarantees, with empirical validation showing improved sample efficiency.

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [202] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入p进值流形学习的概念，提出了一种算法来解决问题，并提出基于 impartial games 的基准测试任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To extend manifold learning techniques to p-adic number spaces and establish benchmark tasks from impartial games for evaluation.

Method: The paper proposes a specific algorithm to solve the p-adic manifold learning problem.

Result: Algorithm and benchmark tasks are proposed for p-adic manifold learning.

Conclusion: The paper introduces a new research direction combining p-adic manifold learning with impartial games, proposing both an algorithm and benchmark tasks for evaluation.

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [203] [GraphPI: Efficient Protein Inference with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04376)
*Zheng Ma,Jiazhen Chen,Lei Xin,Ali Ghodsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphPI 将蛋白质推断（protein inference）建模为异质三部图（蛋白质-肽段-PSM）上的节点分类任务，用定制化 GraphSAGE 图神经网络学习蛋白质得分。模型在无标签的公开 ProteomeXchange 数据集上以现有算法（Epifany）产生的伪标签进行半监督训练，并通过硬负例诱饵蛋白信息与 10 轮自训练迭代精化标签，最终模型可跨数据集通用推理、无需逐数据集微调。在 iPRG2016、Yeast、UPS2、18Mix、Hela/3T3 等基准上识别性能与 Epifany 相当或更优（pAUC 多项第一），且在 Yeast 数据集上推理仅需 88 秒，而 Epifany 超过 14 分钟，运行时随蛋白数量线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质组学中的蛋白质推断（protein inference）需要从质谱检测到的肽段中判定哪些蛋白真实存在。核心难点有三：(1) 高丰度动态范围、酶解与质谱局限导致肽段鉴定存在歧义；(2) 共享肽段（shared/degenerate peptides）与单肽段蛋白（one-hit proteins）使判定复杂化；(3) 现有深度学习方法（Barista、DeepPep）因缺乏大规模高质量标注数据而难以落地，而精确蛋白质标注成本高昂；(4) 现有贝叶斯方法（ProteinProphet、Fido、Epifany）计算与内存开销大、依赖先验概率且需针对每个数据集做参数网格搜索。此外，以往深度学习方法通常需要针对每个新数据集重新训练或微调，计算代价高且易过拟合。

Method: 1) 三部图构建：节点分为蛋白质、肽段、PSM 三类；PSM 节点使用 Percolator 特征（XCorr、Sp、IonFrac、deltCn、dM、酶切位点等 18 维，见 Table S1），蛋白质与肽段节点各使用一个 one-hot 向量并叠加可学习嵌入层以对齐维度并保留归纳（inductive）泛化能力。边属性：(肽段,PSM) 边以肽段鉴定分数 E_uv 为权重；(肽段,蛋白质) 边定义共享肽段属性 S_uv——对连接同一肽段的蛋白集合 C，依据"高分肽段计数" f(k)=Σ 1_{d_l>ε}（ε=0.9）选出最优蛋白，其边权保留 1/|C|，其余置 0，用以惩罚非唯一证据肽段的贡献。2) GNN 架构：基于 GraphSAGE 改造，节点更新 h_u^(k)=σ(W_k·(h_u^(k-1)‖a_u^(k))+b_k)，并为不同边类型使用独立的消息函数与参数：蛋白-肽段边将 S_uv 作为特征拼接（信息较弱，作特征），肽段-PSM 边用 E_uv 作乘性过滤（信息强，作过滤）；共 6 层 GNN，隐藏维度 100，ReLU 激活。3) 半监督训练：以 Epifany 在 17 个无标签公开数据集（PXD 系列，Comet 搜索 + Percolator 打分）上按 5% FDR 阈值化得到伪标签，并将所有诱饵（decoy）蛋白强制标为负例（硬负例）；损失为蛋白节点上的二元交叉熵；随后进行 10 轮自训练，用模型自身的高置信度预测重新生成伪标签并重训练，最终蛋白得分为全部轮次模型的平均集成（score(x)=1/t Σ φ_i(x)）。单一训练好的模型直接应用于所有测试集，无需再训练或微调。

Result: 识别性能：在 entrapment FDR 1%–5% 区间的 pAUC 上，GraphPI 在 iPRG2016 A、iPRG2016 B、Yeast、18Mix、Hela、3T3 上排名第一，在 iPRG2016 AB 与 UPS2 上排名第二（仅次于 PIA / 与 Epifany 相当）；仅 GraphPI 与 Epifany 能在高共享肽段的 Hela/3T3 上取得满意结果。效率：Yeast 数据集上推理耗时 88 秒，Epifany 超过 14 分钟；且因无需逐数据集网格搜索 α/β/γ 与重训练，整体效率大幅提升，运行时随蛋白数量线性扩展。消融与机制分析：(a) 基于 target-decoy 训练的类 Barista 变体在含共享肽段的数据集（iPRG2016 A/B）上明显退化，说明引入 Epifany 伪标签并惩罚退化蛋白至关重要；(b) iPRG2016 B 上 5% entrapment FDR 下 TP 数：正常训练 187，去掉 decoy 176，将 5% FDR 内 decoy 误标为正类 183，证实硬负例 decoy 标签的贡献；(c) 案例分析显示 Epifany 依赖先验概率（由 decoy FDR 网格搜索优化，易过拟合）会高估 decoy 蛋白（约 0.7）、并对共享肽段退化蛋白施加过强惩罚，而 GraphPI 能给出更合理的评分；(d) FDR 估计精度：GraphPI 的估计比 Epifany 更不保守，但在 iPRG2016 AB、Yeast、18Mix 上最准确；(e) 补充实验表明模型可跨物种/跨 isoform-vs-non-isoform 数据库泛化（非人数据集上 pAUC 一致优于 Epifany）。

Conclusion: GraphPI 首次将 GNN 与半监督训练方案引入蛋白质推断问题，通过三部图建模与定制化 GraphSAGE 架构，配合伪标签、硬负例诱饵蛋白和迭代自训练，在多个共享肽段比例差异显著的基准数据集上取得与最佳贝叶斯方法 Epifany 相当或更优且更稳定的表现，同时凭借神经网络的并行性获得数量级的速度优势与线性可扩展性；由于 Percolator 特征的良好归一化，单一模型即可通用推理，消除了逐数据集训练/微调带来的过拟合与重复计算。局限与展望：目前主要依赖 PSM 层面特征，未来可引入蛋白-蛋白相互作用、翻译后修饰等额外信息以进一步提升推断精度。

Abstract: The integration of deep learning approaches in biomedical research has been transformative, enabling breakthroughs in various applications. Despite these strides, its application in protein inference is impeded by the scarcity of extensively labeled datasets, a challenge compounded by the high costs and complexities of accurate protein annotation. In this study, we introduce GraphPI, a novel framework that treats protein inference as a node classification problem. We treat proteins as interconnected nodes within a protein-peptide-PSM graph, utilizing a Graph Neural Network-based architecture to elucidate their interrelations. To address label scarcity, we train the model on a set of unlabeled public protein datasets with pseudo-labels derived from an existing protein inference algorithm, enhanced by self-training to iteratively refine labels based on confidence scores. Contrary to prevalent methodologies necessitating dataset-specific training, our research illustrates that GraphPI, due to the well normalized nature of Percolator features, exhibits universal applicability without dataset-specific fine-tuning, a feature that not only mitigates the risk of overfitting but also enhances computational efficiency. Our empirical experiments reveal notable performance on various test datasets and deliver significantly reduced computation times compared to common protein inference algorithms.

</details>


### [204] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer模型在组合泛化任务中，其"记忆vs推理"的命运在训练过程中存在一个狭窄且可识别关键窗口期（critical window）被决定。仅在一个25%训练周期的窗口内应用权重衰减，就能达到与全训练周期使用权重衰减相当的OOD准确率（0.93 vs 0.91）。关键窗口的边界极其锐利：仅偏移100步就可能导致OOD从随机水平（0.15）跃升至推理水平（0.61）。该窗口位置随初始化规模系统性变化，但小初始化反而会收缩推理解的吸引域，与"小初始化更好"的普遍认知相悖。更重要的是，这种关键窗口现象具有任务特异性——在模算术grokking任务中未观察到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将复杂度控制视为单一的静态超参数选择，未能回答"在训练过程中的哪个阶段"复杂度控制实际上起着决定性作用。本文旨在揭示Transformer在组合泛化任务中，记忆与推理的命运是在何时（when）被确定的。

Method: 在受控的组合任务上设计系统化实验：(i) 全训练周期权重衰减对比单窗口（25%训练期）权重衰减；(ii) 固定总正则化预算，改变权重衰减的时间位置分布；(iii) 探索窗口边界的锐度（100步级别的偏移）；(iv) 研究初始化规模与关键窗口位置的交互关系；(v) 在模算术grokking任务上进行对比实验以验证任务特异性。

Result: 发现Transformer的"记忆-推理"命运在训练中存在一个尖锐的关键窗口期：(i) 单窗口权重衰减与全训练周期效果相当（0.93 vs 0.91 OOD准确率）；(ii) 总正则化预算固定时，中期放置比早期放置的OOD准确率高5-9倍；(iii) 窗口边界极其锐利：仅100步的偏移就导致OOD从0.15跃升至0.61；(iv) 关键窗口位置随初始化规模系统性变化，但小初始化反而缩小了推理解的吸引域，与"小初始化更好"的传统认知相悖；(v) 该现象具有任务特异性——模算术grokking任务中，调优得当的恒定权重衰减即可匹配调度权重衰减的效果。

Conclusion: Transformer的组合泛化能力由训练过程中的特定关键窗口决定，而非简单的静态超参数设置。该窗口的位置和宽度受初始化规模影响，且这种现象具有任务特异性。这些发现对理解深度学习中的泛化机制和设计更有效的训练策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Rigid Geometries: The Spline-Pullback Metric for Universal Diffeomorphic SPD Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04406)
*Tushar Das,Subrata Dutta,Sarmistha Neogy,Koushlendra Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM)的新方法，通过参数化全局微分同胚（使用秩不变、单调约束的B样条）从静态度量选择转向通用几何逼近。SPM作为严格递增C¹微分同胚的通用近似器，理论上映射现有回拉度量，同时实现局部非线性谱建模，提供全局双射回拉几何避免秩交换不连续性和梯度不稳定性。在3个数据集上使用Linear Probes、SPDNet和深度黎曼ResNet达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional SPD matrix integration in deep learning relies on fixed Riemannian metrics (analogous to hand-crafted features), imposing rigid geometries that limit network expressivity and adaptability. Existing parameterized approaches often violate axioms of matrix functions through unconstrained operations, causing spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse.

Method: Parameterizes the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, enabling SPM to act as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing C¹ diffeomorphisms. Two instantiations are proposed: Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM.

Result: Empirically validated with state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets using Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets architectures.

Conclusion: SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry with theoretical guarantees of bijectivity, avoiding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities inherent in prior approaches.

Abstract: The integration of Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) matrices into deep learning has historically relied on fixed algebraic Riemannian metrics. Analogous to hand-crafted features in classical machine learning, these static formulations impose rigid geometries limiting network expressivity and adaptability. Recent attempts to parameterize these geometries often violate the axioms of primary matrix functions through unconstrained powers or rank-dependent scaling, inviting spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse at spectral singularities. In this paper, we introduce the Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM), instantiated as Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM, marking a paradigm shift from static metric selection to universal geometric approximation. By parameterizing the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, SPM acts as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing $C^1$ diffeomorphisms and theoretically subsumes existing pullback metrics while enabling localized non-linear spectral modelling. Topologically, SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry precluding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities. Empirically, SPM achieves a state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets utilizing Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets.

</details>


### [206] [Stabilizing LLM Supervised Fine-Tuning via Explicit Distributional Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04468)
*Xinyu Wang,Changzhi Sun,Yuanbin Wu,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出「Anchored Learning（锚定学习）」——一种通过"动态移动锚点"显式控制分布式漂移的离线 SFT 框架。其核心是用当前模型与冻结的 SFT 参考模型插值构造中间目标分布 q(t)，再通过蒸馏把模型投影到该锚点上，从而把全局微调转化为分布空间中一系列局部信任域更新。论文给出概率空间与 logit 空间下的单步 KL 上界理论保证，并在 iGSM/MedCalc/IFEval 上证明其稳定位于"性能增益–通用能力保持"的 Pareto 前沿：标准 SFT 在 iGSM/MedCalc 上退化超 53%，本方法将其降至 5% 以内，同时保持接近最优的目标任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 后训练存在灾难性遗忘：在目标任务上提升的同时会破坏既有通用能力。近期证据（RL's Razor、约 on-policy 更新更鲁棒）指出该现象主要由优化过程中的"分布式漂移"（distributional drift）过度导致，且遗忘程度与微调策略到基座策略的 KL 散度强相关。现有方法的不足在于：on-policy RL 虽隐式约束漂移但依赖在线采样、优化管线复杂；标准 SFT 对漂移毫无显式控制；静态 KL 正则、信任域约束或固定教师蒸馏只提供间接或全局性的控制，与模型分布的局部几何不匹配，可能导致任务损失与保持损失梯度持续冲突、收敛缓慢或抑制有益的任务更新。作者由此提出核心问题：在纯离线、无通用域数据可回放的现实约束下，能否用一个简单且显式的机制，把 SFT 的效率与 on-policy RL 的稳定性结合起来？

Method: 1) 问题设定：仅使用目标任务标注数据集 D 做纯离线微调，排除回放/混训/参数隔离等方案；先由标准 SFT 得到并冻结参考模型 p_sft，基座分布为 p_base。
2) 双层结构——解耦"锚点选择"与"锚点拟合"：外层在 t=0..T-1 步用插值算子 I_α 在当前可训练模型 p_θ(t) 与冻结参考 p_sft 之间构造锚点 q(t)=I_α(p_θ(t), p_sft)；锚点从计算图分离，作为内层蒸馏的固定目标，内层在固定数据集 D 上以 off-policy 知识蒸馏最小化 KL(q(t)||p_θ)，跑 K 个 epoch（AdamW/SGD），得到 p_θ(t+1)，初始 θ(0) 为基座模型。
3) 两种插值实例化：logit 空间插值 z=(1-α)z_θ+αz_sft 后 softmax；概率空间插值 q=(1-α)p_θ+α p_sft（凸组合）。由于与"当前模型"插值，锚点随轨迹演化，不同于固定教师蒸馏。
4) 理论保证：引理5.1（概率空间）KL(q(t)||p_θ(t)) ≤ α·KL(p_sft||p_θ(t))，由 KL 凸性与 Jensen 不等式得出；引理5.2（logit 空间）锚点等价于反向 KL 重心 (1-α)KL(p||p_θ)+αKL(p||p_sft) 的唯一极小点，即重归一化几何均值，位于两分布间的指数测地线；引理5.3 KL(q(t)||p_θ(t)) ≤ α/(1-α)·KL(p_θ(t)||p_sft)；命题5.4 在内层精确投影假设下递推几何收敛到 p_sft，且 KL(p_θ(t)||p_sft) ≤ (1-α)^t·KL(p_base||p_sft)；并对比静态 KL 正则只会收敛到与当前状态无关的固定重心 p*，导致持续梯度冲突。
5) 实验设置：目标任务 iGSM/MedCalc/IFEval，通用能力用 MMLU-Pro、Countdown、MBPP(+)、HumanEval(+) 评估；模型覆盖 Qwen2.5-Instruct（1.5B/3B/7B/14B）与 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct；基线含 SFT、Low-SFT、KL-SFT、Self-SFT、Iter-SFT、STM、DFT；默认 logit 插值、α=0.5、K=5、T=5。

Result: 1) 权衡：六个模型-任务设置中 Anchored Learning 始终贴近 Pareto 前沿；标准 SFT 目标精度最高但通用能力崩溃（如 Qwen2.5-3B 上通用均分从基座 0.419 跌至 0.031–0.109，iGSM/MedCalc 退化超 53%），本方法将退化压到 5% 以内，同时目标精度接近最优（摘要报告 iGSM 达 75.2%，附表 Qwen2.5-3B 为 0.936、Llama-3.2-3B 为 0.994，数值口径存在差异）；在 MedCalc/IFEval 上通用均分约 0.356/0.409，几乎复原基座 0.419。
2) 缩放性：在 Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B/7B 上，目标精度与 Low-SFT 持平（如 3B 0.936 vs 0.930），而通用性能高出 +2.1/+3.7/+2.4 点，稳定性收益不依赖特定参数规模。
3) 增量学习（iGSM→MedCalc→IFEval）：本方法在 Domain Avg（0.577 vs 0.470；0.505 vs 0.331）与 Overall Avg（0.492 vs 0.459）上持续优于 Low-SFT，说明能抑制跨阶段的累积漂移。
4) 消融：目标精度随 α 增大迅速饱和，而通用性能随 α 增大缓慢且非单调退化，存在"退化盆地"；外层/内层迭代数同样呈路径依赖的非单调效应，说明仅靠固定调度或全局正则不可靠；插值空间上，概率空间为算术均值、偏 mode-covering，塑性更强但遗忘更多；logit 空间为几何均值、偏 mode-seeking，抑制低概率尾部、稳定性更佳。
5) 案例（Countdown "Make 78"）：Low-SFT 退化为照抄 iGSM 的变量定义式模板而答错，基座与本方法均正确给出 77+56−55=78。

Conclusion: 结论是：通过对目标分布的显式塑形，把全局微调改造为分布空间中一串局部保守更新，可获得隐式信任域行为与逐迭代 KL 稳定性界，从而在纯离线 SFT 中以很小额外开销显著抑制灾难性遗忘，位置于"增益–稳定性"Pareto 前沿，是 on-policy RL 隐式稳定机制的可行离线替代方案，并凸显"显式轨迹控制"对后训练稳定性的核心价值。主要局限：相比标准 SFT 需额外构造插值锚点与一次前向蒸馏，训练延迟与显存约增至 1.5×–2×，在资源极度受限时限制可扩展性；此外当前实现为 off-policy 蒸馏、锚点仅依赖当前模型与单一冻结 SFT 参考，on-policy/混合蒸馏与多参考扩展留待未来工作。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) often suffers from catastrophic forgetting, where improvements on a target objective degrade previously acquired capabilities. Recent evidence suggests that this phenomenon is primarily driven by excessive distributional drift during optimization. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Anchored Learning, a simple framework that explicitly controls distributional updates during offline fine-tuning via a dynamically evolving moving anchor. Instead of matching a fixed reference distribution, the anchor interpolates between the current model and a frozen reference to construct an intermediate target that the model distills toward, transforming global fine-tuning into a sequence of local trust-region updates in distribution space. Theoretically, we prove this anchor-based update admits a linear KL-divergence upper bound per iteration, ensuring a stable transition between model distributions. Extensive experiments on iGSM, MedCalc, and IFEval show that Anchored Learning consistently lies on the Pareto frontier of gain-stability trade-offs, achieving near-optimal performance improvements while substantially reducing degradation compared to strong baselines. For example, while standard SFT suffers from over 53% performance degradation on iGSM and MedCalc, Anchored Learning slashes this drop to under 5% while maintaining near-optimal gains (e.g., 75.2% on iGSM).

</details>


### [207] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在放松全局单调性假设后，结构因果模型仍能实现反事实识别的条件。提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），通过机制级可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性，证明这与外生同构等价并蕴含完全反事实可识别性。实验验证表明该框架在非单调机制下具有系统性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型的可识别性结果大多依赖全局单调性假设，但在具身交互中，相同的外生扰动在不同接触情境下可能引发相反响应，导致全局单调性假设失效。需要探索更宽松的识别条件。

Method: 提出NM-TM-SCM框架，保留三角递归结构，用机制级可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替换全局单调性假设。通过CausalInverter实现，包含三角可逆层、定向门和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 理论证明机制级可逆性与上下文无关逆传输等价于外生同构，并蕴含完全反事实可识别性；局部可逆性单独不足。实验表明：合成非单调机制上非单调性增强时反事实收益系统增加；MuJoCo Door任务达到完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线；MuJoCo Push任务上低数据预测器仍具竞争力，符合偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 识别出介于全局单调三角模型与无约束黑盒世界模型之间的更广泛可识别机制，为具身交互中的反事实推理提供了理论基础和有效方法。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [208] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAAST是一种前向-only的联想适应方法，通过单次解析编译将标注样本转化为快速权重，在保持性能的同时将适应时间减少90%以上，内存占用降低95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型适应面临两难权衡：反向传播训练成本高，记忆/上下文学习推理开销大。需要一种高效且资源友好的适应方法。

Method: FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation）采用解析方法将标注样本在单次前向传播中编译为快速权重，无需记忆或上下文依赖，实现恒定时间推理。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准测试中，FAAST匹配或超越反向传播适应方法，适应性时间减少超过90%；相比记忆/上下文学习方法，内存节省达95%。

Conclusion: FAAST为监督任务适应提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，尤其适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [209] [Demystifying Manifold Constraints in LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04418)
*Kang An,Jiaxiang Li,Donald Goldfarb,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training by introducing MACRO, demonstrating that constraints on weights can replace heuristic stabilization techniques (normalization, weight decay) by independently controlling activation scales and rotational equilibrium, with provable convergence guarantees.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM pre-training relies on heuristic stabilization techniques (explicit normalization layers, weight decay) whose underlying mechanisms are not well understood, despite the potential of constrained optimization approaches.

Method: Introduces MACRO (Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer), a single-loop optimization framework that applies manifold constraints directly to weights, separating their effect from RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay mechanisms.

Result: Manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, effectively subsuming the roles of heuristic stabilization mechanisms while achieving competitive performance on large-scale LLM architectures.

Conclusion: MACRO provides a theoretically grounded alternative to heuristic stabilization techniques in LLM pre-training, with manifold constraints serving as a unified mechanism that subsumes normalization layers and weight decay, while maintaining convergence guarantees.

Abstract: The empirical success of large language model (LLM) pre-training relies heavily on heuristic stabilization techniques, such as explicit normalization layers and weight decay. While recent constrained optimization approaches that explicitly restrict weights may improve numerical stability and performance, the mechanism and motivation for adding constraints still remain elusive. This paper systematically demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training. By introducing the Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)-a provably convergent, single-loop optimization framework-our study disentangles weight regularization heuristics from interacting mechanisms like RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay. Theoretical analyses and comprehensive empirical evaluations reveal that manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, thereby subsuming the roles of these heuristic mechanisms. Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [210] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UE-DPO通过引入token级认知不确定性来解决多模态大语言模型中的幻觉问题，克服了现有DPO方法中自评敏感度信号带来的自我参照偏差，实现了更精细的视觉保真度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评的视觉敏感度信号来分配训练权重，但这种方法存在自我参照偏差：强化已学好的视觉线索，忽视难以感知但关键细节，限制了更深入的校准效果。

Method: 1) 从模型未能将token预测扎根于给定图像的失败中量化不确定性；2) 基于不确定性感知探索强度，对首选样本中视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力；3) 减轻对非首选样本中有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多个基准上相比现有方法有显著提升，具有良好的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提供了方法的理论论证，证明通过不确定性引导的探索性学习可以有效识别认知缺陷并进行自我纠正，为多模态对齐提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [211] [FLUID: Continuous-Time Hyperconnected Sparse Transformer for Sink-Free Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04421)
*Waleed Razzaq,Yun-Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUID（Flexible Unified Information Dynamics）是一种新型连续时间（CT）Transformer架构，其核心创新是把注意力logits本身建模为由输入依赖非线性门控调制的线性ODE（Liquid Attention Network, LAN），从而在注意力计算内部引入连续时间动力学，而非仅在输入/输出嵌入层做连续化。LAN在理论上被证明是SDPA与CT-RNN之间的"动力学桥梁"（互为特例），并配合显式的attention-sink门 $G_{sink}$ 与输入依赖的Liquid Hyper-Connections（$\mathcal{HC}_{liquid}$）替代标准残差连接以缓解seesaw效应。在不规则时间序列、长程预测、自动驾驶车道保持控制、稀缺数据下物理退化建模四类任务上全面匹配或超越CT基线，最高提升约47%，且在分布偏移、噪声鲁棒性、低数据泛化上表现突出；运行时间与显存开销处于CT-RNN与Transformer之间的中间位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是：现有连续时间（CT）模型虽在处理不规则采样与长时程依赖上优于离散模型，但存在若干结构性缺陷。
1. 连续动力学错位：已有CT-Transformer（mTAN、CTA、ODEFormer、ContiFormer、PDE-Attention、OT-Transformer）仅在输入或输出嵌入上施加连续动力学，核心的scaled dot-product attention（SDPA）仍在离散索引位置上计算，缺乏对平滑演化轨迹的原生归纳偏置。
2. 注意力汇（attention sink）：softmax归一化会把不成比例的注意力质量分配给初始或无信息token；在不规则采样下这一效应被非均匀采样与时距变化进一步放大，形成系统性偏置。
3. 残差连接的seesaw效应（跷跷板效应）：PreNorm式强残差虽利于梯度传播却导致相邻层表征高度同质、深度贡献退化；PostNorm式弱残差增加多样性却带来梯度消失风险，二者存在根本张力。
4. 数据需求与鲁棒性：对精炼数据量需求大，在分布偏移（尤其未见采样频率）下泛化能力有限。
作者希望把CT动力学直接嵌入注意力计算本身，使logits成为连续动力系统演化的解，同时保留SDPA的长程依赖建模与并行优势。

Method: **1. Liquid Attention Network (LAN)**
把注意力logit $a_t$ 视为连续动力系统状态：
$\dot a_t = -f_\tau(u_t,t;\theta_\tau)\,a_t + f_\phi(u_t,t;\theta_\phi)$，其中 $u_t=[q;k]$，$f_\tau$ 为"时间常数门"（严格正，控制收敛速率与稳态幅度），$f_\phi$ 为"内容目标门"（可正可负，决定强调何种内容）。两个门共享同一递归基座 $\mathcal{R}_g$（LSTM或GRU），分出两个独立投影头：$f_\phi=\tanh(\mathcal{R}_g(\cdot))$，$f_\tau=\mathrm{softplus}(\mathcal{R}_g(\cdot))+\varepsilon$。
前向传播采用显式Euler数值积分（因高阶Runge–Kutta开销随序列长度恶性增长）：$a_{n+1}=a_n+\Delta t(-f_{\tau,n}a_n+f_{\phi,n})$。为避免刚性导致的数值发散，按每次前向预测的最大 $f_\tau$ 自适应钳制 $\Delta t$。

**2. 理论分析**
- 定理1（前向不变性与有界性）：在 $0<\tau_{\min}\le f_\tau$、$f_\phi\in[\phi_{\min},\phi_{\max}]$ 条件下，区间 $\mathcal I=[A_{\min},A_{\max}]$（$A=f_\phi/f_\tau$ 的上下确界）是前向不变集，系统一致有界。
- 引理1（Euler离散数值稳定性）：要求 $\Delta t\le 1/\sup_t f_\tau$，此时更新式 $a_{n+1}=(1-\alpha_n)a_n+\alpha_n A_n$ 成为凸组合，保持无振荡。
- 定理2（动力学桥梁）：当 $\tau_{sys}=1/f_\tau\to0$（$f_\tau\to\infty$）且 $f_\phi/f_\tau\approx q^Tk/\sqrt d$ 时，LAN退化为SDPA（无时间记忆）；当门为前馈且 $f_\tau=1/\tau$、$f_\phi=(1/\tau)\sigma(W_\phi u_t+b_\phi)$ 时，精确还原CT-RNN的漏积分方程；一般情况下 $f_\tau$ 为逐token-pair学习的输入依赖函数，实现两者的连续插值。
- 推论1：$\mathscr F_{LAN}\supsetneq\{\mathscr F_{CT\text{-}RNN},\mathscr F_{SDPA}\}$（两函数类互不可比）。

**3. 稀疏化与多头扩展**
全量query-key拼接（$B\times H\times T_q\times T_k\times 2D$）内存开销过大，改为Top-K稀疏选择：计算 $S=QK^\top$，取 $K_{eff}=\min(K,T_k)$ 个top key，与tiled query拼接形成 $U_{topk}$，在保留最相关交互的同时大幅降低内存与后续计算量。多头扩展时每个头独立参数化 $f_\phi,f_\tau$，从而学到不同的动态兼容性。

**4. Attention-sink门 $G_{sink}$**
在多头输出后加query依赖的sigmoid门，逐元素相乘：$O_{LAN}=\sigma(x_t)\odot(\mathrm{Concat}(h_1..h_H)W_g+b_g)$，选择性抑制无信息节点贡献。

**5. Liquid Hyper-Connections（$\mathcal{HC}_{liquid}$）**
将残差表示扩展为 $n$ 路并行超隐藏流 $\mathbf H\in\mathbb R^{n\times d}$，用结构矩阵 $\mathcal{HC}=\begin{pmatrix}0&\mathbf B\\\mathbf A_m&\mathbf A_r\end{pmatrix}$ 表达宽度连接（WC）、深度连接（DC）与当前层注入。液体版本令 $\mathbf B,\mathbf A_m,\mathbf A_r$ 成为输入的函数：先对 $\mathbf X$ 做Norm，再以小初值缩放因子 $s_b,s_a$ 的 $\tanh(\widetilde{\mathbf X}\mathbf W)$ 偏移静态参数，训练初期保持近似恒等映射以稳定优化。

**6. 架构与训练**
编码器：$N$层，每层 self-LAN + FFN，各自后接 $\mathcal{HC}_{liquid}$ 与LayerNorm；解码器：masked self-LAN、cross-LAN、FFN。编码器与解码器共享输入嵌入参数（既统一历史/预测时段的时序表征，又起正则化作用以抗噪与抗分布偏移）。5折交叉验证，BPTT训练，150次Bayesian优化调参。

Result: **不规则时间序列**：Irregular Spiral重建MAE 0.0046±0.0002，优于最佳CT-Transformer基线ContiFormer（0.0053）13.2%，远优于SDPA-Transformer（0.3194）；重建轨迹在插值与外推下均保持全局连贯平滑。Event-based MNIST准确率97.37±1.03，为全场最高，超越mTAN（96.30）、OT-Transformer（96.23）、Performer（96.22）约1.1%。

**长程建模**：ETTm1 MSE 0.0022±0.0004，较ContiFormer（0.0026）降15.4%；Jena-Climate MSE 0.0021±0.0001，较ContiFormer（0.0040）提升47.5%。对照中ODEFormer（0.1211）、CTA（0.1051）、PDE-Attention（0.1854）在高维快速变化气象动态上严重失效，FLUID是唯一在预测窗口内轨迹始终贴合真值的模型。

**自动驾驶车道保持**：Udacity转向回归MSE 0.0174±0.0018，领先最佳CT基线DeepState（0.0181）约3.9%；OpenAI CarRacing分类准确率80.91±0.23为最高（CT-RNN与SDPA均为80.80）。闭环分析中，SDPA与ODEFormer未能完成赛道（注意力汇导致关键弯道丢失时序上下文），OT-Transformer在急转弯处不稳定，ContiFormer轻微偏出路面，FLUID累积位置误差最低且始终贴合真值；GradCam显示FLUID显著性集中于路面地平线，呈现"自纠正"归纳偏置。调色板随机化噪声测试中FLUID成功率最高，SDPA与ODEFormer崩塌至40%以下。

**稀缺数据物理动力学**：仅用XJTU-SY第一工况1–3号轴承（约占总样本3.81%）训练并零样本迁移至HUST与PRONOSTIA。XJTU-SY Score 21.29±8.94，较最优基线PhasedLSTM（23.19）提升8.2%（SDPA为121.32，ContiFormer为58.24）；HUST上FLUID最佳变体34.71，优于OT-Transformer（35.81）；PRONOSTIA上Top-K×4达109.39±7.18，较CTA（118.61±22.58）提升6.1%且方差降低约三倍。

**消融与敏感性**：LAN是性能主要来源（各变体Spiral MAE稳定在0.0046–0.0048）；去掉 $G_{sink}$ 使初始节点注意力质量下降2.43%（0.255→0.249）、后期节点上升13.5%（0.155→0.176），并减少早期梯度尖峰；消融 $G_{sink}$ 使XJTU-SY Score从21.29升至24.19（退化13.6%）。注意力头数与扩展率均呈非单调效应，8头最优，扩展率取2最优；Top-K在8–16区间取得精度/效率平衡，长序列（≥20,000）时K=32更优。效率方面：推理0.2086±0.1030 s、吞吐4.79 seq/s、峰值内存124.95 MB，明显优于CT-RNN（GRU-ODE 12.57 s、LTC-FC 14.97 s）与CTA（8.89 s），显存远低于SDPA-Transformer（321 MB）、mTAN（790 MB）、PDE-Attention（658 MB），优于OT-Transformer与PDE-Attention的低延迟高显存方案。

Conclusion: FLUID的贡献在于把"连续时间"从CT-Transformer的外围（输入/输出嵌入）推进到核心注意力计算内部：LAN将注意力logit重新表述为一阶线性ODE的解，其动力学由输入依赖的非线性互联门调制，从而在统一框架内同时容纳SDPA与CT-RNN（定理2与推论1给出严格的功能类包含关系），并由定理1与引理1提供前向不变性与Euler数值稳定性的理论保证。架构层面，$G_{sink}$以显式门控消除无信息节点上的过量注意力质量，$\mathcal{HC}_{liquid}$以输入依赖的连通矩阵取代固定强度残差以缓解seesaw效应，共享输入嵌入则同时改善跨时段信息传递与低数据/分布偏移下的稳健性。实验证据表明该设计在四类异构任务上一致匹配或超越CT基线（最高约47%），并展现出对采样频率偏移、视觉扰动与数据稀缺更强的泛化能力，同时在运行时与显存上提供了CT-RNN与Transformer之间的实用折中。局限性方面，模型速度显著慢于纯离散Transformer与部分线性注意力变体，且整体推理仍受Euler积分步数与稀疏Top-K超参影响；作者提出三个后续方向：以随机微分方程替换ODE以引入不确定性量化、引入流形约束超连接（mHC）以稳定大规模训练、发展可学习/硬件感知的Top-K选择策略。

Abstract: Continuous-time (CT) Transformers improve irregular and long-range modeling over CT-RNNs by exploiting inputs or outputs embeddings with continuous dynamics. However, the core scaled-dot-product-attention (SDPA) mechanism remains inherently discrete. We propose FLUID (Flexible Unified Information Dynamics), a CT Transformer that incorporates continuous dynamics directly into the attention computation by replacing it with Liquid Attention Network (LAN). LAN reinterprets attention logits as continuous dynamical system and reformulates them as the solution to a linear ODE modulated by input-dependent nonlinear recurrent gates. Theoretically, we establish stability guarantees for LAN dynamics and show that it serves as an interpolating middle ground between SDPA and CT-RNNs, recovering each as special case under well-defined parameterization of its gating functions. LAN also introduces an explicit attention-sink gate to eliminate disproportionate attention mass on uninformative nodes. FLUID replaces standard residual connections with input-dependent Liquid Hyper-Connections to adaptively regulate interlayer information flow. Empirically, we evaluate FLUID on a broad set of learning tasks, including (i) irregular time-series, (ii) long-range modeling, (iii) lane-keeping control of autonomous vehicles, and (iv) learning physical dynamics under a scarce data regime. Across all the tasks, FLUID consistently matches or outperforms CT baselines, achieving improvements of up to 47% in certain scenarios and enhancing generalization under distributional shifts. Additionally, FLUID demonstrates superior noise robustness and a self-correcting inductive bias in autonomous vehicle control. We also provide a detailed analysis of key hyperparameters to guide tuning and show that FLUID occupies an intermediate position among competing approaches in terms of runtime and memory efficiency.

</details>


### [212] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明现有谱方法在分析注意力操作符时存在结构性方向盲点，并通过引入非对称系数G作为方向控制参数和容量参数φ，构建了可证伪的双轴诊断框架，为大语言模型幻觉的可预测失败模式提供了理论解释和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways through attention routing failures (over-concentration or excessive diffusion). Understanding whether spectral methods can diagnose such failures requires clarifying whether these methods can detect information-flow direction. Current spectral analysis of the symmetric component of attention operators may be fundamentally limited in this regard.

Method: Theoretical analysis: (1) Proved that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of the symmetric component of degree-normalized attention operator is orientation-blind; (2) Established asymmetry coefficient G as the unique directional control parameter via quantitative converse; (3) Derived closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures; (4) Analyzed uniform causal attention (floor φ ≥ 1/5, worst cut at t*/n ≈ 0.32) and window attention (pierces floor as O(w/n)). Experimental validation: length-controlled evaluation on models up to 8B parameters.

Result: 证明转置不变的光谱诊断方法在本质上无法区分操作符与其转置，因此无法检测信息流方向；不对称系数G是方向的唯一控制参数。统一因果注意力的传输容量存在与n无关的下界φ≥1/5，最差切割点在t*/n≈0.32；窗口注意力作为O(w/n)突破该下界。不同失败模式在形状上有本质区别，而非仅是数值差异。两轴诊断（φ表征容量，G表征方向）能够预测瓶颈主导和弥散主导基准测试呈现相反极性。长度控制评估下，传输特征在8B参数模型上保持0.62-0.84的LC-AUROC可解释信号。

Conclusion: transpose-invariant spectral diagnostics are fundamentally limited for detecting information-flow direction; the asymmetry coefficient G is the unique control parameter. A two-axis diagnostic (φ for capacity, G for direction) enables falsifiable polarity prediction. Experimental validation shows transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC 0.62-0.84) on models up to 8B parameters, with polarity correctly reversing between HaluEval and MedHallu as predicted.

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways: attention routing fails by over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or by spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport capacity; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally orientation-blind (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a quantitative converse establishing the asymmetry coefficient $G$ as the unique control parameter for direction.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$ with worst cut at $t^\ast/n \approx 0.32$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (LC-AUROC from 0.62 to 0.84) on tested models up to 8B parameters, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [213] [Discovering Sparse Counterfactual Factors via Latent Adjustment for Survey-based Community Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04460)
*Fatima Ashraf,Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Yufang Zhou,Yan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从交通调查数据中生成稀疏、可执行的干预策略，使目标群体向参考群体迁移。通过固定基非负潜在表示、可迁移 Shapley 归因和熵正则化最优传输相结合，在保持干预稀疏性的同时实现可解释的群体层面调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通调查分析多停留在描述性或预测性层面，难以提供稀疏且政策可执行的干预策略。现有方法缺乏将潜在因子映射回可控变量、保证群体间可比性、并显式控制干预稀疏性的统一框架。

Method: 提出一种基于固定基非负潜在表示的策略可行分布对齐框架：(1) 使用固定基非负矩阵分解保留干预前后可比性；(2) 通过 Shapley 引导归因识别目标相关的潜在因子并映射为干预优先级；(3) 采用熵正则化最优传输最小化分布差异，结合加权 ℓ_{2,1} 惩罚项促进政策杠杆稀疏性。

Result: 在真实交通调查数据集上，该方法生成紧凑、可解释的稀疏干预策略，具有明确调整幅度，显著提升群体转化率。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效融合了因果归因、可解释表示与最优传输，为交通调查数据分析提供了一种从观察到干预的端到端政策支持工具。

Abstract: Transportation surveys are widely used to understand travel preferences and adoption barriers, yet most survey-based analyses remain descriptive or predictive and rarely provide sparse, policy-feasible intervention strategies. We study sparse counterfactual community intervention from survey responses, where the goal is to shift a target respondent group toward a desired reference group through controllable survey-variable adjustments. We formulate this task as a policy-feasible distributional alignment problem using a fixed-basis nonnegative latent representation that preserves pre/post comparability and provides a stable map from latent factors to original variables. To make latent movement actionable, target-relevant latent factors are identified through Shapley-guided attribution and transferred to controllable variables as intervention priorities. Feasible group-level adjustments are then learned by minimizing an entropy-regularized optimal-transport discrepancy between the post-intervention target distribution and the reference distribution, together with a weighted $\ell_{2,1}$ penalty that promotes shared policy-lever sparsity. Experiments on real-world transportation survey datasets show that the proposed framework produces compact and interpretable policy-feasible interventions with explicit adjustment magnitudes, improves population-level conversion, and preserves intervention sparsity. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/latent-group-alignment.git

</details>


### [214] [Reinforcement Learning for Compositional Generalization with Outcome-Level Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04920)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过结果级强化学习（而非传统的词元级监督微调）提升语言模型的组合泛化能力，采用GRPO算法在最终输出层面优化模型，并在多个组合泛化基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合泛化要求模型能够正确解释已知基元的全新组合，但现有监督微调方法在词元级别模仿目标输出，无法捕捉全局组合结构，导致模型在未见过的组合上泛化能力不足。

Method: 采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)进行结果级强化学习，在最终输出层面基于反馈优化模型。实验设计了二元结果奖励和包含额外组合反馈的复合奖励两种变体进行比较。

Result: 强化学习方法在多个组合泛化基准上均优于监督微调。进一步分析表明，监督模型倾向于过拟合训练集中频繁出现的组合类型，而强化学习通过重塑输出分布来改善组合泛化，尤其对复杂组合类型效果显著。

Conclusion: 结果级强化学习是提升模型组合泛化的有效范式，能够克服传统词元级训练的局限性，为构建更具组合能力的语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Compositional generalization refers to correctly interpret novel combinations of known primitives, which remains a major challenge. Existing approaches often rely on supervised fine-tuning, which encourages models to imitate target outputs. This token-level training paradigm fails to capture the global compositional structure required for generalizing to unseen combinations. In this work, we investigate whether compositional generalization can instead be improved through outcome-level reinforcement learning. We adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization to optimize models based on feedback on their final outputs. Within this framework, we explore both a simple binary outcome reward and a composite reward that provides additional composition feedback. Experiments on multiple compositional benchmarks show that reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization compared to supervised fine-tuning. Further analysis reveals that supervised models tend to overfit frequent training compositions, whereas reinforcement learning improves compositional generalization by reshaping the output distribution, particularly for more complex composition types.

</details>


### [215] [Why Geometric Continuity Emerges in Deep Neural Networks: Residual Connections and Rotational Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04971)
*Kyungwon Jeong,Won-Gi Paeng,Honggyo Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper identifies the mechanisms behind geometric continuity in deep network weight matrices: residual connections align gradients across layers, and symmetry-breaking activations (not just any nonlinearity) constrain layers to a shared coordinate frame. The effect is projection-specific in transformers, with components reading from the residual stream showing input-space continuity and those writing to it showing output-space continuity.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Principal singular vectors of adjacent layers in deep networks show geometric continuity (aligned directions), but the origin of this phenomenon is unexplained despite being widely observed.

Method: Controlled experiments on toy MLPs and small transformers comparing: (1) networks with vs without residual connections, (2) symmetry-breaking activations vs rotation-preserving activations (e.g., GELU vs orthogonal weight multiplication), (3) ablation of normalization layers, and (4) projection-specific analysis of attention components.

Result: Two mechanisms produce weight continuity: (1) residual connections create cross-layer gradient coherence that aligns weight updates, and (2) symmetry-breaking nonlinearities constrain layers to a shared coordinate frame. Rotation-preserving nonlinearities fail to maintain continuity, confirming symmetry-breaking as the active ingredient. Activation concentrates continuity in leading singular direction while normalization distributes it across multiple directions. In transformers, continuity is projection-specific and correlates with reading from vs writing to the residual stream.

Conclusion: The active ingredient for weight continuity is symmetry-breaking (not nonlinearity), achieved through specific mechanisms: residual connections align gradients across layers, while activations like GELU constrain layers to shared coordinate frames. Q/K/Gate/Up develop input-space continuity (v₁), while O/Down develop output-space continuity (u₁), with V showing only low continuity due to lack of adjacent nonlinearity.

Abstract: Weight matrices in deep networks exhibit geometric continuity -- principal singular vectors of adjacent layers point in similar directions. While this property has been widely observed, its origin remains unexplained. Through experiments on toy MLPs and small transformers, we identify two mechanisms: residual connections create cross-layer gradient coherence that aligns weight updates across layers, and symmetry-breaking nonlinearities constrain all layers to a shared coordinate frame, preventing the rotation drift that would otherwise destabilize weight structure. Crucially, a nonlinear but rotation-preserving activation fails to retain continuity, isolating symmetry breaking -- not nonlinearity itself -- as the active ingredient. Activation and normalization play distinct roles: activation concentrates continuity in the leading singular direction, while normalization distributes it across multiple directions. In transformers, continuity is projection-specific: Q, K, Gate, and Up (which read from the residual stream) develop input-space ($\mathbf{v}_1$) continuity; O and Down (which write to it) develop output-space ($\mathbf{u}_1$) continuity; V alone, lacking an adjacent nonlinearity, develops only low continuity.

</details>


### [216] [CRAFT: Counterfactual-to-Interactive Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04470)
*Keyu Chen,Nanfei Ye,Yida Wang,Wenchao Sun,Danqi Zhao,Hao Cheng,Sifa Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT（清华大学 + 理想汽车）提出一种闭环后训练框架：把"密集但有偏的反事实监督"作为真实闭环优势的稠密代理（proxy），再用"稀疏但真实的交互事件"作为残差修正（residual），并以 EMA 教师自蒸馏稳定策略。该框架统一了闭环 RL 与反事实微调两种范式，在 Bench2Drive 上于分层规划（HiP-AD）、视觉-语言-动作（MindDrive）、轨迹词表打分（SparseDriveV2）三类策略上均取得最优闭环增益（如 SparseDriveV2 的 DS 66.43→71.10，SR 45.45→50.45），并给出精确的 proxy-residual 梯度分解、条件方差缩减与残差偏差界的理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端/VLA/世界动作模型等驾驶策略主流仍依赖开环模仿学习，开环训练与闭环部署之间的分布偏移（compounding error）是部署瓶颈。现有两类闭环后训练范式各有结构性缺陷：(1) 闭环 RL（PPO、REINFORCE++ 等）在真实模拟器中基于执行动作给出"有据（grounded）但稀疏"的反馈，关键交互事件罕见、样本效率低，价值估计脆弱；(2) 反事实微调（如 GRPO + PDM Score）在同一状态下评估多个候选未来，得到"密集但有偏"的监督，因为反事实世界（离线生成或非反应式仿真）无法忠实还原真实闭环的交互动力学。核心问题即：能否让反事实监督充当稠密代理，而用闭环交互去纠正其与真实世界的残差失配。

Method: 形式化：将真实闭环 MDP 记为 M_real=(S,A,P_real,r_real,γ)，策略 π_θ 对候选轨迹 τ 打分，控制器 κ 跟踪选中轨迹产生实际动作 a_t=κ(s_t,τ_t)，目标 J_real(π_θ) 的真实策略梯度在同分布 s~d_real^{π_θ} 上可精确分解为 proxy 项与 residual 项（Prop 3.1）：
∇J_real = E[∇logπ·Φ_π(s,τ)] + E[∇logπ·Δ_π(s,τ)]，其中 Δ=A^real−Φ 为代理与真实优势的失配（无状态分布错配问题）。
(1) 稠密反事实代理：闭环 rollout 访问到的每个状态 s_i 上，候选组 {τ_g}_{g=1..G} 在反事实世界中以有限虚拟时域 H 仿真，用覆盖效率/安全/规则的标量回报 R̃_{i,g} 做组内均值-方差归一化得到 Ã_{i,g}，离散动作空间下直接取精确期望 L_cp = −(1/B)Σ_i Σ_g π_θ(τ_g|s_i)Ã_{i,g}，即 Φ_π(s_i,τ_g)=Ã_{i,g}。
(2) 有据残差修正：对实际执行动作产生的事件驱动稀疏纠正奖励 r^gr（碰撞、闯红灯、停车标志、越界、偏离路线等），折扣（γ_c=0.8）后缩放裁剪成有界优势 Â^gr_t∈[−1,1]，采用 value-free 的 PPO 裁剪 + dual clipping（c=2）的更新 L_gr，以在稀有灾难事件上保留信息梯度又不让其主导更新（Prop A.2 给出有界性）。
(3) On-policy 自蒸馏：EMA 教师（m=0.99，初始化为预训练权重）提供非对称 KL 约束——反向 KL D_KL(π_θ‖π_T) 为主（β_r=0.5），较弱的正向 KL D_KL(π_T‖π_θ)（β_f=0.1）保留教师支持的备选轨迹、防止坍缩到代理偏好的窄子集；Prop A.1 表明 KL 近端策略改进等价于按代理-残差优势对教师分布做指数重加权。
总目标 L_all = λ_cp L_cp + λ_gr L_gr + β_r L_dist + β_f L_KL（λ_cp=1.0，λ_gr=0.5）。
理论：Prop 3.2 给出条件方差缩减（Var[Y−αC] 的最优 α*=Cov/Var[C]，当 Cov>½Var[C] 时残差严格降方差，说明代理无需无偏、只需足够对齐）；Thm 3.3 用 Cauchy–Schwarz 给出残差近似导致的梯度偏差界 ‖ĝ−g‖₂ ≤ √E‖∇logπ‖²·√E|Δ̂−Δ|²。
实现：仅微调候选打分模块（HiP-AD 的 48 模式分类分支、MindDrive 的 LoRA 决策专家、SparseDriveV2 的 512 词表分类分支），冻结感知/地图/解码；反事实世界用批量 PID 轨迹跟踪器 + 运动学自行车模型、他人 agent 采用 decay rollout、矩形包围盒 SAT 碰撞检测；基础设施基于 RIFT 的异步 collect-and-train（8×RTX 4090D，单次微调 6–10 小时）；另提出打补丁的 Bench2Drive* 协议（actor 清理、超时收紧、碰撞即终止、忽略 Town07 隐形停止牌等 6 项），训练吞吐 >2×、评测吞吐 >3×，并降低评测方差。

Result: 主结果（Bench2Drive 闭环）：CRAFT 在三种架构上均最优。HiP-AD：DS 73.46→75.79，SR 53.64→56.36，Multi-Ability 均值 51.80→55.20（GRPO 75.27/52.20，PPO 73.58/47.70）；MindDrive：DS 62.60→67.45，SR 40.45→46.36，能力均值 40.00→46.60（次优 GRPO 65.12/44.70）；SparseDriveV2†：DS 66.43→71.10，RC 77.53→80.01，IS 0.80→0.84，SR 45.45→50.45（次优 GRPO 68.40/48.18、PPO 67.44/49.09）。细粒度雷达图显示 CRAFT 在 VRU 交互、避障、路口转弯等安全敏感能力上增益最大，说明稠密代理难以覆盖强交互依赖场景。消融（SparseDriveV2）：仅自蒸馏 DS 66.49；+自蒸馏+反事实代理 68.40（+1.91）；三者齐全 71.10（+2.70），残差修正在 IS/SR 上贡献最显著。泛化：Bench2Drive→Longest6 v2 DS +2.18、SR +0.00；LEAD(Town12)→LEAD DS +2.14、SR +1.89；LEAD→Bench2Drive DS +0.58 但 RC −0.34，提示跨场景下策略偏保守。规模化：CRAFT 是唯一随数据量增加持续提升的方法；PPO/REINFORCE++ 增益饱和且不稳，GRPO 在大规模下反而退化（代理偏差成为瓶颈）。稳定性：CRAFT 的 KL 系数变异在动作空间更宽的 HiP-AD/SparseDriveV2 上优势更明显，期望优势的跨架构变异低于 GRPO。定性（行人过街场景）：预训练模型把概率质量集中在"左转加速"模态，PPO/REINFORCE++ 几乎不改变该主峰，GRPO 仅部分移向制动模态（仍为次峰），CRAFT 使制动兼容模态成为主导峰同时保留原峰低概率。

Conclusion: CRAFT 把闭环后训练统一为"代理-残差"优化问题：稠密反事实代理承担主要的广域优化信号，有据的闭环事件残差修正补偿代理偏差，EMA 教师自蒸馏保留预训练可靠行为并稳定适应。理论上给出精确梯度分解、条件方差缩减与残差偏差界，实验上在三类驾驶策略架构上取得最强闭环增益，且具备随数据扩展的持续收益与更好的训练稳定性，验证了"密集代理 + 有据残差"互补设计的有效性。局限包括：跨场景迁移时 Route Completion 提升有限（策略偏保守）、反事实世界仍为运动学近似与 decay 他人模型（存在代理偏差上限）、主实验采用单种子评测（依据 Bench2Drive* 更低方差），以及需要额外构建候选仿真与事件奖励工程成本。

Abstract: Open-loop imitation learning has advanced modern autonomous driving policy architectures, but closed-loop deployment remains vulnerable to policy-induced distribution shift. Existing post-training paradigms exhibit fundamental trade-offs: closed-loop RL fine-tuning provides grounded feedback from executed actions but is constrained by the sparsity of informative events, whereas counterfactual fine-tuning provides dense supervision over candidate futures but inherits bias from imperfect future estimates. We introduce Counterfactual-to-Interactive Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (CRAFT), an on-policy framework that formulates closed-loop post-training as proxy-residual optimization. CRAFT uses group-normalized counterfactual advantages as a dense proxy for real closed-loop advantages and aligns this proxy with the closed-loop world through grounded residual correction from interaction-critical events. To stabilize adaptation, CRAFT regularizes the online policy toward an EMA teacher via asymmetric KL self-distillation. Theoretically, CRAFT decomposes the real closed-loop policy gradient into proxy and residual terms under the same visited-state distribution, reducing residual variance with an aligned proxy while mitigating proxy bias through grounded residual approximation. Empirically, CRAFT achieves the strongest closed-loop gains on Bench2Drive across hierarchical planning, vision-language-action, and vocabulary-scoring architectures. Ablations, scaling behavior, stability analyses, and transfer results further validate the complementary roles of dense counterfactual proxy and grounded residual correction. Project page: https://currychen77.github.io/CRAFT.

</details>


### [217] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用"conceptor"（概念矩阵）替代传统的单向量方向来控制大语言模型行为。Conceptor是从双极概念两极的激活池中估计的软投影矩阵，能够保留概念的完整多维子空间，而非简化为单一方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的基于激活的 steering 方法将每个概念简化为单一方向，但其几何性质缺乏充分检验。这种简化可能丢失概念的多维信息，且仅从有限对比样本对中提取方向存在安全隐患。
