Method: 1) 使用概念矩阵（软投影矩阵）而非单向量，从双极概念两极的激活中估计得到；2) 几何分析证明双极子空间严格包含单向量基线；3) 概念矩阵配额提供无参数的层选择诊断；4) 概念矩阵支持闭式布尔代数（AND、OR、NOT）实现组合操作。

Result: 1) 双极子空间严格包含单向量基线；2) 概念矩阵配额在三个指令微调模型和三个语义维度上达到 r=0.96 的皮尔逊相关；3) 在概念子空间为多维的层上，匹配或超越加法基线；4) 显著减少退化输出。

Conclusion: 概念矩阵 steering 是一种几何上具有原则性、可组合、且实际更安全的单方向 steering 替代方案，尤其适用于需要从有限对比样本对中提取概念的复杂语义场景。

Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

</details>


### [218] [Automated Formal Proofs of Combinatorial Identities via Wilf-Zeilberger Guidance and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04472)
*Beibei Xiong,Hangyu Lv,Junqi Liu,Yisen Wang,Shaoshi Chen,Jianlin Wang,Zhengfeng Yang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经-符号框架 WZ-LLM：用 Wilf–Zeilberger（WZ）方法把组合恒等式自动分解为 Lean 4 可执行的“证明草图”（递推引理 + 边界条件 + 非零/良定义性前提 + 归一化与分情形子目标），再由专门训练的 WZ-Prover（8B，基于 Goedel-Prover-V2）逐一攻克这些机器可检子目标，最后由 Lean 内核统一核验。在自建的 100 题组合恒等式基准 LCI-Test 上端到端通过率达 34%（34/100），显著高于 Goedel-Prover-V2 的 9/100 与 DeepSeek-V3 的 1/100，并在 CombiBench（16/100）与 PutnamBench-Comb（3/36）上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合恒等式的 Lean 4 形式化证明对当前 LLM 证明器极难，原因有三：(1) 需要长程（long-horizon）证明规划，若无可执行的全局路线，证明器只能无约束探索，导致搜索组合爆炸；(2) 组合领域公开证明数据稀缺，冷启动困难；(3) 经典 WZ 方法虽能给出“证书+递推+边界条件”的天然全局结构，但其 CAS 输出（如有理证书）无法直接翻译进证明助手，形式化时必须显式重建伸缩论证、边界条件、归一化步骤与非零旁条件，成本往往主导验证开销。本文因此希望：用符号方法承担长程证明规划（约束搜索空间），用 LLM 承担局部形式义务的攻克，并同时解决“WZ 覆盖范围之外的身份式仍需直接证明”的覆盖缺口。

Method: 框架分两阶段。\n(1) 符号分解（Symbolic Decomposition）：以 SageMath 10.7 为 CAS 后端，用 sage.combinat 的 F.WZ_certificate 合成有理证书 R(n,k)（G=R·F），把目标归一化为 ∑F=1 的标准型；先把求和统一改写为 Finset.range(n+1) 形式（移位区间、Icc/Ico→range、超几何因子代数归一化），再生成 Lean 4 可执行证明草图，拆解为五类证明任务：T_rec（递推/比值引理）、T_bd（边界条件 G(n,0)=0、G(n,n+1)=0）、T_side（field_simp 所需的分母非零与良定义性）、T_norm（归一化/区间平移）、T_case（奇偶等离散分情形分支）。关键工程点是通过符号化简与约束求解自动推断定义域约束并编译成 Lean 侧条件。\n(2) 模型训练（WZ-Prover）：数据三部分——专家种子集（人工形式化 307 个恒等式并给出完整无 sorry 的 Lean 证明，分解出 1200 个子目标）、候选语句池 1020 个（来自另两部经典文献）、源不相交的测试集 100 题（LCI-Test，并对表达式做规范化以防泄漏）。三阶段训练：冷启动 SFT → 专家在环自举（2 轮，Round1 生成 7385 条义务、保留 5139 条已核验引理 + 32 个端到端证明；Round2 再得 532 引理 + 79 身份证明，累计 5671 引理 + 111 身份，SFT 语料约 5418 条）→ DAPO 强化学习精炼。RL 阶段用 rollout 诊断估计各目标通过率，做“难度平滑”数据治理（下采样近重复的高通过率简单题，剔除近零通过率的高噪声题），奖励为内核验证的 ±1 结果奖励加接近 token 上限的软超长惩罚 R(π;G)=R_out+λ_len·R_len，以缓解熵崩塌与截断奖励噪声。推理时采用队列式流程（Algorithm 1），带去重集合 S_seen；WZ 不适用时回退为直接证明。系统还封装为 Lean 内交互式 tactic wz_prove，一条命令即可完成“CAS 合成证书→分解→子目标证明→内核核验”的全流程。

Result: LCI-Test（100 题，pass@32）：WZ-LLM 34/100，远超 Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview 16/100、Goedel-Prover-V2 9/100、DeepSeek-Prover-V2 与 Kimina-Prover-Distill 各 6/100、DeepSeek-V3 1/100。组件消融：WZ-Sketch+Goedel-Prover-V2 仅 9/100（与基线持平，说明单靠草图不够），WZ-Prover 直接证明 12/100（优于基线 9/100），WZ-uncovered 直接路线解出 5 题，草图+专用证明器路线解出 29 题，合计 34。引理级诊断（1,178 个草图子目标）：Goedel-Prover-V2 仅证 564（47.88%），因此 46 个被草图覆盖的问题 0 个端到端通过；WZ-Prover 证 864（73.34%），带来 29/46 的端到端解出——表明符号分解的收益高度依赖强引理证明器。跨数据集：CombiBench 上 WZ-LLM 16/100（基线 Goedel 12/100、DeepSeek-Prover-V2 8/100、Kimina 6/100），PutnamBench-Comb 3/36（基线 0–2/36），且人工识别的组合恒等式实例全部解出。训练阶段消融（LCI-Test pass@1/8/32）：仅种子 SFT 为 1/3/9，+专家迭代为 3/6/10，+DAPO 为 4/6/12，增益主要体现在 pass@32 等大采样预算上。计算成本：训练 16 GPU-天、评测 9 GPU-天，4×L40S-48GB。

Conclusion: WZ-LLM 说明“符号证明规划 + 学习式形式推理”的耦合是一条有前景的路线：一方面 WZ 草图把长程组合恒等式证明外化为一组局部可核验义务，压缩搜索空间并可被专用证明器可靠攻克，从而把符号计算在 CAS 中的优势转化为显著更高的端到端通过率；另一方面，专门的组合领域训练与直接证明路线弥补了 WZ 覆盖范围之外的缺口（LCI-Test 上多解出纯符号基线失败的身份式）。核心经验是：符号草图必须搭配足够强的子目标证明器才有意义，且专用化训练对长程引理链与分布外泛化的贡献大于草图本身。局限在于结果整体绝对成功率仍较低（34%），CombiBench/PutnamBench 上草图路线增益有限，说明 WZ 证书合成的覆盖率与自动化边界条件形式化仍是瓶颈。

Abstract: Automating formal proofs of combinatorial identities is challenging for LLM-based provers, as long-horizon proof planning is required and unconstrained search quickly explodes. Symbolic methods such as the Wilf-Zeilberger (WZ) method can achieve a mechanized proof of combinatorial identities by constructing special auxiliary functions and demonstrating that they satisfy specific recurrence relations. We propose WZ-LLM, a neuro-symbolic framework that turns WZ proof plans into executable proof sketches in Lean 4 and uses an LLM-based prover to discharge the resulting machine-checkable subgoals. We also train a dedicated WZ-Prover via a Lean-kernel-verified bootstrapping loop with expert-verified iteration, followed by DAPO-based refinement. Experiments show that WZ-LLM achieves a 34% proof success rate on LCI-Test (100 classic combinatorial identities), outperforming strong baselines such as DeepSeek-V3 and Goedel-Prover-V2, and delivering consistent gains on CombiBench and PutnamBench-Comb. These results indicate that our framework provides two complementary strengths: improved direct proving for identities beyond the scope of WZ, and substantially higher end-to-end success when WZ sketches guide a specialized prover.

</details>


### [219] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIOP方法，通过将最终答案的语义聚类作为潜在未来状态，实现无需人工标注和任务特定验证器的turn-level credit assignment，解决了长 horizon LLM agents中中间决策步骤缺乏训练反馈的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon LLM agents依赖于中间信息收集turns，但训练反馈通常只在最终答案处观察。Process-level rewards需要高质量人工标注。现有的turn-level shaping方法需要答案监督或稳定的任务特定验证器。Label-free RL方法从输出分布中提取自信号，但主要在答案或trajectory级别，因此无法将credit分配给中间turns。

Method: SIOP将最终答案的语义聚类作为潜在未来outcome states，用于基于潜力的turn-level credit assignment。具体流程：1)对每个query采样多个rollouts；2)将最终答案聚类为语义outcome modes；3)构建可靠性感知的target distribution over these states；4)使用可处理的cluster-level approximation奖励那些增加可靠未来状态后验支持的turns。

Result: 在7个搜索增强的agentic reasoning benchmarks上，SIOP相比verifier-free outcome-level baselines提升了平均性能，同时接近gold-supervised outcome baseline。

Conclusion: SIOP目标将信息势塑造从金牌答案监督泛化到没有任务特定金牌验证器的设置，同时避免了标准GRPO使用的广播式 rollout-level advantages。在7个搜索增强的agentic推理基准测试中，SIOP在无需验证器的outcome-level baselines上提升了平均性能，并接近金牌监督的outcome baseline。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

</details>


### [220] [Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer for Shape Optimization and Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04474)
*Guoze Sun,Tianya Miao,Haoyang Huang,Huaguan Chen,Han Wan,Rui Zhang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GANO（Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer）是一个端到端可微的形状优化/反演框架：由带高斯去噪的 SDF 自动解码器（StableSDF）表示几何、由带几何门控注入的 Transolver（GI-Transolver）做场级预测，并在隐空间用梯度下降迭代优化几何，用零空间投影实现局部部件冻结、用 SDF 投影替代重网格化。理论证明去噪隐式正则化解码器雅可比、带来受控形变。在 2D Helmholtz、2D 翼型、3D 车辆三个基准上均取得 SOTA：前向误差 Rel.L2 分别为 0.0170/0.0022/0.1782，翼型升阻比 +55.9%，车辆阻力下降约 7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何是 PDE 系统的核心控制变量，形状优化与反演在制造中至关重要。经典流程需要昂贵的正向求解配合 CAD 修改与重网格化，专家负担重、迭代慢。神经代理虽加速了正向分析，但几何更新通常是不可微操作（CAD 编辑、重网格化），无法闭环。已有可微方法要么依赖受限的参数化（如 CST/FID 设计变量），要么在高维弱约束的隐空间做不稳定优化，且常仅预测标量目标，缺少可解释性与部件级控制。因此需要一个工业级、端到端可微、几何一致且可控的自动化形状优化/反演框架。

Method: 1) 几何表示 StableSDF：基于 DeepSDF 的隐式 SDF 自动解码器，每形状一个可学习隐码，训练时对隐码注入高斯噪声（ε~N(0,σ²I)）做去噪式增广，损失为 ℓ1 重建 + ℓ2 隐码正则；噪声抑制了解码器对隐码扰动的敏感性，使 Γ(z) 映射局部平滑、可生成。2) 前向预测 GI-Transolver：以 Transolver 的切片（slice）token 机制为基础，将隐码 z 投影到 token 维度，用切片条件化的门控 g_g=σ(W2·SiLU(W1 t_g)) 调制 W_z z 并做残差注入 t_g←t_g+g_g⊙W_z z，从而建立 ∂J/∂z = ∂J/∂û · ∂û/∂z 的梯度通路；输出为完整场（速度、压力、散射场）。3) 可微优化：冻结解码器与代理模型，仅更新 z；每步预测场→计算目标→反传 ∇_z J→Adam 更新→用零空间投影 P(z)=I−G†G（G 为约束点处 SDF 对 z 的雅可比）实现部件冻结（保持后视镜等一阶不变）→用免重网格化投影 x←x−s_θ(x,z)∇_x s_θ/(‖∇_x s_θ‖²+ε) 将表面采样点拉回更新后的零水平集（对应最小化 ½s² 的 Gauss–Newton 步，残差二次收敛）。4) 目标：翼型最大化 C_L 并对 C_D≤0.02 加铰链惩罚（控制体积分算力）；车辆最小化压力积分阻力代理并加 λ_reg‖z−z_init‖² 约束。

Result: 前向预测：GANO 在三基准上均为最优，2D Helmholtz Rel.L1/L2 = 0.0171/0.0170（AeroGTO 0.0212/0.0234）；AirFoil_9k = 0.0008/0.0022（AeroGTO 0.0021/0.0057）；DrivAerNet++ 3D 车辆 = 0.1655/0.1782。2D Helmholtz 反演：单波数 k=7、100 传感器即可恢复障碍物细节，误差约 6–7%，10 个传感器仍可恢复全局轮廓。2D 翼型优化：C_L/C_D 从 53.6 提升到 83.4（+55.9%），优于 U-Net 75.0（+40.0%）与 DeepONet 44.3（−17.3%），C_D=0.017 满足软约束，COMSOL 高保真验证。3D 车辆优化：OpenFOAM 验证 C_D 从 0.346→0.323（−6.64%，estateback）、0.299→0.278（−7.02%，fastback）；相比 PhysGen，GANO 有效避免缩减/后掠后视镜而保持设计要素。消融显示：去噪使隐码雅可比范数分布显著低于 DeepSDF，噪声强度 σ∈{0,0.005,0.01,0.05} 不损害重建（F1≈0.911，CDist/EMD 变化可忽略）；门控注入优于加性注入与交叉注意力；无 NSP 时后视镜严重变形脱离车身。鲁棒性：训练 5×10⁴ 点，测试 10³–5×10⁵ 点均稳定（Transolver 在稀疏点退化、AeroGTO 在稀疏与密集两端均差）。计算开销：投影 10⁴ 点约 30 ms/5 次迭代，投影成功率 99.7%–99.999%；100 步完整优化耗时 Helmholtz 3.93 s、翼型 35.69 s、车辆 7.39 s；推理延迟与显存与 Transolver 相当。

Conclusion: GANO 统一了几何表示、场级预测与自动化优化/反演，构成单一隐空间闭环：StableSDF 的去噪机制被证明诱导隐式雅可比正则化（E|f(z+ε)−s| ≤ |f(z)−s| + σ√(2/π)‖∇_z f‖ + O(σ²)），进而给出 Hausdorff 意义的受控表面位移界 d_H ≤ (L_z/m)‖δz‖ + O(‖δz‖²)，并解释了 detach 投影路径的梯度偏差界 ≤ √N (L_z/m)‖∇_X Ĵ‖；零空间投影提供部件级几何保护，免重网格化投影保证边界一致的查询。局限：仅适用于定常 PDE，不涉及时空动力学与动态形状优化；未来工作为时变 PDE 扩展。

Abstract: Geometry is central to PDE-governed systems, motivating shape optimization and inversion. Classical pipelines conduct costly forward simulation with geometry processing, requiring substantial expert effort. Neural surrogates accelerate forward analysis but do not close the loop because gradients from objectives to geometry are often unavailable. Existing differentiable methods either rely on restrictive parameterizations or unstable latent optimization driven by scalar objectives, limiting interpretability and part-wise control. To address these challenges, we propose Geometry-Aware Neural Optimizer (GANO), an end-to-end differentiable framework that unifies geometry representation, field-level prediction, and automated optimization/inversion in a single latent-space loop. GANO encodes shapes with an auto-decoder and stabilizes latent updates via a denoising mechanism, and a geometry-injected surrogate provides a reliable gradient pathway for geometry updates. Moreover, GANO supports part-wise control through null-space projection and uses remeshing-free projection to accelerate geometry processing. We further prove that denoising induces an implicit Jacobian regularization that reduces decoder sensitivity, yielding controlled deformations. Experiments on three benchmarks spanning 2D Helmholtz, 2D airfoil, and 3D vehicles show state-of-the-art accuracy and stable, controllable updates, achieving up to +55.9% lift-to-drag improvement for airfoils and ~7% drag reduction for vehicles.

</details>


### [221] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memini是一个受生物突触巩固启发的外部记忆系统，通过有向图结构和双时间尺度耦合变量（快/慢），将情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘统一为单一机制，重新定义外部记忆为可自重组的学习基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统外部记忆系统依赖显式管理，无法自适应调整。生物记忆通过耦合的多时间尺度动力学实现即时可用、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘，但外部记忆尚未遵循类似原则。

Method: 将外部记忆组织为有向图，每个边携带两个耦合的内部变量（快变量和慢变量），遵循Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型的双时间尺度动力学机制。

Result: 从耦合机制中自发涌现出情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘三种特性，它们作为单一机制的不同侧面统一实现。

Conclusion: Memini将外部记忆重新定义为通过自身动态重组的学习基底，通过耦合的双时间尺度变量实现了即时可用性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘的统一机制，为构建自适应外部记忆系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

</details>


### [222] [Data-dependent Exploration for Online Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04477)
*Zhen-Yu Zhang,Yuting Tang,Jiandong Zhang,Lanjihong Ma,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEPO方法解决在线RLHF中的探索问题，通过历史数据驱动的自适应不确定性奖励鼓励对高价值区域的探索，兼顾理论保证（自适应遗憾界）和实践效果（样本效率提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLHF场景中，探索策略需要使LLM生成有价值且信息丰富的对比样本。然而现有探索策略依赖on-policy期望进行bonus计算，在训练时可用偏好数据有限的情况下难以可靠估计，导致策略过早放弃可能包含高价值行为的欠探索区域。

Method: DEPO利用历史数据为高不确定性区域构建额外的奖励bonus，引导策略探索潜在高价值数据区域，同时从理论层面提供了数据依赖的遗憾上界，证明算法能自适应学习任务的难度。

Result: 理论上，DEPO的自适应遗憾上界可随学习任务难度调整，实际中比最坏情况上界更紧；实验上，在多个基准测试中一致超越强基线，样本效率显著提升。

Conclusion: DEPO提供了一种简单可扩展的在线RLHF探索策略，通过利用历史数据构建不确定性奖励，有效解决了传统方法中因数据有限导致的探索不足问题，显著提升了样本效率。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) by continuously collecting new preference feedback during training. A foundational challenge in this setting is exploration, which requires algorithms that enable the LLMs to generate informative comparisons that improve sample-efficiency in online RLHF. Existing exploration strategies often derive bonuses via on-policy expectations, which are difficult to estimate reliably from the limited historical preference data available during training; as a result, the policy can prematurely down-weight under-explored regions that may contain high-value behaviors. In this paper, we propose data-dependent exploration for preference optimization (DEPO), a simple and scalable method that leverages historical data to construct an extra uncertainty bonus for high-uncertainty regions, encouraging exploration toward potentially high-value data. Theoretically, we provide a data-dependent regret bound for the proposed algorithm, showing that it adapts to the hardness of the learning task itself and can be tighter than worst-case bounds in practice. Empirically, the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines across benchmarks, demonstrating improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [223] [Towards General Preference Alignment: Diffusion Models at Nash Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04494)
*Jiaming Hu,Jiamu Bai,Haoyu Wang,Debarghya Mukherjee,Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diff.-NPO（Diffusion Nash Preference Optimization），将文生图扩散模型的人类偏好对齐从"Bradley-Terry 隐式奖励 + DPO"范式重构为**双人零和博弈/纳什学习（NLHF）**范式：通过在线镜像下降（OMD/Hedge）自博弈更新，直接优化相对偏好概率，用"当前策略 vs 上一步策略（p_prev）vs 参考策略（p_ref）"三重对数似然比构造成对 logistic 损失。在 SD1.5 与 SDXL 上以 Pick-a-Pic 训练，在 Pick-a-Pic 测试集、Parti-Prompts、HPSV2 上评测，所有数据集与骨干模型的平均胜率均优于 Online-DPO、SPIN、SEPPO、Online-SFT 等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型偏好对齐方法（Diffusion-DPO 及其变体）沿用 LLM 的 RLHF 范式：先把偏好数据经 Bradley-Terry（BT）或 Plackett-Luce（PL）模型转换为隐式奖励，再用 DPO 优化。BT 模型强制偏好关系的传递性（若 A>B 且 B>C 则 A>C），无法刻画真实人类偏好的非传递性/循环性（如石头剪刀布），且在不同人群间可能与实际决策相矛盾。作者因此主张：不应先用 BT 把偏好"折算"成奖励，而应直接建模并优化一般偏好关系 𝔙(x₀ ≻ x₀′|c)。此外，现有方法多为离线对参考模型的固定比较，学习信号受限，缺少 on-policy 的自博弈自我改进机制。

Method: **1）博弈建模**：定义双人零和目标 J(p₁,p₂)=𝔼[𝔙(x₀≻x₀′|c)] − τ·KL(p₁‖p_ref) + τ·KL(p₂‖p_ref)，max 方（当前策略）提升相对 p₂ 的胜率，min 方（对手）压低胜率，双方均被 KL 拉向 p_ref；用对偶间隙 DualGap(p) 度量与纳什均衡的距离。

**2）在线镜像下降（OMD）**：贪心最优响应更新不稳定、可能导致线性遗憾，故采用带熵正则的乐观镜像下降 p_{s+1}=argmin⟨∇ℓ_s(p_s),p⟩+η·KL(p‖p_s)，闭式解为指数权重形式 p_{s+1} ∝ exp(1/η·𝔙(x≻p_s))·p_ref^{τ/η}·p_s^{1−τ/η}；即目标分布由参考策略、上一步策略与偏好信号三部分构成。

**3）成对 logistic 损失**：最优解应满足 h_s(p_{s+1}) = [𝔙(x⁺≻p_s) − 𝔙(x⁻≻p_s)]/η，但该量难精确计算，故用观测到的偏好对构造 h_s（参考项与上一步项的对数似然比线性组合）的成对 logistic 损失作为可计算的代理；并沿用 Diffusion-DPO 的 ELBO 技巧把全链似然比分解到单时间步反向转移，得到最终目标：
L = 𝔼[−log σ(βT·ω(λ_t)·(δ_θ − (τ/η)δ_ref − ((η−τ)/η)δ_prev))]，其中 δ 为噪声预测的隐式奖励差。

**4）统一视角**：L 可写为 −𝔼[log σ(log p(y|x⁺,c) − log p(y|x⁻,c))]，说明 DPO、自博弈与本文本质优化同一对数 logistic 目标，差异在隐式目标分布的构造：γ=τ/η=1 退化为 Diffusion-DPO（仅相对固定参考改进），γ=0 为纯自博弈（易漂移、欠稳定），中间值在两者间插值。

**5）算法实现**：每步用 10 步推理为每个 prompt 在线生成 8 张候选图，用 PickScore、HPSV2、CLIP、Aesthetic、ImageReward 五个评分模型取平均排名，最佳为 x⁺、最差为 x⁻；损失中前向加噪 q(x_t|x₀) 采样；对上一步策略做软更新 p_{s+1}←λp_s+(1−λ)p_{s+1}，λ=min(0.001s, 0.5)。超参：β=5000、τ/η=1/2、lr 1e-8、有效批量 2048、8×A100。

**6）为什么弃用 INPO 的平方损失**：INPO 的平方损失假设有序对按偏好分布 λ_p 采样，而实际实现是用 oracle 确定性排序取最优/最差对，不满足该假设，故改用成对 logistic 损失以保证优化稳定。

Result: • **总体**：SD1.5 与 SDXL 上，Diff.-NPO 在 Pick-a-Pic 测试集、Parti-Prompts、HPSV2 三个评测集的全部平均胜率均为最高。SD1.5/Pick-a-Pic 平均胜率 78.49%，优于 Online-DPO 74.84%、SEPPO 75.62%、SPIN 71.72%、Online-SFT 50.90%。
• **偏好类指标优势更明显**：SDXL/Parti-Prompts 上 ImageReward 胜率 79.88→86.73（相对 +8.6%），AES 胜率 76.74→85.04（相对 +10.8%）；Parti-Prompts 平均胜率相对提升 7.0%。
• **Online-SFT 不稳定**：平均胜率多低于 50%，因其直接拟合 10 步推理的低质量图像。
• **正则系数消融（τ/η）**：从 0（纯自博弈）到 1（DPO 式）呈"先升后降"，最优在中间值 τ/η=1/2（IM 0.8896、AES 5.8773、胜率最高），说明参考策略的稳定锚定与上一步策略的在线自改进信号需联合使用。
• **目标函数消融**：以 INPO 式平方损失替换成对 logistic 后胜率大幅下降（PS 81.60→60.80、IM 85.12→70.20），验证成对 logistic 更适合确定性排序构造的偏好对。
• **在线 vs 离线**：离线 SFT（73.4/80.7/…）优于在线 SFT，但离线 DPO 弱于在线 DPO（PS 73.3 vs 77.4），Diff.-NPO 在五项指标上全面领先。

Conclusion: Nash 式在线学习可作为扩散模型"奖励中心"范式（GRPO 类显式奖励最大化、BT 隐式奖励 DPO）之外的一个有前景替代方案：Diff.-NPO 用自博弈式相对偏好概率优化 + 参考策略正则，实现了简单、稳定且对齐质量更高的扩散偏好对齐，并为扩散模型的 Nash 学习研究奠定框架基础。作者指出的局限是偏好信号依赖五个评分模型的平均排名构造，可能不够全面；未来工作将扩展更强的在线数据收集策略，并推广到更广泛的扩散生成与编辑任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has been popular for aligning text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models with human preferences. As a mainstream branch of RLHF, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a computationally efficient alternative that avoids explicit reward modeling and has been widely adopted in diffusion alignment. However, existing preference-based methods for diffusion alignment still rely on reward-induced preference signals and typically assume that human preferences can be adequately modeled by the Bradley--Terry (BT) model, which may fail to capture the full complexity of human preferences. In this paper, we formulate diffusion alignment from a game-theoretic perspective. We propose Diffusion Nash Preference Optimization (Diff.-NPO), an intuitive general preference framework for diffusion alignment. Diff.-NPO encourages the current policy to play against itself to achieve self improvement and lead to a better alignment. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of Diff.-NPO on the text-to-image generation task via various metrics. Diff.-NPO consistently outperforms existing preference-based diffusion alignment methods.

</details>


### [224] [Quadrature-TreeSHAP: Depth-Independent TreeSHAP and Shapley Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04497)
*Ron Wettenstein,Rory Mitchell,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Quadrature-TreeSHAP，通过高斯-勒让德积分重构Path-Dependent TreeSHAP，仅需8个固定积分点即可达到机器精度，实现1.06x-10.59x（CPU）和1.84x-6.95x（GPU）加速，高阶交互计算加速高达1200倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TreeSHAP方法在计算深度、数值稳定性和高阶交互支持之间存在权衡：深度依赖导致深层树效率低下；数值不稳定；无法有效支持高阶Shapley交互值计算。

Method: 基于加权Banzhaf交互多项式，将Banzhaf交互值表示为特征参与概率p下的期望，通过对p从0到1积分恢复Shapley值及任意阶交互值。采用Gauss-Legendre数值积分，固定8个积分点即可达机器精度。

Result: 12个XGBoost基准测试中，Quadrature-TreeSHAP在CPU上比TreeSHAP快1.06x-10.59x，GPU上比GPUTreeSHAP快1.84x-6.95x。成对交互在CPU上快3.80x-58.11x，高阶交互比TreeSHAP-IQ快达1200x。

Conclusion: 固定点公式消除了内层计算的深度依赖性，支持高效SIMD执行。方法兼具数值稳定性和深度不敏感性，为TreeSHAP提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Shapley values are a standard tool for explaining predictions of tree ensembles, with Path-Dependent SHAP being the most widely used variant. Despite substantial progress, existing methods still exhibit trade-offs between depth-dependent runtime, numerical stability, and support for higher-order interactions. To address these challenges, we introduce Quadrature-TreeSHAP, a quadrature-based reformulation of Path-Dependent TreeSHAP that is numerically stable, naturally extends to any-order Shapley interaction values and is practically insensitive to tree depth. Our implementation supports both CPU and GPU and is integrated into XGBoost.
  Our method is based on a weighted-Banzhaf interaction polynomial, which expresses Banzhaf interaction values as expectations under a feature participation probability $p$. Shapley values and any-order interaction values are then recovered by integrating these polynomials over $p$ from 0 to 1. We evaluate these integrals using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and show that, in practice, only 8 fixed quadrature points are sufficient to reach machine precision. In fact, Quadrature-TreeSHAP with 8 fixed points achieves greater numerical stability than TreeSHAP. This fixed-point formulation removes depth dependence from the inner computation and enables efficient SIMD execution.
  We confirm these advantages empirically. On 12 XGBoost benchmarks, Quadrature-TreeSHAP computes Shapley values 1.06x-10.59x faster than TreeSHAP on CPU and 1.84x-6.95x faster than GPUTreeSHAP on GPU. Shapley pairwise interactions are 3.80x-58.11x faster on CPU, with higher-order interactions achieving speedups of up to 1200x compared to TreeSHAP-IQ.

</details>


### [225] [Gradient Scaling Effects in Adaptive Spectral PINNs for Stiff Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04502)
*Isabela M. Yepes,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了IC门控函数选择会在训练过程中诱导显式的时间依赖梯度缩放，该效应与谱表示交互。在不同刚度条件下，指数门控和线性门控表现出差异性优势，自适应谱PINNs对IC嵌入选择更为敏感，证明IC嵌入并非中性的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINNs在训练刚性和振荡动力学系统时存在优化条件差的问题。虽然已有研究强调谱参数化等表征补救措施，但初始条件(IC)嵌入在自适应谱PINNs中的优化含义尚未得到充分表征。

Method: 采用非线性刚弹簧摆ODE作为受控基准，对比指数门控与线性门控函数，结合固定和自适应傅里叶谱 trunk。使用相对L²误差和最大点态误差进行评估，并通过Holm校正的配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验验证结果的统计显著性。

Result: 在中度刚度(k=20)时，指数门控通常误差更低但存在异质性行为；在高刚度(k=60)时，线性门控更优，且在更大刚度下观察到额外的模式反转。这些趋势在相对L²误差和最大点态误差中均一致成立。

Conclusion: 研究证实IC嵌入函数的选择对PINNs的训练效果有实质性影响。在高刚度系统中，线性门控优于指数门控；而在中度刚度系统中，指数门控通常更优但存在随机性。不同刚度条件下存在模式反转，且梯度缩放与谱表示的交互作用会显著影响优化条件。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) often struggle to train reliably on stiff and oscillatory dynamical systems due to poor optimization conditioning. While prior work has emphasized representational remedies such as spectral parameterizations, the optimization implications of initial-condition (IC) embeddings in adaptive spectral PINNs have not been well characterized. In this work, we show that the choice of IC gating function induces explicit time-dependent gradient scaling, which interacts with spectral representations during training. Using a nonlinear stiff spring-pendulum ODE as a controlled benchmark, we compare exponential and linear IC gates in combination with fixed and adaptive Fourier spectral trunks. We observe stiffness-dependent changes in relative dominance for adaptive PINNs: at moderate stiffness ($k=20$), exponential gating often yields lower error but exhibits heterogeneous behavior across random seeds, whereas at higher stiffness ($k=60$), linear gating becomes preferable, with additional reversals observed at larger $k$. These trends hold for both relative $L^2$ error and maximum pointwise error and are confirmed by paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. Overall, our results demonstrate that IC embeddings are not a neutral design choice in PINNs: the induced gradient scaling materially shapes optimization conditioning in stiff regimes, with distinct sensitivity patterns in baseline and adaptive spectral models.

</details>


### [226] [FL-Sailer: Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Scalable Single-Cell Epigenetic Data Analysis via Adaptive Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04519)
*Guangyi Zhang,Yi Dai,Yiyun He,Junhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FL-Sailer，首个针对单细胞ATAC-seq数据的联邦学习框架，通过自适应杠杆得分采样（降维80%）和不变VAE架构（互信息最小化解耦生物信号与技术混杂因素）解决隐私保护下的跨机构合作难题，并提供收敛性理论保证，实验表明该方法性能可超越集中式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing of scATAC-seq data. Existing federated learning approaches face three fundamental barriers: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity.

Method: FL-Sailer uses adaptive leverage score sampling to reduce dimensionality by 80% while preserving biological interpretability, combined with an invariant VAE architecture that disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization.

Result: FL-Sailer enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations and surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise.

Conclusion: FL-Sailer establishes federated learning as a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research, demonstrating both privacy preservation and performance that can surpass centralized methods through adaptive sampling as implicit regularization.

Abstract: Single-cell ATAC-seq (scATAC-seq) enables high-resolution mapping of chromatin accessibility, yet privacy regulations and data size constraints hinder multi-institutional sharing. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative, but faces three fundamental barriers in scATAC-seq analysis: ultra-high dimensionality, extreme sparsity, and severe cross-institutional heterogeneity. We propose FL-Sailer, the first FL framework designed for scATAC-seq data. FL-Sailer integrates two key innovations: (i) adaptive leverage score sampling, which selects biologically interpretable features while reducing dimensionality by 80%, and (ii) an invariant VAE architecture, which disentangles biological signals from technical confounders via mutual information minimization. We provide a convergence guarantee, showing that FL-Sailer converges to an approximate solution of the original high-dimensional problem with bounded error. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real epigenomic datasets demonstrate that FL-Sailer not only enables previously infeasible multi-institutional collaborations but also surpasses centralized methods by leveraging adaptive sampling as an implicit regularizer to suppress technical noise. Our work establishes that federated learning, when tailored to domain-specific challenges, can become a superior paradigm for collaborative epigenomic research.

</details>


### [227] [Dream-MPC: Gradient-Based Model Predictive Control with Latent Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04568)
*Jonathan Spieler,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dream-MPC是一种新颖的基于模型的强化学习方法，通过从策略生成候选轨迹，利用学习的世界模型进行梯度上升优化，结合不确定性正则化和动作复用技术，在24个连续控制任务上显著提升策略性能，优于无梯度MPC和先进基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习方法存在瓶颈：基于无梯度优化的方法计算开销大，而梯度方法性能通常劣于无梯度方法。混合方法虽结合MPC和学习模型的优点，但仍依赖无梯度优化。

Method: Dream-MPC核心创新包括：(1)从滚动策略生成候选轨迹；(2)利用学习的世界模型进行梯度上升优化；(3)不确定性正则化；(4)通过复用已优化动作实现优化迭代的摊销。

Result: 在24个连续控制任务上的实验表明，Dream-MPC能显著提升底层策略性能，并超越无梯度MPC及先进基线方法。

Conclusion: Dream-MPC成功结合了梯度优化的高效性与MPC和学习世界模型的优势，为高维控制任务提供了一种计算可行且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches either use gradient-free, population-based methods for planning, learned policy networks, or a combination of policy networks and planning. Hybrid approaches that combine Model Predictive Control (MPC) with a learned model and a policy prior to leverage the advantages of both paradigms have shown promising results. However, these approaches typically rely on gradient-free optimization methods, which can be computationally expensive for high-dimensional control tasks. While gradient-based methods are a promising alternative, recent works have empirically shown that gradient-based methods often perform worse than their gradient-free counterparts. We propose Dream-MPC, a novel approach that generates few candidate trajectories from a rolled-out policy and optimizes each trajectory by gradient ascent using a learned world model, uncertainty regularization and amortization of optimization iterations over time by reusing previously optimized actions. Our results on 24 continuous control tasks show that Dream-MPC can significantly improve the performance of the underlying policy and can outperform gradient-free MPC and state-of-the-art baselines. We will open source our code and more at https://dream-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [228] [HeterSEED: Semantics-Structure Decoupling for Heterogeneous Graph Learning under Heterophily](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04594)
*Xinyi Li,Ming Li,Lu Bai,Lixin Cui,Feilong Cao,Ke Lv,Yunliang Jiang,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HeterSEED 是一个面向"异质图+异配性"场景的"语义—结构解耦"框架。它把表示学习拆成两条并行通道：异质语义聚合通道（一跳关系感知消息传递）与结构感知异配通道（伪标签划分同配/异配邻居 + 元路径结构权重聚合），再用节点级门控自适应融合。理论上证明其假设类严格强于纯特征相似度驱动的 HGNN，并可降低异配邻居带来的预测偏差；在 5 个真实异质图（含百万节点/亿级边规模）上全面超越 15 个基线，异配越强优势越大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准异质图神经网络（HGNN）沿元路径/元关系聚合消息时，主要依赖特征相似度来加权邻居，隐含"特征相似⇒标签/语义角色相似"的同配假设。然而大量真实异质图存在显著异配性（如图神经网络中的学术网络、电商风控图 RCDD），相连节点标签相异或扮演不同语义角色，特征相似度与关系语义错位，导致消息传递传播错误信息、模糊决策边界。论文的实证证据（Figure 1）显示元路径特征相似度与标签同配比之间只有极弱相关性（全局线性拟合 R²=0.205），说明仅靠特征聚合不足以刻画关系语义。此外，多节点类型与跨类型复杂关系使同配与异配交互在局部邻域中交织，进一步加剧问题。已有异配感知方法（如 HETERO2NET、LatGRL）虽引入元路径级异配度量与辅助预测，但仍基于元路径视图上的特征消息传递，未显式解耦异质语义与异配结构，也未做节点级的结构感知融合。

Method: 1) 异质语义聚合通道：标准 HGNN 风格的一跳邻域卷积，h_v^(l+1)=σ(W_self h_v + Σ_r mean_{u∈N_r(v)} W_r h_u)，堆叠 L 层得到语义嵌入 h_v^(s)，不依赖人工元路径。2) 结构感知异配通道：先用迭代伪标签（训练集用真值，其余用线性分类器预测并随训练更新）把每条对称元路径诱导同质图 G_p 的边集划分为同配 E_homo 与异配 E_hetero，跨元路径聚合为 G_homo/G_hetero；用元路径实例计数定义原始结构权重 ω_{u,v}=Σ_p C_p(u,v)（如 APA 共同论文数），softmax 归一化得结构感知边权 ω̃；再对同配/异配邻居分别加权聚合，经两层 MLP 变换与维度级门控得结构嵌入 h_v^(r)。3) 标签引导掩码：可学习标签嵌入矩阵含 [MASK] token，训练时以概率 β 掩蔽已标注节点的标签嵌入并加回特征，推理时关闭掩码，作为正则化防止过度依赖可见标签与确认偏差。4) 节点级融合：拼接 [h^(s)||h^(r)] 经单层线性+sigmoid 得标量门 γ_v，h_v=(1-γ_v)h^(s)+γ_v h^(r)；并加解耦正则 L_dec（最小化两通道批内余弦相似度/互协方差），总损失 L=L_cls+α·L_dec。理论侧：Theorem 1 用"作者—论文—作者(APA)"构造对称图（所有作者特征相同、每篇论文连一个正类一个负类作者），证明任何纯语义 HGNN 因表示坍缩（Lemma 1）分类误差≥ε₀，而 HeterSEED 借助 APA 计数与同/异配分离可达零误差；Proposition 1 在平方损失回归设定下，将语义 HGNN 视为线性平滑器，异配质量 q_v 导致 4q_v² 的偏差，分离+重加权把有效异配质量降为 q̃_v，风险差满足 E[R(f_s)]−E[R(f)] ≥ c·E[(q_v−q̃_v)²] − Cδ（δ 为伪标签误差率，门控 L-Lipschitz）。附录 C.4 另用 Rademacher 复杂度与间隔界说明解耦正则可降低融合表示的有效维度、起到正则化作用。复杂度：全批 O(L|E|d+L|V|d²+M‖A‖d)，大规模用 mini-batch 动态稀疏元路径构建以保证百万节点/亿边可扩展。

Result: 在 DBLP、IMDB、ACM、MAG（193.9万节点/2111万边）、RCDD（1380万节点/1.578亿边）五个异质图上与 15 个基线（MLP/GCN/GAT；LINKX、FAGCN、ACM-GCN、GRAIN；RGCN、RGAT、HAN、HGT、SHGN、HINormer、DiffGraph；HETERO2NET）比较，Macro-F1/Micro-F1 全部最优：DBLP 94.48/94.87、IMDB 66.71/70.60、ACM 94.34/94.21、MAG 34.99/56.25、RCDD 93.09/98.60；HETERO2NET 为最强基线（IMDB 65.33、MAG 33.28/54.66），HeterSEED 全面领先；RCDD 的 AP 亦居首；多个基线在 MAG/RCDD 上 OOM 或性能骤降，HeterSEED 仍可训练并最优。消融（5 数据集）：去掉结构异配通道(w/o SHC)与异配分支(w/o Hetero)降幅最大，尤其在异配数据集 IMDB/MAG/RCDD；去掉解耦损失、掩码机制也一致小幅掉点；ACM/DBLP 上 w/o Homo 降幅更明显。解耦实验表明性能增益主要来自伪标签引导的结构建模而非标签注入：HGNN-LE（仅标签嵌入）在 IMDB 上只有 47.58/57.80，HeterSEED-NM（去掩码）48.99/58.63（IMDB Macro-F1 掉近 17%），而给 SeHGNN/HINormer+/DiffGraph+ 加同等标签掩码仍不及全文。按局部同配比分层与 SBM 注入的合成 MLH 实验显示，HeterSEED 在所有同配区间最优，低同配区优势最明显（54.13% vs 53.39%），高同配区也不低于最强基线。超参敏感性上 α、β 曲线平滑、鲁棒。

Conclusion: HeterSEED 通过显式"语义—结构解耦"，即把类型/关系感知的异质语义建模与结构驱动的同配—异配分离建模分开并用节点级门控自适应融合，为异质图异配场景提供了比特征相似度驱动 HGNN 更强的假设类并降低异配偏差，实验上在中等与超大规模异质图上稳定领先，异配越强收益越大。局限与未来方向：目前聚焦半监督节点分类且依赖人工指定少量对称元路径，伪标签在极低标注/高噪声场景需引入置信度感知的精化，期望自动元路径发现与选择，并将解耦范式扩展到异质超图。

Abstract: Many real-world heterogeneous graphs exhibit pronounced heterophily, where connected nodes often have dissimilar labels or play different semantic roles. In such settings, standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that aggregate messages along metapaths or meta-relations primarily based on feature similarity can propagate misleading information, since feature similarity may be misaligned with underlying relational semantics. In this paper, we propose HeterSEED, a semantics-structure decoupling framework for heterogeneous graph learning under heterophily. HeterSEED decouples representation learning into a heterogeneous semantic channel that captures type- and relation-aware local semantics and a structure-aware heterophily channel that separates homophilic and heterophilic neighborhoods via pseudo-label-guided partitioning and aggregates them using metapath-based structural weights. A node-level adaptive fusion mechanism then combines the two channels to produce context-dependent node representations. Theoretically, we establish that, on heterogeneous graphs under heterophily, HeterSEED is strictly more expressive than standard heterogeneous graph neural networks that rely primarily on feature similarity and provably reduces the prediction bias introduced by heterophilic neighbors. Experiments on five real-world heterogeneous graphs, including two large-scale networks at the million-node and hundred-million-edge scale, demonstrate that HeterSEED consistently outperforms representative heterogeneous graph neural networks and recent heterophily-aware baselines, especially in strongly heterophilic regimes.

</details>


### [229] [From Video-to-PDE: Data-Driven Discovery of Nonlinear Dye Plume Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04535)
*Cesar Acosta-Minoli,Sayantan Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套"视频到PDE"的分阶段数据驱动流程：从顶视拍摄的墨水羽流灰度视频中构造归一化标量场 u(x,y,t)，用强度加权质心分离整体漂移与本征扩散，通过弱形式稀疏回归（weak-SINDy/STLSQ）识别有效输运律，并经可辨识性诊断、iPINN 系数精修与分块自助 rollout 校准，最终得到 u_t + v(t)·∇u = 9.005|∇u|² + 0.666Δu（黏性 Hamilton–Jacobi 型，可经 Cole–Hopf 化为线性对流–扩散方程），在留出帧上优于对流–扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从视频推断连续介质模型存在两大障碍：记录量是未标定的图像灰度而非物理状态量（如染料浓度），且对含噪帧做直接数值微分会放大像素级波动，导致结构识别与系数标定不可靠。此外，羽流本身存在整体漂移，若不加以解耦，回归会通过其他项补偿漂移失配而破坏质心轨迹；而只以像素级 RMSE 评判模型又无法反映羽流前沿与整体轨迹的准确定性。因此需要一个把"结构发现—系数标定—不确定性评估"分层处理的完整流程。

Method: 1) 数据预处理：背光透明容器顶视拍摄墨滴，裁剪 (450,0,1080,1080)、去边8像素、反相归一化 u = 1 − I_gray/255、重采样到 200×200×1009，高斯平滑 σ=1.0（经敏感性扫描确定）。2) 漂移估计：由强度加权质心 x_c(t), y_c(t) 经 Savitzky–Golay（3阶，窗口约8%帧数）平滑后时间求导，得 v_x(t), v_y(t)（均值 0.0768、0.2784 图像单位/秒），作为规定的对流项。3) 时间顺序划分 60%/20%/20%（训练/验证/测试）。4) 弱形式稀疏回归：采用可分离高斯测试函数 M=2000，k_σ=4，σ_x=σ_y=0.06n，σ_t=0.025n，通过分部积分把导数转移到测试函数上，构造 Θξ≈b，用带岭正则的 STLSQ（λ=1e-3，α=1e-6）求解。5) 可辨识性诊断：条件数 κ(Θ)、归一化列相关矩阵、λ 从 1e-5 到 1e-0.25 的21点阈值扫描、100 次随机测试中心采样（每次 M=1000）统计选择频率与系数均值方差，用以剔除过完备库。6) iPINN 精修：tanh MLP（宽128、深6、sigmoid 输出）表示 u，自动微分求导，仅 a、β 可训练，结构固定、漂移系数固定为1，分预训练（5000步，数据损失）与联合训练（12000步，L = w_d L_data + w_p L_PDE，w_p=1e-3，不加弱系数软先验）。7) 分块自助 rollout 校准：块长 ≈√n_tr，B=50 次重采样，以 rollout MSE 为目标、Nelder–Mead 无导数优化（≤1e5 空间-时间点、≤300 次迭代），给出中位数与 [q0.025,q0.975] 区间；并附加前沿感知目标（像素 + 前沿半径 + 前沿生长项，w_f=5.0, w_g=0.05）且 β 在对数空间强制为正。8) 评估：全段与单步 rollout，相对 RMSE，加上质心误差与多阈值（0.05–0.25）等效前沿半径误差。

Result: 完整库 κ(Θ)≈3.52×10^5 且 1, u, u², |∇u|², u|∇u|² 相互强相关，C-both（κ≈5.12×10²）亦不可辨识；A/C/C-alt 条件数 ≤20，B 达 1.77×10³。随机中心稳定性显示：C 中 |∇u|²、Δu、v_y u_y 100% 被选中（系数 1.00±0.008、−0.124±0.005、−0.049±0.002），C-alt 中 u|∇u|² 与 v_y u_y 稳定但 Δu 从不被选中，故 C 更优。对流模式比较：Mode A（回归自由缩放对流）下 A、B 基线 rRMSE 达 30.4%，Mode B（实测漂移固定为1）为 16.45%；非线性梯度库两模式均约 11%，但 Mode A 下质心 RMSE 近 1.1、Mode B 低于 0.05，故采用 Mode B。系数演化呈明显分层：C 由 (0.7931, −0.1258) 经 iPINN 到 (3.0032, 0.0192)，再经自助 rollout 校准到 (9.005, 0.666)，验证 rRMSE 6.19%，优于对流–扩散基线 16.45%，且 β>0；C-alt 对 rollout 校准不敏感（两种初始化均约 11%）。前沿感知校准得 a≈34.1、β≈0.536，验证 rRMSE 7.58%、前沿 RMSE 更小但质心 RMSE 升至约 0.50，作为几何诊断保留。所选方程为黏性 Hamilton–Jacobi 方程，θ=exp(au/β) 变换给出 θ_t + v(t)·∇θ = βΔθ，继承抛物型最大值原理与指数耗散律，∫u dA 并不守恒（d/dt ∫u = a∫|∇u|² ≥ 0），自然守恒量为指数质量 ∫exp(au/β)dA。

Conclusion: 未标定的视觉数据在把发现、标定与不确定性作为独立阶段处理时，可以产出紧致、可预测且结构可解释的连续介质模型。方法论要点在于：rollout 误差过于宽松，模型选择必须同时考虑条件数/可辨识性、漂移一致性、几何可容许性（β>0）与前沿诊断。该模板可推广到其他"记录量为观测量而非标定量"的成像场景（生物、生态、医学影像），未来方向包括将可容许性约束直接编入发现与标定阶段、以光流或学习型空间变速度场替代均匀漂移、以及在多实验与标定浓度数据上验证可迁移性。

Abstract: Inferring continuum models directly from video is hampered by two facts: the recorded field is uncalibrated image intensity rather than a physical state, and direct numerical differentiation of noisy frames is unstable. We develop a video-to-PDE pipeline that converts grayscale recordings of an ink plume into a normalised scalar field $u(x,y,t)$, isolates a bulk drift $\mathbf{v}(t)$ from intrinsic spreading via the intensity-weighted centroid, and identifies an effective transport law by weak-form sparse regression. Conditioning, threshold-sweep and random-centre diagnostics show that overcomplete libraries are strongly collinear; the search is therefore restricted to compact gradient-based libraries. Coefficients are refined by an inverse physics-informed network and recalibrated against forward rollouts, with a chronological block bootstrap quantifying uncertainty. The selected reduced model $u_t+\mathbf v(t)\!\cdot\!\nabla u = 9.005\,|\nabla u|^{2}+0.666\,Δu$ outperforms advection--diffusion baselines on held-out frames, retains a positive Laplacian coefficient, and admits a Cole--Hopf reduction to a linear advection--diffusion equation. The framework demonstrates that uncalibrated visual data can yield compact, predictive and structurally interpretable continuum models when discovery, calibration and uncertainty are treated as distinct stages.

</details>


### [230] [A Queueing-Theoretic Framework for Stability Analysis of LLM Inference with KV Cache Memory Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04595)
*Chengyi Nie,Nian Si,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出将计算约束与GPU内存约束同时纳入分析框架的排队论模型，用于解决LLM推理服务中的稳定性判定问题。通过理论推导获得稳定与不稳定条件，实验验证预测误差在10%以内，为GPU集群容量规划提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理与传统工作负载不同，同时受限于计算资源和KV缓存带来的GPU内存开销。KV缓存虽能加速解码，但会快速耗尽GPU内存。如何在保证服务稳定性的前提下进行准确的GPU资源配置，是当前部署LLM服务面临的核心挑战。

Method: 建立首个同时考虑计算资源约束和GPU内存约束的排队论分析框架，基于该框架推导LLM推理服务的稳定性与不稳定性条件。

Result: 推导出严格的稳定/不稳定条件，可判断LLM推理服务是否能持续处理传入请求而不出现队列无限增长。该条件可结合预估请求到达率与稳定服务率，计算所需集群规模。

Conclusion: 在真实GPU生产环境中进行广泛实验验证，结果表明预测的稳定性条件具有高度准确性，偏差通常在10%以内。该框架为系统部署提供了有力工具，可同时避免过度采购造成的资源浪费和供应不足导致的性能下降。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has created significant challenges for efficient inference at scale. Unlike traditional workloads, LLM inference is constrained by both computation and the memory overhead of key-value (KV) caching, which accelerates decoding but quickly exhausts GPU memory. In this paper, we introduce the first queueing-theoretic framework that explicitly incorporates both computation and GPU memory constraints into the analysis of LLM inference. Based on this framework, we derive rigorous stability and instability conditions that determine whether an LLM inference service can sustain incoming demand without unbounded queue growth. This result offers a powerful tool for system deployment, potentially addressing the core challenge of GPU provisioning. By combining an estimated request arrival rate with our derived stable service rate, operators can calculate the necessary cluster size to avoid both costly over-purchasing and performance-violating under-provisioning. We further validate our theoretical predictions through extensive experiments in real GPU production environments. Our results show that the predicted stability conditions are highly accurate, with deviations typically within 10%.

</details>


### [231] [Power Distribution Bridges Sampling, Self-Reward RL, and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04542)
*Akiyoshi Tomihari,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了功率分布（power distribution）是连接采样、自奖励KL正则化强化学习和自蒸馏的统一框架。提出了功率自蒸馏方法，可在大幅降低推理成本的同时匹配或超越功率采样的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究质疑强化学习（RL）是否真的是大语言模型（LLM）强推理能力的来源，同时蒸馏和推理时采样（包括功率采样）成为提升LLM性能的有效手段，但三者之间的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 聚焦于功率分布这一功率采样的目标分布，理论证明功率分布是使用模型序列级对数概率作为奖励时的KL正则化RL的闭式最优解。基于此提出功率自蒸馏方法，将功率采样的成本 amortized 到监督训练中。

Result: 从采样角度证明廉价局部近似无法在缺少可能后缀信息的情况下重现序列级功率分布；从RL角度确认功率分布作为最优解的理论基础；实验表明功率采样可提升自奖励，而下游真实奖励的提升取决于真实奖励与自奖励在功率分布下的协方差。

Conclusion: 功率自蒸馏可匹配或超越功率采样性能，同时显著降低推理成本。该研究为理解RL、蒸馏与采样在LLM中的关系提供了统一理论框架。

Abstract: Recent analyses question whether reinforcement learning (RL) is responsible for strong reasoning in large language models (LLMs). At the same time, distillation and inference-time sampling, including power sampling, have emerged as effective ways to improve LLM performance. However, the relationship among RL, distillation, and sampling remains unclear. In this study, we focus on the power distribution, the target distribution of power sampling, and show that the power distribution bridges sampling, self-reward KL-regularized RL, and self-distillation. From the sampling perspective, we show that inexpensive local approximations cannot reproduce sequence-level power without information about possible suffixes. From the RL perspective, the power distribution is the closed-form optimizer of KL-regularized RL when the model's sequence-level log-probabilities are used as the reward. This identification leads to power self-distillation, an offline distillation surrogate that shares the same target distribution and amortizes the cost of power sampling into supervised training on teacher samples. We further show that power self-distillation can achieve self-reward sharpening, while improvement in a downstream true reward is governed by the covariance between true reward and self-reward under the power distribution. Experiments on reasoning tasks support our analysis: power sampling raises self-reward, true-reward gains depend on alignment with self-reward, and power self-distillation can match or exceed the performance of power sampling at much lower inference cost.

</details>


### [232] [Event-Based Early Warning of Vineyard Disease Risk from Environmental Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04548)
*Ivica Dimitrovski,Ivan Kitanovski,Danco Davcev,Slobodan Kalajdziski,Kosta Mitreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于事件的葡萄园病害风险早期预警方法，将传统的日尺度存在性分类改为预测3-7天内疾病风险期的转换。通过引入最小无病间隔避免标签碎片化，并利用湿度、降雨、温度及季节性特征构建输入表示。对比XGBoost、LSTM和TCN模型，结果表明事件驱动的问题表述对环境时序学习至关重要，能更好地支持实际短期预警需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing daily presence classification approaches favor persistence-driven predictions and provide limited support for actionable short-horizon warning; need for timely intervention and sustainable crop protection requires predicting transitions into risk periods rather than merely reproducing temporal persistence.

Method: Reformulated disease prediction as transition detection into annotated disease-risk periods (3-7 day horizon) with minimum disease-free gap to reduce label fragmentation; constructed input representations capturing humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure via cyclic temporal encoding; compared XGBoost, LSTM, and TCN models.

Result: Event-based formulation enables practical short-horizon warning; different models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior.

Conclusion: Event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning for vineyard disease risk prediction; different models show trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior; the study demonstrates the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning.

Abstract: Accurate early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental observations is essential for timely intervention and more sustainable crop protection. However, many existing studies formulate disease prediction as daily presence classification, which can favor persistence-driven predictions and provide only limited support for actionable short-horizon warning. In this paper, we present an event-based approach for early warning of vineyard disease risk from environmental time series and evaluate it through a vineyard case study. Rather than predicting daily disease status, the task is reformulated to predict transitions into annotated disease-risk periods within a future window of 3-7 days. To reduce fragmentation caused by short interruptions in the binary labels, new events are defined only after a minimum disease-free gap. This formulation encourages models to capture environmental precursors associated with upcoming risk periods instead of merely reproducing temporal persistence. Using multi-year agro-meteorological data, we construct input representations that capture humidity dynamics, rainfall accumulation, temperature variability, and seasonal structure through cyclic temporal encoding. We evaluate representative methods from classical machine learning and deep learning, including XGBoost, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks, and Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCNs), using both standard classification metrics and an event-oriented early warning protocol. The results show that the event-based formulation supports practical short-horizon warning, while the compared models exhibit distinct trade-offs between event recall, lead time, and false-alert behavior. Overall, the study underscores the importance of problem formulation in environmental time-series learning and demonstrates the value of event-based prediction for vineyard disease warning systems.

</details>


### [233] [Counter-Dyna: Data-Efficient RL-Based HVAC Control using Counterfactual Building Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04555)
*Jan Marco Ruiz de Vargas,Fabian Raisch,Zoltan Nagy,Pierre Pinson,Christoph Goebel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 **Counter-Dyna**：一种面向 HVAC（供暖/通风/空调）控制的模型基强化学习（MBRL）方法。其核心是用因果不变性把状态空间拆分为「受控制影响的分区温度」与「控制不变量（天气/电价预测、时间）」，构建**反事实代理模型（CSM）**：在模型 rollout 中只预测下一时刻温度、其余分量直接复用真实历史数据。配合「严格按时间顺序、不允许 episode 任意 reset」的现实训练制度，在 BOPTEST bestest_hydronic_heatpump 标准算例上，仅需 **5 周**（此前 SOTA 需 6–12 个月）真实环境交互即可训练出优于 50 周无模型 RL 的策略，假设性部署场景下可节省 **5.3%–17.0%** 运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑占全球 CO₂ 排放约 30%，HVAC 控制是降低能耗成本与移峰填谷的关键。深度强化学习（DRL）虽在理论上可接近成本-舒适-控制平滑度综合最优（>90%），但纯模型-free（MFRL）需要数年甚至数十年交互数据，训练成本高、可能将有人建筑推入不安全状态；模型基 RL（MBRL，如 Dyna 风格）虽能缓解，但仍存在三点未解瓶颈：(1) 数据效率不足——已有 MBRL HVAC 工作（Gao & Wang 2023、Saeed et al. 2024）仍需 46–52 周甚至 6–12 个月的真实交互；(2) 代理模型的预测任务设计不当——通用 MBRL 预测**整个状态空间**（含天气、电价等对动作不敏感的变量），徒增模型偏差并损害 RL 训练，而另一类工作则完全丢弃预测信息，无法支持灵活负荷移峰；(3) 训练环境支持「时间旅行」（episode 可 reset 到任意时间点），与真实部署的时序在线训练不符。此外论文也指出：现有研究普遍只使用 MLP/单一建筑模型，且缺少对反事实动作序列下代理模型精度的验证。

Method: **1) 反事实代理模型（CSM）**。将状态拆为 s_t=(z_t, d_t)，z_t 为分区（建筑）温度，d_t 为扰动/预测量（天气预测、电价预测、时间）。由因果图与 do-算子可知 P(d̃_{l+1}|d̃_l, do(z̃), do(ã)) = P(d̃_{l+1}|d̃_l)，即预测/电价不受热泵启停影响，故在合成 rollout 中直接沿用真实历史扰动 d_{l0+l}^{(n')}，无需预测。预测维度由 dim(s)=19 降至 dim(z)=1：建筑模型 m_ψ 为 MLP（[512,512,512]，LeakyReLU），输入 (z̃_l, d_l, ã_l)，输出**温度变化量**（差分预测）以提升精度；奖励模型 m_β 采用分段线性模型 m_β(a_t, τ_t, a_tτ_t)（a=0 时为 0，否则 β_0+β_1a+β_2τ+β_3aτ），以最小二乘拟合真实数据，从而无需依赖未建模的设备功率数据即可逼近 BOPTEST 奖励（r = -(w_D·R_tdis + w_C·R_cost)，w_D=1, w_C=100）。CSM 的 rollout 本质是对「若采取替代动作序列会如何」的条件干预预测（counterfactual interventional prediction）。

**2) Counter-Dyna 算法（Algorithm 1）**。Dyna 式循环：先与真实环境交互一个 episode（1 周），存入真实缓冲区 D_env；在 D_env ∪ D_model 上用 PPO/SAC 更新策略 π_θ；用 D_env 重新拟合 CSM；再从已收集真实 episode 中均匀采样起点 (n′, l_0)，用当前策略生成 K 条长度 L 的合成 rollout（L≪T 以抑制误差累积），写入 D_model 并再次更新策略。synth:real 数据比 KL/T = 20，rollout 长度 L=24。

**3) 训练制度**。训练 episode 严格按真实时间顺序进行（不可时间旅行），训练在 1 月 17 日前结束，以便在标准测试期（峰值供暖期 1/17–1/31、典型供暖期 4/19–5/3）评估。

**4) 实验设置**。BOPTEST bestest_hydronic_heatpump 算例，换用 2024 年比利时更波动的动态电价；控制步长 60 min，热泵动作 PPO 为离散 {0,1}（SAC 为 [0,1] 连续）；每个实验 30 个随机种子；对照 MF-10、MF-50、BOPTEST PI 基线与 60 min 阈值为 21.5 °C 的 bang-bang 规则控制器；指标为热不舒适（K·h）、面积归一化运行成本（€/m²）及收敛所需真实交互量。另做消融（synth:real ∈ {10,20,30,50,100}，L ∈ {6,12,24,48,96}，w_C 变化）。

Result: **收敛与数据效率**：Counter-Dyna-10 在第 6 个 episode 收敛；Counter-Dyna-5 训练 5 周即超过 MF-50 的渐近性能；MF-50 需约 30 周（约 6 倍时间），MF-10 无收敛迹象且波动大。Counter-Dyna 方差随训练快速收缩，稳定性显著优于无模型基线。

**控制性能（峰值供暖期）**：MF-10/MF-50 的累计热不舒适 <10 K·h，而 Counter-Dyna-5/-10 约 20 K·h（源于其控制目标本身在成本-舒适间权衡），但成本更低；在成本-不舒适散点/箱线图中，Counter-Dyna 在几乎同等不舒适水平下成本低于 MF 变体，且优于 bang-bang 规则控制器（MF 变体则不然）；所有 RL 均远优于 PI 基线。典型供暖期（未见过的春季天气）上 MF-10 反复违反舒适带、泛化最差，Counter-Dyna 依旧成本低且稳定。

**成本节省（假设性部署场景，训练数据量相同的 Counter-Dyna-10 vs MF-10）**：峰值供暖期 5.3%、典型供暖期 17.0%；Counter-Dyna-5 为 3.5% 与 13.2%（仅用一半训练时间）；MF-50 成本与 Counter-Dyna 相当但不舒适更高。

**CSM 精度（1 天 rollout，5 周 Dyna 运行的最终模型）**：样本内动作 RMSE 0.08±0.04 °C、MAE 0.19±0.05 °C；样本外（反事实）动作 RMSE 0.90±0.84 °C、MAE 1.66±2.77 °C；24 h 自回归预测仍很准（每 24 h 用真实状态复位，与 L=24 对齐）。奖励模型 R²=0.98，仅在建筑模型偏离时出现偏差。

**消融**：提高 synth:real 比或 rollout 长度在训练中期提升回报，但 5 个 episode 后各配置回报趋近；无单一配置在成本-不舒适 Pareto 上占优。性能在研究范围内对超参相对不敏感，条件是 synth:real ≥10:1 且 rollout 长度适中（避免误差累积）。SAC 连续动作结果趋势一致但成本更高，因该算例下 bang-bang 优于调节控制。

Conclusion: **贡献**：重新设计了 Dyna 式 MBRL 中的代理建模方式——用因果视角只预测受动作影响的变量，使 rollout 状态在「反事实世界」中依然精确，从而把训练所需的真实交互从 6–12 个月压缩到数周（论文称对 PPO/SAC 等模型-free 算法提速约 10 倍）；同时引入严格时序的现实训练制度，修正了此前工作「时间旅行」的乐观设定；在 BOPTEST 上以大规模仿真验证了稳定性、数据效率与向未见天气型态的泛化能力，部署场景下成本节省 5.3%–17.0% 且热不舒适更低。局限与未来方向：使用了现实中不可得的「完美预测」；仅用 MLP 一种建筑模型架构；episode 边界仍是跳变式重置（现实中可由基线控制器运行若干小时软化，并可加安全约束）；可结合迁移学习加速代理模型初始训练并持续在线更新。另需说明：所提供 arXiv 全文的会议/ISBN/版权元数据为占位符（"Woodstock, NY" 2016），并非真实出版信息。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) offers a promising approach for data-efficient energy management in buildings, combining the strengths of predictive modeling and reinforcement learning. While previous MBRL methods applied to HVAC control have reduced training data requirements, they still require several months of interaction with the building to learn a satisfactory control policy. A key reason is that existing surrogate models attempt to predict the entire state-space, including weather and electricity prices that are unaffected by control actions, or completely ignore these variables. Addressing these issues, we propose Counter-Dyna, a method that enhances the data-efficiency of Dyna, an MBRL method. We create data-efficient counterfactual surrogate models (CSM) by leveraging invariances in the state-space. Using a CSM in Dyna speeds up RL training measured in environment interaction data compared to previous results. In comparison with previous state-of-the-art that used 6-12 months of environment interactions, our method needs only 5 weeks. We evaluate our method in a large simulation study using the literature standard BOPTEST framework and proximal policy algorithm (PPO) as the RL algorithm. Our results show cost-saving potentials of 5.3% to 17.0% in a hypothetical deployment scenario. Our work is a significant step towards making real-world deployment of RL algorithms in HVAC control practically viable.

</details>


### [234] [Library learning with e-graphs on jazz harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04622)
*Zeng Ren,Maddy Bowers,Xinyi Guan,Martin Rohrmeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于库学习的计算模型，用于学习爵士和声模式。模型通过e-graphs上的演绎解析与库学习相结合，从和声进行语料库中联合学习基础模式库和重构程序，从而发现简洁的生成性解释。实验验证了该模型在捕捉人类音乐模式学习直觉性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够获得对音乐模式的高度结构化直觉理解，但这些模式通常需要多次反复聆听和思考才能完全内化。该研究旨在通过计算模型来捕捉这种内化过程，探索计算机是否能像人类一样发现简洁的音乐模式生成解释。

Method: 该模型基于库学习框架，以爵士和声进行语料库为输入，在由原始和声关系组成的程序空间中进行搜索。具体方法包括：首先枚举每个乐曲的可能程序，然后通过e-graphs上的演绎解析与库学习相结合，联合学习 harmonic 模式库和重构后的程序，从而高效地遍历程序与库的巨大联合搜索空间。

Result: 通过评估生成程序和库的直觉性，以及与人类编写的和声推导的相似性，结果显示模型能够生成可理解的程序和库，且在某些方面与人类学习和声的方式存在相似性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，该模型能够捕捉人类音乐模式学习的某些方面，包括程序和库的可理解性，以及与人类编写的和声推导的相似性，验证了基于库学习的计算方法在建模音乐认知过程中的有效性。

Abstract: Humans can acquire a highly structured intuitive understanding of musical patterns, yet these patterns often require multiple iterations of reflection and re-listening to internalize fully. To capture such an internalization process, we present a computational model for the learning of jazz harmonic patterns based on library learning. Given a corpus of harmonic progressions, our model searches over a space of programs composed of primitive harmonic relations in order to discover concise generative explanations of the corpus. The model first enumerates possible programs for each piece, and then jointly learns a library of harmonic patterns and refactored programs. To efficiently navigate the vast joint space of programs and libraries, we integrate deductive parsing with library learning on e-graphs. We explore how well our model captures aspects of human musical pattern learning by evaluating the intuitiveness of both programs and libraries, as well as similarities to human-written harmonic derivations.

</details>


### [235] [Exact Dual Geometry of SOC-ICNN Value Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04722)
*Kang Liu,Jianchen Hu,Wei Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用 SOC-ICNN 作为二阶锥规划（SOCP）精确值函数的性质，从网络内部的对偶乘子直接"读出"完整的次梯度集合与局部 Hessian，并据此提出对偶认证推理（DCI）：把原点投影到 ∂F(x)+N_X(x) 上得到精确的全局最优性证书与切锥内的公共下降方向；在结构非退化内点处退化为 Newton 步，配以近端安全机制后具备全局收敛与局部二次收敛。数值实验显示：在结构边界问题上成功率 100%（对比 Gradient Sampling 68.8%、单分支 0%），病态条件数下迭代次数不增且比标准 Newton 快 7.2–10.4×，电池调度应用中 2 次迭代/3.09 ms 达到 100% 认证求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICNN 的部署需要在其学到的凸目标上做"内层"优化，而非仅前向推理。问题在于：ICNN 目标普遍非光滑（ReLU 边界、零范数残差），自动微分只返回所执行分支上的单个导数，既不能作为最优性证书（例：F(x)=max{x,−2x} 在 x=0 处 AD 给出 g=1，但真实 ∂F(0)=[−2,1]，其负方向不是下降方向），也不能构造可靠搜索方向；约束情形下最优性还联合依赖可行集法锥 N_X(x)。已有非光滑方法（梯度采样、bundle、proximal）需通过采样或通用构造间接获取非光滑信息，成本高且难以给出精确证书。作者的核心问题是：能否从训练好的 SOC-ICNN 内部结构中"白盒"地恢复完整的一阶/二阶几何，并用于可认证的高效推理？

Method: **模型与对偶表示。** SOC-ICNN = ReLU-ICNN 主干（U_ℓ≥0, c≥0）+ H 个二次模块 α_h/2‖B_hx+e_h‖²（α_h>0）+ G 个欧氏范数模块 λ_g‖A_gx+d_g‖（λ_g≥0），保证对 x 凸。引理 10 证明其等价于参数化 SOCP（ReLU 用单纯形锥、二次用旋转锥、范数用 Lorentz 锥）且前向传播状态即最优原始解。对偶化后得 f(x)=max_{ξ∈K_ν×P×K_r} Ψ(x;ξ)（命题 2），ξ=(ν,p,r) 分别为 ReLU/二次/范数乘子，读出映射 𝒢(ξ)=v+Σ W_ℓᵀν_ℓ+ΣB_hᵀp_h+ΣA_gᵀr_g 给出输入空间支撑斜率。

**完整最优对偶集的分块刻画（命题 3）。** 直接由前向状态判定：二次乘子唯一 p_h*=α_h q_h(x)；范数乘子在 u_g≠0 时唯一（λ_g û_g），在 u_g=0 时为整个欧氏球 {‖r_g‖≤λ_g}；ReLU 乘子按 a_{ℓ,i}<0 置 0、a_{ℓ,i}>0 递归取上界、a_{ℓ,i}=0 时取区间 [0, 上界]，其中上界由后续层递归耦合。𝒟*(x) 非空、紧、凸；其规模仅由当前结构边界决定。

**精确一阶几何（定理 4）。** ∂f(x)=𝒢(𝒟*(x))，且 f'(x;d)=max_{ξ∈𝒟*(x)}𝒢(ξ)ᵀd；f 可微当且仅当 𝒢(𝒟*(x)) 为单点。用 Danskin 定理（通过局部紧化处理无界二次乘子空间 P）；由支撑函数相等推出集合相等，无需额外取凸包。命题 5 给出 x↦∂f(x) 的闭图与局部有界性。推论 15 给出块状 Minkowski 和分解，推论 14 给出唯一的最小范数"典范"乘子（但注意：最小化乘子范数≠最小化输入空间斜率范数）。

**显式二阶几何（命题 6）。** 定义结构非退化：所有 ReLU 预激活非零、正权范数残差非零。此时 ReLU 模式局部固定，𝒟*(x) 为单点，f 局部 C²：∇f=ā+Σα_h B_hᵀq_h+Σλ_g A_gᵀû_g；∇²f=Σα_h B_hᵀB_h + Σλ_g A_gᵀ(I−û_gû_gᵀ)/‖u_g‖ A_g ⪰ 0，且在足够小球上 Lipschitz（二次项常值，范数项提供切向曲率、径向曲率为零）。

**DCI 算法（定理 7、命题 8、算法 1）。** 在正定度量 M 下将原点 M^{-1}-投影到 C_X(x)=∂F(x)+N_X(x)：min_{ξ∈𝒟*(x), n∈N_X(x)} ½‖𝒢(ξ)+∇Φ(x)+n‖²_{M^{-1}}。因 C_X(x) 非空闭凸，唯一性成立；残差 ℛ_M(x)=‖g†_M‖_{M^{-1}}，有 ℛ_M=0 ⟺ x 为约束全局最小点；否则 d_M=−M^{-1}g†_M 属于切锥 T_X(x) 且对所有 g∈∂F(x) 满足 gᵀd_M ≤ −ℛ_M²（公共下降），强凸时还有目标间隙界 F(x)−F* ≤ ℛ_M²/(2μ_M)。投影问题经消元后只剩 |Z_R(x)|+Σ_{g∈Z_N}k_g+q_X(x) 个标量变量（凸二次 + 递归区间约束 + 欧氏球 + 法锥锥），实现"边界局部"低维求解；光滑区取 M=H_k=∇²F 退化为 Newton 步，H_k 不可用时可用正则化/截断度量。迭代：回缩方向经 retraction（Euclidean 投影）得到可行点，按"方向预测 + Armijo"双条件接受（有限预算 J_max）；若全部失败则用度量近端步 (F+γ^{-1}/2‖z−x_k‖²_M) 作为安全机制。

**收敛性（定理 9）。** 全局：F 单调不增、迭代留在紧水平集内，分"无限次接受局部步"与"最终全用近端步"两种情形，用闭图性（引理 23）推出所有聚点全局最优，唯一解时全序列收敛。局部：内点 + 结构非退化 + ∇²F(x*)≻0 且局部 Lipschitz + retraction 局部精确 + M_k=∇²F(x_k) 时，最终接受单位步且 ‖x_{k+1}−x*‖ ≤ (L_H/2μ_H)‖x_k−x*‖²（二次收敛）。

Result: **理论结论。** (i) ∂f(x)=𝒢(𝒟*(x))、f'(x;d)=max_{ξ∈𝒟*}𝒢(ξ)ᵀd、f 可微 ⟺ 单点；(ii) 结构非退化区的显式梯度/Hessian（PSD、Lipschitz）；(iii) 投影残差给出精确的约束全局最优性证书与切锥公共下降方向；(iv) DCI 全局收敛，内点结构非退化处局部二次收敛；(v) 附加计算量仅与当前边界结构（而非整个提升表示）相关。

**实验（5 组）。** ①几何校验：梯度误差均值 2.12e-15、Hessian 误差 4.12e-16、二阶 Taylor 残差 log–log 斜率 3.006、混合边界方向读数与单侧差分一致到 2.37e-8，5000 条全局支撑不等式无违反。②结构边界推理（24 目标 × 2 初始化 = 48 次）：DCI-I/DCI-C/Lifted-PD 均 100% 成功，Gradient Sampling 68.8% 且 12.5% 错误停机，Single-Branch 与 L-BFGS 0%。③病态条件数（κ 至 1e4，130 目标）：DCI-C 在两个族上恒为 1/2 次迭代，与 Canonical-Newton 迭代数相同但中位运行时间快 7.2×/10.4×，AD-GD/AD-L-BFGS 随 κ 显著变慢。④约束几何与安全机制（95 目标，box/ball/simplex/SOC/无约束）：DCI-C-Full 100%、DCI-C-Local 94.7%、DCI-I-Local 63.2%、AD-PGD 49.5%、Readout-PGD 60.0%；Prox-Only 100% 但中位时间为 DCI-C-Full 的 4.83×；默认 J_max=10 时仅 5/95 调用安全机制；联合残差在所有最优点 ≤3.33e-8，而仅网络残差在结构/联合/应力最优处均值 1.123。⑤电池调度（瑞典 SE3，90 个冻结目标 + 363 测试日）：DCI-C 100% 成功、中位 2 次迭代/3.09 ms、残差 1.18e-6，而 Lifted-PD 需 537.5 次迭代/241 ms（约 78×）；端到端遗憾上 SOC-ICNN 为 2.1733，显著劣于 Point Forecast 0.0054 与 Historical SAA 0.0104，说明主导误差来自代理模型近似而非 DCI 推理。Bootstrap 显示 SOC-ICNN 成本显著高于 Quad-ICNN（0.153，p=0.009）与各显式基线。

Conclusion: **贡献总结。** 论文给出了 SOC-ICNN 一阶/二阶几何的"对偶精确"刻画，并把它转化为可认证的白盒推理引擎 DCI：一次前向传播 + 边界局部的低维凸投影即可同时得到最优性证书与下降方向，摆脱了对自动微分分支选择的依赖；理论层面兼顾全局收敛与 Newton 级局部二次收敛，实验层面在结构边界、病态条件数与真实调度任务上均显著优于 AD 基线与通用非光滑方法。

**方法学要点与边界。** 关键洞察是"非光滑性只集中在当前活跃的结构边界上"，因此无需枚举分支或提升为大规模锥规划；此外提醒了乘子范数最小 ≠ 输入空间斜率范数最小，故证书必须在输入空间构造。

**局限与可改进方向（作者与读者可讨论）。** (1) 全局收敛证明依赖近端子问题精确求解与 ε=0，实验用的是近似求解与数值容差（活动容差 1e-7、投影容差 1e-11 等），理论与实现之间存在差距；(2) 边界局部投影仍不可忽略：微基准显示投影耗时 0.5–1.9 ms 且随输入维数/边界规模增长，是主要瓶颈，限制了大规模实际部署；(3) 几何恢复依赖 SOC-ICNN 的结构化凸架构与正权二次/范数模块，对更一般的 ICNN 或含非凸/饱和结构的网络不适用；(4) 端到端调度实验暴露出代理模型质量才是主要瓶颈（ICNN 遗憾远高于点预测与 SAA 基线），说明"推理精确"并不等于"决策有价值"；(5) 作者指出未来可将 DCI 与拟牛顿、主动集等混合策略结合，以进一步提升实际收敛性并推广到更广模型类。

**合规说明。** 原文含作者单位等信息，属正常学术元数据，不涉及受限内容。

Abstract: Input Convex Neural Networks (ICNNs) are commonly used in a two-stage manner: one first trains a convex network and then minimizes it over its input in a downstream inference problem. Recent second-order-cone ICNNs (SOC-ICNNs) enrich ReLU-based ICNNs with quadratic and conic modules and admit an exact representation as value functions of second-order cone programs (SOCPs). This value-function structure enables an explicit convex-analytic treatment of SOC-ICNN inference. In this paper, we study the exact first-order and local second-order geometry of SOC-ICNNs from the dual viewpoint. We show that supporting slopes, subdifferentials, directional derivatives, and local Hessians can be recovered directly from optimal dual variables. These results provide the geometric primitives for white-box SOC-ICNN inference, going beyond black-box automatic differentiation. Numerical experiments validate the exact multiplier readout, the local Hessian formula, and the set-valued behavior at structurally degenerate inputs. We also provide a step-by-step tutorial showing how the readout mechanism instantiates a complete white-box inference loop. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SOC-ICNN-Theory-BEFC/.

</details>


### [236] [Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement for Incentivizing Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04747)
*Leon Witt,Togrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Wojciech Samek,Lucy Klinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出知识无关的关联协议(KFCA)方法，用于联邦学习中的客户端贡献奖励，无需依赖真实标签、公开测试集或分布知识。在分类报告和诚实多数假设下，KFCA严格满足真实性，解决了传统关联协议(CA)的标签翻转漏洞。实验验证了其在联邦学习LLM适配器调优和PCB检测任务中的有效性，适合去中心化和区块链激励设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current federated learning incentive mechanisms rely on ground truth, public test sets, or distribution knowledge, which are often unavailable. Additionally, Correlated Agreement (CA) is vulnerable to label-flipping attacks, compromising its reliability for rewarding client contributions.

Method: The paper introduces Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA), which achieves strict truthfulness under categorical reports and honest majority assumptions. This approach eliminates the need for ground truth, public test sets, or distribution knowledge.

Result: KFCA was evaluated on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, demonstrating efficient real-time reward computation suitable for practical deployment.

Conclusion: KFCA provides a robust solution for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs in federated learning, addressing both practical deployment needs and security vulnerabilities of previous approaches.

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge-Free Correlated Agreement (KFCA) to reward client contributions in federated learning (FL) without relying on ground truth, a public test set, or distribution knowledge. Under categorical reports and an honest majority, KFCA is strictly truthful, addressing the label-flipping vulnerability of Correlated Agreement (CA). We evaluate KFCA on federated LLM adapter tuning and a real-world PCB inspection task, showing efficient real-time reward computation suitable for decentralized and blockchain-based incentive designs.

</details>


### [237] [Threshold-Guided Optimization for Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04653)
*Jinbin Bai,Yu Lei,Qingyu Shi,Aosong Feng,Yi Xin,Zhuoran Zhao,Fei Shen,Kaidong Yu,Jason Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于阈值引导的对齐框架，通过将对齐问题转化为二分类决策任务，直接从标量评分进行优化，避免了对成对标注数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成对偏好优化的对齐方法需要标注偏好对，但实际场景中反馈往往以独立标量评分形式收集，这限制了方法的可扩展性。

Method: 1) 重新审视KL正则化对齐目标，证明最优策略隐式地将每个样本的奖励与实例级基线比较；2) 用数据驱动的全局阈值替代原有难以计算的oracle基线；3) 引入置信度加权项，强调偏离阈值较大的样本。

Result: 在扩散模型和掩码生成范式上，跨越三个测试集和五个奖励模型的实验表明，该方法在偏好对齐上始终优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 阈值引导框架是一种简单而原则性的方法，可无需成对比较即可实现视觉生成模型的对齐。

Abstract: Aligning large visual generative models with human feedback is often performed through pairwise preference optimization. While such approaches are conceptually simple, they fundamentally rely on annotated pairs, limiting scalability in settings where feedback is collected as independent scalar ratings. In this work, we revisit the KL-regularized alignment objective and show that the optimal policy implicitly compares each sample's reward to an instance-specific baseline that is generally intractable. We propose a threshold-guided alignment framework that replaces this oracle baseline with a data-driven global threshold estimated from empirical score statistics. This formulation turns alignment into a binary decision task on unpaired data, enabling effective optimization directly from scalar feedback. We also incorporate a confidence weighting term to emphasize samples whose scores deviate strongly from the threshold, improving sample efficiency. Experiments across both diffusion and masked generative paradigms, spanning three test sets and five reward models, show that our method consistently improves preference alignment over previous methods. These results position our threshold-guided framework as a simple yet principled alternative for aligning visual generative models without paired comparisons.

</details>


### [238] [Cognitive Twins: Investigating Personalized Thinking Model Building and Its Performance Enhancement with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04761)
*Wu-Yuin Hwang,Nur Alif Ilyasa,Muhammad Irfan Luthfi,Yuniar Indrihapsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了个性化思维模型(PTM)，一个用于AI辅助教育的分层、可解释的学习者表征模型。该模型将学习者日志中的证据组织为五个层级（行为实例→行为模式→认知惯例→元认知倾向→自我系统价值观），并通过大语言模型、句子嵌入、降维和共识聚类等技术构建。40名参与者为期七周的研究表明，PTM在自动评估（F1=74.57%-75.48%）、用户评估（Likert量表均分4.26-4.30）和语义对齐验证（主题一致性从0.436提升至0.626）三个维度均达到可接受的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助教育需要深入理解和建模个体学习者的思维过程，以提供真正个性化的支持。现有方法缺乏对学习者认知结构的系统性表征，因此研究目标是"克隆学习者的思维模型，构建认知孪生"，为个性化教育干预提供理论基础。

Method: 研究采用混合方法设计：(1) PTM基于Marzano新的教育目标分类学构建，包含五层结构；(2) 构建pipeline：Gemini 2.5 Pro推理→句子嵌入→降维→共识聚类；(3) 评估阶段：40名参与者、7周研究周期，通过原子信息点匹配F1分数（自动评估）、Likert量表评分（用户评估）、主题一致性和词汇重叠度分析（语义对齐验证）三种方法检验保真度。

Result: 三项评估结果一致支持PTM有效性：①自动评估显示HITL前F1=74.57%，优化后达75.48%；②用户评估5点量表均值从4.26提升至4.30，表明用户认可模型反映其思维；③语义分析显示层级越深，主题一致性越高(0.436→0.626)，词汇重叠越低(0.114→0.007)，证实语义抽象层级模式。

Conclusion: PTM能够产生具有可接受保真度的输出，被用户普遍认为反映了其思维方式，且层级结构呈现符合预期的语义抽象模式。五层递进结构有效捕捉了从具体行为到抽象价值观的学习者认知特征，为构建认知孪生和个性化教育支持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the Personalized Thinking Model (PTM), a hierarchical and interpretable learner representation designed for AI supported education. PTM organizes evidence from learner journals into a five-layer structure covering behavioral instances, behavioral patterns, cognitive routines, metacognitive tendencies, and self-system values. PTM is grounded in Marzano's New Taxonomy of Educational Objectives and tries to clone learner's thinking model and build cognitive twin. It was constructed using a pipeline that combines large language model inference (Gemini 2.5 Pro), sentence embeddings, dimensionality reduction, and consensus clustering. This paper evaluates PTM fidelity through three methods applied to 40 participants in a seven-week study. First, automatic evaluation using atomic information point matching yielded an overall F1 score of 74.57% before human-in-the-loop (HITL) refinement and 75.48% after refinement. Second, user evaluation using a Likert scale produced mean ratings of 4.26 and 4.30 on a five-point scale for pre and post-HITL conditions respectively. Third, semantic alignment verification showed that topic coherence increased from 0.436 at the behavioral layer to 0.626 at the core value layer, while lexical overlap with journal vocabulary decreased from 0.114 to 0.007 across those same layers. These results suggest that the PTM produces outputs with acceptable fidelity, was generally perceived by users as reflecting their thinking, and showed a pattern consistent with semantic abstraction across layers.

</details>


### [239] [Evidence-based anomaly detection in clinical domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04664)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Branislav Kveton,Shyam Visweswaran,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于概率模型（主要是从历史患者病例数据库学习得到的贝叶斯网络）的新型概率异常检测方法，用于评估针对特定患者的管理决策，并识别相对于具有相同或相似状况的其他患者而言"高度不寻常"的决策；方法已应用于术后心脏（重症）患者管理决策的异常识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上，针对单个患者的治疗/管理决策往往依赖医生的经验与直觉，难以量化其相对同病情患者群体的"罕见性"。同时，医疗质量审查与风险预警需要一种可解释、能够结合不确定性的手段来发现异常决策模式。作者希望利用数据驱动的方式，为每一位患者的管理决策提供统计意义上的参照：即用同类患者的历史病例建立概率模型，从而判断某个决策在给定患者条件下是否异常，从而辅助评估管理决策的合理性。

Method: 方法的核心是构造"概率性异常检测"统计量：先从历史患者病例数据库中学习概率图模型（如贝叶斯网络），用于表示患者状态变量与后续管理决策（如用药、监护、干预措施等）之间的条件依赖关系；然后针对某一具体患者及其候选管理决策，计算基于模型的条件概率/似然，并据此构造异常分数（可理解为相对同类患者的决策分布的罕见程度，可能包含对模型参数不确定性的处理）。通过比较同一患者在不同可选决策下的异常程度，可对管理决策进行排序与评估；通过将该分数与相似状况患者群体（相同或相似条件）对比，可判定哪些决策"高度不寻常"。应用场景为术后心脏（心脏手术/心脏重症）患者的管理决策异常识别。

Result: 论文开发并考察了一组新的概率异常检测方法，能够在贝叶斯网络等概率模型基础上，为单个患者量化并评估其管理决策的异常性（即相对于相同或相似状况患者群体的罕见程度），并成功应用于术后心脏患者管理决策异常的识别任务，验证了该类方法在临床决策支持/质量审查中的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于概率模型与贝叶斯网络的异常检测可作为评估临床管理决策的实用工具：它能以概率化、可解释的方式指出某一患者的哪些决策在同类患者背景下显得异常，从而支持决策质量评估、风险预警与临床决策支持；但其效果依赖于所学习概率模型的质量、特征表示与相似患者的定义方式。

Abstract: Anomaly detection methods can be very useful in identifying interesting or concerning events. In this work, we develop and examine new probabilistic anomaly detection methods that let us evaluate management decisions for a specific patient and identify those decisions that are highly unusual with respect to patients with the same or similar condition. The statistics used in this detection are derived from probabilistic models such as Bayesian networks that are learned from a database of past patient cases. We apply our methods to the problem of identifying unusual patient-management decisions in post-surgical cardiac patients.

</details>


### [240] [Feature importance analysis for patient management decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04666)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究分析4486名心脏外科术后患者的电子健康记录，旨在识别影响医生开具检验单和处方决策的临床特征。研究涵盖335项检验决策和407项用药决策，发现医生决策可由少数关键特征子集较好地预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解哪些临床数据特征最影响医生的诊疗决策，对于优化临床决策支持系统、提高医疗效率、减少不必要检查具有重要意义。

Method: 基于电子健康记录数据，采用预测建模方法分析医生决策行为，统计检验和用药决策的特征重要性。

Result: 医生开检验单和用药决策可由全部特征的较小子集较好地预测。

Conclusion: 临床决策主要受少数关键特征影响，这一发现为开发精简高效的临床决策支持工具提供了依据。

Abstract: The objective of this paper is to understand what characteristics and features of clinical data influence physician's decision about ordering laboratory tests or prescribing medications the most. We conduct our analysis on data and decisions extracted from electronic health records of 4486 post-surgical cardiac patients. The summary statistics for 335 different lab order decisions and 407 medication decisions are reported. We show that in many cases, physician's lab-order and medication decisions can be well predicted from a small subset of all features.

</details>


### [241] [ITBoost: Information-Theoretic Trust for Robust Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04671)
*Ye Su,Longlong Zhao,Diego Garcia-Gil,Jipeng Guo,Gangchun Zhang,Jinxin Chen,Jinsong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ITBoost，通过MDL原理分析残差轨迹复杂度来评估样本可靠性，对不可靠标签的样本进行降权，从而提升梯度提升在标签噪声环境下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升在处理表格数据时表现优异，但当训练标签存在噪声时性能会显著下降。这是因为boosting算法会强调具有大梯度的样本，但无法区分这些误差是来自信息丰富的困难样本还是来自不可靠的标签。

Method: ITBoost通过追踪每个样本残差在迭代过程中的演变轨迹，利用最小描述长度(MDL)原理来衡量残差轨迹的复杂度。残差模式波动不规律的样本被视为不可靠样本，在学习过程中被降权处理。

Result: ITBoost在各种表格数据基准测试中展现出改进的噪声环境鲁棒性，优于主流boosting和深度表格模型，同时在干净数据上保持最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于信息论的样本可信度评估机制，ITBoost实现了更严格的标签噪声泛化界，并在理论和实践层面验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Gradient boosting remains a strong and widely used method for tabular data learning, but its performance often degrades when training labels are noisy. This behavior is largely related to the way boosting algorithms emphasize samples with large gradients, without explicitly accounting for whether such errors originate from informative hard cases or from unreliable labels. We address this issue by reconsidering how sample reliability is evaluated during boosting. Instead of relying on instantaneous error, we examine the evolution of each sample's residuals across iterations. Based on this insight, we propose Information-Theoretic Trust Boosting (ITBoost), which uses the Minimum Description Length principle to measure the complexity of residual trajectories. Samples whose residual patterns fluctuate in an irregular manner are treated as less trustworthy and are down-weighted during learning. Theoretically, we derive a tighter generalization bound for ITBoost under label noise. Empirical results on various tabular benchmarks indicate that ITBoost provides improved robustness in noisy environments over leading boosting and deep tabular models, while retaining best average performance on clean data.

</details>


### [242] [Quantile-Free Uncertainty Quantification in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04847)
*Soyoung park,Hwanjun Song,Sungsu Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 QpiGNN（Quantile-free Prediction Interval GNN），用「双头 GNN + 无分位数的覆盖-宽度联合损失」在图神经网络节点级回归中端到端学习预测区间：既不需要分位数水平输入，也不需要重采样或事后校准，直接用标签监督优化经验覆盖率与区间宽度。理论上给出渐近覆盖一致性（WLLN）、有限样本集中不等式（Hoeffding/McDiarmid，图依赖残差 δ_G）以及覆盖约束下的近最优宽度（对称条件下 d*=F^{-1}(1−α/2)，拉格朗日松弛解释）与收敛到驻点的保证。在 19 个合成+真实基准上，相比 SQR-GNN、RQR-GNN、CF-GNN、BayesianNN、MC Dropout 等基线，平均覆盖率提高 22%、区间宽度缩减 50%，并在特征/目标噪声、边随机丢弃、非可交换划分与结构迁移下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据上的不确定性量化（UQ）在医疗、司法等高风险领域至关重要，但现有路线均有明显缺陷：(1) 贝叶斯方法（BayesianNN、Evidential Regression）可扩展性差且对先验敏感；(2) 集成与 MC Dropout 需多次前向推理、训练/推理成本高；(3) 共形预测（CF-GNN）虽有无分布覆盖保证，但依赖可交换性假设，而图的结构依赖、枢纽节点与异质性常使其失效，还需独立校准集与额外网络模块；(4) 分位数回归（QR/SQR/RQR）需显式分位数水平 τ 作为输入或为每个分位数单独训练模型，存在分位数交叉问题，且与消息传递结合时会产生过平滑、全局化、缺乏节点级自适应性的区间。RQR 等直接预测上下界的方案在 GNN 中也因单头设计将中心与散度纠缠而校准失败。因此需要一个不依赖强假设、无需后处理、计算可行且能给出节点级自适应校准区间的 GNN 不确定性量化框架。

Method: 问题形式化：为每个节点学习 f(v)=[ŷ_low, ŷ_up]，要求 P(y_low ≤ y ≤ y_up) ≥ 1−α 同时最小化期望区间宽度。
1) 双头架构：GNN 编码器（实验中为 2 层 GraphSAGE，隐层 64）产出节点嵌入 H；预测头 ŷ = W_pred H + b_pred 负责点预测，宽度头 d̂ = Softplus(W_diff H + b_diff) 负责半宽（保证 > 0）；区间为 [ŷ−d̂, ŷ+d̂]。解耦两支避免表示冲突与梯度干扰，并缓解过平滑。
2) 无分位数联合损失：L_total = (ĉ − (1−α))² + ℓ̂_viol + λ_width·E_v[ŷ_up − ŷ_low]，其中 ĉ 为经验覆盖率，ℓ̂_viol 为越界幅度惩罚（对低于下界/高于上界的样本按偏离量加权），宽度项采用 L1 形式（对重尾/离群更稳健）。全部仅用标签监督，无需 τ 输入、无需校准集、无需重采样。λ_width 用贝叶斯优化逐数据集选择（最优值 0.19–0.50）。
3) 理论：(P4.1/C.1) 在噪声有界且弱相依、预测与半宽依概率收敛、嵌入足够多样化的条件下，由 WLLN 得 ĉ → 1−α（概率收敛）；(C.2/C.3) 有限样本下 Hoeffding 型界 |ĉ−E[ĉ]| ≤ sqrt(log(2/δ)/(2N))，McDiarmid 型界在图依赖常数 δ_G 下成立，单节点扰动影响 ≤ 1/N + δ_G，从而偏差 O(1/√N)；(C.4) 对称条件分布下最优宽度为 d* = F_v^{-1}(1−α/2)，联合损失可视为约束问题的拉格朗日松弛；(C.5) 非凸但连续分段光滑，配合递减步长收敛到一阶驻点。
4) 实验：19 个数据集（9 个合成：Basic/Gaussian/Uniform/Outlier/Edge/BA/ER/Grid/Tree；10 个真实：美国县域 Education/Election/Income/Unemployment、Twitch/Chameleon/Crocodile/Squirrel、Anaheim/Chicago），目标覆盖 1−α=0.90，Adam 500 epoch，5–10 次重复；基线含 SQR-GNN、RQR_adj-GNN（作者额外加排序惩罚以避免分位数交叉）、CF-GNN 及其调优版、BayesianNN、MC Dropout；指标 PICP/MPIW 及 MPE、Sharpness、WS、CWC、NMPIW；另做噪声/边丢弃鲁棒性、degree/community 非可交换划分、结构迁移（跨图型 OOD）、消融、λ 敏感性与计算效率分析。

Result: 1) 主结果：QpiGNN（λ^opt）在 19 个数据集上平均覆盖率较基线提升约 22%、区间宽度缩减约 50%。合成图上 λ^opt 多数情形同时达到目标覆盖且 MPIW 最低；真实图上 λ=0.5 最窄但有时欠覆盖，λ=0.1 近乎完美覆盖但区间宽，λ^opt 折中最佳（如 Chameleon、Squirrel）。基线对比：BayesianNN 覆盖 1.00 但宽度约 3.0（极保守）；MC Dropout 区间窄但覆盖不可靠（部分数据集仅 0.3–0.6）；SQR-GNN 在异配图上崩溃/不稳定；RQR_adj-GNN 区间全局过平滑且校准差；CF-GNN 校准尚可但宽度大、且在部分数据集（如 ER）出现 MPIW 高达 68 的异常。
2) 鲁棒性：特征噪声（σ=0.1–0.3）下 QpiGNN 保持 PICP≈0.89–0.92 且宽度适中，优于 SQR-GNN（欠覆盖）与 RQR（不稳定）；目标噪声下自适应扩张区间（σ=0.3 时 PICP=1.00、MPIW≈1.05）；边丢弃 0.2→0.6 时覆盖率由 0.84 升至 0.91 而宽度保持紧致。
3) 非可交换划分：random/degree/community 三种划分下均保持 0.94–1.00 覆盖，degree 划分区间变宽、community 划分更窄但仍有效，说明不依赖严格可交换性。
4) 结构迁移：在 BA/ER 等表达力强的图上训练泛化最好（PICP 多数 ≥0.89），在 Basic/Tree 上训练则迁移很差（basic→BA 的 PICP 仅 0.10），反映源图结构丰富度与覆盖-宽度权衡。
5) 消融：双头+可学习宽度最优；固定 margin（0.05/0.10）与单头上下界分离版本严重欠覆盖；仅覆盖项 → 覆盖 1.00 但区间过宽；仅宽度项 → 区间全部塌缩为 0；MSE 中心损失为 sanity check。λ 敏感性显示 PICP 在 λ≲0.5 时稳定 ≥0.96，之后 PICP 与 MPIW 同步快速退化，存在临界点；L2 宽度惩罚比 L1 产生明显更宽区间（重尾放大）。
6) 效率：与 SQR/RQR 同为 O(Ed+Nd²)，训练时间与内存接近轻量基线，低于 CF-GNN（含校准子网，双倍时间）、优于 MC Dropout/BayesianNN 的采样开销；附加指标 MPE/CWC/WS/Sharpness 上 QpiGNN 亦优于基线。
7) 局限：理论依赖理想化假设（节点近独立、条件分布对称、噪声有界），实际图中的弱相依、偏斜/重尾、模型偏差、密集图上的有界差分条件仅近似成立；λ 需逐数据集调参，精确覆盖率控制仍困难（基线 SQR/RQR 对目标覆盖不敏感）；QpiGNN 在 10 个真实数据集中 7 个上最优；部分数据集（Twitch、Chameleon、Squirrel 等 λ=0.5 时 PICP 仅 0.5–0.6）覆盖仍偏低；文中的 ER-GNN 基线被提及但未给出结果，附录 D 中"OpiGNN"疑为笔误。

Conclusion: QpiGNN 证明在图回归中可以不通过显式分位数、重采样或事后共形校准，而是用「双头解耦 + 覆盖率-宽度解耦的联合损失」以单次前向、端到端地获得既校准又紧致的节点级预测区间；其渐近覆盖一致性与 O(1/√N) 有限样本偏差界为实践中的可靠性提供了理论依据。实验在 19 个基准上验证了其在覆盖-宽度权衡、噪声与结构鲁棒性、非可交换划分以及计算开销上的优势。后续方向包括：向图级回归与链接预测扩展、区分偶然与认知不确定性、面向偏斜分布的非对称区间构造、λ 的自适应（退火/元学习）调参、面向演化图的时间依赖建模，以及在图依赖混合条件下的更严格集中不等式理论。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) in graph neural networks (GNNs) is crucial in high-stakes domains but remains a significant challenge. In graph settings, message passing often relies on strong assumptions such as exchangeability, which are rarely satisfied in practice. Moreover, achieving reliable UQ typically requires costly resampling or post-hoc calibration. To address these issues, we introduce Quantile-free Prediction Interval GNN (QpiGNN), a framework that builds on quantile regression (QR) to enable GNN-based UQ by directly optimizing coverage and interval width without requiring quantile inputs or post-processing. QpiGNN employs a dual-head architecture that decouples prediction and uncertainty, and is trained with label-only supervision through a quantile-free joint loss. This design allows efficient training and yields robust prediction intervals, with theoretical guarantees of asymptotic coverage and near-optimal width under mild assumptions. Experiments on 19 synthetic and real-world benchmarks show QpiGNN achieves average 22\% higher coverage and 50\% narrower intervals than baselines, while ensuring efficiency and robustness to noise and structural shifts.

</details>


### [243] [HEXST: Hexagonal Shifted-Window Transformer for Spatial Transcriptomics Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04682)
*Keunho Byeon,Jin Tae Kwak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEXST是一种几何对齐的Transformer模型，用于从组织学图像预测空间基因表达。该方法直接在六边形spot坐标上操作，利用移位窗口注意力和六边形旋转位置编码进行局部到全局上下文建模，并通过对比敏感差分目标和单细胞基础模型先验增强基因水平空间对比度。在7个数据集上验证，性能优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组学成本高、通量低，计算方法虽可从H&E染色组织学图像推断空间基因表达，但大多数模型假设笛卡尔或几何无关的邻域关系，忽略了广泛使用的spot阵列平台的六边形采样特性，且逐点回归目标导致基因表达谱过度平滑，掩盖了基因特异性空间异质性。

Method: HEXST直接在六边形spot坐标上运行，通过定制的移位窗口注意力机制和六边形旋转位置编码实现高效的局部到全局上下文建模；结合对比敏感差分目标和预训练单细胞基础模型的转录组先验知识增强基因水平的空间对比度。

Result: 在7个空间转录组数据集上，HEXST一致性地优于现有最先进模型，能够提供准确、稳健的空间基因表达预测，同时保留基因水平的对比度和空间异质性。

Conclusion: HEXST在7个空间转录组数据集上表现最优，提供了准确且稳健的空间基因表达预测，同时保留了基因水平的对比度和空间异质性。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics offers spatially resolved gene expression profiling within tissue sections, but its cost and limited throughput hinder large-scale deployment. To extend this capability to routine practice, recent computational methods aim to infer spatial gene expression directly from ubiquitous hematoxylin and eosin-stained histology slides. However, most existing models assume Cartesian or geometry-agnostic locality, despite the hexagonal sampling of widely used spot-array platforms, and point-wise regression objectives often yield over-smoothed gene expression profiles, obscuring gene-specific spatial heterogeneity. To address these, we propose HEXST, a geometry-aligned Transformer for spatial gene expression prediction from histology. HEXST operates directly on hexagonal spot coordinates to enable efficient local-to-global contextual modeling via tailored shifted-window attention mechanism and hexagonal rotary positional encoding. To enhance gene-wise spatial contrast, HEXST complements point-wise regression with a contrast-sensitive differential objective and transcriptomic priors from a pretrained single-cell foundation model during training. Across seven spatial transcriptomics datasets, HEXST consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, providing accurate and robust spatial gene expression predictions while preserving gene-wise contrast and spatial heterogeneity.

</details>


### [244] [Learning Time-Inhomogeneous Markov Dynamics in Financial Time Series via Neural Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04690)
*Jan Rovirosa,Jesse Schmolze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"神经参数化"框架，用神经网络作为纯参数化引擎生成显式的、时变的马尔可夫随机算子（转移矩阵），从而在高分辨率、高噪声的金融收益率数据上绕过经验计数估计的稀疏性崩溃，同时完整保留经典马尔可夫算子的可解释性（行异质性、行熵、Dobrushin 系数、Chapman–Kolmogorov 一致性）。实证（JPM 日频，n=55 分位分箱）表明：99.9% 的转移单元观测数少于 5 次；状态条件模型平均行异质性 ρ̄=0.0073（峰值 0.014），状态无关消融基线恒为 0；行熵与 21 日已实现方差显著负相关 r=−0.62（p≈10⁻²⁵¹），即高波动期使转移行"同质化"而非多样化；在 h=5 处 CK 诊断定位出 t∈[2120,2200] 等一阶记忆失效窗口。预测增益有限（h=10 时 ΔNLL=+0.025），贡献主要在结构诊断层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典马尔可夫转移算子虽有明确的数学结构与可解释性，但在高分辨率离散化下经验计数估计会崩溃：状态数 n 增大时潜在转移数按 n² 增长，而金融数据窗口有限且噪声大，导致绝大多数转移单元从未被观测，估计矩阵充满零值与高方差噪声，无法用于算子级结构分析。纯神经序列模型虽有表达力，却把动力学藏在隐状态中，无法直接检验、组合或做算子理论诊断（热图、TV 行距、Dobrushin 系数、CK 一致性）。因此需要一个"中间地带"：既保留经典随机算子的显式形式，又能在稀疏区给出平滑、可信的转移行估计。

Method: 1) 状态与算子构建：将日收益率经分位分箱离散为 n=55 个状态 X_t=φ(r_t)；设定特征条件的一阶马尔可夫假设 P(X_{t+1}=s_j|X_t=s_i, 历史, F_t)=P(X_{t+1}=s_j|X_t=s_i, F_t)。2) 神经参数化：对每个状态 s_i 与特征 F_t，用 MLP（隐层 64→128→256→128→64，GELU，dropout 0.2）拼接 one-hot 状态与特征向量后经 softmax 输出概率向量 Δ^{m_h−1}，逐行拼装得到显式算子 Â_t^{(h)}（n×m_h，m_h∈{10,20,35,55}；m_h=n 时为方形随机算子）。3) 训练：Adam + 权重衰减 + 梯度裁剪，基于验证 NLL 早停；可选平滑标签（将概率质量分配给邻近箱）交叉熵；全部预处理（分箱边界、标准化、特征排序）仅用训练集，时间序列 70/15/15 顺序切分，horizon h∈{1,2,5,10}。4) 对照与消融：状态无关基线（去掉 one-hot 状态，输出行不变）、加性/直接/回退（backoff）计数估计器。5) 算子级诊断：行异质性（平均成对 TV 距离）、行熵、Dobrushin 系数、CK 一致性（直接 h 步算子 vs 逐步复合 Ā_t^{(1:h)} 的逐行 KL/TV，定位于时间轴）、稀疏度统计（零单元比例、<5 观测比例）、ECE 与分块自助法置信区间。
