The offline trained policy determines routing strategies, while online bisection search iteratively adjusts the offloading decisions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed scheme can reduce the service delay by up to 31.85% compared with the benchmarks.

</details>


### [318] [Communication Offloading on SmartNIC DPUs: A Quantitative Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04842)
*Jacob Wahlgren,Andong Hu,Roger Pearce,Maya Gokhale,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计并评估了通信卸载引擎 Buddy——一种可在 Nvidia BlueField-3 SmartNIC DPU 或通用 x86 主机上运行的“fire-and-forget”消息路由服务。核心发现：内存访问/通信量之比（M:C ratio）是预测 DPU 卸载性能的关键指标；主机计算主导型负载（如 Quicksilver、Sparse Transpose）卸载后获得最高 1.55× 加速；而 DPU 缺乏 Direct Cache Access（DCA）支持导致相比 x86 高达 625× 的 DRAM 流量增长，成为未来 SmartNIC 设计的关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心正广泛采用 SmartNIC/DPU 以卸载主机 CPU 负载、降低能耗。"fire-and-forget" 异步通信模型通过消息聚合与多跳软件路由提升网络效率，但其路由服务传统上与应用进程共跑、争抢 CPU 算力与缓存（本为内存受限型，易污染缓存、拖慢核心计算）。因此需要把消息路由服务解耦并卸载到 DPU，同时需定量回答：哪些应用适合卸载、卸载的瓶颈在 DPU 的何处、未来 SmartNIC 应如何改进。

Method: 1) 设计 Buddy 通信库：runtime library（应用侧非阻塞发送/接收任意大小消息、聚合零拷贝缓冲区）+ routing agent（独立服务，处理本地进程与远端节点间的收发与按目的节点聚合，含 8 字节头+载荷的消息格式、O(n+m) 路由内核、预计算下一跳路由表、无可用缓冲区时的 blocklist 重试、空闲超时 flush）。C++ 实现，MPI 做引导与地址，ibverbs 实现 RDMA（对比发现 DPU DMA API 无性能优势），缓冲区预注册，OpenMP 多线程 + 每线程独立发送缓冲区避免锁竞争。2) 对比三种部署场景：None（同主机 SMT）、x86（卸载到独立 socket，作性能上界）、Dpu（卸载到 BlueField-3）。3) 五个应用：Histogram、Sparse Transpose、Triangle Counting、SSSP、QuickSilver。4) 用 HPCToolkit/perf 测量网络利用率、DRAM 流量、LLC miss 比例；通过注入干扰实验（8 核跑内存密集/通信密集负载）区分内存与链路瓶颈；在 Grace CPU + ConnectX-7 上改造 ib_send_bw 测量 cache miss 比例以检验 DCA 支持。5) 调参维度：远程通信缓冲大小、运行时缓冲数量、路由线程数；并在 8 节点集群做弱扩展测试。

Result: x86 卸载在全部应用均有收益（1.35×–1.77×）；DPU 卸载仅在三个应用受益：Histogram 1.13×、Sparse Transpose 1.40×、Quicksilver 1.55×，SSSP 0.99×、Triangle Counting 0.83× 无收益。M:C 比率区分明显：Quicksilver ≈72、Sparse Transpose 2.3（主机主导，收益最大），Histogram 1.5（均衡），SSSP 0.56、Triangle Counting 0.53（通信主导，收益消失）；网络利用率与该结论一致（Histogram 77%、Tri.Count 64%、SSSP 60%，Quicksilver 仅 0.1%）。调参收益：通信粒度 8 KiB 起 2–3× 提升、16–32 KiB 饱和（Triangle Counting 达 9.5×）；运行时缓冲增至约 8 个达平台期（SSSP 8.0×、Transpose 12.4×）；多线程在 7–8 线程时 Histogram 达 6.2×、SSSP 4.2×、Transpose 4.3×、Tri.Count 3.7×。可扩展性：Quicksilver 接近理想弱扩展，Histogram 的 agent-to-application 平均传输量从 3 节点 26 KB 降至 8 节点 8 KB，成为瓶颈。瓶颈分析：链路干扰几乎无影响，DPU 内存干扰导致 17% 降速；DPU 场景 DRAM 载入 153 GiB vs x86 249 MiB（约 625×），因 Intel DDIO 支持 DCA 而 BlueField-3 ARM 的 Cache Stashing 未生效；Grace CPU + CX-7 实测 cache miss 比例 48%（BlueField-3 为 30%，Intel 近乎 0），说明升级 CPU 并不能自动获得 DCA。作者亦指出此前关于 BlueField-2 已支持 DCA 的结论有误（未修改基准导致 CPU 不访问数据）。

Conclusion: DPU 卸载通信路由是可行的，但收益高度依赖应用的本地计算/通信平衡：M:C 比率可作为配置卸载的实用指标，主机主导型负载最受益，通信主导型负载无益甚至退化。关键瓶颈是 DPU 内存子系统——缺乏 Direct Cache Access 导致网络数据必须经主内存，产生 625× 的 DRAM 流量放大。因此未来 SmartNIC 设计应将 DCA 视为关键特性（并配合更强大的 CPU/内存系统，如 BlueField-4/Grace），同时需进一步调优 agent-to-application 的缓冲大小与空闲超时以改善均衡/通信主导负载的多节点可扩展性。

Abstract: SmartNIC Data Processing Units (DPUs) offer a promising solution for saving high-end CPU resources by offloading tasks to programmable cores near the network interface. In this work, we explore the feasibility of SmartNIC DPUs in supporting an asynchronous communication model called "fire-and-forget", particularly its core message routing service. We design a communication offloading engine called Buddy that decouples communication tasks from the application process. Buddy runs flexibly on SmartNIC DPUs such as the Nvidia BlueField-3 DPU and generic x86 CPUs. Our evaluation results in five applications identify the memory-to-communication ratio as a key predictor of the offloading performance. Host-dominated workloads, such as Quicksilver and Sparse Matrix Transpose, achieved up to 1.55x speedup with communication offloaded to the DPU. We further identify a 625x increase in DRAM traffic due to the absence of Direct Cache Access support on the DPU, highlighting a critical need in future SmartNIC designs.

</details>


### [319] [Piper: Efficient Large-Scale MoE Training via Resource Modeling and Pipelined Hybrid Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05049)
*Sajal Dash,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出面向HPC平台的MoE训练框架 Piper：在专家并行（EP）之上叠加流水线并行（PP），用数学资源模型 + 微基准测试 + MFU预测器自动搜索最优(PP, EP)配置，并配套拓扑感知分层All-to-All算法 HALO 与基于专家迁移的负载均衡机制。在Frontier超算上，相对 X-MoE 等SOTA框架 MFU 提升 2–3.6×，All-to-All 带宽提升 1.2–9×，并实现万亿参数MoE模型在20% MFU下训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE（Mixtral、DeepSeek、Qwen、Kimi 等）已成为前沿大模型的主流架构，但其在 HPC 平台上的训练面临三类固有难题：(1) 内存压力大——参数、优化器状态与激存活化叠加，且细粒度专家因激活数被放大 m 倍；(2) 通信量巨大——专家并行在每个 MoE 层触发 4 次 All-to-All（前后向各 2 次），在层次化、带宽不均的 Dragonfly/以太网互连上延迟主导；(3) 负载不均衡——路由器偏好导致“专家坍塌”，产生大量高瘦（tall-and-skinny）GEMM，GPU 利用率极低。已有框架（DeepSpeed-MoE、DeepSpeed-TED、X-MoE、Tutel）缺乏平台感知的混合并行规划，无法联合权衡内存、算力与通信：X-MoE 在 500B+ 规模上仅约 5% MFU。因此需要一个可量化、可自动选择并行策略的通用框架。

Method: 1) 资源建模：建立 MoE 训练的解析模型，覆盖静态内存（每参数 16 B：fp16 参数+梯度、fp32 主副本、动量与方差）、激活内存（注意力 12bsd+4bHs²、专家 2bsk(3d_ffn+d_model)，FlashAttention 下 4bHs²→2bHs）、专家数据并行下每 GPU 承担 E/EP 专家的模型（式 2）、以及 PP×EP 混合下 GPipe 与 1F1B 调度下的逐阶段峰值内存（式 3–5），指出 1F1B 首末阶段激活内存差达 (PP-1) 倍；通信模型推导 All-to-All 下界 T_a2a ≥ 4bsk·d_model/(EP·B_NIC)，并给出五条可行性约束（PP×EP=总GPU数、EP 整除专家数、PP≤L、EP≤g·N_h 限定于快速互连域、1F1B stage-0 峰值不超 HBM）以剪枝搜索空间。2) 微基准测试：对注意力（batch_size × head_dim）与专家 GEMM（num_tokens × batch_size × expert_dim）分别标定最优 kernel 组合，测量 2–64 GPU、跨 1–8 节点的 All-to-All 带宽与 P2P 延迟，代入 MFU 估计式（硬件效率 × 计算占比）用于候选配置打分排序。3) 执行器：基于 PyTorch 流水线并行 + 扩展 Tutel 专家并行，插桩 1F1B 调度并在 EP 组内加同步机制，实现 PP×EP×DP 三维并行。4) HALO All-to-All：按 GPU-NIC 亲和性构建通信组，三阶段（Phase I 节点内抽取与 AllToAllSingle；Phase II 跨节点 IndexSelect 打包 + RDMA 批量 P2P；Phase III 节点内再分发），依赖关系为 Phase I ∥ (Phase II → Phase III)，从而把节点内通信隐藏在跨节点传输之后；节点分配尽量约束在同机架，跨机架流量按 Rosetta 交换机分组批处理。5) 专家迁移：扩展路由器记录 token 分布，外部调度器检测失衡超阈值后，用爬山式交换算法求最小交换集，在 EP 组内迁移专家参数与优化器状态。

Result: Piper 在 Frontier 超算上相对 X-MoE 达到 2–3.6× MFU（摘要给出 2–3.5×），且用 8/32/80/512 张 MI250X 即可训练小/中/大/超大模型，而 X-MoE 分别需要 256/1024 张；HALO 相对厂商 RCCL 的 torch.dist.all_to_all 延迟降低 1.1–9×（摘要 1.2–9× 带宽提升，贡献节写 1.5–4×），16 节点以上收益最显著，小规模时两者相当；专家迁移摊销开销 < 5% 总训练时间，且即使发生专家坍塌也不损害最终模型质量；对 DeepSeek-V2/V3、Mixtral 8×7B/8×22B、Qwen3、Kimi K2 等 SOTA 模型可达到 20–50% MFU；在 1024 GPU 上以 33 TFLOPS 训练 1.7 万亿参数 MoE（64→1024 GPU 弱扩展效率 73%），达 20% MFU，远高于 X-MoE 的 5.23% MFU。

Conclusion: Piper 通过“数学资源建模—微基准标定—性能估计—流水线执行”的协同设计，证明将流水线并行叠加到专家并行之上能有效约束通信组规模、把 All-to-All 延迟局部化，是 HPC 上 MoE 训练 MFU 的决定性因素；同时表明平台感知（Dragonfly 拓扑、节点亲和、机架约束）与设备级负载再平衡（专家迁移）是与算法级路由损失互补的必要手段。框架用 Python/PyTorch 实现，具备跨平台可移植性，其模块化设计便于后续增量集成 kernel 优化、更好的调度与动态负载均衡策略。局限方面：模型对细粒度专家的性能预测仍偏低（粗粒度专家吞吐更优）、迁移在超大层（如 GLaM 1.2T 单 GPU 需 100+ GB 传输）时延显著、且整体收益强依赖 Frontier 式层次互连与节点分配策略。

Abstract: Frontier models increasingly adopt Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures to achieve large-model performance at reduced cost. However, training MoE models on HPC platforms is hindered by large memory footprints, frequent large-scale communication across heterogeneous networks, and severe workload imbalance. To characterize these challenges, we develop a mathematical model that quantifies memory, compute, and communication requirements for MoE configurations under various parallelization schemes, verified through micro-benchmarking, code instrumentation, and hardware profiling. Our analysis identifies performance bottlenecks: all-to-all latency at scale from expert parallelism, insufficient compute-communication overlap, low GPU utilization from imbalanced skinny GEMMs, and the absence of platform-aware hybrid parallelization strategies. To address these, we introduce Piper, a framework that leverages resource modeling to identify efficient training strategies for MoE models on target HPC platforms, applying pipeline parallelism with optimized schedules. Piper achieves 2-3.5X higher MFU than state-of-the-art frameworks such as X-MoE, and a novel all-to-all algorithm delivers 1.2-9X bandwidth over vendor implementation.

</details>


### [320] [Toward a Risk Assessment Framework for Institutional DeFi: A Nine-Dimension Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05145)
*Eva Oberholzer,Valeriy Zamaraiev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个扩展至9个维度的DeFi风险评估框架，在原有6个维度基础上新增可组合性风险、理解债务和时间风险动态三个维度，并引入透明度置信度修正量。通过对8000多个DeFi协议的本体论分析和12起重大DeFi事件（总计约25亿美元损失）的回顾性验证，证明了新增维度对完整根本原因表征的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi风险评估方法强调协议特定参数优化或概念性分类法，缺乏可解释性、可组合性感知且结构独立的评估方法，无法满足机构级DeFi采用的风险评估需求。

Method: 扩展Moody's Analytics和Gauntlet的六维度分类法，新增三个维度（可组合性风险、理解债务、时间风险动态），并引入透明度置信度修正量；在基于本体论的协议智能基础设施上进行协议依赖关系结构分析；对12起重大DeFi事件进行回顾性分析。

Result: 12起事件中有5起至少需要一个新维度才能完成根本原因表征，其中包括数据集中两起系统性影响最高的事件，验证了新增维度的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个包含9个维度的DeFi风险评估框架，通过基于本体论的协议智能基础设施对8000多个DeFi协议进行结构分析，并对2024-2026年12起重大DeFi安全事件（约25亿美元损失）进行回顾性验证，证明了新增维度的必要性和实用性。

Abstract: Decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols now intermediate over USD 100 billion in value, including regulated stablecoins and tokenized assets deployed as collateral, yet no widely adopted framework operationalizes risk assessment at the rigor institutional adoption demands. Existing approaches emphasize protocol-specific parameter optimization or conceptual taxonomies without providing explainable, composability-aware, and structurally independent assessment methodologies.
  We propose a nine-dimension DeFi risk assessment framework extending the six-dimension taxonomy introduced by Moody's Analytics and Gauntlet with three novel dimensions: composability risk, comprehension debt, and temporal risk dynamics. We additionally introduce a transparency confidence modifier separating assessment reliability from risk severity.
  The framework is grounded in structural analysis of protocol dependencies conducted through an ontology-based protocol intelligence infrastructure covering more than 8,000 DeFi protocols. We retrospectively analyze 12 major DeFi-related incidents from 2024-2026 representing approximately USD 2.5 billion in direct losses. Five of the 12 incidents require at least one novel dimension for complete root-cause characterization, including the two highest-systemic-impact events in the dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [321] [Nearly-Tight Bounds for Zonotope Containment and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04183)
*Friedrich Eisenbrand,Thomas Rothvoss,Matteo Russo,Ruben Skorupinski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究凸体包含问题 max{s>0 : sZ ⊆ Q}，其中外部体Q由 membership oracle 描述，内部体Z是 zonotope（胞腔）。主要贡献包括：(1) 给出采样-based O(√d) 近似算法，几乎匹配 oracle 模型中 Khot-Naor 的 Ω(√(d/lod d)) 下界；(2) 若 Talagrand 猜想成立（zonotope 可被线性数量生成元稀疏化），则可达最优因子 Θ(√(d/log d))；(3) 证明了 Δ-modular zonotope 的 Talagrand 猜想；(4) 对一般凸体给出 Θ(d/log d) 近似算法及下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体包含问题（最大包含缩放因子）是计算几何和凸优化的核心问题，在最优化、机器学习、鲁棒优化等领域有广泛应用。现有算法的近似因子存在差距，且 Talagrand 猜想（zonotope 稀疏化）未被证明。论文旨在缩小这一差距并建立 zonotope 稀疏化与谱稀疏化的联系。

Method: (1) 采样技术 + 随机化算法；(2) 利用子行列式界（Δ-modular 条件）证明 Talagrand 猜想；(3) 利用 Naszódi 的多面体逼近结果推导一般凸体的包含问题近似；(4) 通过归约（circumradius 计算）证明下界。

Result: (1) zonotope 包含问题：O(√d) 近似，假设 Talagrand 猜想则可达 Θ(√(d/log d))；(2) 证明 Δ-modular zonotope（Δ 为常数）满足 Talagrand 猜想，建立与 Batson-Spielman-Srivastava 谱稀疏化的联系；(3) 一般凸体包含问题：多项式时间 Θ(d/log d) 近似算法，且下界同样为 Ω(d/log d)；(4) 下界：zonotope 和中心对称凸集均存在 Ω(√(d/log d)) 或 Ω(d/log d) 的通用下界。

Conclusion: zonotope 包含问题接近最优解（假设 Talagrand 猜想），并首次证明 Δ-modular zonotope 的 Talagrand 猜想，建立其与谱稀疏化的深刻联系。一般凸体情形存在固有的 Ω(d/log d) 近似壁垒，Barvinok 的最优 O(√d) 多面体逼近在多项式时间内无法有效计算。

Abstract: We investigate the convex-body containment problem $\max\{s >0 : s Z \subseteq Q\}$, where the outer body $Q \subseteq \mathbb R^d$ is described by a membership oracle and the inner body $Z \subseteq \mathbb R^d$ is a zonotope. Our main result is a sampling-based $O(\sqrt{d})$-approximation algorithm for this problem that almost matches the lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{d/\log d})$ by Khot and Naor in the oracle model. Assuming zonotopes can be sparsified by a linear number of generators, which is referred to as Talagrand conjecture, our approach attains the optimal approximation factor of $Θ(\sqrt{d/\log d})$. Our second main result is a proof of Talagrand's conjecture for $Δ$-modular zonotopes whenever $Δ$ is constant. Those zonotopes are of the form $Z = \{ Wx \colon \| x\|_\infty \leq 1\}$ where the non-zero $d \times d$ sub-determinants of $W$ are between $1$ and $Δ$. This result establishes a connection between zonoid sparsification and spectral sparsification of Batson, Spielman and Srivastava. We complement these results with a universal $Ω(\sqrt{d/\log d})$ lower bound holding for all zonotopes.
  Finally, we consider containment problems $\max\{s >0 : s K \subseteq Q\}$, for general convex bodies $K \subseteq \mathbb R^d$. A result of Naszódi on approximating $K \subseteq \mathbb R^d$ by a polytope implies a $Θ(d/\log d)$ approximation algorithm in polynomial time. We show the tightness of this approximation factor in the oracle model via a reduction to the circumradius computation. Our lower bound holds for centrally symmetric convex sets, implying that Barvinok's optimal $O(\sqrt{d})$-approximation of a centrally symmetric convex body by a polytope with a polynomial number of vertices cannot be computed in polynomial time.

</details>


### [322] [Faster Iterative $φ$ Queries on the Positional BWT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04244)
*Paola Bonizzoni,Travis Gagie,Younan Gao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对PBWT数据结构的φ查询操作，提出基于精炼片段分解的新方法，获得两种优于先前工作的空间-时间权衡方案，特别适用于单倍型数量远小于位点数量的现代基因组数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效支持k次迭代的φ_j(i)查询是单倍型匹配和变异分析的关键，现有方法在空间或时间效率上存在改进空间。

Method: 将每条单倍型行分解为子区间（称为精炼片段），利用精炼片段的两个关键性质：(i) 每个片段至多与S_{φ_e(i)}的常数个片段重叠；(ii) 片段总数受O(Ð + h)约束。基于此分解设计两种数据结构。

Result: 方案一：O((Ð + h)log n)比特空间，O(log log_w min(m,h) + k)查询时间；方案二：O(Ð log h + h log n)比特空间，O(k log log_w h)查询时间。

Conclusion: 提出了两种空间-时间权衡方案，在特定条件下优于先前方法，特别是当h远小于m的实际基因组数据场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: The Positional Burrows-Wheeler Transform (PBWT) is a fundamental data structure for the efficient representation and analysis of large-scale haplotype panels. For a panel of $h$ sequences $\{S_1, \dots, S_h\}$ over $m$ sites, a key operation is the $φ_j(i)$ query, which returns the haplotype index immediately preceding $S_i$ in co-lexicographic order at site $j$. Efficient support for $k$ iterative queries $φ^1, \dots, φ^k$ is essential for haplotype matching and variation analysis.
  In this work, we introduce a simple and novel decomposition scheme that decomposes each haplotype row into sub-intervals, called refined segments, within which a haplotype's co-lexicographic predecessor for the sites remains unchanged. We show that refined segments satisfy two key properties: (i) each segment $[b,e]$ associated with $S_i$ overlaps with at most a constant number of segments of $S_{φ_e(i)}$, and (ii) the total number of segments is bounded by $O(\tilde{r} + h)$, where $\tilde{r}$ denotes the number of runs in the PBWT. Building on this decomposition, we present two space-time tradeoffs for supporting $k$ iterative $φ$ queries: (i) a structure using $O((\tilde{r} + h)\log n)$ bits of space that answers $k$ iterative queries in $O(\log \log_w \min(m,h) + k)$ time, where $n = m \cdot h$, and (ii) a more compact structure using $O(\tilde{r} \log h + h \log n)$ bits of space that supports queries in $O(k \log \log_w h)$ time.
  Prior to our work, supporting these queries required $O((\tilde{r} + h)\log n)$ bits of space and $O(k \cdot \log \log_w m)$ time. Our second tradeoff is expected to be effective in practice for modern genomic datasets, where the number $h$ of haplotypes is typically much smaller than the number $m$ of sites.

</details>


### [323] [Constructing Suffixient Arrays Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04258)
*Paola Bonizzoni,Younan Gao,Brian Riccardi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的单趟线性时间算法来构造后缀数组子集——后缀适数组（suffixient array），改进了之前Cenzato等人需要多次扫描或O(n + r̄ log σ)时间的构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cenzato等人提出的suffixient array虽然可以在给定SA、LCP和BWT的条件下线性构造，但现有算法需要多次扫描这些数组。单趟线性时间构造算法更具实用价值。

Method: 提出新的单趟（one-pass）算法，在标准RAM模型下直接构造suffixient array。

Result: 实现了在标准RAM模型下的线性时间单趟构造算法，时间复杂度为O(n)。

Conclusion: 该工作改进了suffixient array的构造效率，为其实际应用提供了更高效的算法基础。

Abstract: Recently, Cenzato et al.\ proposed a new text index, called the \emph{suffixient array}, which is a subset of the suffix array and supports locating a single pattern occurrence or finding its maximal exact matches (MEMs), assuming random access to the input text $T[1..n]$ is available. They show that, given the suffix array, the longest common prefix array, and the Burrows--Wheeler transform (BWT) of the reverse of $T[1..n]$ over an alphabet $\{1,\ldots,σ\}$, a suffixient array can be constructed in linear time. However, their construction algorithms require multiple scans of these arrays. When restricted to a single pass over the arrays, they present an alternative construction algorithm running in $O(n + \overline{r} \log σ)$ time, where $\overline{r}$ is the number of runs in the BWT of the reversed text. In this paper, we present a new one-pass algorithm that constructs a suffixient array in linear time under the standard RAM model.

</details>


### [324] [Submodular Ground-Set Pruning: Monotone Tightness and a Non-Monotone Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04428)
*Alan Kuhnle*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究子模最大化的"地集剪枝"（containment pruning）问题：先把地集 N 缩减为核 P（|P|≤p），使 P 对所有下游预算 k′≤k 都包含近似最优可行解（containment 因子 α）。结果：单调情形 1−1/e 是紧的（贪心达到，且任何剪枝预算 p≥k 都无法超越）；非单调情形首次给出 1/2−ε 的预算区间包含保证（顺序不相交贪心与单遍窗口法），并推广到背包约束（带边界碎片项，小物品条件下可得干净的 1/2−ε）。该 1/2 超过已知最优非单调最大化比 0.401 与硬度 0.478，说明剪枝可证明地比优化更容易。实验上 MaxCut 剪枝后精确 IP 求解加速约 620×，LLM 上下文选择中 38.7% HotpotQA 问题呈非单调，MuSiQue 上相关性门控子模多样性使金证据召回提升 9.2 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模子集选择（种子用户、传感器、特征、LLM 上下文段落等）需要从巨大地集中挑出小而有用的一组。子模最大化虽是标准模型，但在 value-oracle 模型下任何算法都需与地集规模成线性时间，数据（NCBI SRA、训练语料每年约 3.7× 增长）持续膨胀导致查询代价与下游求解器规模不可接受。两阶段工作流（先剪枝再在核上优化）在剪枝子模图、多阶段选择、学习式剪枝等文献中已有大量启发式，但此前缺乏"剪枝后是否仍保留高质量解"的理论刻画；已有的唯一理论结果 Nath & Kuhnle 的 QuickPrune 仅对单调弱 DR 函数给出 1/24−ε，且非单调情形基本空白。核心动机是：剪枝是否比直接优化更容易（例如能否突破单调 1−1/e 壁垒、非单调能否获得常数因子）？

Method: （1）形式化"预算区间子模剪枝"（Problem 1.1）：对所有 k′≤k 需 max_{T⊆P,|T|≤k′} f(T) ≥ α·OPT_{k′}；用密度相关组合界 α ≤ α*(k/n)/α*(k/p) 作为基准，说明其随 p 增大而变弱。（2）单调硬度（Thm 3.1）：以 HFWK 植入集构造为基础，把地集规模放大为 |N|=⌈60p/ε⌉，使输出与植入最优集的期望重叠 E|P∩T̃| ≤ pk/|N| ≤ ε′k/3 与 p 无关——"p 抵消"机制使额外预算无法泄露最优集；用两区间（加性/乘性）超几何尾界做零实例不可区分性论证；再经 SVW 线性增广推广到总曲率 κ，得到 1−κ/e 的紧界（Cor 3.3）。（3）单调快速版（Thm C.1）：小预算网格 + 几何扫描调用 Badanidiyuru–Vondrák 阈值贪心，随机瘦身至精确尺寸，|P|=O(k/ε)、Õ(n/ε²) 查询，达 1−1/e−ε。（4）非单调 1/2−ε（Thm 4.1）：ℓ=⌈1/ε⌉ 次不相交贪心，平均化选出最佳 run（损失 ≤1−1/ℓ），贪心支配给出 2f(A)−f(∅) ≥ f(Ô∪A)，再用格不等式分解得 max{f(A), f(Ȯ∪A)} ≥ (1−1/ℓ)OPT/2。（5）窗口法（Thm 4.3，单遍 O(nk) 查询）：维护 top-ωk 边际窗口并随机选取，同时获得"支配性"与"退化控制" e^{−1/ω}，得 e^{−1/ω}/2·OPT。（6）背包扩展（Thm 4.5）：改用密度贪心 + Feldman–Kuhnle 的 γ-拆分与 θ-构造，得 (1/2−ε)OPT_{B'}−Δ_{B'}；小物品条件下 Δ 可被吸收（Cor 4.6）。（7）附录 I 分析 BFNS 连续双贪心 warm-start 上界 4/9(1−1/ℓ)。实验包括 MaxCut（ER、植入分区、SNAP）、COMBHelper 学习基线、合成干扰覆盖分离实例、HotpotQA/MuSiQue 上下文选择与非单调代理 f̂(S)=FL(S)−θ(|S|)。

Result: 理论：单调基数剪枝 1−1/e 紧（Thm 3.1），且额外预算 p≥k 无用；γ-弱 DR 对应 1−e^{−γ}，曲率 κ 对应 1−κ/e 紧（Cor 3.3）；快速版 1−1/e−ε，|P|=O(k/ε)，Õ(n/ε²) 查询。非单调：首次给出 1/2−ε 的预算区间包含保证（Thm 4.1 SeqDisjoint 与 Thm 4.3 窗口法，后者输出 k+ωk²，α≥e^{−1/ω}/2，ω=5 时 0.410>0.401、ω=20 时 0.476 逼近 Qi 的 0.478）；背包下为 (1/2−ε)OPT−边界碎片项，小物品条件下恢复干净 1/2−ε（Thm 4.5、Cor 4.6），查询 O(n²/ε)、总代价 O(B/ε)。由于 1/2 高于已知最优非单调最大化比 0.401 与硬度 0.478，剪枝可证明地比优化更容易（且非 bicriteria：解规模严格 ≤ k）。实验：MaxCut 上 SeqDisjoint/WindowMax 含入率 α≥0.99（5 个 ER 种子均值 ≥0.996，std≤0.004），QuickPrune 停滞于 ≈0.87，Random 在 Wiki-Vote 降至 0.13；G(300,4500) 剪到 p=50 后精确 IP 从 120 s 降到 0.19 s（约 620× 加速，α=1.000）；COMBHelper 在分布内相当但分布外（SNAP/Facebook，teacher α=0.975）退化且需要精确标签；合成实例上 SeqDisjoint 含入真最优 78% vs 单次贪心 60%，出现 6.2%–16.5% 的价值分离。LLM：38.7%（77/199）HotpotQA 问题呈现非单调（更短前缀 F1 更高），代理上 α=1.0，代理引导剪枝仅用 3 段即达 F1/EM 0.599/0.42 优于全 10 段基线 0.556/0.40，而 QuickPrune 仅 0.262；MuSiQue 50 题试点上 RFL（嵌入门控）k=10 金证据召回 0.740，比 top-k TF-IDF 0.648 高 9.2 个百分点（bootstrap 95% CI [+2.8,+15.5]），但读者 F1 未同步提升（reader saturation）。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了子模剪枝（containment pruning）的复杂度：单调情形的 1−1/e 是信息论上界且紧，额外剪枝预算无法突破（曲率依赖版本同样紧）；非单调情形首次获得 1/2−ε 的常数因子预算区间包含保证并延伸至背包约束，说明"只要求包含好解"的剪枝任务可证明地比直接优化更容易（1/2 > 0.401 且 > 0.478 硬度）。实验表明包含核能显著加速精确求解（IP 约 620×），且非单调子模代理对 LLM 上下文选择是可用工具。开放问题：非单调包含因子能否从 1/2 提升到 1−1/e；非单调下界需超越组合法的信息论技术（区分"有用"与"有害"元素）、是否存在密度相关硬度、能否以 O(k) 核与 o(nk) 查询达到全预算 1−1/e−ε；单调下更复杂约束的紧算法；以及如何缓解 MuSiQue 上的"读者饱和"（更多金证据提升召回但 F1 不增）。

Abstract: Large-scale subset selection asks for a small useful set of examples, features, sensors, seed users, or context passages from an enormous ground set. Submodular maximization is a canonical model for such diminishing-returns problems, but rapidly growing datasets make even linear-time algorithms ever costlier. We study \emph{containment pruning}: first reduce the ground set to a smaller core $P$, then require that $P$ contain a near-optimal feasible solution for every downstream budget up to~$k$. Prior work has formulated many heuristics, but the theoretical limits of this preprocessing problem are largely unknown. For monotone submodular objectives, we prove that $1-1/e$ is tight: greedy achieves this containment factor, and no algorithm can beat it even with a larger pruning budget. For non-monotone objectives, we give the first$1/2-\varepsilon$ containment algorithms under cardinality constraints and extend the approach to knapsack constraints. This $1/2$ factor exceeds the best known algorithmic ratio and the known hardness threshold for non-monotone maximization, showing that pruning can be provably easier than optimization. Empirically, pruning lets an exact IP solver run on the reduced MaxCut instance with a ${\approx}620\times$ speedup, and proof-of-concept experiments on LLM context selection demonstrate the utility of non-monotone submodular proxies and our proposed containment algorithms.

</details>


### [325] [Robust Inverse Quadratic Error Decay with Meshing and Beam Search for Random Subset Sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04465)
*Edwin Chen,Christof Teuscher*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: The paper presents an efficient mesh-based algorithm for Random Subset Sum Problem with O(w log w) complexity, combined with a novel beam search heuristic achieving O(B/(n·w²)) expected error under mean-field assumptions, establishing a new practical baseline for subset sum approximation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Subset Sum Problem is a fundamental NP-complete problem critical to cryptography and combinatorial optimization with many real-world applications. The Random Subset Sum Problem (RSSP) provides a more applicable formulation where inputs follow i.i.d. distributions. The need exists for efficient algorithms that achieve practical error decay in approximation settings.

Method: Two-stage approach: (1) An O(w log w) time algorithm that constructs an O(B/w) mesh while trimming to w elements, achieving success probability 1-δ; (2) A novel beam search heuristic with linearithmic complexity w.r.t. list size n and beam width w, applied on the mesh structure for approximate solution finding.

Result: Under standard mean-field assumption with equal standard deviation, the beam search heuristic achieves expected error of O(B/(n·w²)). The algorithm demonstrates practical effectiveness of meshing for error decay and is empirically robust to multiple input distributions.

Conclusion: The proposed beam search heuristic is empirically robust across multiple input distributions, with simple extensibility to variants via modifications to the scoring heuristic. This work establishes a new practical baseline for robust subset sum error decay and ε-approximation theory.

Abstract: The Subset Sum Problem is a fundamental NP-complete problem in cryptography and combinatorial optimization, with many real-world applications. The Random Subset Sum Problem (RSSP) is a more applicable version of subset sum, where numbers are drawn from some i.i.d input distribution. We present an algorithm that, with probability $1-δ$, constructs the same $O(B/w)$ mesh as Da Cunha et al. (2023), while trimming to $w$ elements throughout and running in $O(w\log w)$ time. Then, we present a novel beam search heuristic running in linearithmic time w.r.t list size $n$ and beam width $w$ using the mesh that gives an expected error of $O\!\left(\frac{B}{nw^2}\right)$ under a standard mean-field assumption with equal standard deviation, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of meshing to achieve error decay. The algorithm is empirically robust to multiple input distributions and can naturally extend to variants with simple changes to the scoring heuristic, establishing a new practical baseline for robust subset sum error decay and $ε$-approximation theory.

</details>


### [326] [Online Orthogonal Vectors Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04798)
*Karthik Gajulapalli,Alexander Golovnev,Samuel King,Sidhant Saraogi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线正交向量问题(OnlineOV)，设计了新的确定性数据结构，在低维匹配最优随机算法，在中维首次超越2002年的结果，并利用结构-随机性分解方法否定了2017年的一个猜想。同时在非均匀强指数时间假设下证明了任意大的多项式空间下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OnlineOV是理论计算机科学的核心问题之一，此前最好的确定性算法停留在2002年，且存在未解决的 hardness 猜想。该研究旨在改进算法性能并澄清问题复杂度下界。

Method: 1. 新的结构-随机性分解技术(design algorithm)
2. 非均匀强指数时间假设(Non-Uniform SETH)作为复杂性假设
3. 通过条件规约证明下界

Result: 算法结果：在d=c log n时匹配Chan的随机算法；在d=n^ε时超越Charikar等人的2002年结果；否定Goldstein-Lewenstein-Porat的猜想。
下界结果：在Non-Uniform SETH下，任何亚线性查询时间的数据结构需要任意大的多项式空间，推广到Partial Match、Orthogonal Range Search、Approximate Nearest Neighbors等问题。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅在算法层面取得进展，还通过条件硬度下界深化了对OnlineOV及相关问题复杂性的理解，建立了结构化方法与随机性在算法设计中的作用联系。

Abstract: We prove new upper and lower bounds for the Online Orthogonal Vectors Problem ($\mathsf{OnlineOV}_{n,d}$). In this problem, a preprocessing algorithm receives $n$ vectors $x_1,\ldots,x_n\in\{0,1\}^d$ and constructs a data structure of size $S$. A query algorithm subsequently receives a query vector $q\in\{0,1\}^d$ and in time $T$ decides whether $q$ is orthogonal to any of the input vectors $x_i$.
  We design a new deterministic data structure for $\mathsf{OnlineOV}_{n,d}$. In low dimensions ($d = c \log n$), our data structure matches the performance of the best known randomized algorithm due to Chan [SoCG 2017]. Furthermore, in moderate dimensions ($d=n^{\varepsilon}$), we give the first improvement since Charikar, Indyk and Panigrahy [ICALP 2002]. Along the way, we give the first deterministic refutation of a conjecture on the hardness of $\mathsf{OnlineOV}$ posed by Goldstein, Lewenstein and Porat [ISAAC 2017]. This data structure also extends to a number of problems, including Partial Match, Orthogonal Range Search, and DNF Evaluation. We use a novel structure-versus-randomness decomposition to design our algorithm.
  Under the Non-Uniform Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis, we also prove arbitrarily large polynomial space lower bounds for any $\mathsf{OnlineOV}$ data structure with sublinear query time even with computationally unbounded preprocessing. These lower bounds extend to several other problems, including Polynomial Evaluation, Partial Match, Orthogonal Range Search, and Approximate Nearest Neighbors. We also prove similar lower bounds for $\mathsf{3-SUM}$ with preprocessing under the Non-Uniform Hamiltonian Path Conjecture.

</details>


### [327] [Faster Algorithms for Shortest Unique or Absent Substrings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04826)
*Panagiotis Charalampopoulos,Manal Mohamed,Solon P. Pissis,Hilde Verbeek,Wiktor Zuba*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了字符串中最短唯一子串（SUS）和最短缺失子串（SAS）问题，提出了一种O(n log σ/√log n)时间复杂度的算法，通过将问题分解为几何问题并结合同步集合、游程分析和小波树等技术，优于传统后缀树方法，并将算法扩展到SAS问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于后缀树的O(n)时间算法对于小字母表场景在word RAM模型下并非最优，需探索更高效的算法以匹配字符串存储和读取的O(n log σ/log n)复杂度上界。

Method: 采用分解策略，按子串的长度和周期对问题进行分析；综合运用同步集合（synchronizing sets）、游程（runs）分析和小波树（wavelet trees）等数据结构与算法技术；将SUS问题归约为几何问题求解；对于SAS问题，结合de Bruijn序列的高效构造方法进行扩展。

Result: 提出了时间复杂度为O(n log σ/√log n)的SUS算法；进一步将该算法扩展至SAS问题，同样达到O(n log σ/√log n)时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将问题分解为几何问题，并结合同步集合、游程分析和小波树等工具，实现了比传统后缀树方法更优的时间复杂度，同时将算法扩展到SAS问题，并利用de Bruijn序列的高效构造实现了类似的复杂度改进。

Abstract: We revisit two well-known algorithmic problems on strings: computing a shortest unique substring (SUS) and a shortest absent substring (SAS) of a string $S$ of length $n$. Both problems admit folklore $\mathcal{O}(n)$-time solutions using the suffix tree of $S$. However, for small alphabets, this complexity is not necessarily optimal in the word RAM model, where a string of length $n$ over alphabet $[0,σ)$ can be stored in $\mathcal{O}(n \log σ/\log n)$ space and read in $\mathcal{O}(n \log σ/\log n)$ time.
  We present an $\mathcal{O}(n \log σ/\sqrt{\log n})$-time algorithm for computing a SUS of $S$. This algorithm decomposes the problem according to the length and the period of the sought substring and uses several tools and techniques, such as synchronizing sets, the analysis of runs, and wavelet trees, to reduce the computation of a SUS to a simple geometric problem. Further, we adapt this algorithm and combine it with an efficient construction of de Bruijn sequences in order to obtain an $\mathcal{O}(n \log σ/\sqrt{\log n})$-time algorithm for computing a SAS of $S$.

</details>
